+// Standing PLAY server: real UI + local Claude proxy, pointed at the spring-ddd-bank (Java 11)
+// benchmark project, with JAVA_HOME=JDK11 so the agent can build/test it. Not a test (run-all
+// skips _-prefixed files). Run in the background; open http://127.0.0.1:18888.
+process.env.JAVA_HOME = 'D:\\tmp\\jdks\\jdk11\\jdk-11.0.31+11';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const PROXY = process.env.CB_PROXY || 'http://127.0.0.1:8080';
+const MODEL = LIVE_MODEL;
+const PORT = Number(process.env.CB_PORT || 18888);
+const PROJECT = process.env.CB_PROJECT || 'D:/tmp/bench/spring-ddd-bank';
+
+const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: () => 'x' });
+const html = (await readFile(new URL('../src/ui.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8'))
+  .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: MODEL, baseURL: PROXY }) + ';</script></head>');
+await startServer({
+  html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' },
+  defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: PROJECT,
+  modelsList: [{ id: 'claude-sonnet-4-6' }, { id: 'claude-opus-4-8' }, { id: 'claude-haiku-4-5' }],
+  adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic',
+});
+console.log(`PLAY server up: http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}  project=${PROJECT}  model=${MODEL}  JAVA_HOME=jdk11`);

+/**
+ * _pound.mjs — a long autonomous "real use" driver. Boots the REAL code_boss UI against the REAL
+ * Claude proxy and acts like a developer building a sizable app over many iterations via Puppeteer,
+ * having the code_boss AGENT do the building + testing. The point is NOT to assert app correctness —
+ * it's to POUND on code_boss and surface UX/render/flow papercuts. After every step it captures a
+ * screenshot + the transcript and runs an ANOMALY SCAN (leaked signal tags, duplicate bubbles,
+ * clean-verdict leaks, JS console/page errors, stuck status, stuck modals), appending findings to
+ * ISSUES.md.
+ *
+ * Resumable: the app project + its git + conversation persist on disk (D:\tmp\cb-pound\app); a
+ * state.json records completed step ids so a re-run skips them.
+ *
+ *   CB_POUND_BATCH=smoke,core,propose   node test/_pound.mjs   (comma-separated, runs in order)
+ *
+ * env: CB_PROXY (default http://127.0.0.1:8080) · CB_MODEL (default claude-sonnet-4-6) ·
+ *      CB_API_KEY · CB_ADAPTER=anthropic|openai (default anthropic; use openai for Gemini/genai.mil) ·
+ *      CB_POUND_DIR (default D:\tmp\cb-pound) · CB_PORT (default 28980) ·
+ *      CB_STEP_TIMEOUT_MS (default 480000) · CB_HEADFUL=1

+function findChrome() {
+  const c = process.platform === 'win32' ? [
+    (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+  ] : ['/usr/bin/google-chrome', '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome'];
+  return c.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });

+async function loadState() { try { return JSON.parse(await readFile(STATE, 'utf8')); } catch { return { done: [] }; } }
+async function saveState(s) { await writeFile(STATE, JSON.stringify(s, null, 2)); }
+
+let issueCount = 0;
+async function logIssue(stepId, kind, detail) {
+  issueCount++;
+  const line = `\n### [${stepId}] ${kind}\n${detail}\n`;
+  await appendFile(ISSUES, line);
+  console.log(`  ⚠ ISSUE [${stepId}] ${kind}: ${String(detail).slice(0, 160).replace(/\s+/g, ' ')}`);
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const chrome = findChrome();
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('No Chrome found'); process.exit(2); }
+  await mkdir(SHOTS, { recursive: true });
+  if (!existsSync(ISSUES)) await writeFile(ISSUES, `# code_boss pound-session issues\n\nproxy=${PROXY} model=${MODEL}\n`);
+
+  // First-time scaffold: a clean, committed project so it opens in subbranch mode (clean at open →
+  // CODE_BOSS branch auto-created on first write — exercises that path).
+  const fresh = !existsSync(path.join(PROJECT, '.git'));
+  if (fresh) {
+    await mkdir(PROJECT, { recursive: true });
+    await writeFile(path.join(PROJECT, 'README.md'), '# tasks\nA tiny task-tracker app (built iteratively by code_boss).\n');
+    await runGit(PROJECT, ['init', '-q', '-b', 'main']);
+    await runGit(PROJECT, ['config', 'user.email', 'pound@test']);
+    await runGit(PROJECT, ['config', 'user.name', 'pound']);
+    await runGit(PROJECT, ['add', '-A']);
+    await runGit(PROJECT, ['commit', '-q', '-m', 'init']);
+    console.log('scaffolded fresh project at', PROJECT);
+  }
+
+  // Default git poll to 4s in the pound test so the resolver batch doesn't wait 60s for conflict detection.
+  if (!process.env.CB_GIT_FETCH_MS) process.env.CB_GIT_FETCH_MS = '4000';
+  const adapterKey = process.env.CB_API_KEY || (ADAPTER_FORMAT === 'anthropic' ? 'proxy-adds-key' : undefined);
+  // CB_SUPABASE_KEY: Supabase publishable key sent as `apikey` header (required by some Supabase proxy deployments).
+  const supabaseKey = process.env.CB_SUPABASE_KEY;
+  // CF Access headers for a Cloudflare-Access-fronted proxy (home-proxy.philhignight.com).
+  const extraHeaders = {};
+  if (supabaseKey) extraHeaders.apikey = supabaseKey;
+  if (process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_ID) extraHeaders['CF-Access-Client-Id'] = process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_ID;
+  if (process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_SECRET) extraHeaders['CF-Access-Client-Secret'] = process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_SECRET;
+  const adapter = ADAPTER_FORMAT === 'openai'
+    ? createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: adapterKey, onRateLimit: (ms) => console.error(`  [rate-limit] ${ms}ms`), ...(Object.keys(extraHeaders).length ? { extraHeaders } : {}) })
+    : createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: adapterKey, onRateLimit: (ms) => console.error(`  [rate-limit] ${ms}ms`), ...(Object.keys(extraHeaders).length ? { extraHeaders } : {}) });
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace(
+    '</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: MODEL, baseURL: PROXY }) + ';</script></head>'
+  );
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: process.env.CB_API_KEY || 'proxy-adds-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], initialProject: PROJECT,
+    apiFormat: ADAPTER_FORMAT,
+  });
+
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: process.env.CB_HEADFUL ? false : 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  await page.setViewport({ width: 1440, height: 1000 });
+  const jsErrors = [];
+  page.on('pageerror', (e) => jsErrors.push('pageerror: ' + e.message));
+  page.on('console', (m) => { if (m.type() === 'error' && !/Failed to load resource/i.test(m.text())) jsErrors.push('console: ' + m.text()); });
+
+  const getStatus = () => page.evaluate(() => window.__sessionRef?.status);
+  const getEvents = () => page.evaluate(() => (window.__sessionRef?.events ?? []).map((e) => ({ type: e.type, taskId: e.taskId, kind: e.kind, content: typeof e.content === 'string' ? e.content.slice(0, 200) : undefined })));
+
+  let shotN = (await loadState()).shotN || 0;
+  const shot = async (name) => { try { shotN++; await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(SHOTS, `${String(shotN).padStart(3, '0')}-${name}.png`), fullPage: false }); } catch {} };
+
+  async function waitStarted() { await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__sessionRef?.status === 'running', { timeout: 30000 }).catch(() => {}); }
+  async function waitDone(ms = STEP_TIMEOUT) {
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) {
+      const s = await getStatus().catch(() => null);
+      if (s === 'done' || s === 'idle' || s === 'error') return s;
+      await sleep(700);
+    }
+    return 'TIMEOUT';
+  }
+  async function sendChat(text) {
+    await page.evaluate(() => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = ''; i.focus(); });
+    await page.type('#input', text);
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+  }
+  async function getLogText() { return page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('log')?.innerText || ''); }
+
+  // The anomaly scanner — high-signal checks against the rendered DOM + transcript.
+  async function scan(stepId, jsErrBefore) {
+    // new JS errors during this step
+    const newErrs = jsErrors.slice(jsErrBefore);
+    for (const e of newErrs) await logIssue(stepId, 'js-error', e);
+
+    const dom = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const log = document.getElementById('log');
+      const text = log ? log.innerText : '';
+      const LEAK = ['<verdict', '<task-progress', '<next-task', '<phase', '<checklist', '<plan ', '<plan>', '<remote-workspace', '<reviewer-finding', '<workflow-run', '<workflow-suggest', '<rollback', '<tick', '<design-attack'];
+      const leaks = LEAK.filter((t) => text.includes(t));
+      const bodies = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#log .body, #log .bubble-content')).map((b) => b.innerText.trim()).filter(Boolean);
+      // Tool-call rows legitimately repeat (e.g. two separate edits to the same file render as two
+      // "edit(file)" rows with different diffs) — exclude them; only flag duplicated PROSE bubbles.
+      const isToolRow = (t) => /^(edit|write|read|search|find|list|info|powershell|next-task|task-progress|remember|forget|git_clone|read_docx|edit_docx|grep|glob)\b\s*\(/i.test(t);
+      const dups = [];
+      const dupDiag = [];  // ALL adjacent-equal pairs (tool rows too) for diagnosis
+      for (let i = 1; i < bodies.length; i++) {
+        if (bodies[i] && bodies[i].length > 12 && bodies[i] === bodies[i - 1]) {
+          const isTool = isToolRow(bodies[i]);
+          dupDiag.push({ text: bodies[i].slice(0, 80), isTool });
+          if (!isTool) dups.push(bodies[i].slice(0, 80));
+        }
+      }
+      const cleanLeak = /\bno problems? found\b/i.test(text);
+      const emptyBackticks = /\bthe `` \b|use the `` /.test(text);
+      // filler "self-review" notes (the reviewer flagged a non-finding) + redundant closers
+      const reviewerNotes = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#log .reviewer-note')).map((n) => n.innerText.replace(/^\s*🔍\s*Self-review\s*•?\s*/i, '').trim());
+      const fillerReviews = reviewerNotes.filter((t) => /^\s*((i'?m |i am )?(ready (for|to)|all set|standing by|good to go|happy to (help|assist)|glad to (help|assist))|let me know|feel free|whenever you('re| are)|what would you like|what do you (want|need)|what'?s next|how (can|may) i (help|assist)|is there anything|anything else|shall i (proceed|continue))/i.test(t) || /next (instruction|task) whenever you are/i.test(t));
+      const closers = (text.match(/ready for your next (task|instruction)|let me know if you|happy to help|feel free to ask|whenever you('re| are) ready/gi) || []).length;
+      // visible overlays/modals still showing
+      const overlays = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[id$="Bg"], .modal-bg')).filter((m) => {
+        try { const s = getComputedStyle(m); return s.display !== 'none' && s.visibility !== 'hidden' && m.offsetParent !== null && m.classList.contains('show'); } catch { return false; }
+      }).map((m) => m.id || m.className);
+      // P3: a checklist panel stuck at N-1/N after the step completed (its task closed) is the
+      // "never reaches done" bug. (May be a soft signal mid-multi-step — verify against the shot.)
+      const clTitles = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.cl-title')).map((e) => e.innerText.trim());
+      // Only flag a checklist as incomplete when at least 1 item IS done but not all are done.
+      // 0/N is "not yet started" — expected in multi-step flows (e.g. propose-plan creates a plan
+      // but the checklist items are only checked off during the subsequent propose-approve step).
+      const clIncomplete = clTitles.map((t) => { const m = t.match(/(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*done/i); return m && Number(m[1]) > 0 && Number(m[1]) < Number(m[2]) ? t : null; }).filter(Boolean);
+      return { textLen: text.length, leaks, dups, dupDiag, cleanLeak, emptyBackticks, overlays, clIncomplete };
+    });
+    if (dom.leaks.length) await logIssue(stepId, 'leaked-tags', `Visible chat shows raw tags: ${dom.leaks.join(' ')}`);
+    if (dom.dupDiag?.length) console.log(`  [${stepId}] dup-pairs (ALL): ${dom.dupDiag.map((d) => (d.isTool ? '(tool)' : '!') + d.text).join(' | ')}`);
+    if (dom.dups.length) await logIssue(stepId, 'duplicate-bubbles', `Consecutive identical bubbles: ${dom.dups.join(' | ')}`);
+    if (dom.cleanLeak) await logIssue(stepId, 'clean-verdict-leak', `"no problems found" visible in chat`);
+    if (dom.emptyBackticks) await logIssue(stepId, 'empty-backticks', `Dangling empty backticks visible (stripped tag artifact)`);
+    if (dom.overlays.length) await logIssue(stepId, 'stuck-overlay', `Overlay still shown after step: ${dom.overlays.join(', ')}`);
+    if (dom.fillerReviews?.length) await logIssue(stepId, 'filler-self-review', `Reviewer flagged a NON-finding (filler) as a 🔍 Self-review: ${dom.fillerReviews.map((t) => '"' + t.slice(0, 60) + '"').join(' ; ')}`);
+    if (dom.closers >= 2) await logIssue(stepId, 'redundant-closers', `${dom.closers} redundant "ready for your next / let me know" closers in the transcript`);
+    if (dom.clIncomplete?.length) await logIssue(stepId, 'checklist-incomplete', `Checklist panel not N/N after step completed: ${dom.clIncomplete.join(' ; ')} (verify vs the shot — may be mid-multi-step)`);
+    return dom;
+  }
+
+  // ── feature drivers ──
+  async function setToggle(which, to) {
+    // Auto-approve: #reviewLabel has class 'off' when Auto-approve is OFF (review ON).
+    // Discuss: #editToggle reads "Edit" (write mode) vs read-only (discuss). The hidden #discussToggle is the truth.
+    const state = await page.evaluate(() => ({
+      reviewOff: document.getElementById('reviewLabel')?.classList.contains('off'),
+      discussOn: document.getElementById('discussToggle')?.checked,
+    }));
+    if (which === 'autoapprove') {
+      const wantOff = to === 'off';
+      if (!!state.reviewOff !== wantOff) await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('reviewLabel')?.click());
+    } else if (which === 'discuss') {
+      const wantOn = to === 'on';
+      if (!!state.discussOn !== wantOn) await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('editToggle')?.click());
+    }
+    await sleep(300);
+  }
+  async function approvePlan(stepId) {
+    // Poll for up to 3 s (15 × 200 ms) — the approve span may take a moment to
+    // become visible after the status-done SSE event fires (plan card render, panel expand, etc.)
+    let clicked = null;
+    let debugState = null;
+    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 15 && !clicked; attempt++) {
+      ({ clicked, debugState } = await page.evaluate(() => {
+        // The approve affordance is a <span id="approveImplementBtn"> in the bottom chrome.
+        const span = document.getElementById('approveImplementBtn');
+        const spanDisplay = span ? getComputedStyle(span).display : 'missing';
+        if (span && spanDisplay !== 'none') { span.click(); return { clicked: '#approveImplementBtn', debugState: null }; }
+        const b = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('button')).reverse().find((x) => /approve|implement/i.test(x.textContent) && x.offsetParent !== null);
+        if (b) { b.click(); return { clicked: b.textContent.trim(), debugState: null }; }
+        return { clicked: null, debugState: `display=${spanDisplay}` };
+      }));
+      if (!clicked) await sleep(200);
+    }
+    if (!clicked) {
+      await logIssue(stepId, 'no-approve-button', `Expected an approve/implement affordance after a propose run; none found/visible. Last state: ${debugState}`);
+      return false;
+    }
+    console.log('  approved via:', clicked);
+    return true;
+  }
+  async function doIntake(stepId, text, clarify) {
+    await page.evaluate(() => { try { selectSideTab('assignments'); } catch {} try { openIntakeModal(); } catch {} });
+    const formOk = await page.waitForFunction(() => { const bg = document.getElementById('intakeModalBg'); return bg && bg.classList.contains('show'); }, { timeout: 6000 }).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+    if (!formOk) { await logIssue(stepId, 'intake-no-form', 'intake modal did not open via openIntakeModal()'); return; }
+    await page.type('#im-input', text);
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    const hasCardFn = () => { const r = document.getElementById('im-req'); return !!(r && r.value.trim().length > 0); };
+    await page.waitForFunction(hasCardFn, { timeout: 150000 }).catch(() => {});
+    let hasCard = await page.evaluate(hasCardFn);
+    if (!hasCard && clarify) {
+      await page.type('#im-input', clarify);
+      await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+      await page.waitForFunction(hasCardFn, { timeout: 150000 }).catch(() => {});
+      hasCard = await page.evaluate(hasCardFn);
+    }
+    if (!hasCard) { await logIssue(stepId, 'intake-no-card', 'intake produced no requirement card (manager kept clarifying)'); return; }
+    await page.evaluate(() => { const b = document.getElementById('addFromIntakeBtn'); if (b && !b.disabled) b.click(); });
+    const ready = await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelectorAll('#backlogList .backlog-row .asg-play').length > 0, { timeout: 10000 }).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+    if (!ready) { await logIssue(stepId, 'intake-no-capture', 'captured card did not appear as a ready backlog assignment'); return; }
+    await shot(stepId + '-captured');
+    // The side panel may still be animating its expand transition (120ms CSS).
+    // Use a direct DOM click so we don't depend on the element being fully visible.
+    const clicked = await page.evaluate(() => { const btn = document.querySelector('#backlogList .asg-play'); if (btn) { btn.click(); return true; } return false; });
+    if (!clicked) { await logIssue(stepId, 'no-play-btn', 'play button vanished before click'); return; }
+    await waitStarted();
+    const s = await waitDone();
+    if (s === 'TIMEOUT') await logIssue(stepId, 'assignment-timeout', 'assignment run never terminated');
+    const gate = await page.waitForFunction(() => !!document.querySelector('.row.assignment-gate'), { timeout: 10000 }).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+    if (!gate) await logIssue(stepId, 'no-acceptance-gate', 'assignment finished but no acceptance-gate row appeared');
+  }
+  async function openMemoryPanel(stepId) {
+    const mem = await page.evaluate(async () => { try { return await (await fetch('/api/memory')).json(); } catch { return { entries: [] }; } });
+    await page.evaluate(() => { try { openMemory(); } catch {} });
+    const ok = await page.waitForFunction(() => { const p = document.getElementById('pane-memory'); return p && !p.classList.contains('hidden') && !!document.getElementById('memoryList'); }, { timeout: 6000 }).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+    if (!ok) { await logIssue(stepId, 'memory-panel-no-open', 'memory tab did not open'); return; }
+    const boxes = await page.$$eval('#memoryList .bullet-box', (els) => els.length).catch(() => 0);
+    console.log(`  memory entries=${mem.entries?.length ?? 0} listBoxes=${boxes}`);
+    if ((mem.entries?.length ?? 0) > 0 && boxes === 0) await logIssue(stepId, 'memory-panel-empty', `/api/memory has ${mem.entries.length} entries but the panel shows 0 boxes`);
+  }
+  async function doSteer(stepId, text, steerText) {
+    await sendChat(text);
+    await waitStarted();
+    await sleep(2500);
+    await page.evaluate(() => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = ''; i.focus(); });
+    await page.type('#input', steerText);
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    const s = await waitDone();
+    if (s === 'TIMEOUT') await logIssue(stepId, 'steer-timeout', 'run never terminated after steer');
+  }
+  async function doCancel(stepId, text) {
+    await sendChat(text);
+    await waitStarted();
+    await sleep(3500);
+    // Capture the state Esc will act on AND install a /api/cancel post counter. The OLD
+    // version waited 180s for terminal — longer than this task naturally takes (~150s on
+    // haiku) — so a no-op Esc "passed" because the run simply finished on its own. A real
+    // cancel posts /api/cancel and reaches terminal within a few seconds; measure both.
+    const pre = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      window.__cancelPosts = 0;
+      if (!window.__cancelHooked) {
+        window.__cancelHooked = true;
+        const orig = window.fetch.bind(window);
+        window.fetch = (u, o) => { if (String(u).includes('/api/cancel')) window.__cancelPosts++; return orig(u, o); };
+      }
+      // A CAPTURE-phase probe records that the Escape keydown was actually dispatched to
+      // the document AND the overlay state at that instant — so a swallowed Esc names its
+      // swallower. Capture fires before any bubble-phase stopImmediatePropagation (the
+      // mermaid-viewer handler) and a same-target capture stopPropagation (the ad-hoc
+      // confirm modal) does NOT stop other capture listeners, so this always sees it.
+      // Is an element actually VISIBLE (not just .show)? A .show overlay that is
+      // display:none / detached / zero-box is "stuck-invisible" — the developer sees
+      // nothing yet it silently swallows Esc (the real "frozen page" bug). A visible
+      // overlay swallowing Esc is just correct layering (press Esc again).
+      const vis = (el) => {
+        if (!el) return false;
+        try { const s = getComputedStyle(el); return s.display !== 'none' && s.visibility !== 'hidden' && el.offsetParent !== null && el.getClientRects().length > 0; } catch { return false; }
+      };
+      const mm = document.getElementById('mmViewer');
+      const dm = document.getElementById('docMode');
+      const overlays = () => ({
+        mmViewerShow: mm?.classList.contains('show') || false,
+        mmViewerVisible: !!(mm?.classList.contains('show') && vis(mm)),
+        // #docMode is a capture-phase Esc swallower too (ui.html:7266) — a left-panel
+        // side view, so it can be .show while the chat is live; it was the actual
+        // cancel-not-posted culprit, missed by the earlier overlay-only probe.
+        docModeShow: dm?.classList.contains('show') || false,
+        modalShow: document.getElementById('modalBg')?.classList.contains('show') || false,
+        settingsShow: document.getElementById('settingsModalBg')?.classList.contains('show') || false,
+        adhoc: [...document.querySelectorAll('.modal-bg.show')].map((m) => `${m.id || m.className}${vis(m) ? ':vis' : ':hidden'}`),
+        activeEl: (document.activeElement && (document.activeElement.id || document.activeElement.tagName)) || '',
+      });
+      if (!window.__escProbe) {
+        window.__escProbe = { seen: 0, last: null };
+        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
+          if (e.key === 'Escape') { window.__escProbe.seen++; window.__escProbe.last = overlays(); }
+        }, true);
+      }
+      return { status: window.__sessionRef?.status, subagents: (typeof activeSubagentCount === 'function') ? activeSubagentCount() : -1, ...overlays() };
+    });
+    await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    const s = await waitDone(25000);
+    const elapsed = Date.now() - t0;
+    const post = await page.evaluate(() => ({ posts: window.__cancelPosts || 0, escSeen: window.__escProbe?.seen || 0, esc: window.__escProbe?.last || null }));
+    const hadWork = pre.status === 'running' || pre.subagents > 0;
+    // Esc had active work to stop but never posted /api/cancel → a swallowed Esc. The
+    // overlays at Esc time name the swallower: mmViewerShow=true → the mermaid-viewer
+    // keydown handler (stopImmediatePropagation); adhoc=[…] → an ad-hoc confirm modal's
+    // capture listener; escSeen=0 → the keydown never reached the document at all.
+    if (hadWork && post.posts === 0)
+      await logIssue(stepId, 'cancel-not-posted', `Esc did not post /api/cancel though work was active (status=${pre.status} subagents=${pre.subagents} modalShow=${pre.modalShow} settingsShow=${pre.settingsShow} mmViewerShow=${pre.mmViewerShow} mmViewerVisible=${pre.mmViewerVisible} adhoc=[${pre.adhoc.join('|')}] activeEl=${pre.activeEl} || atEsc: escSeen=${post.escSeen} ${post.esc ? JSON.stringify(post.esc) : 'no-capture'})`);
+    if (s === 'TIMEOUT')
+      await logIssue(stepId, 'cancel-no-stop', `run never reached terminal within 25s after Esc (pre: status=${pre.status} subagents=${pre.subagents} cancelPosts=${post.posts})`);
+    else if (hadWork && post.posts > 0 && elapsed > 20000)
+      await logIssue(stepId, 'cancel-slow', `cancel posted but took ${Math.round(elapsed / 1000)}s to reach terminal — Esc should abort promptly`);
+    if (s === 'error') await logIssue(stepId, 'cancel-error-state', 'cancel left the session in error state');
+  }
+  async function doSettings(stepId) {
+    await page.evaluate(() => { try { openSettings(); } catch {} });
+    const opened = await page.waitForFunction(
+      () => document.getElementById('settingsModalBg')?.classList.contains('show'),
+      { timeout: 5000 }
+    ).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+    if (!opened) { await logIssue(stepId, 'settings-no-open', 'settings modal did not open'); return; }
+    const proj = await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('settingsProject')?.textContent || '');
+    if (!proj || proj === '(none)') await logIssue(stepId, 'settings-no-project', `#settingsProject shows "${proj}" — expected a real path`);
+    const plugins = await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('settingsPlugins')?.children?.length || 0);
+    if (plugins === 0) await logIssue(stepId, 'settings-no-plugins', 'settings plugins list is empty');
+    await page.evaluate(() => { try { closeSettings(); } catch {} });
+    const closed = await page.waitForFunction(
+      () => !document.getElementById('settingsModalBg')?.classList.contains('show'),
+      { timeout: 3000 }
+    ).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+    if (!closed) await logIssue(stepId, 'settings-stuck', 'settings modal did not close');
+  }
+  async function doViewportResize(stepId, width, height) {
+    await page.setViewport({ width, height });
+    await sleep(500);
+    const overflow = await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth > document.documentElement.clientWidth);
+    if (overflow) await logIssue(stepId, 'layout-overflow', `horizontal overflow at ${width}px viewport`);
+    const inputOk = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const el = document.getElementById('input');
+      if (!el) return false;
+      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
+      return r.width > 20 && r.height > 0;
+    });
+    if (!inputOk) await logIssue(stepId, 'input-invisible', `#input not visible/usable at ${width}px`);
+  }
+  // Create an add/add conflict: local main has only README.md; we set origin/main to also have
+  // src/server.js (simple content), which conflicts with the CODE_BOSS branch's src/server.js.
+  async function doResolverSetup(stepId) {
+    const alreadySetup = existsSync(REMOTE);
+    if (!alreadySetup) {
+      // Init bare remote and push local main (README.md only — the scaffold commit)
+      await runGit(PROJECT, ['init', '--bare', REMOTE]);
+      await runGit(PROJECT, ['remote', 'add', 'origin', REMOTE]);
+      await runGit(PROJECT, ['push', 'origin', 'main:main']);
+      // Clone the bare remote, add src/server.js (simple content), push back
+      await runGit(ART, ['clone', REMOTE, CLONE]);
+      await runGit(CLONE, ['config', 'user.email', 'remote@test']);

+      await runGit(CLONE, ['add', '-A']);
+      await runGit(CLONE, ['commit', '-m', 'Remote: add stub server']);
+      await runGit(CLONE, ['push', 'origin', 'main']);
+    } else {
+      const remoteOk = await runGit(PROJECT, ['remote', 'get-url', 'origin']);
+      if (remoteOk.exitCode !== 0) await runGit(PROJECT, ['remote', 'add', 'origin', REMOTE]);
+    }
+    // Fetch so origin/main tracking ref exists and is up to date
+    await runGit(PROJECT, ['fetch', 'origin']);
+    // Wait for git poll to detect the conflict badge (poll runs every CB_GIT_FETCH_MS, default 4s)
+    const badge = await page.waitForFunction(
+      () => !!document.querySelector('.pull-btn-conflict'),
+      { timeout: 90000 }
+    ).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+    if (!badge) await logIssue(stepId, 'resolver-no-conflict-badge', 'merge bar did not show conflict badge within 90s after git setup');
+  }
+  async function doResolverOpen(stepId) {

+  async function doImagePaste(stepId) {
+    // Create a 40×20 canvas image (solid color with a text-like checkerboard so Windows OCR
+    // has something to chew on) and inject it via ocrAttachFiles() directly — bypasses the
+    // DataTransfer/ClipboardEvent read-only restriction in headless Chrome.

+        ctx.fillStyle = '#000000'; ctx.font = '14px Arial'; ctx.fillText('Hello OCR', 5, 20);
+        return await new Promise((resolve) => {
+          canvas.toBlob((blob) => {
+            if (!blob) { resolve({ ok: false, error: 'canvas.toBlob returned null' }); return; }
+            const file = new File([blob], 'pound-test.png', { type: 'image/png' });
+            try {
+              ocrAttachFiles([file]);
+              resolve({ ok: true, fileSize: blob.size });
+            } catch (e) { resolve({ ok: false, error: e.message }); }
+          }, 'image/png');
+        });
+      } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: e.message }; }
+    });
+    if (!injected.ok) { await logIssue(stepId, 'img-inject-failed', `ocrAttachFiles injection failed: ${injected.error}`); return; }
+    console.log(`  [${stepId}] injected ${injected.fileSize}b PNG into ocrAttachFiles`);

+    // Attachment strip should become visible

+      () => { const s = document.getElementById('attStrip'); return s && !s.hidden; },
+      { timeout: 3000 }
+    ).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+    if (!stripVisible) { await logIssue(stepId, 'img-strip-not-visible', '#attStrip did not become visible after ocrAttachFiles'); return; }

+    ).then(() => true).catch(() => false);

+    if (thumbCount === 0) await logIssue(stepId, 'img-no-thumb', 'no .att-thumb rendered in #attThumbs after OCR');

+    await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const x = document.querySelector('#attThumbs .att-x');
+      if (x) x.click();

+    const cleaned = await page.waitForFunction(
+      () => { const s = document.getElementById('attStrip'); return !s || s.hidden; },
+      { timeout: 3000 }
+    ).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+    if (!cleaned) await logIssue(stepId, 'img-strip-not-cleared', '#attStrip still visible after removing attachment');
+  }
+  async function doTaskUndo(stepId) {
+    // Find the last task-end event that has commits so we can revert it.
+    const taskId = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const events = window.__sessionRef?.events ?? [];
+      const last = [...events].reverse().find((e) => e.type === 'task-end' && (e.commitHash || (e.commits && e.commits.length > 0)));
+      return last?.taskId ?? null;
+    });
+    if (!taskId) { await logIssue(stepId, 'tu-no-committed-task', 'no task-end event with commits found in session'); return; }
+    console.log(`  [${stepId}] undoing task ${taskId}`);
+    const beforeLen = await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('log')?.innerText?.length ?? 0);
+    // Call the API directly — bypasses the confirm() dialog (non-interactive headless run).
+    const result = await page.evaluate(async (id) => {
+      const r = await fetch('/api/task-undo', {
+        method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ taskId: id }),
+      });
+      const data = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
+      return { ok: r.ok, status: r.status, data };
+    }, taskId);
+    if (!result.ok) { await logIssue(stepId, 'tu-api-error', `POST /api/task-undo returned ${result.status}: ${JSON.stringify(result.data).slice(0, 200)}`); return; }
+    const results = result.data?.results ?? [];
+    const ok = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'ok').length;
+    const skip = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'skip').length;
+    const err = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'error').length;
+    console.log(`  [${stepId}] undo response: ok=${ok} skip=${skip} err=${err}`);
+    if (err > 0) await logIssue(stepId, 'tu-revert-error', `git revert failed in ${err} repo(s): ${results.filter((r) => r.status === 'error').map((r) => r.message).join('; ')}`);
+    // Server broadcasts a task-undo event via SSE → UI renders a row in #log.
+    const rowAppeared = await page.waitForFunction((n) => (document.getElementById('log')?.innerText?.length ?? 0) > n, { timeout: 8000 }, beforeLen).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+    if (!rowAppeared) await logIssue(stepId, 'tu-no-row', '#log did not update after task-undo (expected SSE task-undo row)');
+    else {
+      const logText = await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('log')?.innerText ?? '');
+      if (!/revert/i.test(logText)) await logIssue(stepId, 'tu-row-text', '#log updated but no "revert" text found (task-undo row may not have rendered)');
+    }
+  }
+  async function doInstanceLockCheck(stepId) {
+    const lockPath = path.join(PROJECT, '.code_boss', 'instance.lock');
+    if (!existsSync(lockPath)) { await logIssue(stepId, 'mi-no-lock', 'instance.lock not found while project is open'); return; }
+    try {
+      const content = JSON.parse(await readFile(lockPath, 'utf8'));
+      if (!content.pid) await logIssue(stepId, 'mi-lock-no-pid', `instance.lock missing pid field: ${JSON.stringify(content)}`);
+      if (!content.port) await logIssue(stepId, 'mi-lock-no-port', `instance.lock missing port field: ${JSON.stringify(content)}`);
+      console.log(`  [${stepId}] lock ok: pid=${content.pid} port=${content.port}`);
+    } catch (e) { await logIssue(stepId, 'mi-lock-invalid', `instance.lock is not valid JSON: ${e.message}`); }
+  }
+  async function doInstanceLockReject(stepId) {
+    const lockPath = path.join(PROJECT, '.code_boss', 'instance.lock');
+    // Close project to release our lock
+    await page.evaluate(() => fetch('/api/close-project', { method: 'POST' }));
+    await sleep(800);
+    // Write a fake lock with PID 4 (Windows System process — process.kill(4,0) → EPERM = "alive").
+    await mkdir(path.join(PROJECT, '.code_boss'), { recursive: true });
+    await writeFile(lockPath, JSON.stringify({ pid: 4, port: PORT, startedAt: Date.now() }));
+    // Try to open the project — must be rejected with "already open" error.
+    const resp = await page.evaluate(async (p) => {
+      const r = await fetch('/api/open-project', {
+        method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ path: p }),
+      });
+      const data = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));
+      return { ok: r.ok, status: r.status, error: data.error };
+    }, PROJECT);
+    if (resp.ok) {
+      await logIssue(stepId, 'mi-lock-not-enforced', 'open-project succeeded despite live-PID lock file (expected rejection)');
+    } else if (!/already open/i.test(resp.error || '')) {
+      await logIssue(stepId, 'mi-wrong-error', `open-project blocked but unexpected error: "${resp.error}"`);
+    } else {
+      console.log(`  [${stepId}] correctly rejected: "${resp.error?.slice(0, 120)}"`);
+    }
+    // Remove fake lock so mi-reopen can succeed.
+    await rm(lockPath, { force: true });
+  }
+  async function doInstanceReopen(stepId) {

+    for (const BATCH of BATCHES_TO_RUN) {
+      const steps = BATCHES[BATCH];
+      await shot(`${BATCH}-loaded`);
+
+      for (const step of steps) {
+        if (state.done.includes(step.id)) { console.log(`(skip ${step.id} — already done)`); continue; }
+        console.log(`\n=== [${BATCH}] ${step.id} (${step.kind}) ===`);
+        const jsErrBefore = jsErrors.length;
+        const t0 = Date.now();

+          if (step.kind === 'chat') {

+            const appeared = await page.waitForFunction(() => {
+              const t = document.getElementById('log')?.innerText || '';
+              return t.length > 0;
+            }, { timeout: 8000 }).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+            await waitStarted();
+            const s = await waitDone();
+            if (s === 'TIMEOUT') await logIssue(step.id, 'status-timeout', `status never reached terminal within ${STEP_TIMEOUT}ms`);
+            if (s === 'error') await logIssue(step.id, 'status-error', `run ended in error state`);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'toggle') {
+            await setToggle(step.toggle, step.to);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'approvePlan') {
+            const ok = await approvePlan(step.id);
+            if (ok) { await waitStarted(); const s = await waitDone(); if (s === 'TIMEOUT') await logIssue(step.id, 'status-timeout', 'implement run never terminated'); }
+          } else if (step.kind === 'intake') {
+            await doIntake(step.id, step.text, step.clarify);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'memoryPanel') {
+            await openMemoryPanel(step.id);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'steer') {
+            await doSteer(step.id, step.text, step.steerText);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'cancel') {
+            await doCancel(step.id, step.text);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'settingsModal') {
+            await doSettings(step.id);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'viewportResize') {
+            await doViewportResize(step.id, step.width, step.height);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'resolverSetup') {
+            await doResolverSetup(step.id);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'resolverOpen') {
+            await doResolverOpen(step.id);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'resolverChat') {
+            await doResolverChat(step.id, step.text);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'resolverCancel') {
+            await doResolverCancel(step.id);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'imagePaste') {
+            await doImagePaste(step.id);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'taskUndo') {
+            await doTaskUndo(step.id);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'instanceLockCheck') {
+            await doInstanceLockCheck(step.id);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'instanceLockReject') {
+            await doInstanceLockReject(step.id);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'instanceReopen') {
+            await doInstanceReopen(step.id);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'resolverApprove') {
+            await doResolverApprove(step.id);
+          } else if (step.kind === 'resolverApply') {
+            await doResolverApply(step.id);
+          }
+        } catch (e) {
+          await logIssue(step.id, 'driver-threw', e?.message || String(e));
+        }
+        await sleep(600);
+        await shot(step.id);
+        const dom = await scan(step.id, jsErrBefore);
+        const evs = await getEvents().catch(() => []);
+        console.log(`  done in ${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s · logChars=${dom.textLen} · events=${evs.length} · leaks=${dom.leaks.length} dups=${dom.dups.length}`);
+        // A step that produced NOTHING is the one failure this scan used to miss. On 2026-08-14 a stale instance
+        // lock stopped the project from opening, so all four chat steps sent a message into a dead session and
+        // came back in 31s with events=0 — and the session was reported CLEAN, because every check here looks for
+        // something WRONG in the output and there was no output to be wrong. A hunt that cannot fail is worse
+        // than no hunt. Steps that legitimately produce no chat events are exempted by kind.
+        const NO_CHAT_KINDS = new Set(['toggle', 'viewportResize', 'settingsModal', 'memoryPanel', 'instanceLockCheck',
+          'instanceLockReject', 'instanceReopen', 'resolverOpen', 'resolverCancel', 'imagePaste']);
+        if (!NO_CHAT_KINDS.has(step.kind) && evs.length === 0) {
+          await logIssue(step.id, 'no-output', `the step ran to completion but the session produced ZERO events (logChars=${dom.textLen}) — nothing reached the model, or nothing came back`);
+        }
+        state.done.push(step.id); state.shotN = shotN; await saveState(state);
+      }
+      await shot(`${BATCH}-final`);
+      console.log(`\n[${BATCH}] complete. issues this run: ${issueCount}. shots in ${SHOTS}`);
+    }
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close();
+    server.close();
+  }
+  process.exit(0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(1); });

+/**
+ * HOW MANY TIMES DOES IT SAY WHAT IT IS ABOUT TO DO?
+ *
+ * From a production screenshot: ONE call to gemini-3.1-pro-preview, 216 seconds, and inside that single response
+ * the model stated its plan FOUR times in different words before finally emitting a tool block —
+ *
+ *   "I will write a script to apply all of these updates:"   + 4 numbered items
+ *   "I will read index.html, update the labels back to Group By, refactor the Pool into a table…"
+ *   "I will update index.html to address all of those visual and behavioral requests."
+ *   "This includes:"                                          + the same 4 items reworded
+ *
+ * That is not multiple calls, not the fabricated continuation, and not a rendering fault. It is one generation
+ * padding itself before acting, and the developer sees it as the agent repeating itself.
+ *
+ * The worker prompt already forbids it three separate ways — "do not restate the whole plan", "ONE lead-in per
+ * block, not two… that doubling is the most common thing this rule gets wrong", "SAY EACH THING ONCE". So the
+ * question is not what to tell it; it is whether anything we say lands. This measures that.
+ *
+ * The metric is the PREAMBLE: characters of prose before the first tool block, and how many separate statements
+ * of intent are in it. A tight lead-in is one sentence, ~100 characters.
+ *
+ *   node test/_preamble-probe.mjs            # against Gemini (gemini.local.json)
+ *   CB_PROBE_MODEL=... node test/_preamble-probe.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { runMinimalWorker } from '../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs';
+import { streamDiagSnapshot, streamDiagClear } from '../src/agent/turn-stream.mjs';
+import { createHttpAdapter } from '../src/llm/openai-http.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const cfg = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'gemini.local.json'), 'utf8'));
+const MODEL = process.env.CB_PROBE_MODEL || cfg.model;
+
+// A request shaped like the one that produced the screenshot: several distinct UI changes at once, against a
+// file the model must read first. Multi-part asks are what invite a restated plan.
+const ASK = 'A few changes to index.html, all at once:\n'
+  + '1. Rename the "Assign By" button back to "Group By" on the first screen.\n'
+  + '2. Turn the pool list into a table, and match its patient-count colour to the TO list (#01579b).\n'
+  + '3. Highlight the columns in the TO table that correspond to the active pool categories.\n'
+  + '4. Under each PCM row, add a row explaining exactly why patients did not fit.\n';
+
+/** Everything before the first tool block — the preamble the developer reads as "it said it again". */
+function preambleOf(text) {
+  const i = text.indexOf('<remote-workspace');
+  return (i < 0 ? text : text.slice(0, i)).trim();
+}
+/** Separate statements of intent. Crude on purpose: these are the exact openings the screenshot repeated. */
+function intentStatements(pre) {
+  return (pre.match(/^\s*(?:I will|I'll|I am going to|This includes|Here is (?:the|my) plan|My plan)\b/gim) || []).length;
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-preamble-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'index.html'), [

+  console.log(`\npreamble probe — ${MODEL}\n`);

+  // Measured from the CHAT EVENTS, which is what the developer sees: every prose segment emitted before the
+  // first tool call is preamble. Reading the parsed return value instead would miss it entirely - by then the
+  // segments have been split apart and the tool block stripped.
+  const prose = [];
+  let sawTool = false;
+  const t0 = Date.now();

+    await runMinimalWorker({
+      adapter, model: MODEL, signal: new AbortController().signal,
+      messages: [{ content: ASK }], projectDir: dir, maxTurns: 1,
+      onChatEvent: (e) => {
+        if (e && e.type === "tool-call" && e.verb !== "llm") sawTool = true;
+        if (e && e.type === "assistant-text" && !sawTool) prose.push(String(e.content || ""));
+      },

+    console.log("ERROR:", String(e?.message || e).slice(0, 200));
+  }

+  const pre = prose.join(String.fromCharCode(10, 10)).trim();
+  console.log(`call:              ${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s · ${(rec.chars || 0).toLocaleString()} chars generated`);
+  console.log(`preamble:          ${pre.length.toLocaleString()} chars before the first tool block`);
+  console.log(`prose segments:    ${prose.length}`);
+  const NL = String.fromCharCode(10);
+  const shown = pre.slice(0, 1400) + (pre.length > 1400 ? NL + "...[+" + (pre.length - 1400) + " more]" : "");
+  console.log(NL + "--- preamble ---" + NL + shown + NL);
+  await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+}

=== add test/_proxy-creds.mjs
new 3b1312435f68ed88f90249ea
+// ONE place the live harnesses get their proxy credentials from, so a rotated key is a one-line edit to a
+// gitignored file rather than a hunt through a dozen probes. Everything here comes out of proxy.local.json
+// (`*.local.json` is gitignored); nothing is ever printed but a prefix.
+//
+//   import { PROXY_BASE, PROXY_HEADERS, PROXY_API_KEY, proxyModel, describeCreds } from './_proxy-creds.mjs';
+//   const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY_BASE + '/v1', apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY, extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS });
+//
+// The proxy needs BOTH: the CF-Access pair to get through Cloudflare, and an x-api-key of its own (the adapter
+// sets that header whenever a key is present). Harnesses used to pass a placeholder 'x' as the key, which was
+// fine while the proxy supplied its own and 401s the moment it stops.
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+
+let cfg = {};
+try { cfg = JSON.parse(await readFile(new URL('../proxy.local.json', import.meta.url), 'utf8')); } catch { cfg = {}; }
+const ext = cfg.external || {};
+
+export const PROXY_CONFIG = cfg;
+export const PROXY_BASE = (process.env.CB_PROXY || ext.baseUrl || '').replace(/\/+$/, '');
+export const PROXY_HEADERS = { ...(ext.headers || {}) };
+export const PROXY_API_KEY = process.env.CB_API_KEY || ext.apiKey || cfg.apiKey || '';
+
+/** Resolve an alias ("sonnet") or a full model id. The DEFAULT is not decided here — see _live-model.mjs, which
+ *  is the single place the live model is chosen (a second fallback literal here is how those two drift). */
+export function proxyModel(nameOrAlias) {
+  const aliases = cfg.modelAliases || {};
+  const want = nameOrAlias || LIVE_MODEL;
+  return aliases[want] || want;
+}
+
+/** A one-line banner safe to print: says whether a key is present, never what it is. */
+export function describeCreds(extra = '') {
+  return `[live] ${PROXY_BASE || '(no baseUrl)'}${extra ? ' · ' + extra : ''}`
+    + ` · x-api-key ${PROXY_API_KEY ? 'set (' + PROXY_API_KEY.slice(0, 7) + '…)' : 'MISSING — add external.apiKey to proxy.local.json'}`
+    + ` · CF-Access ${PROXY_HEADERS['CF-Access-Client-Id'] ? 'set' : 'absent'}`;
+}
+
+/** Push the same values into the env, for harnesses that read CB_* rather than importing. */
+export function applyProxyEnv() {
+  if (PROXY_BASE) process.env.CB_PROXY = PROXY_BASE;
+  if (PROXY_API_KEY) process.env.CB_API_KEY = PROXY_API_KEY;
+  if (PROXY_HEADERS['CF-Access-Client-Id']) process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_ID = PROXY_HEADERS['CF-Access-Client-Id'];
+  if (PROXY_HEADERS['CF-Access-Client-Secret']) process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_SECRET = PROXY_HEADERS['CF-Access-Client-Secret'];
+}

=== add test/_r3-modes-abort.mjs
new ed5a72d0725c421e785aed35
+// Edge-case server-level mocked E2E (R3): Discuss/read-only guard + mid-run abort.
+// REAL server + a scripted mock adapter (NO network, NO proxy, NO browser). Mirrors the harness
+// pattern of test-minimal-e2e.mjs. Two scenarios:
+//   (a) mode:"discuss" — a scripted <write> must be REFUSED (tool-result status=error, kind=guard)
+//       and the file must NOT appear on disk.
+//   (b) a normal (write-capable) run is cancelled mid-stream via POST /api/cancel — status must
+//       settle to idle/error (never crash), and /api/state must keep responding afterwards.
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, rm, stat } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+
+// Scripted adapter — each LLM call returns the next scripted response (last repeats). completeStream
+// yields 40-char deltas with a small per-chunk delay so a mid-stream abort has a real window; it also
+// honors opts.signal so a cancel actually stops generation.
+function scriptedAdapter(script) {
+  let i = 0;
+  const next = () => script[Math.min(i++, script.length - 1)];
+  return {
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next() } }] }; },
+    async *completeStream(opts) {
+      const t = next();
+      for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) {
+        if (opts?.signal?.aborted) return;
+        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 25));
+        yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] };
+      }
+    },
+    async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }; },
+  };
+}
+
+const PORT = 18993;
+// [0],[1] = discuss run (write refused → final text). [2] = a long slow prose so scenario (b) can
+// cancel mid-stream. Index persists across both runs (single shared adapter).
+const LONG = 'Thinking through the change step by step. ' + 'x'.repeat(4000) + ' done.';
+const SCRIPT = [
+  'I will create the requested file now.\n<remote-workspace>\n<write path="new.txt">hello from the agent</write>\n</remote-workspace>',
+  'Understood — in Discuss mode I cannot write files. I would create new.txt containing a greeting.',
+  LONG,
+];
+
+const J = (res) => res.json();
+async function main() {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-r3-modes-'));
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<!doctype html>',
+    baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'mock', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'mock-model', modelsList: [{ id: 'mock-model' }],
+    adapter: scriptedAdapter(SCRIPT), apiFormat: 'openai', port: PORT,
+  });
+  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  const post = (p, b) => fetch(`${base}${p}`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(b) });
+  try {
+    const opened = await J(await post('/api/open-project', { path: dir.replace(/\\/g, '/') }));
+    ok(opened.activeProject, 'project opens');
+
+    // ---- (a) Discuss / read-only: a <write> must be REFUSED, no file on disk ----
+    const sentA = await J(await post('/api/message', { text: 'Create new.txt with a greeting.', model: 'mock-model', mode: 'discuss' }));
+    ok(sentA.ok, '[discuss] message accepted');
+
+    let stA;
+    for (let n = 0; n < 100; n++) {
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+      stA = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/state`));
+      if (stA.status === 'done' || stA.status === 'error') break;
+    }
+    ok(stA.status === 'done', `[discuss] run settles to done (got ${stA.status})`);
+
+    const evA = stA.events || stA.chatEvents || [];
+    ok(evA.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'write'), '[discuss] write tool-call parsed');
+    const refusal = evA.find((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.verb === 'write');
+    ok(refusal, '[discuss] a write tool-result exists');
+    ok(refusal && refusal.status === 'error', '[discuss] write tool-result status=error');
+    ok(refusal && refusal.kind === 'guard', '[discuss] write tool-result kind=guard (read-only guard, not a real failure)');
+
+    let created = true;
+    try { await stat(path.join(dir, 'new.txt')); } catch { created = false; }
+    ok(!created, '[discuss] new.txt was NOT created on disk (write truly blocked)');
+
+    // ---- (b) Abort mid-run: cancel a normal write-capable run, expect clean settle ----
+    const sentB = await J(await post('/api/message', { text: 'Do a long piece of work.', model: 'mock-model' }));
+    ok(sentB.ok, '[abort] message accepted');
+
+    // Wait until the run is actually streaming, then cancel mid-stream.
+    let running = false;
+    for (let n = 0; n < 30; n++) {
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
+      const s = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/state`));
+      if (s.status === 'running') { running = true; break; }
+      if (s.status === 'done' || s.status === 'error') break;
+    }
+    ok(running, '[abort] run reached running before cancel');
+
+    const cancelled = await J(await post('/api/cancel', {}));
+    ok(cancelled.ok, '[abort] /api/cancel returns ok');
+
+    let stB;
+    for (let n = 0; n < 100; n++) {
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+      stB = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/state`));
+      if (stB.status === 'idle' || stB.status === 'error' || stB.status === 'done') break;
+    }
+    ok(stB && (stB.status === 'idle' || stB.status === 'error'), `[abort] status settles to idle/error after cancel (got ${stB && stB.status})`);
+
+    // Server must still respond after a mid-run abort (no crash / hang).
+    const afterCancel = await fetch(`${base}/api/state`);
+    ok(afterCancel.ok, '[abort] /api/state still responds after cancel (HTTP ok)');
+    const stC = await J(afterCancel);
+    ok(typeof stC.status === 'string', '[abort] /api/state returns a valid status shape after cancel');
+
+    // A fresh message after cancel must be accepted (session not wedged).
+    const stillIdle = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/state`));
+    if (stillIdle.status !== 'running') {
+      const sentC = await J(await post('/api/message', { text: 'quick follow-up', model: 'mock-model' }));
+      ok(sentC.ok, '[abort] a follow-up message is accepted after cancel (session not wedged)');
+    } else {
+      ok(false, '[abort] session stuck in running after cancel — cannot send follow-up');
+    }
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('E2E harness error:', e); process.exit(2); });
+

+// EDGE-CASE mocked E2E (NO network, NO browser): multi-turn conversation over the REAL server + a
+// scripted mock adapter. Focus: a SECOND message must (a) run to completion AND (b) receive a
+// reconstructed history that includes the prior assistant tool-CALL turn (the historyFromChatEvents
+// fix — L8: never show the model a tool RESULT for a call absent from its own prior turn). We record
+// the exact `messages` the adapter receives on run 2 and assert the prior <remote-workspace> read
+// CALL + its result are present, in order, with no two consecutive same-role messages.
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+
+// Scripted adapter that ALSO records the messages it receives on each streaming call, so the test can
+// inspect exactly what history the worker reconstructed for run 2.
+function scriptedAdapter(script) {
+  let i = 0;
+  const next = () => script[Math.min(i++, script.length - 1)];
+  const calls = [];   // one entry per completeStream invocation: [{ role, content }, ...]
+  const adapter = {
+    async complete(opts) { calls.push((opts?.messages || []).map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: String(m.content ?? '') }))); return { choices: [{ message: { content: next() } }] }; },
+    async *completeStream(opts) {
+      calls.push((opts?.messages || []).map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: String(m.content ?? '') })));
+      const t = next();
+      for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] };
+    },
+    async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }; },
+    _calls: calls,
+  };
+  return adapter;
+}
+
+const PORT = 18991;
+// Run 1: read greeting.txt, then a final text answer (2 calls). Run 2: a single final text turn (1 call).
+const SCRIPT = [
+  'Let me read the greeting file.\n<remote-workspace>\n<read path="greeting.txt"/>\n</remote-workspace>',
+  'The greeting file says "hola mundo". That is the greeting.',
+  'Based on what I read earlier ("hola mundo"), that greeting is in Spanish.',
+];
+
+const MSG1 = 'Please read greeting.txt and tell me exactly what it says.';
+const MSG2 = 'Now, based on what you just read, what language is that greeting in?';
+
+const J = (res) => res.json();
+
+async function runToDone(base) {
+  let state;
+  for (let n = 0; n < 150; n++) {
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+    state = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/state`));
+    if (state.status === 'done' || state.status === 'error') break;
+  }
+  return state;
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-r3-mt-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'greeting.txt'), 'hola mundo\n');
+
+  const adapter = scriptedAdapter(SCRIPT);
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<!doctype html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'mock', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'mock-model', modelsList: [{ id: 'mock-model' }],
+    adapter, apiFormat: 'openai', port: PORT,
+  });
+  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  try {
+    const opened = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/open-project`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ path: dir.replace(/\\/g, '/') }) }));
+    ok(opened.activeProject, 'project opens');
+
+    // ── Message 1 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    const sent1 = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: MSG1, model: 'mock-model' }) }));
+    ok(sent1.ok, 'message 1 accepted');
+    const state1 = await runToDone(base);
+    ok(state1.status === 'done', `run 1 reaches done (got ${state1.status})`);
+
+    const evs1 = state1.events || state1.chatEvents || [];
+    const verbs1 = evs1.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call').map((e) => e.verb);
+    ok(verbs1.includes('read'), 'run 1 emitted a read tool-call');
+    ok(evs1.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.verb === 'read' && e.status === 'ok'), 'run 1 read tool-result ok (real fs)');
+    ok(evs1.some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && /hola mundo/.test(e.content)), 'run 1 final assistant-text emitted');
+
+    const callsBeforeRun2 = adapter._calls.length;
+    ok(callsBeforeRun2 >= 2, `run 1 made >=2 adapter calls (got ${callsBeforeRun2})`);
+
+    // ── Message 2 (the follow-up) ────────────────────────────────────────────
+    const sent2 = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: MSG2, model: 'mock-model' }) }));
+    ok(sent2.ok, 'message 2 accepted (multi-turn — no 409/lock)');
+    const state2 = await runToDone(base);
+    ok(state2.status === 'done', `run 2 reaches done (got ${state2.status})`);
+    ok((state2.events || []).some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && /Spanish/.test(e.content)), 'run 2 produced its follow-up answer');
+
+    // ── The historyFromChatEvents contract on run 2's FIRST adapter call ───────
+    const run2Calls = adapter._calls.slice(callsBeforeRun2);
+    ok(run2Calls.length >= 1, `run 2 issued a fresh adapter call (got ${run2Calls.length})`);
+    const convo = run2Calls[0] || [];
+
+    // The reconstructed prior turn: an ASSISTANT message carrying the actual run-1 read CALL
+    // (greeting.txt) inside <remote-workspace> — NOT the priming few-shot (which reads Person.java).
+    const callIdx = convo.findIndex((m) => m.role === 'assistant' && /<remote-workspace>/.test(m.content) && /<read\s+path="greeting\.txt"/.test(m.content));
+    ok(callIdx !== -1, 'run 2 history includes the prior assistant tool-CALL turn (<remote-workspace><read greeting.txt/>)');
+
+    // The matching tool RESULT arrives as a USER turn, AFTER the call.
+    const resultIdx = convo.findIndex((m) => m.role === 'user' && /<remote-workspace-result[^>]*op="read"[^>]*status="ok"/.test(m.content) && /hola mundo/.test(m.content));
+    ok(resultIdx !== -1, 'run 2 history includes the read tool-RESULT as a user turn');
+    ok(callIdx !== -1 && resultIdx !== -1 && callIdx < resultIdx, 'tool-CALL precedes its tool-RESULT in reconstructed history');
+
+    // The original msg1 and run-1 final answer are both present, before the fresh msg2.
+    const msg1Idx = convo.findIndex((m) => m.role === 'user' && m.content.includes(MSG1));
+    const finalIdx = convo.findIndex((m) => m.role === 'assistant' && /hola mundo/.test(m.content) && !/<remote-workspace>/.test(m.content));
+    const msg2Idx = convo.findIndex((m) => m.role === 'user' && m.content.includes(MSG2));
+    ok(msg1Idx !== -1, 'run 2 history includes the original msg1');
+    ok(finalIdx !== -1, "run 2 history includes run 1's final assistant answer");
+    ok(msg2Idx !== -1, 'run 2 convo includes the fresh msg2');
+    ok(msg1Idx < callIdx && callIdx < resultIdx && resultIdx < finalIdx && finalIdx < msg2Idx,
+      'reconstructed history is in causal order: msg1 → read-call → read-result → answer → msg2');
+
+    // No two consecutive USER messages anywhere in the run-2 request. Doubled ASSISTANT turns ARE
+    // expected and correct here — a worker turn emits BOTH prose (assistant-text) AND a <remote-workspace>
+    // block (tool-call) as separate events, so historyFromChatEvents yields two adjacent assistant
+    // entries that the real adapters coalesce (see its "adapters coalesce consecutive same-role turns"
+    // note). A doubled USER turn, by contrast, would mean a result-without-its-call or a lost turn — the
+    // exact failure the historyFromChatEvents fix prevents.
+    let doubledUser = null;
+    for (let k = 1; k < convo.length; k++) if (convo[k].role === 'user' && convo[k - 1].role === 'user') { doubledUser = `${k - 1}&${k}`; break; }
+    ok(doubledUser === null, `no two consecutive USER messages in run 2 request${doubledUser ? ' (' + doubledUser + ')' : ''}`);
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('E2E harness error:', e); process.exit(2); });
+
=== add test/_r3-persistence.mjs
new e05f451ef19a5e977826a787
+// EDGE-CASE mocked E2E — Persistence + reset. REAL server + a scripted mock adapter (NO network, NO proxy,
+// NO browser). Drives /api/message over the <remote-workspace> protocol against a THROWAWAY temp project
+// that is deliberately NOT a git repo (exercises the git-status-failure-contract path). Asserts:
+//   (a) conversation.jsonl is written under <project>/.code_boss and contains the run's turn
+//   (b) POST /api/clear-conversation resets /api/state events to empty AND deletes the on-disk log
+//   (c) opening a DIFFERENT project then re-opening the first RELOADS its persisted conversation
+// Mirrors the harness pattern of test/test-minimal-e2e.mjs.
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm, access } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { readFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+
+// Scripted adapter — same surface the engine uses. Global cursor; last entry repeats.
+function scriptedAdapter(script) {
+  let i = 0;
+  const next = () => script[Math.min(i++, script.length - 1)];
+  return {
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next() } }] }; },
+    async *completeStream() { const t = next(); for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] }; } },
+    async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }; },
+  };
+}
+
+const PORT = 18995;
+// run1: read a.txt -> final. Then the cursor clamps to the last entry (a plain final text) so any
+// SUBSEQUENT run resolves in a single call with no tool — enough to persist a fresh turn.
+const SCRIPT = [
+  'Reading a.txt.\n<remote-workspace>\n<read path="a.txt"/>\n</remote-workspace>',
+  'Done — a.txt says hello.',
+  'Second run complete — nothing else to do.',
+];
+
+const J = (res) => res.json();
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+
+async function open(dir) {
+  return J(await fetch(`${base}/api/open-project`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ path: dir.replace(/\\/g, '/') }) }));
+}
+async function send(text) {
+  return J(await fetch(`${base}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text, model: 'mock-model' }) }));
+}
+async function state() { return J(await fetch(`${base}/api/state`)); }
+async function runToDone(text) {
+  const sent = await send(text);
+  if (!sent.ok) return { sent, st: null };
+  let st;
+  for (let n = 0; n < 100; n++) {
+    await sleep(100);
+    st = await state();
+    if (st.status === 'done' || st.status === 'error') break;
+  }
+  return { sent, st };
+}
+// The conversation save is debounced (~300ms) — wait for the log to land.
+async function waitForJsonl(jsonlPath, want = true) {
+  for (let n = 0; n < 40; n++) {
+    if (existsSync(jsonlPath) === want) return true;
+    await sleep(100);
+  }
+  return existsSync(jsonlPath) === want;
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const dirA = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-r3-A-'));
+  const dirB = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-r3-B-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(dirA, 'a.txt'), 'hello from A\n');
+  await writeFile(path.join(dirB, 'b.txt'), 'hello from B\n');
+  const jsonlA = path.join(dirA, '.code_boss', 'conversation.jsonl');
+
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<!doctype html>',
+    baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'mock', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'mock-model', modelsList: [{ id: 'mock-model' }],
+    adapter: scriptedAdapter(SCRIPT), apiFormat: 'openai', port: PORT,
+  });
+  try {
+    // ── open A (a NON-git temp dir — must not crash on the git-status path) ──
+    const openedA = await open(dirA);
+    ok(openedA.activeProject, 'project A opens (non-git dir)');
+    ok(openedA.git && openedA.git.isRepo === false, 'git.isRepo === false for a non-repo (no git-status crash)');
+
+    // ── (a) run a message; the turn persists to conversation.jsonl ──
+    const r1 = await runToDone('Read a.txt.');
+    ok(r1.sent.ok, 'message accepted');
+    ok(r1.st && r1.st.status === 'done', `run reaches done (got ${r1.st && r1.st.status})`);
+    const evs1 = (r1.st && r1.st.events) || [];
+    ok(evs1.some((e) => e.type === 'user-message'), 'user-message event present in state');
+    ok(evs1.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'read'), 'read tool-call emitted');
+    ok(evs1.some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text'), 'final assistant-text emitted');
+
+    const landed = await waitForJsonl(jsonlA, true);
+    ok(landed, 'conversation.jsonl exists under <A>/.code_boss');
+    let jsonlText = landed ? readFileSync(jsonlA, 'utf8') : '';
+    const lines = jsonlText.split('\n').filter((s) => s.trim());
+    const parsed = lines.map((l) => { try { return JSON.parse(l); } catch { return null; } }).filter(Boolean);
+    ok(parsed.length > 0 && parsed.length === lines.length, `jsonl lines all parse (${parsed.length} events)`);
+    ok(parsed.some((e) => e.type === 'user-message'), 'jsonl contains the user-message turn');
+    ok(parsed.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'read'), 'jsonl contains the read tool-call turn');
+
+    // ── (b) clear-conversation: in-memory events reset AND disk log deleted ──
+    const cleared = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/clear-conversation`, { method: 'POST' }));
+    ok(cleared.ok, 'clear-conversation returns ok');
+    const stAfterClear = await state();
+    ok((stAfterClear.events || []).length === 0, `state events reset to empty (got ${(stAfterClear.events || []).length})`);
+    const goneA = await waitForJsonl(jsonlA, false);
+    ok(goneA, 'conversation.jsonl deleted from disk after clear');
+
+    // ── (c) re-populate A, then open B, then re-open A → A's log reloads ──
+    const r2 = await runToDone('Anything else?');
+    ok(r2.sent.ok, 'second message accepted (post-clear)');
+    ok(r2.st && r2.st.status === 'done', `second run reaches done (got ${r2.st && r2.st.status})`);
+    const reland = await waitForJsonl(jsonlA, true);
+    ok(reland, 'conversation.jsonl re-created for A after the second run');
+
+    const openedB = await open(dirB);
+    ok(openedB.activeProject && /cb-r3-B-/.test(openedB.activeProject), 'project B opens (different project)');
+    const stB = await state();
+    ok((stB.events || []).length === 0, `B starts with an empty conversation (got ${(stB.events || []).length})`);
+
+    const reopenedA = await open(dirA);
+    ok(reopenedA.activeProject && /cb-r3-A-/.test(reopenedA.activeProject), 're-open project A');
+    const stReopen = await state();
+    const reEvs = stReopen.events || [];
+    ok(reEvs.length > 0, `A's conversation reloads from disk (${reEvs.length} events)`);
+    ok(reEvs.some((e) => e.type === 'user-message'), 'reloaded conversation contains a user-message');
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    await rm(dirA, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+    await rm(dirB, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('E2E harness error:', e); process.exit(2); });
+
