+// EDGE-CASE mocked-E2E for the USER-PLUGIN RUNTIME (see USER-PLUGIN-SYSTEM-SPEC.md + src/plugins/*).
+// REAL server + scripted adapter (NO network, NO proxy, NO browser). Exercises the on-disk plugin
+// lifecycle: colleague-drop QUARANTINE -> trust gate -> Enable -> load -> tool live to the worker,
+// plus the robustness surface: a MALFORMED (syntax-error) plugin, a BAD-SHAPE (no impl) plugin, and
+// a MISNAMED file must all be surfaced as plugin ISSUES and MUST NOT crash the server.
+//
+// Harness pattern copied from test/test-minimal-e2e.mjs (startServer DI seam + scriptedAdapter).
+// Run: node test/_r3-plugins-runtime.mjs
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, mkdir, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+
+function scriptedAdapter(script) {
+  let i = 0;
+  const next = () => script[Math.min(i++, script.length - 1)];
+  return {
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next() } }] }; },
+    async *completeStream() { const t = next(); for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] }; } },
+    async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }; },
+  };
+}
+
+const PORT = 18996;
+// After the plugin is enabled, the worker calls the plugin's NEW verb, then wraps up in text.
+const SCRIPT = [
+  'Trying the freshly-enabled plugin tool.\n<remote-workspace>\n<demo_do msg="hello"/>\n</remote-workspace>',
+  'Done — the plugin tool returned its content.',
+];
+
+const VALID_PLUGIN = `export default {
+  description: 'demo plugin for the runtime test',
+  author: 'tester',
+  promptAddition: 'Use <demo_do msg="..."/> to echo a message.',
+  tools: [{
+    verb: 'demo_do',
+    name: 'demo_do',
+    schema: { description: 'echoes a message', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: { msg: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['msg'] } },
+    impl: async (args) => ({ content: 'did: ' + (args && args.msg || '') }),
+  }],
+};
+`;
+// A CONFORMING filename whose module has an ESM SYNTAX ERROR — import() must reject, caught into an issue row.
+const BROKEN_SYNTAX = `export default { this is not valid javascript >>> `;
+// A CONFORMING filename whose plugin is structurally invalid (tool has no impl) — validatePluginShape rejects.
+const BAD_SHAPE = `export default { tools: [{ verb: 'nope', name: 'nope', schema: { parameters: { type: 'object', properties: {} } } }] };`;
+// A MISNAMED .mjs (no "__" separator) — surfaced as a soft "misnamed" issue, never imported.
+const MISNAMED = `export default {};`;
+
+const J = (res) => res.json();
+async function main() {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-r3-plug-'));
+  const pdir = path.join(dir, '.code_boss', 'plugins');
+  await mkdir(pdir, { recursive: true });
+  // Pre-existing files = COLLEAGUE DROP (not session-authored) → subject to the trust gate.
+  await writeFile(path.join(pdir, 'demoplug__1.0.0.mjs'), VALID_PLUGIN);
+  await writeFile(path.join(pdir, 'brokensyntax__1.0.0.mjs'), BROKEN_SYNTAX);
+  await writeFile(path.join(pdir, 'badshape__1.0.0.mjs'), BAD_SHAPE);
+  await writeFile(path.join(pdir, 'notaplugin.mjs'), MISNAMED);
+
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<!doctype html><title>mock</title>',
+    baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'mock', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'mock-model', modelsList: [{ id: 'mock-model' }],
+    adapter: scriptedAdapter(SCRIPT), apiFormat: 'openai', port: PORT,
+  });
+  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  const get = async (p) => J(await fetch(`${base}${p}`));
+  const post = async (p, b) => J(await fetch(`${base}${p}`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(b) }));
+  const rowFor = (snap, name) => (snap || []).find((r) => r.name === name);
+
+  try {
+    const opened = await post('/api/open-project', { path: dir.replace(/\\/g, '/') });
+    ok(opened.activeProject, 'project opens');
+
+    // armPluginWatch runs the initial reconcile async — poll /api/state until the plugin rows appear.
+    let state, demo;
+    for (let n = 0; n < 60; n++) {
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+      state = await get('/api/state');
+      demo = rowFor(state.plugins, 'demoplug');
+      if (demo) break;
+    }
+    ok(Array.isArray(state.plugins), '/api/state carries a plugins snapshot array');
+
+    // ── Trust gate: a valid colleague-drop plugin is QUARANTINED, not auto-loaded ──
+    ok(demo && demo.quarantined === true && demo.enabled !== true, 'valid dropped plugin is QUARANTINED (trust gate), not auto-enabled');
+    ok(demo && demo.description === 'demo plugin for the runtime test', 'quarantine row carries the static description (no import) for the review popup');
+
+    // ── Robustness: malformed / bad-shape / misnamed files are surfaced, server stays UP ──
+    const misnamed = rowFor(state.plugins, 'notaplugin.mjs');
+    ok(misnamed && !!misnamed.error && /misnamed/i.test(misnamed.error), 'misnamed .mjs surfaced as a "misnamed" issue row (not silently dropped)');
+    // brokensyntax + badshape are conforming names but untrusted → also quarantined until Enable (no import yet).
+    ok(rowFor(state.plugins, 'brokensyntax'), 'malformed-syntax file present as a plugin row');
+    ok(rowFor(state.plugins, 'badshape'), 'bad-shape file present as a plugin row');
+    // The server must still answer after ingesting the bogus files.
+    ok((await get('/api/state')).plugins !== undefined, 'server still responsive after scanning bogus plugin files (no crash)');
+
+    // ── Enable the MALFORMED (syntax-error) plugin: trust it, reconcile imports it, import() rejects → error row, NOT enabled, NOT a crash ──
+    const enBroken = await post('/api/plugin-enable', { name: 'brokensyntax' });
+    ok(enBroken.ok === true, 'plugin-enable(brokensyntax) returns ok (trust recorded) without throwing');
+    const brokenRow = rowFor(enBroken.plugins, 'brokensyntax');
+    ok(brokenRow && brokenRow.enabled !== true && !!brokenRow.error, 'malformed plugin failed to load: surfaced as an error row, NOT enabled');
+    ok(brokenRow && /load failed|import/i.test(brokenRow.error || ''), 'malformed-plugin error mentions the import/load failure');
+
+    // ── Enable the BAD-SHAPE plugin: import succeeds but validation rejects (tool has no impl) → error row, NOT enabled ──
+    const enBad = await post('/api/plugin-enable', { name: 'badshape' });
+    ok(enBad.ok === true, 'plugin-enable(badshape) returns ok without throwing');
+    const badRow = rowFor(enBad.plugins, 'badshape');
+    ok(badRow && badRow.enabled !== true && !!badRow.error, 'bad-shape plugin rejected by the validation gate: error row, NOT enabled');
+
+    // Server survived importing/validating two bad plugins.
+    ok((await get('/api/state')).plugins !== undefined, 'server still responsive after enabling two bad plugins (no crash)');
+
+    // ── Enable the VALID plugin: it loads, and its tool/verb goes live ──
+    const enGood = await post('/api/plugin-enable', { name: 'demoplug' });
+    ok(enGood.ok === true, 'plugin-enable(demoplug) returns ok');
+    const goodRow = rowFor(enGood.plugins, 'demoplug');
+    ok(goodRow && goodRow.enabled === true && !goodRow.quarantined, 'valid plugin ENABLED (not quarantined) after trust');
+    ok(goodRow && Array.isArray(goodRow.tools) && goodRow.tools.includes('demo_do'), "enabled plugin's tool shows in the snapshot (enabledTools) — verb demo_do live");
+    ok(goodRow && goodRow.source === 'runtime' && goodRow.version === '1.0.0', 'enabled plugin row is source=runtime with the parsed version');
+
+    // ── The newly-enabled plugin tool is actually invokable by the worker (extraVerbs/extraTools wiring) ──
+    const sent = await post('/api/message', { text: 'Call the demo plugin tool.', model: 'mock-model' });
+    ok(sent.ok, 'message accepted');
+    let run;
+    for (let n = 0; n < 120; n++) {
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+      run = await get('/api/state');
+      if (run.status === 'done' || run.status === 'error') break;
+    }
+    ok(run.status === 'done', `run reaches done (got ${run.status})`);
+    const evs = run.events || [];
+    const verbs = evs.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call').map((e) => e.verb);
+    ok(verbs.includes('demo_do'), 'worker dispatched the plugin verb <demo_do> (extraVerbs wired)');
+    ok(evs.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.verb === 'demo_do' && e.status === 'ok' && /did: hello/.test(e.content || '')), "plugin impl RAN in-process and returned its content ('did: hello')");
+    ok(evs.some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && e.content), 'final assistant-text emitted after the plugin tool ran');
+  } catch (e) {
+    fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL harness error: ${e && e.stack || e}\n`);
+  } finally {
+    try { server.close(); } catch {}
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('E2E harness error:', e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/_r3-termination.mjs
new 5f6f6f97f3d9bbe3aefcddba
+// _r3-termination.mjs — EDGE-CASE mocked E2E for the minimal worker's TERMINATION paths.
+// REAL server + a scripted mock adapter (NO network, NO proxy, NO browser) driving /api/message over
+// the <remote-workspace> protocol. Asserts the three ways a run ends WITHOUT the model volunteering a
+// clean close: (a) churn (same tool block every turn), (b) empty-after-tools (a read then silence),
+// (c) a bare greeting. Terminated cause is read from /api/snapshot's agent_run event; the visible
+// hand-back / synthesized closer is read from the chat-event contract in /api/state.events.
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+
+// Scripted adapter — each LLM call returns the next scripted response (last repeats forever). Mirrors the
+// real adapter surface the engine uses: complete() + completeStream() + listModels().
+function scriptedAdapter(script) {
+  let i = 0;
+  const next = () => script[Math.min(i++, script.length - 1)];
+  return {
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next() } }] }; },
+    async *completeStream() { const t = next() || ''; for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] }; },
+    async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }; },
+  };
+}
+
+const J = (res) => res.json();
+
+// Boot a fresh server on `port`, open a temp project (seeded with a.txt), fire one message, poll to a
+// terminal status, then read the chat events + the agent_run snapshot. Returns everything the asserts need.
+async function runScenario({ port, script, question }) {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-r3-term-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'a.txt'), 'hello from a.txt\n');
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<!doctype html><title>mock</title>',
+    baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'mock', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'mock-model', modelsList: [{ id: 'mock-model' }],
+    adapter: scriptedAdapter(script), apiFormat: 'openai', port,
+  });
+  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`;
+  try {
+    const opened = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/open-project`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ path: dir.replace(/\\/g, '/') }) }));
+    if (!opened.activeProject) throw new Error('project failed to open');
+    const sent = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: question, model: 'mock-model' }) }));
+    if (!sent.ok) throw new Error('message rejected');
+
+    let state;
+    for (let n = 0; n < 200; n++) {
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+      state = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/state`));
+      if (state.status === 'done' || state.status === 'error') break;
+    }
+    const snapshot = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/snapshot`));
+    const events = state.events || [];
+    const agentRuns = (snapshot.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'agent_run');
+    const lastRun = agentRuns[agentRuns.length - 1] || null;
+    return { state, events, lastRun };
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  // ── (a) CHURN: the SAME tool block every turn (no prose) → task-churn + a visible hand-back. ──
+  {
+    process.stdout.write('\n[a] churn — same tool block every turn\n');
+    const block = '<remote-workspace>\n<read path="a.txt"/>\n</remote-workspace>';
+    const { state, events, lastRun } = await runScenario({ port: 18992, script: [block], question: 'Keep reading a.txt.' });
+    ok(state.status === 'done', `run settles (got ${state.status})`);
+    ok(lastRun && lastRun.terminated === 'task-churn', `terminated === task-churn (got ${lastRun && lastRun.terminated})`);
+    const texts = events.filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text');
+    const handback = texts.find((e) => /repeat|progress|approach it differently/i.test(e.content || ''));
+    ok(!!handback, 'visible hand-back assistant-text emitted (not a silent churn stop)');
+    ok(events.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'read'), 'read tool-call was emitted before the churn wrap-up');
+  }
+
+  // ── (b) EMPTY-AFTER-TOOLS: a read, then an empty reply forever → synthesized closer + terminated stop. ──
+  {
+    process.stdout.write('\n[b] empty-after-tools — read then silence\n');
+    const readBlock = '<remote-workspace>\n<read path="a.txt"/>\n</remote-workspace>';
+    const { state, events, lastRun } = await runScenario({ port: 18993, script: [readBlock, ''], question: 'Read a.txt.' });
+    ok(state.status === 'done', `run settles (got ${state.status})`);
+    ok(lastRun && lastRun.terminated === 'stop', `terminated === stop (got ${lastRun && lastRun.terminated})`);
+    ok(events.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.status === 'ok'), 'read tool-result (ok) emitted — real tool ran against temp fs');
+    const texts = events.filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text');
+    const lastText = texts[texts.length - 1];
+    ok(!!lastText && String(lastText.content || '').trim().length > 0, 'run ends with a NON-EMPTY synthesized closer assistant-text (never a silent empty end)');
+  }
+
+  // ── (c) GREETING: prose, no tools → immediate stop on the first turn. ──
+  {
+    process.stdout.write('\n[c] greeting — no tools, immediate stop\n');
+    const { state, events, lastRun } = await runScenario({ port: 18994, script: ['Hello! How can I help you today?'], question: 'hi' });
+    ok(state.status === 'done', `run settles (got ${state.status})`);
+    ok(lastRun && lastRun.terminated === 'stop', `terminated === stop (got ${lastRun && lastRun.terminated})`);
+    ok(lastRun && lastRun.turns === 1, `stopped on the FIRST turn (got turns=${lastRun && lastRun.turns})`);
+    ok(events.some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && /how can i help/i.test(e.content || '')), 'greeting assistant-text emitted');
+    ok(!events.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-call'), 'no tool-call emitted for a plain greeting');
+  }
+
+  process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('E2E harness error:', e); process.exit(2); });
+
=== add test/_r3-tools-events.mjs
new 4b97adb7fe84a1ab4e5ed4d2
+// _r3-tools-events.mjs — EDGE-CASE server-level mocked E2E: TOOL INTEGRATION + the chat-event CONTRACT.
+// Real server + a scripted mock adapter (NO network, NO proxy, NO browser) driving /api/message over the
+// <remote-workspace> protocol. git/fs are real but on a throwaway temp dir. Mirrors the harness pattern in
+// test/test-minimal-e2e.mjs. Scenario focus:
+//   (a) an <edit> that changes a file → a 'diff' chat-event fires AND the file changed on disk.
+//   (b) a <read> of a MISSING file → a tool-result with status 'error' (no crash; run still reaches done).
+//   (c) a MULTI-BLOCK turn (two <remote-workspace> blocks in one response) → BOTH execute, BOTH results appear.
+//   (d) per-run event ordering: user-message first → every tool-call immediately paired with its tool-result
+//       → a final assistant-text after the last tool-result; appendedAtVersion strictly increases (monotonic).
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, readFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+
+// Scripted adapter — each LLM call returns the next scripted response (last repeats). Same surface the
+// engine uses: complete() + completeStream() + listModels().
+function scriptedAdapter(script) {
+  let i = 0;
+  const next = () => script[Math.min(i++, script.length - 1)];
+  return {
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next() } }] }; },
+    async *completeStream() { const t = next(); for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] }; } },
+    async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }; },
+  };
+}
+
+const PORT = 18994;
+
+// Turn 1: edit util.js (scenario a). Turn 2: TWO blocks in one response (scenario c) — a MISSING read
+// (scenario b → error) and a VALID read (ok). Turn 3: prose-only → ends the run.
+const SCRIPT = [
+  'Fixing the bug in util.js.\n<remote-workspace>\n<edit path="util.js"><find>return a - b;  // BUG</find><replace>return a + b;</replace></edit>\n</remote-workspace>',
+  'Now checking two files in one turn.\n<remote-workspace>\n<read path="does-not-exist.js"/>\n</remote-workspace>\nAnd now the real one:\n<remote-workspace>\n<read path="util.js"/>\n</remote-workspace>',
+  'All done. util.js now returns a + b; the missing-file read errored as expected and the util.js read succeeded.',
+];
+
+const J = (res) => res.json();
+async function main() {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-r3-tools-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'util.js'), 'function add(a, b) {\n  return a - b;  // BUG\n}\nmodule.exports = { add };\n');
+
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<!doctype html><title>mock</title>',
+    baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'mock', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'mock-model', modelsList: [{ id: 'mock-model' }],
+    adapter: scriptedAdapter(SCRIPT), apiFormat: 'openai', port: PORT,
+  });
+  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  try {
+    const opened = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/open-project`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ path: dir.replace(/\\/g, '/') }) }));
+    ok(opened.activeProject, 'project opens');
+
+    const sent = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'Fix util.js, then read a missing file and util.js in one turn.', model: 'mock-model' }) }));
+    ok(sent.ok, 'message accepted');
+
+    let state;
+    for (let n = 0; n < 150; n++) {
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+      state = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/state?limit=1000`));
+      if (state.status === 'done' || state.status === 'error') break;
+    }
+    ok(state.status === 'done', `run reaches done, not error/crash (got ${state.status})`);
+
+    const evs = state.events || state.chatEvents || [];
+    ok(evs.length > 0, 'events emitted');
+
+    // ── (a) edit → diff event + on-disk change ──
+    const editCall = evs.find((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'edit');
+    ok(!!editCall, '(a) edit tool-call emitted');
+    const editResult = evs.find((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.callId === editCall?.callId);
+    ok(editResult?.status === 'ok', '(a) edit tool-result status ok');
+    const diffEv = evs.find((e) => e.type === 'diff' && e.callId === editCall?.callId);
+    ok(!!diffEv && typeof diffEv.diff === 'string' && diffEv.diff.length > 0, '(a) diff chat-event fires for the edit');
+    const after = await readFile(path.join(dir, 'util.js'), 'utf8');
+    ok(/return a \+ b;/.test(after) && !/a - b/.test(after), '(a) util.js edited on disk (a - b -> a + b)');
+
+    // ── (b) missing read → tool-result status error, no crash ──
+    const readCalls = evs.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'read');
+    const readResults = readCalls.map((c) => evs.find((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.callId === c.callId)).filter(Boolean);
+    const errReads = readResults.filter((r) => r.status === 'error');
+    const okReads = readResults.filter((r) => r.status === 'ok');
+    ok(errReads.length >= 1, '(b) missing-file read produced a tool-result with status error');
+    ok(errReads.every((r) => e2eNoThrow(r)), '(b) error result carries a message (no undefined content, no crash)');
+
+    // ── (c) multi-block turn → BOTH reads execute, BOTH results present ──
+    ok(readCalls.length === 2, `(c) both blocks executed — 2 read tool-calls (got ${readCalls.length})`);
+    ok(readResults.length === 2, `(c) both read tool-results present (got ${readResults.length})`);
+    ok(okReads.length >= 1 && errReads.length >= 1, '(c) the valid read is ok and the missing read is error (both distinct blocks ran)');
+
+    // ── (d) ordering + monotonic versions ──
+    ok(evs[0]?.type === 'user-message', '(d) first event is user-message');
+    // every tool-call is followed by its matching tool-result (call index < result index)
+    let pairingOk = true;
+    for (const c of evs.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call')) {
+      const ci = evs.indexOf(c);
+      const ri = evs.findIndex((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.callId === c.callId);
+      if (ri < 0 || ri < ci) pairingOk = false;
+    }
+    ok(pairingOk, '(d) each tool-call precedes its matching tool-result (callId paired)');
+    // every tool-result has a preceding tool-call (no orphan results)
+    const orphanResult = evs.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && !evs.some((c) => c.type === 'tool-call' && c.callId === e.callId));
+    ok(!orphanResult, '(d) no orphan tool-result (every result has a tool-call)');
+    // a final assistant-text comes AFTER the last tool-result
+    const lastResultIdx = evs.map((e) => e.type).lastIndexOf('tool-result');
+    const finalTextIdx = evs.map((e) => e.type).lastIndexOf('assistant-text');
+    const finalText = evs[finalTextIdx];
+    ok(lastResultIdx >= 0 && finalTextIdx > lastResultIdx, '(d) final assistant-text is emitted after the last tool-result');
+    ok(finalText && /a \+ b/.test(finalText.content || ''), '(d) final assistant-text carries the closing summary');
+    // appendedAtVersion strictly increasing (monotonic) across persisted events
+    let monotonic = true;
+    for (let k = 1; k < evs.length; k++) {
+      if (!(evs[k].appendedAtVersion > evs[k - 1].appendedAtVersion)) monotonic = false;
+    }
+    ok(monotonic, '(d) appendedAtVersion strictly increases (monotonic) across events');
+    ok(typeof state.version === 'number' && state.version > 0, '(d) top-level state.version advanced past 0');
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+
+// A missing-file error result must carry a non-empty message rather than blow up the worker.
+function e2eNoThrow(r) { return typeof r.content === 'string' && r.content.length > 0; }
+
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('E2E harness error:', e); process.exit(2); });

+// Deeper mocked-E2E for the BACKGROUND-PROCESS lifecycle driven THROUGH the worker (Round-5 #1/#6):
+// the REAL server + a scripted mock adapter (NO network, NO proxy, NO browser) drives the
+// <remote-workspace> spawn-background → bg-status → kill-background protocol against REAL, short-lived
+// PowerShell/node processes, then verifies close-project REAPS a still-live process (no leak).
+//
+// Harness pattern lifted from test-minimal-e2e.mjs: scriptedAdapter DI seam, mkdtemp temp project,
+// startServer, poll /api/state, read state.events. Ground-truth for "alive/reaped" comes from the
+// bg-procs registry (ESM singleton — the server and this test share the one instance).
+//
+// Run: node test/_r7-bg-lifecycle.mjs
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { bgLiveCount, reapAllBackground, killBackground } from '../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+
+// Scripted adapter — same surface the engine uses (complete + completeStream + listModels); each call
+// returns the next scripted turn (last repeats). Tool results are fed back a turn later by the server,
+// so the ORDER here defines the worker's turn sequence.
+function scriptedAdapter(script) {
+  let i = 0;
+  const next = () => script[Math.min(i++, script.length - 1)];
+  return {
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next() } }] }; },
+    async *completeStream() { const t = next(); for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] }; } },
+    async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }; },
+  };
+}
+
+const PORT = 18992;
+// A long-lived process that will NOT exit on its own (setInterval keeps node's event loop alive) — so it
+// is genuinely RUNNING for the status check, and something real must be killed/reaped. In a FRESH process
+// the first spawn is deterministically id "bg-1", the second "bg-2".
+const LIVE_CMD = 'node -e "setInterval(()=>{},1000)"';
+const SCRIPT = [
+  // turn 1: start a long-lived bg process (P1). Command is the tag BODY (no attribute).
+  `Starting a long-lived background process.\n<remote-workspace>\n<run wait="false">${LIVE_CMD}</run>\n</remote-workspace>`,
+  // turn 2: is it up?
+  `Checking whether it is still running.\n<remote-workspace>\n<bg-status id="bg-1"/>\n</remote-workspace>`,
+  // turn 3: stop it by id.
+  `Stopping it now that I have confirmed it.\n<remote-workspace>\n<kill-background id="bg-1"/>\n</remote-workspace>`,
+  // turn 4: start ANOTHER long-lived process (P2) and leave it running — so close-project has something to reap.
+  `Starting a second background process and leaving it running.\n<remote-workspace>\n<run wait="false">${LIVE_CMD}</run>\n</remote-workspace>`,
+  // turn 5: no tool call → the run ends.
+  'All background-process steps are done.',
+];
+
+const J = (res) => res.json();
+async function main() {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-r7-bg-'));
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<!doctype html><title>mock</title>',
+    baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'mock', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'mock-model', modelsList: [{ id: 'mock-model' }],
+    adapter: scriptedAdapter(SCRIPT), apiFormat: 'openai', port: PORT,
+  });
+  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  try {
+    const opened = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/open-project`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ path: dir.replace(/\\/g, '/') }) }));
+    ok(opened.activeProject, 'project opens');
+
+    const sent = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'Manage a background process end to end.', model: 'mock-model' }) }));
+    ok(sent.ok, 'message accepted');
+
+    let state;
+    for (let n = 0; n < 200; n++) {
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+      state = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/state`));
+      if (state.status === 'done' || state.status === 'error') break;
+    }
+    ok(state.status === 'done', `run reaches done (got ${state.status})`);
+
+    const evs = state.events || state.chatEvents || [];
+    const results = evs.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result');
+    const verbs = evs.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call').map((e) => e.verb);
+    const resultFor = (verb) => results.filter((e) => e.verb === verb);
+
+    // --- The two detached runs parsed as tool calls (<run wait="false"> → run tool → spawn_background) ---
+    ok(verbs.filter((v) => v === 'run').length >= 2, `both detached <run wait="false"> calls parsed (got ${verbs.filter((v) => v === 'run').length})`);
+    ok(verbs.includes('bg-status'), 'bg-status call parsed');
+    ok(verbs.includes('kill-background'), 'kill-background call parsed');
+
+    // --- spawn returns an id ---
+    const spawnResults = resultFor('run');
+    ok(spawnResults.length >= 2, `two spawn results emitted (got ${spawnResults.length})`);
+    ok(spawnResults.every((e) => e.status === 'ok'), 'spawn results are status=ok');
+    const spawn1 = spawnResults[0];
+    ok(spawn1 && /Started background process\s+bg-1\b/.test(spawn1.content || ''), `spawn returns an id (bg-1); got: ${(spawn1?.content || '').slice(0, 80)}`);
+    ok(spawn1 && /pid\s+\d+/.test(spawn1.content || ''), 'spawn reports a real pid');
+
+    // --- bg-status shows it ALIVE (RUNNING) ---
+    const statusRes = resultFor('bg-status')[0];
+    ok(statusRes && statusRes.status === 'ok', 'bg-status result is status=ok');
+    ok(statusRes && /RUNNING/.test(statusRes.content || ''), `bg-status shows the process RUNNING; got: ${(statusRes?.content || '').slice(0, 120)}`);
+    ok(statusRes && /bg-1/.test(statusRes.content || ''), 'bg-status references the spawned id (bg-1)');
+
+    // --- kill stops it ---
+    const killRes = resultFor('kill-background')[0];
+    ok(killRes && killRes.status === 'ok', 'kill-background result is status=ok');
+    ok(killRes && /Stopped\s+1\s+background process/i.test(killRes.content || ''), `kill reports it stopped 1; got: ${(killRes?.content || '').slice(0, 120)}`);
+    ok(killRes && /bg-1/.test(killRes.content || ''), 'kill references the killed id (bg-1)');
+
+    // --- ground truth: P1 killed, P2 still live (registry singleton shared with the server) ---
+    // P1's alive flag flips synchronously on kill; P2 was spawned after and left running.
+    ok(bgLiveCount() >= 1, `a background process is genuinely alive at run end — leaks without reap (live=${bgLiveCount()})`);
+
+    // --- close-project REAPS the surviving process (no leak) ---
+    const closed = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/close-project`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: '{}' }));
+    ok(closed && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(closed, 'activeProject') && closed.activeProject === null, 'close-project accepted (activeProject cleared)');
+    // reapBackgroundKey flips alive=false synchronously + drops the bucket; give the async tree-kill a beat
+    // but the LIVE COUNT (rec.alive) should be 0 immediately.
+    let live = bgLiveCount();
+    for (let n = 0; n < 30 && live !== 0; n++) { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50)); live = bgLiveCount(); }
+    ok(live === 0, `all background processes reaped on close-project — none leaked (live=${live})`);
+  } finally {
+    // Belt-and-suspenders: stop anything this test spawned, even on failure.
+    try { killBackground({ key: path.resolve(dir) }); } catch {}
+    try { reapAllBackground(); } catch {}
+    server.close();
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('E2E harness error:', e); try { reapAllBackground(); } catch {} process.exit(2); });
+
=== add test/_r7-codespan-exec.mjs

+// Deeper mocked-E2E for the Round-6 #1 "code-span vs execution" safety fix, driven through the REAL
+// server + a scripted mock adapter (NO network, NO proxy, NO browser). Proves the full flow:
+//   (1) an assistant turn whose ONLY tool-shaped content is a backtick-wrapped EXAMPLE runs NOTHING —
+//       no tool-call / tool-result events, no file on disk — and ends as ordinary assistant-text; then
+//   (2) a follow-up message with a REAL <write> block DOES execute and the file appears.
+// Same harness pattern as test/test-minimal-e2e.mjs: startServer + scriptedAdapter DI seam, real git/fs
+// on a throwaway temp dir, /api/message over the <remote-workspace> protocol, poll /api/state.
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, rm, access } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+
+function scriptedAdapter(script) {
+  let i = 0;
+  const next = () => script[Math.min(i++, script.length - 1)];
+  return {
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next() } }] }; },
+    async *completeStream() { const t = next(); for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] }; } },
+    async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }; },
+  };
+}
+
+const PORT = 18993;
+
+// Turn 1: PURE prose — the only <remote-workspace> content is INSIDE a backtick code span (an EXAMPLE the
+// agent is SHOWING, not a real call). Must run nothing and end the turn as normal assistant-text.
+const EXAMPLE_TURN =
+  'Sure — the syntax for a write looks like this: ' +
+  '`<remote-workspace><write path="HACKED.txt">x</write></remote-workspace>`. ' +
+  'That is just an illustration; I have not created anything. Let me know if you want me to actually do it.';
+
+// Turn 2 (after the 2nd user message): a REAL write block → must execute and create the file.
+const REAL_WRITE_TURN =
+  'Okay, creating it now.\n<remote-workspace>\n<write path="REAL.txt">hello from the real block</write>\n</remote-workspace>';
+const FINAL_TURN = 'Done — REAL.txt has been created.';
+
+const SCRIPT = [EXAMPLE_TURN, REAL_WRITE_TURN, FINAL_TURN];
+
+const J = (res) => res.json();
+
+async function pollDone(base, maxN = 150) {
+  let state;
+  for (let n = 0; n < maxN; n++) {
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+    state = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/state`));
+    if (state.status === 'done' || state.status === 'error') break;
+  }
+  return state;
+}
+
+async function fileExists(p) { try { await access(p); return true; } catch { return false; } }
+
+async function main() {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-r7-codespan-'));
+
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<!doctype html>',
+    baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'mock', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'mock-model', modelsList: [{ id: 'mock-model' }],
+    adapter: scriptedAdapter(SCRIPT), apiFormat: 'openai', port: PORT,
+  });
+  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  try {
+    const opened = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/open-project`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ path: dir.replace(/\\/g, '/') }) }));
+    ok(opened.activeProject, 'project opens');
+
+    // ── Message 1: the backtick EXAMPLE turn ──
+    const sent1 = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'How do I write a file?', model: 'mock-model' }) }));
+    ok(sent1.ok, 'message 1 accepted');
+
+    const state1 = await pollDone(base);
+    ok(state1.status === 'done', `run 1 reaches done (got ${state1.status})`);
+
+    const evs1 = state1.events || state1.chatEvents || [];
+    const toolCalls1 = evs1.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call');
+    const toolResults1 = evs1.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result');
+    ok(toolCalls1.length === 0, `NO tool-call events fired for the code-span example (got ${toolCalls1.length})`);
+    ok(toolResults1.length === 0, `NO tool-result events fired for the code-span example (got ${toolResults1.length})`);
+    ok(evs1.some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text'), 'run 1 ends as normal assistant-text');
+    ok(!(await fileExists(path.join(dir, 'HACKED.txt'))), 'HACKED.txt NOT created on disk (example did not execute)');
+
+    // ── Message 2: a REAL write block ──
+    const sent2 = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'Yes, actually create REAL.txt.', model: 'mock-model' }) }));
+    ok(sent2.ok, 'message 2 accepted');
+
+    const state2 = await pollDone(base);
+    ok(state2.status === 'done', `run 2 reaches done (got ${state2.status})`);
+
+    const evs2 = state2.events || state2.chatEvents || [];
+    const writeCalls = evs2.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'write');
+    ok(writeCalls.length >= 1, `real <write> tool-call emitted (got ${writeCalls.length})`);
+    ok(evs2.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.status === 'ok'), 'tool-result (ok) emitted for the real write');
+
+    ok(await fileExists(path.join(dir, 'REAL.txt')), 'REAL.txt created on disk (real block executed)');
+    if (await fileExists(path.join(dir, 'REAL.txt'))) {
+      const body = await readFile(path.join(dir, 'REAL.txt'), 'utf8');
+      ok(/hello from the real block/.test(body), 'REAL.txt has the written content');
+    }
+    // The example file must STILL not exist even after a real write ran.
+    ok(!(await fileExists(path.join(dir, 'HACKED.txt'))), 'HACKED.txt still NOT created after the real run');
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('E2E harness error:', e); process.exit(2); });
+
=== add test/_r7-concurrency-switch.mjs
new b63cd0cfe2f0adb126a7316b
+// R7 — Concurrency + the switch-lock (Round-5 #3), server-level mocked E2E.
+// REAL server + a scripted mock adapter (NO network, NO proxy, NO browser) driving /api/* to probe:
+//   (a) second /api/message while a slow run is live -> 409 "chat is already running"; first run intact.
+//   (b) /api/open-project (different dir) while a run is live -> 409 (backgroundBusy).
+//   (c) TOCTOU: fire /api/open-project + /api/message concurrently; the switch-lock must keep them
+//       MUTUALLY EXCLUSIVE (exactly one accepted) or the accepted message must run against the correct
+//       new project WITHOUT its events being wiped. The open-project handler checks backgroundBusy()
+//       BEFORE `await readBody()` and only sets session.switching AFTER it — so a large open body widens
+//       that await window, letting a tiny /api/message slip past both the switching AND status checks and
+//       launch a run that openProject() then resets mid-flight (resetChatState nulls abortController +
+//       wipes events, reassigns activeProject). This test tries to catch exactly that.
+//   (d) rapid /api/clear-conversation -> /api/message: clear must succeed and the follow-up run runs clean.
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+const PORT = 18991;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const failures = [];
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+// Record a real bug (schema-shaped) AND assert it in one call.
+const bug = (cond, rec) => { ok(cond, rec.caseName + ' :: ' + rec.scenario); if (!cond) failures.push(rec); };
+
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+// Stateful scripted adapter: .set(script, delayMs) resets the per-run turn index + per-call latency.
+// A non-zero delay keeps an accepted run in 'running' long enough to race a second request.
+function makeAdapter() {
+  let script = ['done.'];
+  let i = 0;
+  let delayMs = 0;
+  const next = () => script[Math.min(i++, script.length - 1)];
+  return {
+    set(s, d = 0) { script = s; i = 0; delayMs = d; },
+    async complete() { if (delayMs) await sleep(delayMs); return { choices: [{ message: { content: next() } }] }; },
+    async *completeStream() {
+      if (delayMs) await sleep(delayMs);
+      const t = next();
+      for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] };
+    },
+    async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }; },
+  };
+}
+
+// A slow 2-turn run: read a file that exists in BOTH temp projects, then a plain-text final turn (ends run).
+const slowScript = (delay) => ['Reading a.txt.\n<remote-workspace>\n<read path="a.txt"/>\n</remote-workspace>', 'Done reading a.txt.'];
+
+async function main() {
+  const adapter = makeAdapter();
+  const dirA = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-r7-A-'));
+  const dirB = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-r7-B-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(dirA, 'a.txt'), 'alpha file A\n');
+  await writeFile(path.join(dirB, 'a.txt'), 'beta file B\n');
+  const A = dirA.replace(/\\/g, '/');
+  const B = dirB.replace(/\\/g, '/');
+
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<!doctype html><title>mock</title>',
+    baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'mock', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'mock-model', modelsList: [{ id: 'mock-model' }],
+    adapter, apiFormat: 'openai', port: PORT,
+  });
+  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+
+  const post = async (p, obj) => {
+    const res = await fetch(`${base}${p}`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(obj) });
+    let body = null; try { body = await res.json(); } catch {}
+    return { status: res.status, body };
+  };
+  const getState = async () => { const r = await fetch(`${base}/api/state`); return r.json(); };
+  const waitFor = async (pred, timeoutMs = 8000, stepMs = 50) => {
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    let st;
+    for (;;) {
+      st = await getState();
+      if (pred(st)) return st;
+      if (Date.now() - t0 > timeoutMs) return st;
+      await sleep(stepMs);
+    }
+  };
+  const terminal = (s) => s.status === 'idle' || s.status === 'done' || s.status === 'error';
+  const settle = () => waitFor(terminal, 10000);
+  const verbs = (st) => (st.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call').map((e) => e.verb);
+  const hasUserMsg = (st) => (st.events || []).some((e) => e.type === 'user-message' || e.type === 'user_message');
+
+  try {
+    // Baseline: open project A.
+    const opened = await post('/api/open-project', { path: A });
+    ok(opened.status === 200 && opened.body?.activeProject, 'baseline: project A opens');
+
+    // ── (a) second message while a slow run is live -> 409, first run unaffected ──
+    adapter.set(slowScript(250), 250);
+    const m1 = await post('/api/message', { text: 'Read a.txt (run 1).', model: 'mock-model' });
+    ok(m1.status === 200 && m1.body?.ok, '(a) first message accepted (200)');
+    const running = await waitFor((s) => s.status === 'running', 4000);
+    ok(running.status === 'running', '(a) first run is running before the second fire');
+    const m2 = await post('/api/message', { text: 'Second concurrent message.', model: 'mock-model' });
+    bug(m2.status === 409, {
+      caseName: '(a) concurrent-second-message',
+      scenario: 'POST /api/message while a run is already running',
+      expected: '409 (chat is already running)',
+      actual: `HTTP ${m2.status} ${JSON.stringify(m2.body)}`,
+      bugDescription: 'A second /api/message during an active run was not rejected with 409 — the single-run chat lock leaked, risking two runs writing the same event stream.',
+      severity: 'high',
+    });
+    ok(/already running|running/i.test(m2.body?.error || ''), '(a) 409 body mentions "running"');
+    const doneA = await settle();
+    ok(doneA.status === 'done', `(a) first run completed unaffected (got ${doneA.status})`);
+    ok(verbs(doneA).includes('read'), '(a) first run still emitted its read tool-call (not clobbered)');
+    ok(doneA.activeProject === A || doneA.activeProject?.replace(/\\/g, '/') === A, '(a) still on project A after the run');
+
+    // ── (b) open-project (different dir) while a run is live -> 409 backgroundBusy ──
+    adapter.set(slowScript(250), 250);
+    const m3 = await post('/api/message', { text: 'Read a.txt (run for switch test).', model: 'mock-model' });
+    ok(m3.status === 200, '(b) run started for the switch test');
+    const running2 = await waitFor((s) => s.status === 'running', 4000);
+    ok(running2.status === 'running', '(b) run is running before switch attempt');
+    const sw = await post('/api/open-project', { path: B });
+    bug(sw.status === 409, {
+      caseName: '(b) switch-while-running',
+      scenario: 'POST /api/open-project (different dir) while a run is running',
+      expected: '409 (a run is in progress)',
+      actual: `HTTP ${sw.status} ${JSON.stringify(sw.body)}`,
+      bugDescription: 'Switching projects mid-run was not rejected — openProject() would reset chat state + reassign activeProject under the live run (cross-project corruption).',
+      severity: 'high',
+    });
+    const doneB = await settle();
+    ok(doneB.status === 'done', `(b) run completed after blocked switch (got ${doneB.status})`);
+    const stillA = doneB.activeProject === A || doneB.activeProject?.replace(/\\/g, '/') === A;
+    bug(stillA, {
+      caseName: '(b) project-unchanged-after-blocked-switch',
+      scenario: 'activeProject after a switch that should have been 409-blocked',
+      expected: `activeProject stays A (${A})`,
+      actual: `activeProject=${doneB.activeProject}`,
+      bugDescription: 'A switch that returned 409 nevertheless changed activeProject — the busy 409 path leaked a partial project reassignment.',
+      severity: 'high',
+    });
+
+    // ── (c) TOCTOU: concurrent open-project + message. Widen open's readBody await with a big body so a
+    // tiny message can slip past the switching/status checks and launch a run that openProject then resets. ──
+    let toctouHit = false;
+    let toctouDetail = '';
+    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 6 && !toctouHit; attempt++) {
+      // Start from idle on project A with a known conversation, then re-clear so events are clean.
+      await settle();
+      const cur = await getState();
+      const onA = cur.activeProject === A || cur.activeProject?.replace(/\\/g, '/') === A;
+      if (!onA) { await post('/api/open-project', { path: A }); }
+      await post('/api/clear-conversation', {});
+      adapter.set(slowScript(300), 300);   // an accepted message stays 'running' ~300ms+ (definitely live during the switch)
+      const pad = 'x'.repeat(4 * 1024 * 1024);   // ~4MB filler forces multi-chunk readBody -> widens the pre-switching await window
+      const pOpen = post('/api/open-project', { path: B, _pad: pad });
+      const pMsg = post('/api/message', { text: `TOCTOU race msg attempt ${attempt}.`, model: 'mock-model' });
+      const [rOpen, rMsg] = await Promise.all([pOpen, pMsg]);
+      // Mutual exclusion: with a guaranteed-slow (still-running) message, open + message must NOT both be
+      // accepted. Both-200 means openProject() reset chat state while a live run was accepted = corruption.
+      if (rOpen.status === 200 && rMsg.status === 200) {
+        toctouHit = true;
+        const after = await settle();
+        toctouDetail = `attempt ${attempt}: open=200 msg=200; post-settle status=${after.status} activeProject=${after.activeProject} events=${(after.events || []).length}`;
+      } else {
+        // Correct outcome: exactly one accepted (or the message legitimately 409'd for switching/running).
+        ok((rOpen.status === 200) !== (rMsg.status === 200) || rMsg.status === 409,
+           `(c) attempt ${attempt}: mutual exclusion held (open=${rOpen.status} msg=${rMsg.status})`);
+        // If the message was accepted (open lost the race), it must have run against the correct project
+        // without its events being wiped, and the server must settle (no stuck 'running').
+        const after = await settle();
+        bug(terminal(after), {
+          caseName: '(c) no-stuck-run-after-race',
+          scenario: `concurrent open+message settles (attempt ${attempt}, open=${rOpen.status} msg=${rMsg.status})`,
+          expected: 'server settles to a terminal status',
+          actual: `status=${after.status} after 10s`,
+          bugDescription: 'After a concurrent open-project/message race the run never reached a terminal state — a wiped/nulled abortController left the run orphaned and the UI stuck at running.',
+          severity: 'high',
+        });
+        if (rMsg.status === 200) {
+          bug(hasUserMsg(after), {
+            caseName: '(c) accepted-message-events-not-wiped',
+            scenario: `message accepted during a concurrent switch (attempt ${attempt})`,
+            expected: 'the accepted run\'s user-message event survives (not reset mid-run)',
+            actual: `events=${(after.events || []).length}, hasUserMessage=${hasUserMsg(after)}`,
+            bugDescription: 'A message accepted concurrently with a project switch had its events wiped by openProject\'s resetChatState() — cross-project state corruption.',
+            severity: 'high',
+          });
+        }
+        const apOk = after.activeProject === A || after.activeProject === B
+          || after.activeProject?.replace(/\\/g, '/') === A || after.activeProject?.replace(/\\/g, '/') === B;
+        bug(apOk, {
+          caseName: '(c) activeProject-consistent',
+          scenario: `activeProject after concurrent open+message (attempt ${attempt})`,
+          expected: 'activeProject is one of the two known projects',
+          actual: `activeProject=${after.activeProject}`,
+          bugDescription: 'A concurrent open/message race left activeProject null/corrupted.',
+          severity: 'high',
+        });
+      }
+    }
+    if (toctouHit) {
+      bug(false, {
+        caseName: '(c) TOCTOU-switch-lock-race',
+        scenario: 'concurrent /api/open-project (large body) + /api/message',
+        expected: 'the two requests are mutually exclusive (exactly one accepted) — the switch-lock closes the window',
+        actual: `both returned 200 while the message run was live — ${toctouDetail}`,
+        bugDescription: 'TOCTOU in /api/open-project: backgroundBusy() is checked BEFORE `await readBody()`, and session.switching is set only AFTER it. A large open-project body widens that await window so a concurrent /api/message passes its switching+status checks and launches a run; openProject() then calls resetChatState() (which nulls the live run\'s abortController and wipes chatEvents) and reassigns activeProject mid-run — cross-project corruption + an uncancellable orphaned run. Fix: set session.switching = true (or re-check backgroundBusy) synchronously BEFORE awaiting readBody.',
+        severity: 'medium',
+      });
+    } else {
+      ok(true, '(c) TOCTOU window not observed across attempts (switch-lock held)');
+    }
+
+    // ── (d) rapid clear-conversation -> message ──
+    await settle();
+    // ensure on A
+    const cur2 = await getState();
+    if (!(cur2.activeProject === A || cur2.activeProject?.replace(/\\/g, '/') === A)) await post('/api/open-project', { path: A });
+    adapter.set(slowScript(0), 0);   // fast run for the clear-then-send flow
+    const clr = await post('/api/clear-conversation', {});
+    ok(clr.status === 200 && clr.body?.ok, '(d) clear-conversation succeeds when idle');
+    const m4 = await post('/api/message', { text: 'Post-clear message: read a.txt.', model: 'mock-model' });
+    bug(m4.status === 200 && m4.body?.ok, {
+      caseName: '(d) message-after-clear-rejected',
+      scenario: 'POST /api/message immediately after /api/clear-conversation',
+      expected: '200 (message accepted)',
+      actual: `HTTP ${m4.status} ${JSON.stringify(m4.body)}`,
+      bugDescription: 'A message sent right after clear-conversation was rejected — a stale busy/switching flag survived the clear.',
+      severity: 'medium',
+    });
+    const done4 = await settle();
+    ok(done4.status === 'done', `(d) post-clear run completed (got ${done4.status})`);
+    ok(verbs(done4).includes('read'), '(d) post-clear run emitted its read (clean state)');
+    ok(hasUserMsg(done4), '(d) post-clear run recorded its user message');
+
+    // ── (d2) concurrent clear + message (robustness probe): must not corrupt / stick ──
+    await settle();
+    adapter.set(slowScript(150), 150);
+    const [rc, rm] = await Promise.all([post('/api/clear-conversation', {}), post('/api/message', { text: 'Concurrent clear+send.', model: 'mock-model' })]);
+    ok([200, 409].includes(rc.status), `(d2) concurrent clear returns 200 or 409 (got ${rc.status})`);
+    ok([200, 409].includes(rm.status), `(d2) concurrent message returns 200 or 409 (got ${rm.status})`);
+    const after2 = await settle();
+    bug(terminal(after2), {
+      caseName: '(d2) no-stuck-after-concurrent-clear',
+      scenario: 'concurrent clear-conversation + message',
+      expected: 'server settles to a terminal status',
+      actual: `status=${after2.status} after 10s`,
+      bugDescription: 'A concurrent clear-conversation + message left the run stuck (never terminal).',
+      severity: 'high',
+    });
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    await rm(dirA, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+    await rm(dirB, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+  if (failures.length) process.stdout.write(`FAILURES:\n${JSON.stringify(failures, null, 2)}\n`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('R7 harness error:', e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/_r7-diag-snapshot.mjs

+// R7 deeper E2E: Diagnostics / Snapshot / pagination / error-path robustness.
+// The REAL server + a scripted mock adapter (NO network, NO proxy, NO browser).
+// After running a couple of messages we exercise the read-only introspection and
+// error-handling routes and assert none of them crash the process:
+//   - GET  /api/snapshot        -> 200, valid JSON, no RangeError (Round-5 #11 maxParallel reduce)
+//   - GET  /api/state?limit=1 / ?limit=1000 -> pagination sane (hasMoreOlder correct)
+//   - GET  /api/diagnostics + /api/queries  -> 200 (incl. hostile grid dims -> clamped, no RangeError)
+//   - GET  /api/nonexistent     -> 404 (not 500)
+//   - POST /api/message w/ malformed JSON body -> 400 (not a crash)
+//   - readBody cap: a moderate over-limit body is rejected, server survives (not OOM)
+// Server stays alive throughout (asserted by a final /api/state 200).
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+
+function scriptedAdapter(script) {
+  let i = 0;
+  const next = () => script[Math.min(i++, script.length - 1)];
+  return {
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next() } }] }; },
+    async *completeStream() { const t = next(); for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] }; } },
+    async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }; },
+  };
+}
+

+async function main() {

+    ok(Array.isArray(st1.events) && st1.events.length <= 1, `?limit=1 returns <=1 event (got ${st1.events?.length})`);
+    const stBig = await J(await fetch(`${base}/api/state?limit=1000`));
+    const total = stBig.events.length;
+    ok(total >= 1, `?limit=1000 returns all events (got ${total})`);
+    // hasMoreOlder must be TRUE when the window is smaller than the total, FALSE when it covers all.
+    ok(st1.hasMoreOlder === (total > 1), `?limit=1 hasMoreOlder correct (=${st1.hasMoreOlder}, total=${total})`);
+    ok(stBig.hasMoreOlder === false, `?limit=1000 hasMoreOlder=false (covers all ${total})`);
+    // Degenerate limits must not crash.
+    const stZero = await fetch(`${base}/api/state?limit=0`);
+    ok(stZero.status === 200, `?limit=0 -> 200 (got ${stZero.status})`);
+    const stNeg = await fetch(`${base}/api/state?limit=-5`);
+    ok(stNeg.status === 200, `?limit=-5 -> 200 (got ${stNeg.status})`);
+
+    // ── /api/diagnostics ──

+    ok(diagRes.status === 200, `/api/diagnostics -> 200 (got ${diagRes.status})`);
+    let diagParsed = true, diag = null;
+    try { diag = await diagRes.json(); } catch { diagParsed = false; }
+    ok(diagParsed && diag && typeof diag === 'object', '/api/diagnostics returns valid JSON object');
+
+    // ── /api/queries (default) ──
+    const qRes = await fetch(`${base}/api/queries`);
+    ok(qRes.status === 200, `/api/queries -> 200 (got ${qRes.status})`);
+    let qParsed = true, q = null;
+    try { q = await qRes.json(); } catch { qParsed = false; }
+    ok(qParsed && q && Array.isArray(q.tiles) && q.layout, '/api/queries returns tiles + layout');
+
+    // ── /api/queries hostile grid dims: negative + absurdly large must be clamped, not a RangeError-500 ──
+    const qHostile = await fetch(`${base}/api/queries?cols=-5&rows=999999`);
+    ok(qHostile.status === 200, `/api/queries?cols=-5&rows=999999 -> 200 (clamped, got ${qHostile.status})`);
+    let qh = null; try { qh = await qHostile.json(); } catch {}
+    ok(qh && qh.pageCols > 0 && qh.pageRows > 0 && qh.pageCols <= 32 && qh.pageRows <= 32,
+      `hostile dims clamped to sane range (cols=${qh?.pageCols}, rows=${qh?.pageRows})`);
+    // Bogus/nonexistent query ids must not crash.
+    const qBogus = await fetch(`${base}/api/queries?ids=99999,abc,-1`);
+    ok(qBogus.status === 200, `/api/queries?ids=99999,abc,-1 -> 200 (got ${qBogus.status})`);
+
+    // ── Unknown route -> 404 (not 500) ──
+    const nf = await fetch(`${base}/api/nonexistent`);
+    ok(nf.status === 404, `/api/nonexistent -> 404 (got ${nf.status})`);
+
+    // ── Malformed JSON body -> 400 (not a crash) ──
+    const badRes = await fetch(`${base}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: '{ this is not json' });
+    ok(badRes.status === 400, `POST /api/message malformed JSON -> 400 (got ${badRes.status})`);
+
+    // ── readBody cap: a moderate over-limit body is rejected, server survives (not OOM) ──
+    // readBody reads CB_MAX_BODY_BYTES at call time, so lowering it now only affects this test.
+    process.env.CB_MAX_BODY_BYTES = String(64 * 1024);   // 64 KB cap
+    const overBody = 'x'.repeat(512 * 1024);             // 512 KB -> 8x over cap
+    let overRejected = false;
+    try {
+      const overRes = await fetch(`${base}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: overBody });
+      // Either a non-2xx status OR the socket is destroyed (fetch throws) — both are "rejected, not OOM".
+      overRejected = overRes.status >= 400;
+    } catch {
+      overRejected = true;   // socket destroyed by readBody's req.destroy() -> fetch network error
+    } finally {
+      delete process.env.CB_MAX_BODY_BYTES;
+    }
+    ok(overRejected, 'over-limit body rejected (non-2xx or socket destroyed), not OOM');
+
+    // ── Server survived every hostile request ──
+    const alive = await fetch(`${base}/api/state`);
+    ok(alive.status === 200, `server still alive after all probes (/api/state -> ${alive.status})`);
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('R7 diag/snapshot harness error:', e); process.exit(2); });

+// _r7-error-injection.mjs — deeper mocked-E2E: ADAPTER + TOOL error resilience.
+// Same harness pattern as test-minimal-e2e.mjs (REAL server + scripted mock adapters, NO network, NO
+// browser, throwaway temp project). Each scenario spins a fresh server on PORT, drives /api/message over
+// the <remote-workspace> protocol, polls /api/state, and asserts the run reaches a TERMINAL state (never
+// hangs at 'running') and the HTTP layer stays healthy (no crash, /api/state keeps answering).
+//
+// Injected faults:
+//  (a) adapter completeStream / complete THROWS       -> run ends status=error; /api/state ok; follow-up works + RECOVERS
+//  (b) adapter returns EMPTY / MALFORMED response      -> run ends done with a synthesized closer; no crash
+//  (c) worker <read> of a MISSING file mid-run         -> tool-result error, run CONTINUES to done
+//  (d) a tool that THROWS internally (binary read)     -> surfaced as a tool-result error, not a server crash/500
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+const PORT = 18994;
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+const J = (res) => res.json();
+
+// ── scripted stream adapter: turn N -> script[N] (last repeats). Standard shape. ──
+function scriptedAdapter(script) {
+  let i = 0;
+  const next = () => script[Math.min(i++, script.length - 1)];
+  return {
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next() } }] }; },
+    async *completeStream() { const t = next(); for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] }; } },
+    async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }; },
+  };
+}
+
+async function startOn(dir, adapter) {
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<!doctype html><title>mock</title>',
+    baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'mock', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'mock-model', modelsList: [{ id: 'mock-model' }],
+    adapter, apiFormat: 'openai', port: PORT,
+  });
+  return server;
+}
+
+const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+async function open(dir) {
+  return J(await fetch(`${base}/api/open-project`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ path: dir.replace(/\\/g, '/') }) }));
+}
+async function send(text) {
+  return J(await fetch(`${base}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text, model: 'mock-model' }) }));
+}
+// Poll to a terminal status. Returns { state, terminal } — terminal false means it never settled (a hang).
+async function poll(maxMs = 12000) {
+  let state = null, terminal = false;
+  for (let n = 0; n < maxMs / 100; n++) {
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+    let r; try { r = await fetch(`${base}/api/state`); } catch { continue; }
+    if (!r.ok) return { state: null, terminal: false, httpBad: true };
+    state = await r.json();
+    if (state.status === 'done' || state.status === 'error' || state.status === 'idle') { terminal = true; break; }
+  }
+  return { state, terminal };
+}
+const evsOf = (s) => (s?.events || s?.chatEvents || []);
+
+let _dir = null;
+function dir1Path() { return _dir; }
+
+async function scenario(name, { files = {}, adapter, run }) {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-r7-'));
+  _dir = dir;
+  for (const [rel, content] of Object.entries(files)) {
+    await writeFile(path.join(dir, rel), content);
+  }
+  const server = await startOn(dir, adapter);
+  process.stdout.write(`\n[${name}]\n`);
+  try {
+    await run(dir);
+  } catch (e) {
+    ok(false, `${name}: harness threw (${e.message})`);
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 150)); // let the port free before the next scenario
+  }
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+  // (a1) completeStream THROWS on the first run; a follow-up message RECOVERS.

+      const p = await poll();
+      ok(p.terminal, 'b1: empty response still reaches a terminal state (no hang)');
+      ok(p.state?.status === 'done', `b1: empty response settles to done (got ${p.state?.status})`);
+      ok(evsOf(p.state).some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && (e.content || '').trim().length > 0), 'b1: a synthesized closer assistant-text was emitted');
+    },

+// _r7-steer-cancel.mjs — deeper mocked-E2E for STEER + CANCEL over the real server (NO network, NO browser).
+// Harness pattern lifted from test/test-minimal-e2e.mjs: real startServer + a scripted mock adapter driving
+// /api/message over the <remote-workspace> protocol, polling /api/state for the chat-event contract.
+//
+// Focus:
+//   (a) STEER  — start a multi-turn run, POST /api/steer mid-run, assert the steer text is drained into a
+//                later turn (the adapter sees it) and the run continues.
+//   (b) CANCEL — start a run, POST /api/cancel mid-run, assert status flips to idle/error promptly with no
+//                orphaned "running", and a fresh /api/message afterward is accepted + completes.
+//   (c) IDLE CANCEL — POST /api/cancel with nothing running → benign no-op.
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const findings = [];
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+
+// A controllable scripted adapter: the test re-loads a fresh script per scenario and reads back every
+// `messages` array the engine handed the model (so we can prove whether steer text reached a later turn).
+function makeAdapter() {
+  const state = { script: ['Done.'], i: 0, delayMs: 0, calls: [] };
+  const next = () => { const v = state.script[Math.min(state.i, state.script.length - 1)]; state.i++; return v ?? 'Done.'; };
+  return {
+    state,
+    load(script, delayMs = 0) { state.script = script.slice(); state.i = 0; state.delayMs = delayMs; state.calls = []; },
+    async complete(opts) { state.calls.push(opts?.messages || []); return { choices: [{ message: { content: next() } }] }; },
+    async *completeStream(opts) {
+      state.calls.push(opts?.messages || []);
+      const t = next();
+      for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) {
+        if (state.delayMs) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, state.delayMs));
+        if (opts?.signal?.aborted) return;   // honor cancel promptly, like a real streaming adapter
+        yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] };
+      }
+    },
+    async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }; },
+  };
+}
+
+const PORT = 18995;
+const J = (res) => res.json();
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const rw = (body) => `<remote-workspace>\n${body}\n</remote-workspace>`;
+
+async function main() {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-r7-'));
+  for (const f of ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd', 'e', 'f', 'g', 'h']) await writeFile(path.join(dir, `${f}.txt`), `contents of ${f}\n`);
+
+  const adapter = makeAdapter();
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<!doctype html><title>mock</title>',
+    baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'mock', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'mock-model', modelsList: [{ id: 'mock-model' }],
+    adapter, apiFormat: 'openai', port: PORT,
+  });
+  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+
+  const state = async () => J(await fetch(`${base}/api/state`));
+  const post = async (p, body) => fetch(`${base}${p}`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body ?? {}) });
+  const send = (text) => post('/api/message', { text, model: 'mock-model' });
+  const pollUntil = async (pred, timeoutMs = 6000, every = 40) => {
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    while (Date.now() - t0 < timeoutMs) { const s = await state(); if (pred(s)) return { s, ms: Date.now() - t0 }; await sleep(every); }
+    return { s: await state(), ms: Date.now() - t0, timedOut: true };
+  };
+
+  try {
+    const opened = await J(await post('/api/open-project', { path: dir.replace(/\\/g, '/') }));
+    ok(opened.activeProject, 'project opens');
+
+    // ── (a) STEER ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    // A genuinely multi-turn run: read a different file each turn (distinct churn signatures) so the run
+    // does NOT wrap up early, giving a wide mid-run window to inject a steer.
+    const STEER_MARK = 'STEER_MARK_check_the_config_xyz';
+    adapter.load([
+      rw('<read path="a.txt"/>'),
+      rw('<read path="b.txt"/>'),
+      rw('<read path="c.txt"/>'),
+      rw('<read path="d.txt"/>'),
+      'All files reviewed — done.',
+    ], 120);
+
+    const sA = await J(await send('Review a.txt through d.txt one at a time.'));
+    ok(sA.ok, '(a) multi-turn message accepted');
+
+    // Wait until we're demonstrably MID-RUN: first tool-result landed (turn 1 done, more turns to come).
+    const mid = await pollUntil((s) => (s.events || s.chatEvents || []).some((e) => e.type === 'tool-result'), 4000);
+    ok(!mid.timedOut && mid.s.status === 'running', `(a) run is mid-flight after turn 1 (status=${mid.s.status})`);
+
+    // Inject the steer.
+    const steerRes = await post('/api/steer', { text: STEER_MARK });
+    const steerStatus = steerRes.status;
+    let steerBody = null; try { steerBody = await steerRes.clone().json(); } catch { try { steerBody = await steerRes.text(); } catch {} }
+
+    // Let the run finish.
+    const doneA = await pollUntil((s) => s.status === 'done' || s.status === 'error' || s.status === 'idle', 8000);
+    ok(doneA.s.status === 'done', `(a) run continues to completion after steer (status=${doneA.s.status})`);
+    ok(adapter.state.calls.length >= 3, `(a) run was genuinely multi-turn (${adapter.state.calls.length} model calls)`);
+
+    // The steer endpoint must be handled (not a 404) — the UI's steer button POSTs to it.
+    const steerHandled = steerStatus !== 404;
+    ok(steerHandled, `(a) POST /api/steer is handled by the server (got HTTP ${steerStatus})`);
+    if (!steerHandled) {
+      findings.push({
+        caseName: '(a) steer', scenario: 'Mid-run POST /api/steer {text} while a run is in flight.',
+        expected: 'The server accepts the steer (2xx) and queues the text for the running worker.',
+        actual: `HTTP ${steerStatus} — /api/steer has no route handler and falls through to the generic "Not found" 404.`,
+        bugDescription: 'The steer button in ui.html POSTs to /api/steer, but src/server.mjs has NO /api/steer route handler (only a steerBuffer field + a "see drainSteer below" comment that references a function that does not exist). Every user click on the mid-run "Send now" steer button hits a 404; the feature is entirely unwired on the server.',
+        severity: 'high',
+      });
+    }
+
+    // Even if the endpoint were reachable, the steer text must actually reach the model on a LATER turn.
+    const sawSteer = adapter.state.calls.some((msgs) => JSON.stringify(msgs).includes(STEER_MARK));
+    ok(sawSteer, '(a) steer text was drained into a later model turn (adapter saw it)');
+    if (!sawSteer) {
+      findings.push({
+        caseName: '(a) steer drain', scenario: 'After a successful mid-run steer, subsequent worker turns should include the steered text.',
+        expected: 'A later completeStream call carries the steered text in its messages so the worker acts on it.',
+        actual: 'No model turn ever received the steered text. runMinimalWorker (the active worker) takes no steer/drainSteer callback and never reads session.steerBuffer, so even a queued steer is dropped.',
+        bugDescription: 'session.steerBuffer is declared and (via bg-wake) pushed to, but the worker loop never drains it. runMinimalWorker in src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs has no getSteer/drainSteer parameter and the /api/message call site passes none. The documented "worker loop drains this at each turn boundary" behavior is unimplemented — steered text can never influence a running run.',
+        severity: 'high',
+      });
+    }
+
+    // ── (b) CANCEL mid-run ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    // Wait for scenario (a) to fully settle to idle-controller state before starting the next run.
+    await pollUntil((s) => s.status !== 'running', 4000);
+    adapter.load([
+      rw('<read path="e.txt"/>'),
+      rw('<read path="f.txt"/>'),
+      rw('<read path="g.txt"/>'),
+      rw('<read path="h.txt"/>'),
+      'Long review complete.',
+    ], 200);
+
+    const sB = await J(await send('Do a long review of e.txt through h.txt.'));
+    ok(sB.ok, '(b) long-run message accepted');
+
+    const midB = await pollUntil((s) => s.status === 'running' && adapter.state.calls.length >= 1, 4000);
+    ok(!midB.timedOut && midB.s.status === 'running', `(b) long run is mid-flight (status=${midB.s.status})`);
+
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    const cancelRes = await J(await post('/api/cancel'));
+    ok(cancelRes.ok, '(b) /api/cancel accepted');
+
+    const cancelled = await pollUntil((s) => s.status !== 'running', 3000, 25);
+    const flipMs = Date.now() - t0;
+    ok(!cancelled.timedOut && (cancelled.s.status === 'idle' || cancelled.s.status === 'error' || cancelled.s.status === 'done'),
+      `(b) status flips out of running promptly after cancel (status=${cancelled.s.status}, ${flipMs}ms)`);
+    ok(flipMs < 2500, `(b) cancel is prompt (<2.5s; took ${flipMs}ms)`);
+    if (cancelled.timedOut || cancelled.s.status === 'running') {
+      findings.push({
+        caseName: '(b) cancel flip', scenario: 'POST /api/cancel while a run is in flight.',
+        expected: 'session.status leaves "running" promptly (idle/error).',
+        actual: `Status was still "${cancelled.s.status}" ${flipMs}ms after cancel.`,
+        bugDescription: 'A user cancel did not clear the running state — the UI would stay frozen at "running" (orphaned run).',
+        severity: 'high',
+      });
+    }
+
+    // No orphaned running: it should stay non-running after a short settle window.
+    await sleep(400);
+    const settled = await state();
+    ok(settled.status !== 'running', `(b) no orphaned "running" after cancel settles (status=${settled.status})`);
+
+    // A fresh message afterward must be accepted and complete (409 "cancelling" is transient → retry).
+    adapter.load(['Fresh reply after cancel — all good.'], 0);
+    let acceptedNew = false, newStatus = 0, newBody = null;
+    for (let n = 0; n < 20 && !acceptedNew; n++) {
+      const r = await send('New task after the cancel.');
+      newStatus = r.status; newBody = await r.json().catch(() => null);
+      if (r.ok && newBody?.ok) { acceptedNew = true; break; }
+      await sleep(75);
+    }
+    ok(acceptedNew, `(b) a fresh /api/message after cancel is accepted (last HTTP ${newStatus})`);
+    if (!acceptedNew) {
+      findings.push({
+        caseName: '(b) post-cancel message', scenario: 'Send a new /api/message after cancelling the prior run.',
+        expected: 'The new message is accepted (200 ok) once the cancelled run settles.',
+        actual: `Never accepted after retries (last HTTP ${newStatus}, body ${JSON.stringify(newBody)}).`,
+        bugDescription: 'The session stayed wedged after cancel — new work is rejected, so cancel leaves the chat permanently unusable.',
+        severity: 'high',
+      });
+    }
+    const doneB = await pollUntil((s) => s.status === 'done' || s.status === 'error', 6000);
+    ok(doneB.s.status === 'done', `(b) the post-cancel run completes normally (status=${doneB.s.status})`);
+    if (acceptedNew && doneB.s.status !== 'done') {
+      findings.push({
+        caseName: '(b) post-cancel completion', scenario: 'The accepted post-cancel run should run to completion.',
+        expected: 'status === done',
+        actual: `status === ${doneB.s.status}`,
+        bugDescription: 'A new run started after a cancel did not complete cleanly — the cancel left residual state that broke the next run.',
+        severity: 'medium',
+      });
+    }
+
+    // ── (c) IDLE CANCEL — benign no-op ─────────────────────────────────────────
+    await pollUntil((s) => s.status !== 'running', 3000);
+    const before = (await state()).status;
+    const idleCancel = await post('/api/cancel');
+    const idleCancelBody = await idleCancel.json().catch(() => null);
+    ok(idleCancel.status === 200 && idleCancelBody?.ok, `(c) cancel while idle returns 200 ok (HTTP ${idleCancel.status})`);
+    const after = (await state()).status;
+    ok(after !== 'running' && after !== 'error', `(c) idle cancel does not flip state to running/error (before=${before}, after=${after})`);
+    if (idleCancel.status !== 200 || after === 'running' || after === 'error') {
+      findings.push({
+        caseName: '(c) idle cancel', scenario: 'POST /api/cancel with nothing running.',
+        expected: 'A benign 200 no-op that leaves the idle/done state untouched.',
+        actual: `HTTP ${idleCancel.status}, status before=${before} after=${after}.`,
+        bugDescription: 'Cancelling while idle was not a clean no-op (it errored or corrupted the session status).',
+        severity: 'low',
+      });
+    }
+    // steer HTTP body is only informative; surface it in the log for the record.
+    process.stdout.write(`  note: /api/steer returned HTTP ${steerStatus} body=${JSON.stringify(steerBody)}\n`);
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+
+  process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+  // Emit a machine-readable findings block for the harness.
+  process.stdout.write(`\n__FINDINGS__${JSON.stringify(findings)}__FINDINGS__\n`);
+  process.exit(0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('E2E harness error:', e); process.exit(2); });
