+// ── The seed codebase: a small notes API with deliberate cruft (a sync file read on every request,
+// a store that reloads the file every call, inline duplicated routing, no validation, no tests) —
+// so the vague "make it better / faster" asks have something real to chew on. ──
+const SEED = {
+  'package.json': JSON.stringify({
+    name: 'notes-api', version: '0.1.0', description: 'a tiny notes service',
+    scripts: { start: 'node src/server.js', test: 'node --test' },
+  }, null, 2) + '\n',
+  'README.md': '# notes-api\n\nA tiny HTTP notes service. `npm start` runs it on port 3000.\n',
+  'src/store.js': `const fs = require('fs');
+const path = require('path');
+
+const FILE = process.env.NOTES_FILE || path.join(__dirname, '..', 'notes.json');
+
+// Loads the whole file from disk every single call.
+function all() {
+  try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(FILE, 'utf8')); } catch { return []; }
+}
+function save(notes) { fs.writeFileSync(FILE, JSON.stringify(notes)); }
+
+function add(text) {
+  const notes = all();
+  const note = { id: notes.length ? notes[notes.length - 1].id + 1 : 1, text: text, created: Date.now() };
+  notes.push(note);
+  save(notes);
+  return note;
+}
+function get(id) { return all().find((n) => n.id === id); }
+function remove(id) { const notes = all().filter((n) => n.id !== id); save(notes); }
+
+module.exports = { all, add, get, remove };
+`,
+  'src/server.js': `const http = require('http');
+const store = require('./store');
+
+const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
+  const url = req.url;
+  const method = req.method;
+
+  if (method === 'GET' && url === '/notes') {
+    res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json');
+    res.end(JSON.stringify(store.all()));
+    return;
+  }
+
+  if (method === 'POST' && url === '/notes') {
+    let body = '';
+    req.on('data', (c) => { body += c; });
+    req.on('end', () => {
+      const data = JSON.parse(body);
+      const note = store.add(data.text);
+      res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json');
+      res.end(JSON.stringify(note));
+    });
+    return;
+  }
+
+  if (method === 'GET' && url.indexOf('/notes/') === 0) {
+    const id = Number(url.split('/')[2]);
+    const note = store.get(id);
+    res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json');
+    res.end(JSON.stringify(note));
+    return;
+  }
+
+  if (method === 'DELETE' && url.indexOf('/notes/') === 0) {
+    const id = Number(url.split('/')[2]);
+    store.remove(id);
+    res.end('ok');
+    return;
+  }
+
+  res.statusCode = 404;
+  res.end('not found');
+});
+
+server.listen(process.env.PORT || 3000);
+module.exports = server;
+`,
+};
+
+// ── The vague, complex asks. Under-specified on purpose: no acceptance criteria, no scope, the
+// kind of thing a busy developer actually types. Each builds on the last (one evolving session). ──
+const TASKS = [
+  'the notes app feels kind of sluggish and honestly the code is a bit of a mess. can you make it better?',
+  'people keep saying they cant find their old notes. can we do something about that?',
+  "i'd like users to be able to group their notes together somehow. design something reasonable and build it.",
+];
+
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+// ── UI-shaped renderer ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+// Maps the onChatEvent stream to lines that read like the chat log. Tool-call + its tool-result
+// (matched by callId) collapse into ONE row ending in a status — never the result body.
+const W = 78;
+const rule = (ch) => ch.repeat(W);
+function wrap(text, indent = '') {
+  const out = [];
+  for (const para of String(text ?? '').replace(/\r/g, '').split('\n')) {
+    if (!para.trim()) { out.push(''); continue; }
+    let line = '';
+    for (const word of para.split(/\s+/)) {
+      if (line && (line.length + 1 + word.length) > (W - indent.length)) { out.push(indent + line); line = word; }
+      else line = line ? line + ' ' + word : word;
+    }
+    if (line) out.push(indent + line);
+  }
+  return out.join('\n');
+}
+function toolSummary(ev) {
+  const a = ev.args || {};
+  const s = a.path || a.file || a.pattern || a.query || a.cmd || a.command || a.url || a.name || '';
+  return String(s).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 54);
+}
+const STATUS_GLYPH = { ok: 'ok', error: 'error', skip: 'skip', aborted: 'aborted' };
+
+function renderTranscript(events, meta) {
+  // index tool-results by callId for the one-line collapse
+  const resultByCall = new Map();
+  for (const e of events) if (e.type === 'tool-result' && e.callId) resultByCall.set(e.callId, e);
+
+  const L = [];
+  L.push(rule('━'));
+  L.push(` code_boss session transcript`);
+  L.push(` model: ${meta.model}   ·   ${meta.when}`);
+  L.push(` proxy: ${meta.proxy}`);
+  L.push(` (UI-shaped: developer + agent + reviewer messages, tool rows w/ status only — no result bodies)`);
+  L.push(rule('━'));
+
+  let lastAgentHeader = false;       // group consecutive assistant-text under one "Agent" header
+  const flushAgent = () => { lastAgentHeader = false; };
+
+  for (const e of events) {
+    switch (e.type) {
+      case 'user-message': {
+        flushAgent();
+        L.push('');
+        L.push(rule('─'));
+        L.push(' ▎ Developer' + (e.imageCount ? `  (+${e.imageCount} image)` : ''));
+        L.push(wrap(e.content, ' ▎ '));
+        L.push(rule('─'));
+        break;
+      }
+      // Also dormant after the port: the old loop emitted a <phase> WORKING MODE (exploring/designing/…)
+      // as a chat event. The minimal worker's onPhase reports NETWORK phase instead (uploading/thinking/
+      // downloading), which is not conversational — main() drops it rather than render it here.
+      case 'phase': {
+        flushAgent();
+        L.push('');
+        L.push(`   · · · working mode: ${e.phase} · · ·`);
+        break;
+      }
+      case 'assistant-text': {
+        const txt = String(e.content ?? '').trim();
+        if (!txt) break;
+        if (!lastAgentHeader) { L.push(''); L.push(' Agent'); lastAgentHeader = true; }
+        L.push(wrap(txt, '  '));
+        break;
+      }
+      case 'tool-call': {
+        // tool rows live under the current agent turn; keep the header
+        if (!lastAgentHeader) { L.push(''); L.push(' Agent'); lastAgentHeader = true; }
+        const verb = (e.verb || e.name || '?').toLowerCase();
+        const summ = toolSummary(e);
+        const res = e.callId ? resultByCall.get(e.callId) : null;
+        const status = res ? (STATUS_GLYPH[res.status] || res.status || '?') : '…';
+        const left = `   ${verb}${summ ? '  ' + summ : ''}`;
+        const dots = Math.max(3, W - 8 - left.length);
+        L.push(`${left} ${'·'.repeat(dots)} ${status}`);
+        break;
+      }
+      case 'tool-result':       // consumed by its tool-call row
+      case 'diff':              // result detail — intentionally omitted
+      case 'context-fill':      // gauge — not conversational
+        break;
+      // The continuous reviewer was removed from the product with the old agent loop, so this arm no
+      // longer fires. Kept because it costs nothing and documents the shape a reviewer note had, in
+      // case a review pass returns.
+      case 'reviewer-note': {
+        flushAgent();
+        L.push('');
+        L.push(` 🔍 Self-review${e.level ? ' · ' + e.level : ''}`);
+        L.push(wrap(e.content, '  '));
+        break;
+      }
+      case 'task-end': {
+        flushAgent();
+        const commit = e.commitHash ? ` (${String(e.commitHash).slice(0, 7)})` : (e.commits?.length ? ` (${e.commits.length} commits)` : '');
+        L.push('');
+        L.push(` ── task closed${commit}: ${String(e.summary || e.userRequest || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 70)} ──`);
+        if (e.commitError) L.push(`    ⚠ commit error: ${String(e.commitError).slice(0, 80)}`);
+        break;
+      }
+      default:
+        break;
+    }
+  }
+  L.push('');
+  L.push(rule('━'));
+  L.push(` end · ${events.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call').length} tool calls · ` +
+         `${events.filter((e) => e.type === 'reviewer-note').length} self-reviews · ` +
+         `${events.filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && String(e.content || '').trim()).length} agent messages`);
+  L.push(rule('━'));
+  return L.join('\n');
+}
+
+async function scaffold() {
+  if (!KEEP) await rm(PROJECT, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  if (existsSync(path.join(PROJECT, '.git'))) return;
+  for (const [rel, content] of Object.entries(SEED)) {
+    const abs = path.join(PROJECT, rel);
+    await mkdir(path.dirname(abs), { recursive: true });
+    await writeFile(abs, content);
+  }
+  await runGit(PROJECT, ['init', '-q', '-b', 'main']);
+  await runGit(PROJECT, ['config', 'user.email', 'tx@test']);
+  await runGit(PROJECT, ['config', 'user.name', 'tx']);
+  await runGit(PROJECT, ['add', '-A']);
+  await runGit(PROJECT, ['commit', '-q', '-m', 'init: tiny notes-api with cruft']);
+  console.log('scaffolded notes-api at', PROJECT);
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!process.env.CB_API_KEY && !process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_ID) { console.error('CB_API_KEY or CB_CF_ACCESS_ID required'); process.exit(2); }
+  await mkdir(TX_DIR, { recursive: true });
+  await scaffold();
+
+  const eh = {};
+  if (process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_ID) eh['CF-Access-Client-Id'] = process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_ID;
+  if (process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_SECRET) eh['CF-Access-Client-Secret'] = process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_SECRET;
+  if (process.env.CB_SUPABASE_KEY) eh.apikey = process.env.CB_SUPABASE_KEY;
+  const adapter = assertingAdapter(createAnthropicAdapter({
+    baseURL: PROXY + '/v1',
+    apiKey: process.env.CB_API_KEY || 'x',
+    onRateLimit: (ms) => console.error(`  [rate-limit] ${ms}ms`),
+    ...(Object.keys(eh).length ? { extraHeaders: eh } : {}),
+  }), 'transcript-proxy');
+
+  const which = (process.env.CB_TX_TASKS || '').split(',').map((s) => Number(s.trim())).filter((n) => n >= 1);
+  const tasks = which.length ? which.map((n) => TASKS[n - 1]).filter(Boolean) : TASKS;
+
+  const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-').slice(0, 19);
+  const txtPath = path.join(TX_DIR, `${stamp}-transcript.txt`);
+  const jsonlPath = path.join(TX_DIR, `${stamp}-events.jsonl`);
+  await writeFile(txtPath, '');     // start clean; we rewrite the full render after each task
+
+  const events = [];
+  const onChatEvent = (ev) => {
+    events.push(ev);
+    appendFile(jsonlPath, JSON.stringify(ev) + '\n').catch(() => {});
+    // live console heartbeat (compact)
+    if (ev.type === 'assistant-text' && String(ev.content || '').trim()) process.stdout.write('.');
+    else if (ev.type === 'tool-call') process.stdout.write('t');
+    else if (ev.type === 'reviewer-note') process.stdout.write('R');
+    else if (ev.type === 'task-end') process.stdout.write('|');
+    return { id: 'ev-' + events.length };   // emitChat callers may read .id
+  };
+
+  const meta = { model: MODEL, when: stamp, proxy: PROXY };
+
+  for (let i = 0; i < tasks.length; i++) {
+    const text = tasks[i];
+    console.log(`\n\n=== task ${i + 1}/${tasks.length}: "${text.slice(0, 60)}…" ===`);
+    const ac = new AbortController();
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => ac.abort(), TASK_TIMEOUT);
+    // ONE CONTINUOUS CONVERSATION. The old loop carried context across asks in its task store; the
+    // minimal worker is stateless and takes `history` per run, exactly as the server passes it. Rebuild
+    // it from the events captured so far — i.e. every prior task's turns — before adding this ask.
+    const history = historyFromChatEvents(events);
+    try {
+      await runMinimalWorker({
+        adapter,
+        model: MODEL,
+        projectDir: PROJECT,
+        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: text }],
+        history,
+        maxTurns: MAX_TURNS,
+        signal: ac.signal,
+        onChatEvent,
+        onPhase: () => {},
+        onLog: () => {},
+        // DROPPED in the prompted.mjs → minimal-worker port, all features that no longer exist:
+        //   parallelCritic     — the continuous reviewer was removed with the old loop
+        //   phaseAdversaries   — the break-the-design adversary fleet went with it
+        //   enforceMinTaskSize — belonged to the task system (src/agent/tasks.mjs), also removed
+        //   repoRoots          — the worker takes projectDir and has no repo-roots concept
+      });
+    } catch (e) {
+      const msg = e?.name === 'AbortError' ? `(task ${i + 1} hit the ${Math.round(TASK_TIMEOUT / 1000)}s timeout)` : `(task ${i + 1} threw: ${e?.message || e})`;
+      console.error('\n' + msg);
+      events.push({ type: 'task-end', summary: msg });
+    } finally {
+      clearTimeout(timer);
+    }
+    // re-render the whole transcript after each task so partial progress is always on disk
+    await writeFile(txtPath, renderTranscript(events, meta));
+    // Brief settle before the next task. This used to be here so a trailing ASYNC reviewer note could
+    // land; the reviewer is gone, but the worker's last chat events are still emitted through async
+    // appendFile above, so the pause (and the second render below) is what keeps the on-disk transcript
+    // complete if the process is interrupted between tasks.
+    await sleep(1500);
+    await writeFile(txtPath, renderTranscript(events, meta));
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n\ntranscript : ${txtPath}`);
+  console.log(`raw events : ${jsonlPath}`);
+  process.exit(0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('\nFATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(1); });

+// TYPO TOLERANCE, MEASURED (piishape typoOf and splitTypo, 2026-10-11). Run: node test/_typo-measure.mjs
+//   (a) every name in the lexicon's sources - all 2010 surnames, every given name, every place - written lower-case
+//       mid-sentence: how many does the masker now show AS A TYPO? Must be 0.
+//   (b) the same with typoNames built WITHOUT the given names, tested on the given names alone: an estimate of how
+//       often a name missing from every list slips through.
+//   (c) misspellings of names (one swap of a common given name or surname) that are shown as a typo.
+//   (d) common typing slips: which are shown, which stay hidden, and why.
+import { load, typoNames, typoNear, swaps, splitNames } from './pii-contest/build-lexicon.mjs';
+import { createShapeMasker, loadLexicon } from './pii-contest/piishape.mjs';
+
+const lex = loadLexicon();
+const { english, surnames, given, places, everyGiven } = load();
+
+function shownAsTypo(masker, words) {
+  const out = [];
+  for (let k = 0; k < words.length; k += 2000) {
+    const chunk = words.slice(k, k + 2000);
+    const text = chunk.map((w) => `see the ${w} here`).join('\n');
+    for (const r of masker.analyze(text, { filePath: 'notes.md' }).runs) if (r.why === 'typo') out.push(r.t);
+  }
+  return out;
+}
+
+const masker = createShapeMasker({ lexicon: lex });
+const allNames = [...new Set([...surnames.keys(), ...everyGiven, ...places])].filter((n) => !english.has(n));
+const leaks = shownAsTypo(masker, allNames);
+console.log(`(a) names in the sources (not English words): ${allNames.length}; shown as a typo: ${leaks.length}${leaks.length ? ' - ' + leaks.slice(0, 50).join(' ') : ''}`);
+
+const heldOut = createShapeMasker({ lexicon: { ...lex, typoNames: typoNames({ english, surnames, everyGiven: new Set(), places }), typoNear: typoNear({ english, surnames, given: new Set(), places }) } });
+const givenOnly = [...everyGiven].filter((n) => !english.has(n) && !surnames.has(n) && !places.has(n));
+const slip = shownAsTypo(heldOut, givenOnly);
+console.log(`(b) typoNames without the given names; given names on no other list: ${givenOnly.length}; shown as a typo: ${slip.length} (${(100 * slip.length / givenOnly.length).toFixed(2)}%) - ${slip.slice(0, 40).join(' ')}`);
+
+for (const [what, list] of [['surnames, given names over the floor, places', [...surnames.keys(), ...given, ...places]], ['every given name (the rarest are not on typoNear)', [...everyGiven]]]) {
+  const names = [...new Set(list)].filter((n) => !english.has(n));
+  const variants = [...new Set(names.flatMap(swaps))].filter((v) => !english.has(v));
+  const mis = shownAsTypo(masker, variants);
+  console.log(`(c) one-swap misspellings of ${names.length} names (${what}): ${variants.length}; shown as a typo: ${mis.length} (${(100 * mis.length / variants.length).toFixed(2)}%)${mis.length ? ' - ' + mis.slice(0, 30).join(' ') : ''}`);
+}
+
+// (e) A MISPLACED SPACE (splitTypo): can a name be shown because a letter moved between it and the word beside it
+//     makes two words? Every name, against EVERY English word on either side: the candidate pairs are found by
+//     construction, then each is put through the real masker in a sentence.
+{
+  const E = english;
+  // per letter c, one word w of each kind (indexes, so each name costs 26 lookups, not one per English word)
+  const takesAfter = {}, endsIn = {}, takesBefore = {}, startsWith = {};
+  const letters = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz';
+  for (const w of E) {
+    if (w.length < 2 || !/^[a-z]+$/.test(w)) continue;
+    for (const c of letters) {
+      if (!takesAfter[c] && E.has(w + c)) takesAfter[c] = w;          // w, and w+c is a word
+      if (!takesBefore[c] && E.has(c + w)) takesBefore[c] = w;        // w, and c+w is a word
+    }
+    const c1 = w.slice(-1), c0 = w[0];
+    if (!endsIn[c1] && E.has(w.slice(0, -1))) endsIn[c1] = w;         // w = (word) + c
+    if (!startsWith[c0] && E.has(w.slice(1))) startsWith[c0] = w;     // w = c + (word)
+  }
+  const lookup = (n) => {
+    const pairs = [];
+    if (E.has(n.slice(1)) && takesAfter[n[0]]) pairs.push(`${takesAfter[n[0]]} ${n}`);             // "w n" -> "w+n0" "n[1:]"
+    for (const c of letters) if (E.has(c + n) && endsIn[c]) { pairs.push(`${endsIn[c]} ${n}`); break; }   // "w n" -> "w[:-1]" "c+n"
+    if (E.has(n.slice(0, -1)) && takesBefore[n.slice(-1)]) pairs.push(`${n} ${takesBefore[n.slice(-1)]}`); // "n w" -> "n[:-1]" "c+w"
+    for (const c of letters) if (E.has(n + c) && startsWith[c]) { pairs.push(`${n} ${startsWith[c]}`); break; } // "n w" -> "n+c" "w[1:]"
+    return pairs;
+  };
+  const cand = [];
+  for (const n of allNames) { if (n.length < 2 || !/^[a-z]+$/.test(n)) continue; const p = lookup(n); if (p.length) cand.push([n, p]); }
+  let shownNames = new Set();
+  const lines = cand.flatMap(([n, ps]) => ps.map((p) => ({ n, text: `see the ${p} here` })));
+  for (let k = 0; k < lines.length; k += 2000) {
+    const chunk = lines.slice(k, k + 2000);
+    const res = masker.analyze(chunk.map((x) => x.text).join('\n'), { filePath: 'notes.md' }).runs;
+    const names = new Set(chunk.map((x) => x.n));
+    for (const r of res) if (r.why === 'typo-split' && names.has(r.t)) shownNames.add(r.t);
+  }
+  console.log(`(e) names one moved letter from a word pair: ${cand.length} of ${allNames.length}; shown by a misplaced space: ${shownNames.size}${shownNames.size ? ' - ' + [...shownNames].slice(0, 40).join(' ') : ''}`);
+  // (f) held out, as (b): splitNames built WITHOUT the given names, tested on given names on no other list - how often
+  //     a name missing from every list is shown by a misplaced space
+  const held = createShapeMasker({ lexicon: { ...lex, splitNames: splitNames({ english, surnames, everyGiven: new Set(), places }) } });
+  const gcand = cand.filter(([n]) => !surnames.has(n) && !places.has(n));
+  const glines = gcand.flatMap(([n, ps]) => ps.map((p) => ({ n, text: `see the ${p} here` })));
+  const heldShown = new Set();
+  for (let k = 0; k < glines.length; k += 2000) {
+    const chunk = glines.slice(k, k + 2000);
+    const names = new Set(chunk.map((x) => x.n));
+    for (const r of held.analyze(chunk.map((x) => x.text).join('\n'), { filePath: 'notes.md' }).runs) if (r.why === 'typo-split' && names.has(r.t)) heldShown.add(r.t);
+  }
+  const gTotal = [...everyGiven].filter((n) => !english.has(n) && !surnames.has(n) && !places.has(n)).length;
+  console.log(`(f) splitNames without the given names; given names on no other list: ${gTotal}, ${gcand.length} of them beside a word that a moved letter completes; shown: ${heldShown.size} (${(100 * heldShown.size / gTotal).toFixed(2)}% of all) - ${[...heldShown].slice(0, 30).join(' ')}`);
+  const splits = ['yo ushould deploy', 'th equick fix', 'we should deplo ythis', 'i hav eto go', 'run th etests', 'chec kthe logs', 'op en the file', 'ad dthe line', 'fi xthe build', 'wha tis it', 'tha tworks', 'pus hthe branch', 'sav ethe file', 'pin gthe server'];
+  const shownSplit = [];
+  for (const s of splits) { const rs = masker.analyze(s, { filePath: 'notes.md' }).runs; if (rs.every((r) => r.show)) shownSplit.push(s); }
+  console.log(`    slips fully shown: ${shownSplit.length}/${splits.length} - shown: ${shownSplit.join(' | ')} - hidden: ${splits.filter((s) => !shownSplit.includes(s)).join(' | ')}`);
+}
+
+const slips = ['hte', 'ifles', 'teh', 'recieve', 'wiht', 'fiel', 'functoin', 'becuase', 'thier', 'adn', 'jsut', 'waht', 'taht', 'whcih', 'shoudl', 'woudl', 'coudl', 'chnage', 'udpate', 'retrun', 'fucntion', 'lenght', 'widht', 'heigth', 'strign', 'flie', 'yuo', 'yoru', 'mroe', 'frmo', 'hwo', 'tihs', 'thsi', 'fiels', 'tset', 'tets', 'erorr', 'sevrer', 'servre', 'clinet', 'reuqest', 'repsonse', 'databse', 'confg'];
+const shown = new Set(shownAsTypo(masker, slips));
+console.log(`(d) common slips shown: ${shown.size}/${slips.length}`);
+console.log('    shown:  ' + slips.filter((w) => shown.has(w)).join(' '));
+console.log('    hidden: ' + slips.filter((w) => !shown.has(w)).map((w) => `${w}${lex.typoNames.includes(w) ? ' (a name)' : lex.typoNear.includes(w) ? ' (one swap from a name)' : swaps(w).some((s) => english.has(s)) ? ' (?)' : ' (not one swap)'}`).join(', '));

+/**
+ * _ui-step.mjs — ONE action on the code_boss held open by _live-ui-drive.mjs, then a screenshot to look at.
+ *
+ * Each call does exactly one thing, so the next one is chosen after looking at the screen:
+ *   shot                      screenshot only
+ *   click <css>               click the first match (the LAST with --last)
+ *   clicktext <text> [css]    click the visible element whose own text is exactly <text> (within css, default *)
+ *   type <css> <text>         focus and type
+ *   send <text>               type into the message box and press Enter
+ *   key <Key>                 press a key (Escape, Enter, ...)
+ *   scroll <css> <y|bottom>   scroll an element
+ *   hover <css>               move the mouse over it
+ *   wait [maxSec]             wait until the agent is not running (default 600 s), then screenshot
+ *   read [css] [n]            print the visible text of the last n matches (default the last 12 log rows); no shot
+ *   gw [n]                    the gateway's last n requests (lane, session or whole, chars read); no shot
+ *   errs                      page errors so far; no shot
+ *   eval <js>                 evaluate an expression in the page and print it; no shot
+ * Screenshots go to CB_SHOT_DIR (default the OS temp dir) as step-NNN-<action>.png; the path is printed.
+ */
+import { readFileSync, existsSync, readdirSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const cfg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '.live-ui-drive.json'), 'utf8'));
+const SHOTS = process.env.CB_SHOT_DIR || path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-ui-steps');
+if (!existsSync(SHOTS)) mkdirSync(SHOTS, { recursive: true });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const [act, ...rest] = process.argv.slice(2);
+const last = rest.includes('--last'); const args = rest.filter((a) => a !== '--last');
+
+if (act === 'gw') {
+  const j = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${cfg.gwLogPort}/?n=${Number(args[0] || 12)}`)).json();
+  for (const e of j) console.log(`${e.status} ${e.model.padEnd(32)} ${e.lane || ''} ${e.sentSession ? 'on-session' : 'whole'} read ${Math.round((e.readChars || 0) / 1000)}k turns ${e.historyTurns ?? ''} session ${String(e.session || '').slice(0, 8)}`);
+  process.exit(0);
+}
+
+const browser = await puppeteer.connect({ browserURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:' + cfg.debugPort, defaultViewport: null });
+const page = (await browser.pages()).find((p) => p.url().includes('127.0.0.1:' + cfg.port));
+if (!page) { console.error('no code_boss page open - is _live-ui-drive.mjs running?'); process.exit(2); }
+const status = () => page.evaluate(() => (window.__sessionRef || {}).status || 'unknown');
+const pick = async (css) => { const all = await page.$$(css); if (!all.length) throw new Error('nothing matches ' + css); return last ? all[all.length - 1] : all[0]; };
+
+async function shot(label) {
+  const n = readdirSync(SHOTS).filter((f) => /^step-\d+/.test(f)).length + 1;
+  const file = path.join(SHOTS, `step-${String(n).padStart(3, '0')}-${String(label).replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '-').slice(0, 30)}.png`);
+  await page.screenshot({ path: file });
+  console.log(`status=${await status()}  shot: ${file}`);
+}
+
+try {
+  switch (act) {
+    case 'shot': break;
+    case 'click': await (await pick(args[0])).click(); await sleep(700); break;
+    case 'clicktext': {
+      const h = await page.evaluateHandle((t, css, useLast) => {
+        const els = [...document.querySelectorAll(css || '*')].filter((el) => el.offsetParent !== null && [...el.childNodes].some((c) => c.nodeType === 3 && c.textContent.trim() === t) || (el.offsetParent !== null && (el.innerText || '').trim() === t && !el.children.length));
+        return useLast ? els[els.length - 1] : els[0];
+      }, args[0], args[1] || '', last);
+      const el = h.asElement(); if (!el) throw new Error('no visible element reads ' + JSON.stringify(args[0]));
+      await el.click(); await sleep(700); break;
+    }
+    case 'type': await (await pick(args[0])).click(); await page.keyboard.type(args.slice(1).join(' ')); await sleep(300); break;
+    // (lines of one message apart with Shift+Enter: typed as they are, each newline sent the message - live 2517 made
+    // three messages of one test case)
+    case 'send': {
+      // AN ERROR BOX IS DISMISSED FIRST, as a developer would: typed into, its Enter closed it and the message was
+      // lost (round 2 of the comparison, 2026-10-28, twice - the ticket rule never reached the agent)
+      const errorBox = await page.evaluate(() => { const bg = document.getElementById('modalBg'); if (!bg || !bg.classList.contains('show')) return null; const t = (document.getElementById('modalTitle') || {}).textContent || ''; try { closeModal(); } catch { bg.classList.remove('show'); } return t; });
+      if (errorBox != null) { console.log(`dismissed an open box first: ${errorBox}`); await sleep(400); }
+      await page.click('#input');
+      const lines = args.join(' ').split('\n');
+      for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
+        if (i) { await page.keyboard.down('Shift'); await page.keyboard.press('Enter'); await page.keyboard.up('Shift'); }
+        await page.keyboard.type(lines[i]);
+      }
+      await page.keyboard.press('Enter'); await sleep(1500); break;
+    }
+    // pastefile <file>: the file's text put in the input at once, as a paste is (a 14 KB message typed a key at a time
+    // took minutes); send it with "key Enter"
+    case 'pastefile': {
+      const text = (await import('node:fs')).readFileSync(args[0], 'utf8');
+      await page.click('#input');
+      const cdp = await page.createCDPSession();
+      await cdp.send('Input.insertText', { text });
+      await sleep(500); break;
+    }
+    case 'key': await page.keyboard.press(args[0]); await sleep(500); break;
+    case 'scroll': await page.evaluate((css, y) => { const e = [...document.querySelectorAll(css)].find((x) => x.offsetParent !== null && x.scrollHeight > x.clientHeight) || document.querySelector(css); e.scrollTop = y === 'bottom' ? e.scrollHeight : Number(y); }, args[0], args[1]); await sleep(300); break;
+    case 'zoom': {   // zoom <x> <y> <w> <h>: a clip of the screen, drawn twice as large
+      const n = readdirSync(SHOTS).filter((f) => /^step-\d+/.test(f)).length + 1;
+      const file = path.join(SHOTS, `step-${String(n).padStart(3, '0')}-zoom.png`);
+      const cdp = await page.createCDPSession();
+      const [x, y, w, h] = args.map(Number);
+      const { data } = await cdp.send('Page.captureScreenshot', { format: 'png', clip: { x, y, width: w, height: h, scale: 2 } });
+      (await import('node:fs')).writeFileSync(file, Buffer.from(data, 'base64'));
+      console.log('zoom: ' + file); browser.disconnect(); process.exit(0);
+    }
+    case 'select': await page.select(args[0], args[1]); await sleep(900); break;
+    case 'viewport': await page.setViewport({ width: Number(args[0]), height: Number(args[1] || 900) }); await sleep(500); break;
+    // the WINDOW itself, as a developer resizes it (viewport alone left innerWidth/innerHeight as they were in the
+    // headless-but-windowed Chrome the driver runs - 2026-10-29, "when the window isn't maximized")
+    case 'window': {
+      const cdp = await page.target().createCDPSession();
+      const { windowId } = await cdp.send('Browser.getWindowForTarget');
+      await cdp.send('Browser.setWindowBounds', { windowId, bounds: { windowState: 'normal' } });
+      await cdp.send('Browser.setWindowBounds', { windowId, bounds: { width: Number(args[0]), height: Number(args[1] || 800) } });
+      await page.setViewport(null).catch(() => {});
+      await cdp.send('Emulation.clearDeviceMetricsOverride').catch(() => {});   // the driver's fixed viewport, gone: the window decides
+      await sleep(700);
+      console.log('window ' + JSON.stringify(await page.evaluate(() => ({ inner: [innerWidth, innerHeight], dpr: devicePixelRatio }))));
+      break;
+    }
+    case 'move': await page.mouse.move(Number(args[0]), Number(args[1])); await sleep(700); break;
+    case 'clickxy': await page.mouse.click(Number(args[0]), Number(args[1])); await sleep(700); break;
+    case 'wheel': await page.mouse.move(Number(args[1] || 700), Number(args[2] || 450)); await page.mouse.wheel({ deltaY: Number(args[0] || 400) }); await sleep(400); break;
+    case 'hover': await (await pick(args[0])).hover(); await sleep(500); break;
+    case 'wait': {
+      const max = Number(args[0] || 600) * 1000, t0 = Date.now();
+      await sleep(1500);
+      let idleFor = 0;
+      while (Date.now() - t0 < max) { await sleep(1000); idleFor = (await status()) === 'running' ? 0 : idleFor + 1; if (idleFor >= 3) break; }
+      console.log(`waited ${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)} s`);
+      break;
+    }
+    case 'read': {
+      const t = await page.evaluate((css, n) => [...document.querySelectorAll(css || '#log > *')].filter((el) => el.offsetParent !== null).slice(-n).map((el) => (el.innerText || '').replace(/\n{2,}/g, '\n').trim()), args[0] || '', Number(args[1] || 12));
+      console.log(t.join('\n-----\n')); browser.disconnect(); process.exit(0);
+    }
+    case 'errs': console.log((await page.evaluate(() => window.__errs || [])).join('\n') || '(none)'); browser.disconnect(); process.exit(0);
+    case 'eval': console.log(JSON.stringify(await page.evaluate(args.join(' ')), null, 1)); browser.disconnect(); process.exit(0);
+    default: console.error('unknown action ' + act); browser.disconnect(); process.exit(2);
+  }
+  await shot(act + (args[0] ? '-' + args[0] : ''));
+} catch (e) { console.error('step failed: ' + (e?.message || e)); await shot('failed').catch(() => {}); }
+browser.disconnect();

+/**
+ * VOLUME + EDGE CASES: the test-data outline (build 2195+) with a big store, through the real UI and the real plugin.
+ *   [N] normal: 30 families (4 people x 8 fields each, ~1200 lines) - drawn, edited deep down, a family added at the
+ *       end, fast typing saved as its last state, few save requests
+ *   [E] awkward values: unicode, quotes/backslashes/markup (saved literally, drawn as text), a bad family name, a
+ *       duplicate family, a 10 KB value, removing a family
+ *   [B] big: 140 families (~5500 lines) - drawn, and whether an edit is saved

+ * Run: node test/_vol-outline.mjs
+ */

+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const until = async (fn, ms = 8000) => { const t = Date.now() + ms; let v; while (Date.now() < t) { v = await fn(); if (v) return v; await sleep(120); } return v; };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => p && existsSync(p));

+    const tk = Date.now();
+    await focusBox(sp, 'value', 'end');
+    await page.keyboard.type('-z', { delay: 0 });
+    const keyMs = Date.now() - tk;
+    const t1 = Date.now(), toasts0 = toasts.length;
+    await until(() => page.evaluate((P) => { const o = document.querySelector(P + ' .pp-outline'); return o && !o.dataset.dirty; }, P), 20000);
+    await sleep(500);
+    f = await readFam('fam130');
+    const stored = f && f.members.sponsor.phone === 'phone-130-0-z';
+    note(`edit at 140 families: typing 2 keys ${keyMs} ms; ${stored ? 'stored' : 'NOT stored'} after ${Date.now() - t1} ms; largest save request ${Math.round(saveBytes / 1024)} KB; toasts: ${JSON.stringify(toasts.slice(toasts0))}`);
+    ok(stored, `B2: an edit in a ${bigN}-line outline is stored (was refused over 5000 lines) (${f && f.members.sponsor.phone}${toasts.length > toasts0 ? '; said: ' + toasts.slice(toasts0).join(' | ') : ''})`);
+
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${errs.slice(0, 3).join(' | ')})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);

+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}

+import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
+import path from 'node:path';
+const P = await import('../examples/plugins/piiscan__1.mjs');
+const root = process.argv[2], want = process.argv[3], limit = Number(process.argv[4] || 12);
+const files = [];
+(function walk(d, dep = 0) { if (dep > 9) return;
+  let e; try { e = readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true }); } catch { return; }
+  for (const x of e) { if (/^(node_modules|\.git|dist|build|target|out|vendor)$/.test(x.name)) continue;
+    const p = path.join(d, x.name);
+    if (x.isDirectory()) walk(p, dep + 1); else { try { if (statSync(p).size < 3000000) files.push(p); } catch {} } } })(root);
+let n = 0;
+const byKey = {};
+for (const f of files) {
+  const cls = P.classifyFile(f); if (cls.cls === 'skip') continue;
+  let t; try { t = readFileSync(f, 'utf8'); } catch { continue; }
+  if (t.indexOf('\0') >= 0) continue;
+  for (const m of P.findMatches(t, { cls: cls.cls, dictionary: null, allow: () => false })) {
+    if (want && m.type !== want) continue;
+    byKey[m.why] = (byKey[m.why] || 0) + 1;
+    if (n < limit) { n++;

+      console.log(m.type.padEnd(5), m.why.padEnd(24), JSON.stringify(t.slice(m.start, m.end).slice(0, 44)));
+      console.log('      ctx: ' + line.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 190));
+    }
+  }
+}

=== add test/e2e-puppeteer.mjs
new 56e5865d1e131948e340ba55
+/**
+ * End-to-end test: runs the real server (with a scripted mock adapter) and
+ * drives the UI in Chrome via puppeteer-core. Verifies:
+ *
+ *   - chat input + send streams assistant text
+ *   - tool call row appears for <write>
+ *   - diff block renders with green + lines
+ *   - second turn calls <next-task> and the tool result row reflects it
+ *   - third turn calls <list-tasks> and the result shows both tasks
+ *
+ * Run: node test/e2e-puppeteer.mjs
+ *
+ * Saves screenshots to test/e2e-screenshots/ for visual verification.
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const SCREENSHOTS = path.join(__dirname, 'e2e-screenshots');
+const PORT = 28888;
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) {
+  if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ✓', label); }
+  else { fail++; console.log('  ✗', label); }
+}
+
+function findChrome() {
+  const candidates = process.platform === 'win32' ? [
+    (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+  ] : process.platform === 'darwin' ? [
+    '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome',
+  ] : [
+    '/usr/bin/google-chrome',
+    '/usr/bin/chromium-browser',
+  ];
+  return candidates.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+}
+
+// Scripted adapter: each call returns the next response from `script`.
+function scriptedAdapter(script) {
+  let i = 0;
+  return {
+    async complete() {
+      if (i >= script.length) throw new Error('mock script exhausted at call ' + i);
+      const text = script[i++];
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: text } }] };
+    },
+    // Streaming impl: chunk the response in 40-char pieces so the UI sees real streaming.
+    async *completeStream() {
+      if (i >= script.length) throw new Error('mock script exhausted at call ' + i);
+      const text = script[i++];
+      const chunkSize = 40;
+      for (let off = 0; off < text.length; off += chunkSize) {
+        const piece = text.slice(off, off + chunkSize);
+        yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: piece } }] };
+        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 12));
+      }
+    },
+    async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }; },
+  };
+}
+
+const SCRIPT = [
+  // Turn 1, LLM call 1: agent decides to write a file
+  `I'll create a hello file for you.
+
+<remote-workspace>
+<write path="hello.txt">
+hello from the agent
+this is line two
+</write>
+</remote-workspace>`,
+  // Turn 1, LLM call 2: tool result processed, finish
+  `Done — wrote hello.txt with two lines.`,
+  // Turn 2, LLM call 1: close current task, open new
+  `Closing the current task to start a fresh one.
+
+<remote-workspace>
+<next-task user-request="I want a tagline added to the project" summary="created hello.txt with two lines of greeting"/>
+</remote-workspace>`,
+  // Turn 2, LLM call 2: finish
+  `New task is open — say "tagline" and I'll write one.`,
+  // Turn 3, LLM call 1: list tasks
+  `Here are the tasks so far.
+
+<remote-workspace>
+<list-tasks/>
+</remote-workspace>`,
+  // Turn 3, LLM call 2: explain
+  `As you can see above, we have a root session task and the new tagline task.`,
+];
+
+async function main() {
+  const chrome = findChrome();
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found — install Chrome or set CHROME_PATH'); process.exit(2); }
+  console.log('Chrome at:', chrome);
+
+  await mkdir(SCREENSHOTS, { recursive: true });
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'code-boss-e2e-'));
+  // Seed a simple project so the agent has something to "look at"
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'README.md'), '# demo project\n', 'utf8');
+  console.log('temp project:', projectDir);
+
+  console.log('starting server on port', PORT);
+  const html = await (await import('node:fs/promises')).readFile(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const htmlWithConfig = html.replace(
+    '</head>',
+    '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'mock-model', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>'
+  );
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: htmlWithConfig,
+    baseURL: '(mock)',
+    apiKey: 'mock', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'mock-model',
+    port: PORT,
+    adapter: scriptedAdapter(SCRIPT),
+    modelsList: [{ id: 'mock-model' }],
+    initialProject: projectDir,
+  });
+
+  console.log('launching Chrome via puppeteer-core...');
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({
+    executablePath: chrome,
+    headless: 'new',
+    args: ['--no-sandbox', '--disable-dev-shm-usage'],
+  });
+
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1280, height: 900 });
+
+    console.log('\n[1] page loads and shows welcome');
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('.welcome', { timeout: 5000 });
+    const welcomeVisible = await page.$('.welcome');
+    ok(welcomeVisible != null, 'welcome banner present');
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(SCREENSHOTS, '1-loaded.png') });
+
+    console.log('\n[2] send first message → write_file → diff block renders');
+    await page.waitForSelector('#input');
+    await page.click('#input');
+    await page.type('#input', 'create a hello file');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+
+    // Wait for the diff block to appear (most distinctive end-state)
+    await page.waitForSelector('.diff-block', { timeout: 15000 });
+    ok(true, 'diff block rendered');
+    const addLines = await page.$$eval('.diff-block .dl.add', (els) => els.map((e) => e.textContent));
+    ok(addLines.some((l) => l.includes('hello from the agent')), `diff has +hello (got ${addLines.length} +lines)`);
+    const toolRows = await page.$$('.row.tool');
+    ok(toolRows.length >= 1, `at least one tool row (got ${toolRows.length})`);
+
+    // Wait for the assistant's final message ("Done — wrote hello.txt")
+    await page.waitForFunction(
+      () => Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.row.assistant .body')).some((el) => /wrote hello\.txt/i.test(el.textContent)),
+      { timeout: 10000 }
+    );
+    ok(true, 'final assistant text appeared');
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(SCREENSHOTS, '2-diff-rendered.png') });
+
+    console.log('\n[3] second message → next-task closes task → task wrap appears');
+    await page.click('#input');
+    await page.type('#input', 'now switch to a tagline task');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+
+    // Phase 5: the close should produce a .task-wrap whose header carries the user-request.
+    await page.waitForFunction(
+      () => Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.task-wrap .task-head')).some((el) => /tagline/i.test(el.textContent)),
+      { timeout: 30000 }
+    );
+    ok(true, 'task wrap with tagline user-request rendered');
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(SCREENSHOTS, '3-next-task.png') });
+
+    console.log('\n[4] third message → list-tasks shows both tasks');
+    await page.click('#input');
+    await page.type('#input', 'show me the tasks');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+
+    await page.waitForFunction(
+      () => {
+        const rows = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.row-result'));
+        const last = rows[rows.length - 1];
+        return last && /tasks/i.test(last.textContent);
+      },
+      { timeout: 15000 }
+    );
+    // Open the last result's expand to inspect
+    const expanders = await page.$$('.row-result .expand');
+    if (expanders.length) {
+      await expanders[expanders.length - 1].click();
+    }
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300));
+    const bodyText = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector('#log').textContent);
+    ok(/T-1/.test(bodyText), 'T-1 mentioned somewhere in log');
+    ok(/T-2/.test(bodyText), 'T-2 mentioned somewhere in log');
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(SCREENSHOTS, '4-list-tasks.png'), fullPage: true });
+
+    console.log('\n[5] verify tasks on disk');
+    const { readdir, readFile } = await import('node:fs/promises');
+    const tasksDir = path.join(projectDir, '.code_boss', 'tasks');
+    const files = await readdir(tasksDir);
+    ok(files.includes('state.json'), 'state.json exists');
+    const tFiles = files.filter((f) => /^T-\d+\.json$/.test(f));
+    ok(tFiles.length >= 2, `at least 2 task files on disk (got ${tFiles.length}: ${tFiles.join(', ')})`);
+
+    // Verify the hello.txt was actually written by the write tool
+    const helloPath = path.join(projectDir, 'hello.txt');
+    const helloContent = await readFile(helloPath, 'utf8');
+    ok(/hello from the agent/.test(helloContent), 'hello.txt written to disk with expected content');
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close();
+    server.close();
+    // Keep the project dir if there were failures, otherwise clean up
+    if (fail === 0) {
+      await rm(projectDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
+    } else {
+      console.log('preserving project dir for inspection:', projectDir);
+    }
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\nScreenshots saved to ${SCREENSHOTS}`);
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/live-adhoc-mask.mjs
new 09d128d157bcd6a5282cf7aa
+/**
+ * LIVE (real model via the proxy): a SQL-style plugin masks its rows AD HOC through host.use('pii-testdata').maskFields
+ * — no family, no panel entry — and the REAL agent works with @@@(<field>#<id>) tokens it has never seen before:
+ * reads the result, hands a token to <write> (the file holds the real value, no request ever carried it), reads an
+ * app log that prints the same value and recognises the SAME token, and answers a follow-up with the SSN token.
+ * `live-` prefix → run-all skips it; skips cleanly if the proxy or Chrome is unavailable. Run: node test/live-adhoc-mask.mjs
+ */
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile, readdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+import { PROXY_BASE, PROXY_HEADERS, PROXY_API_KEY, describeCreds } from './_proxy-creds.mjs';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-ladhoc-home-'));   // fresh install → bundled plugins auto-install
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { userPluginsDir } = await import('../src/plugins/paths.mjs');
+const { cleanupDir } = await import('./_helpers.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PROXY = PROXY_BASE;
+const MODEL = LIVE_MODEL;
+const PORT = 17946;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const note = (l) => console.log('  ·', l);
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const ROWS = [
+  { id: 1042, last_name: 'Okonkwo-Zed', first_name: 'Gwendolyn', ssn: '482-11-9037', status: 'active' },
+  { id: 1043, last_name: 'Vantongeren', first_name: 'Harold', ssn: '333-22-1111', status: 'active' },
+];
+const RAW = ['Okonkwo-Zed', 'Gwendolyn', '482-11-9037', 'Vantongeren', 'Harold', '333-22-1111'];
+
+const PLUGIN = `export default {
+  description: 'SQL: run a query against the enrollment database; sensitive columns come back as plugin-issued tokens. app_log: read the application log.',
+  dependencies: { 'pii-testdata': true },
+  tools: [
+    { verb: 'sql_query', name: 'sql_query',
+      schema: { description: 'Run a SQL query. Rows come back as JSON; the sensitive columns are plugin-issued TOKENS (#@<column>#<id>) which you can pass to any tool as if they were the values.', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: { sql: { type: 'string', description: 'the query' } }, required: ['sql'] } },
+      impl: async (args, ctx) => {
+        const rows = ${JSON.stringify(ROWS)};
+        const masked = await ctx.host.use('pii-testdata').maskFields({ projectDir: ctx.projectDir, data: rows, fields: ['last_name', 'first_name', 'ssn'] });
+        return { content: 'query: ' + args.sql + '\\n' + JSON.stringify(masked, null, 1) };
+      } },
+    { verb: 'app_log', name: 'app_log',
+      schema: { description: 'Read the last lines of the application log.', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: {} } },
+      impl: async () => ({ content: '2026-09-16 10:41:02 INFO  login ok user=Okonkwo-Zed\\n2026-09-16 10:41:07 WARN  ssn lookup 482-11-9037 took 1200ms' }) },
+  ],
+};
+`;
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  console.log(`[live] ${PROXY} · model=${MODEL} · ${describeCreds()}`);
+  try { const r = await fetch(PROXY + '/v1/models', { headers: { ...PROXY_HEADERS, 'x-api-key': PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', 'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01' } }); if (!r.ok && r.status !== 404) throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status); }
+  catch (e) { console.log(`SKIP — proxy not reachable at ${PROXY} (${e.message})`); process.exit(0); }
+  const pdir = userPluginsDir();
+  await mkdir(pdir, { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(pdir, 'sqladhoc__1.mjs'), PLUGIN, 'utf8');
+  const project = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-ladhoc-proj-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'README.md'), '# enrollment tools\n');
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const requests = [];
+  const inner = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: () => (PROXY_API_KEY || 'x'), extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS });
+  const adapter = { ...inner, completeStream: (req) => { requests.push(req); return inner.completeStream(req); }, complete: (req) => { requests.push(req); return inner.complete(req); } };
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' }, defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: project, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic' });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  const waitIdle = (timeout = 240000) => page.waitForFunction(() => { const s = (window.__sessionRef || {}).status; return s === 'idle' || s === 'done' || s == null; }, { timeout }).catch(() => {});
+  const send = async (text) => { await waitIdle(); await page.evaluate((t) => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = t; i.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); i.focus(); }, text); await page.keyboard.press('Enter'); await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1500)); };
+  const toolEvents = () => page.evaluate(() => (session.events || []).filter((e) => /^tool-/.test(e.type)).map((e) => ({ t: e.type, v: e.name || e.verb, s: e.status, c: String(e.content || '').slice(0, 2000), a: e.args ? JSON.stringify(e.args).slice(0, 1500) : '' })));
+  // THE TRANSCRIPT, not the screen: the chat reveals masked tokens to the developer (revealMasked is on by default),
+  // so the bubble's text shows VALUES the model never saw. What the model said is the assistant-text events.
+  const lastReply = () => page.evaluate(() => { const ev = session.events || []; let i = ev.length - 1; while (i >= 0 && ev[i].type !== 'user-message') i--; return ev.slice(i + 1).filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').map((e) => String(e.content || '')).join('\n').trim(); });
+  const reqText = () => requests.map((r) => JSON.stringify(r)).join('\n');
+  const TOK = /#@(last_name|first_name|ssn)(?:~[^#\s]+)?#[0-9a-z]{7}/g;   // with the value's format (2324)
+  try {
+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => note('pageerror: ' + e.message));
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 850 });
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+
+    console.log('\n[1] the agent runs the query, reads plugin-issued tokens, and hands one to <write>');
+    await send('Use the sql_query tool to run: SELECT * FROM enrollees. Then create the file out.txt containing ONLY the last_name of the row with id 1042 — use the token the tool gave you exactly as it is; the system substitutes the real value when the file is written. Finally tell me which token you wrote.');
+    await waitIdle(300000);
+    const evs = await toolEvents();
+    note('tools: ' + evs.filter((e) => e.t === 'tool-call').map((e) => e.v).join(' → '));
+    const viaTag = evs.some((e) => e.t === 'tool-call' && e.v === 'sql_query');
+    const jsCalls = evs.filter((e) => e.t === 'tool-call' && e.v === 'js_eval' && /sql_query\s*\(/.test(e.a));
+    ok(viaTag || jsCalls.length > 0, `the agent called sql_query (${viaTag ? 'by tag' : jsCalls.length ? 'from <js>' : 'not at all'})`);
+    // the query's own result (the plugin's "query: …" text), whether the tool was called by tag or from a snippet - not a
+    // later snippet's result that merely quotes a token (the write's diff does)
+    const qres = evs.filter((e) => e.t === 'tool-result' && (e.v === 'sql_query' || (e.v === 'js_eval' && /query: /.test(e.c)))).pop();
+    const toks = [...new Set(((qres && qres.c) || '').match(TOK) || [])];
+    ok(!!qres && toks.length >= 4 && !RAW.some((v) => qres.c.includes(v)), `the query result reached the transcript as #@<field>#<id> tokens only (${toks.length} distinct; ${(qres && qres.c || '').replace(/\n/g, ' ').slice(0, 90)})`);
+    // every plugin-issued token came through whole (the four distinct ones above) and nothing re-wrapped one; the shape
+    // filter may still mask an unknown word nearby (the mock's table name "enrollees" is not in its lexicon)
+    ok(!!qres && !/\[PII:|#[Xx0]+#@|#@[a-z_]+#[0-9a-z]{7}[0-9a-z]/.test(qres.c), 'the shape filter left the plugin-issued tokens alone (no token of a token)');
+    const wcall = evs.filter((e) => e.t === 'tool-call' && (/write/.test(e.v) || (e.v === 'js_eval' && /\bwrite\s*\(/.test(e.a)))).pop();
+    const nameTok = toks.find((t) => /^#@last_name[~#]/.test(t));
+    // the token is in the call literally, or the snippet pipes it from the query result to the write - the file check
+    // below proves the value landed either way
+    ok(!!wcall && !!nameTok && (wcall.a.includes(nameTok) || /sql_query\s*\(/.test(wcall.a)), `the write call carries the last_name TOKEN, or pipes it from the query (${(wcall && wcall.a || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 100)})`);
+    const fileText = await readFile(path.join(project, 'out.txt'), 'utf8').catch(() => null);
+    ok(fileText != null && fileText.trim() === 'Okonkwo-Zed', `out.txt holds the REAL value via H2 (${JSON.stringify(fileText)})`);
+    const leaked = RAW.filter((v) => reqText().includes(v));
+    ok(leaked.length === 0, `no request to the model ever carried a row value (${requests.length} requests; leaked: ${leaked.join(',') || 'none'})`);
+    const reply = await lastReply();
+    ok(!!nameTok && reply.includes(nameTok), `the agent names the token it used (${reply.slice(0, 120)})`);
+    ok(!RAW.some((v) => reply.includes(v)), 'and never a value in its reply');
+    // ad hoc tokens live in the store outside (host.tokens, 2255): nothing of them lands in the project
+    const files = await readdir(path.join(project, 'testdata')).catch(() => []);
+    ok(!existsSync(path.join(project, 'testdata')) || !files.some((f) => /\.json$/i.test(f)), `nothing was written into the project for the ad hoc values (${files.join(', ') || 'no testdata folder'})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[2] the app log prints the same value → the agent sees the SAME token and says so');
+    await send('Use the app_log tool to read the application log. Which user logged in? Answer with the token from the log, and say whether it is the same token you wrote to out.txt.');
+    await waitIdle(240000);
+    const evs2 = await toolEvents();
+    const lres = evs2.filter((e) => e.t === 'tool-result' && (e.v === 'app_log' || (e.v === 'js_eval' && /login ok/.test(e.c)))).pop();
+    ok(!!lres && !!nameTok && lres.c.includes(nameTok) && !RAW.some((v) => lres.c.includes(v)), `the log result carries the same last_name token and no value (${(lres && lres.c || '').replace(/\n/g, ' | ').slice(0, 120)})`);
+    const reply2 = await lastReply();
+    ok(!!nameTok && reply2.includes(nameTok) && /same/i.test(reply2), `the agent recognises the same token (${reply2.slice(0, 140)})`);
+    ok(!RAW.some((v) => reply2.includes(v)), 'still no value in its reply');
+
+    console.log('\n[3] a follow-up in tokens: the SSN of the other row');
+    await send('What is the ssn of the row with id 1043? Answer with its token only.');
+    await waitIdle(180000);
+    const reply3 = await lastReply();
+    const ssnToks = toks.filter((t) => /^#@ssn[~#]/.test(t));
+    ok(ssnToks.some((t) => reply3.includes(t)) && !RAW.some((v) => reply3.includes(v)), `the agent answers with an ssn token (${reply3.slice(0, 100)})`);
+    ok(RAW.filter((v) => reqText().includes(v)).length === 0, 'still no value in any request');
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(ROOT, 'test', 'live-adhoc-mask.png') }).catch(() => {});
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(project); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();
+

+/**
+ * LIVE adapter smoke test against the local Anthropic proxy (real Claude).
+ * Not part of run-all (no `test-` prefix) — needs the proxy at 127.0.0.1:8080.
+ * Run: node test/live-anthropic-adapter.mjs
+ */
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) { if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ok', label); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', label); } }
+
+async function main() {
+  const a = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:8080/v1', debug: false });
+
+  // reachability
+  try { await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:8080/health'); }
+  catch { console.log('  SKIP — proxy not reachable at 127.0.0.1:8080'); process.exit(0); }
+
+  console.log('[1] listModels');
+  const models = await a.listModels();
+  const ids = (models.data || []).map((m) => m.id);
+  ok(ids.includes('claude-sonnet-4-6'), `models listed (${ids.join(', ')})`);
+
+  console.log('[2] complete — text round-trips, OpenAI shape out');
+  const r = await a.complete({ model: 'claude-sonnet-4-6', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'Reply with exactly one word: PONG' }] });
+  const text = r?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content ?? '';
+  ok(/PONG/i.test(text), `got a completion (${JSON.stringify(text).slice(0, 60)})`);
+  ok(r?.choices?.[0]?.finish_reason === 'stop', `finish_reason mapped (${r?.choices?.[0]?.finish_reason})`);
+  ok(r?.usage && r.usage.total_tokens > 0, `usage mapped (in=${r?.usage?.prompt_tokens} out=${r?.usage?.completion_tokens})`);
+
+  console.log('[3] system role is hoisted to top-level system');
+  const r2 = await a.complete({ model: 'claude-sonnet-4-6', messages: [
+    { role: 'system', content: 'You always answer in ALL CAPS.' },
+    { role: 'user', content: 'say hello in one word' },
+  ] });
+  const t2 = r2?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content ?? '';
+  ok(/[A-Z]/.test(t2) && t2 === t2.toUpperCase(), `system honored — all caps (${JSON.stringify(t2).slice(0, 40)})`);
+
+  console.log('[4] completeStream — chunks accumulate to a full answer');
+  let streamed = '', chunks = 0;
+  for await (const c of a.completeStream({ model: 'claude-sonnet-4-6', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'Count: one two three. Reply with just those three words.' }] })) {
+    const d = c?.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content;
+    if (d) { streamed += d; chunks++; }
+  }
+  ok(streamed.length > 0, `stream produced text (${chunks} chunk(s): ${JSON.stringify(streamed).slice(0, 60)})`);
+  ok(/one/i.test(streamed) && /three/i.test(streamed), 'streamed answer is coherent');
+
+  console.log('[5] multi-turn alternation (assistant turn echoed back)');
+  const r3 = await a.complete({ model: 'claude-sonnet-4-6', messages: [
+    { role: 'user', content: 'Remember the number 42.' },
+    { role: 'assistant', content: 'Got it, 42.' },
+    { role: 'user', content: 'What number did I ask you to remember? Reply with just the number.' },
+  ] });
+  ok(/42/.test(r3?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content ?? ''), `multi-turn context preserved (${JSON.stringify(r3?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content).slice(0, 40)})`);
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.message || e); process.exit(2); });
