=== add test/live-bughunt.mjs
new 7a4b1c097472d10e3d110756
+/**
+ * LIVE BUG HUNT — the real model, the real UI, a developer who answers back.
+ *
+ * Not an assertion suite. It drives code_boss the way a developer actually uses it, against a live model, and
+ * RECORDS what happens: every chat row, every anomaly, timings, and screenshots at each step. Nothing here
+ * "passes"; the output is a report to read.
+ *
+ * It responds like a developer (the standing rule): when the agent asks a question it answers, when the agent
+ * wants approval it approves. A hunt that leaves the agent hanging only ever finds the hang.
+ *
+ * The tasks are deliberately the shapes that have produced real bugs: writing XML with tags that collide with
+ * ours, a long-running foreground process, a question mid-task, and an interruption.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/live-bughunt.mjs          (writes test/live-bughunt-out/report.md + screenshots)
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+// ITS OWN HOME. This hunt changes settings (it sets the compaction limit to 12,000 tokens to force a fold). Without a
+// home of its own it wrote that into the developer's REAL ~/.code_boss/prefs.json, and every session after it - theirs
+// included - compacted on every message (found 2026-10-09: three later live suites ran against that limit).
+if (!process.env.CB_HOME) process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-bughunt-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import { runGit, commitAll } from './_git.mjs';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+import { PROXY_API_KEY, PROXY_BASE, PROXY_HEADERS } from './_proxy-creds.mjs';
+import { streamDiagSnapshot } from '../src/agent/turn-stream.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const OUT = path.join(__dirname, 'live-bughunt-out');
+const PORT = Number(process.env.CB_HUNT_PORT || 28994);
+const PROXY = PROXY_BASE || process.env.CB_PROXY || 'https://home-proxy.philhignight.com';
+const MODEL = process.env.CB_MODEL || LIVE_MODEL;
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const SEP = String.fromCharCode(92);
+const chrome = [
+  'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '').split(SEP).join('/') + '/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  'C:/Program Files (x86)/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+
+const findings = [];
+const note = (severity, title, detail) => { findings.push({ severity, title, detail }); console.log(`  [${severity}] ${title}`); };
+const log = (s) => console.log(s);
+
+/** Everything currently rendered in the chat, in order, as {kind, text}. */
+async function readChat(page) {
+  return page.evaluate(() => [...document.querySelectorAll('#log > *')].map((el) => {
+    const cls = el.className || '';
+    const kind = /bubble user/.test(cls) ? 'developer'
+      : /row assistant|bubble assistant/.test(cls) ? 'agent'
+      : /row-result/.test(cls) ? 'result'
+      : /row tool/.test(cls) ? 'tool'
+      : /status/.test(cls) ? 'status' : 'other';
+    return { kind, cls, text: (el.innerText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 600) };
+  }));
+}
+
+let _seen = 0;
+/** Only the rows added since the last step — a cumulative dump buries the step you are reading about. */
+async function newRows(page) {
+  const all = await readChat(page);
+  const fresh = all.slice(_seen);
+  _seen = all.length;
+  return fresh;
+}
+
+// A step that takes minutes when its neighbours take seconds is the phenomenon reported from production (a 140s
+// call on a short prompt). The in-memory stream diagnostics say WHICH it is — a stall then a re-delivery, a
+// silence with no content deltas (the model reasoning), or simply a lot of tokens — and this harness runs the
+// server in-process, so it can just read them. Without this the evidence dies with the run.
+const slowSteps = [];
+const SLOW_MS = Number(process.env.CB_HUNT_SLOW_MS || 40000);
+function captureSlow(label, ms) {
+  if (ms < SLOW_MS) return;
+  let recs = [];
+  try { recs = streamDiagSnapshot(); } catch {}
+  const worst = recs.slice().sort((a, b) => b.ms - a.ms)[0];
+  slowSteps.push({ label, ms, recs: recs.slice(-6) });
+  // A step is many calls. Attributing its seconds to ONE long call versus several ordinary ones is the whole
+  // question — 200s in a single stream is the phenomenon; 200s across eight turns is just work.
+  const longest = recs.slice().sort((a, b) => b.ms - a.ms).slice(0, 3).map((r) => Math.round(r.ms / 1000) + 's');
+  log(`    step ${Math.round(ms / 1000)}s across ${recs.length} recorded stream(s); longest: ${longest.join(', ') || 'n/a'}`);
+  const gaps = worst ? worst.gaps.map((g) => `${(g.gapMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s@${g.atChars}->${g.after}${g.overlapPct ? '/' + g.overlapPct + '%' : ''}`).join(' ') : '';
+  log(`    SLOW (${Math.round(ms / 1000)}s) — slowest stream: ${worst ? Math.round(worst.ms / 1000) + 's, ' + worst.chunks + ' chunks, ' + worst.chars + ' chars' : 'n/a'}${gaps ? ', gaps: ' + gaps : ', no gaps over the note threshold'}`);
+  // A stream that generated far more than the chat shows means the turn was spent writing text we discard —
+  // measured live at 54,000 characters generated for ~120 surfaced, over 184 seconds.
+  if (worst && worst.chars > 20000) {
+    note('HIGH', 'a slow stream generated a huge amount of text', `${worst.chars} chars in ${Math.round(worst.ms / 1000)}s with ${worst.gaps.length} notable gap(s) — check the server log for "dropped … chars written after the last tool block"`);
+  }
+  if (worst && worst.doubled) note('HIGH', 'a slow stream delivered the answer TWICE', JSON.stringify(worst));
+  else if (worst && worst.gaps.some((g) => g.after === 'exact' || g.after === 'prefix')) {
+    note('HIGH', 'a slow stream re-delivered its own text after a gap', JSON.stringify(worst.gaps));
+  }
+}
+
+async function shot(page, name) {
+  try { await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(OUT, name + '.png'), fullPage: false }); } catch {}
+}
+
+/** Wait until the run settles, answering the agent if it asks something. Returns how it ended. */
+async function settle(page, { timeoutMs = 300000, label = '' } = {}) {
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  let lastLen = -1, lastChange = Date.now();
+  for (;;) {
+    const st = await page.evaluate(() => (window.__sessionRef || {}).status || 'unknown');
+    if (['done', 'idle', 'error'].includes(st)) return { end: st, ms: Date.now() - t0 };
+    const rows = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('#log > *').length);
+    if (rows !== lastLen) { lastLen = rows; lastChange = Date.now(); }
+    // A run that renders nothing new for four minutes is stuck, whatever the status says.
+    if (Date.now() - lastChange > 240000) { note('HIGH', 'run went silent', `${label}: no new rows for 4 minutes while status was "${st}"`); return { end: 'stalled', ms: Date.now() - t0 }; }
+    if (Date.now() - t0 > timeoutMs) { note('HIGH', 'run exceeded the budget', `${label}: still "${st}" after ${Math.round(timeoutMs / 1000)}s`); return { end: 'timeout', ms: Date.now() - t0 }; }
+    await sleep(1500);
+  }
+}
+
+/** Approve anything waiting on the developer, the way a developer would. */
+async function approveIfAsked(page) {
+  const clicked = await page.evaluate(() => {
+    const btn = [...document.querySelectorAll('button, .btn')]
+      .find((b) => /^(approve|allow|yes|continue|proceed)\b/i.test((b.textContent || '').trim()) && b.offsetParent !== null);
+    if (btn) { btn.click(); return (btn.textContent || '').trim(); }
+    return null;
+  });
+  if (clicked) log(`    (approved: ${clicked})`);
+  return clicked;
+}
+
+async function send(page, text) {
+  // page.type() is per-character over CDP — fine for a sentence, but a long note (the compaction seed is tens of
+  // thousands of characters) makes it hang for minutes. Set the value directly, fire one input event so the app's
+  // handlers see it, type a trailing space to leave focus/composition sane, then Enter.
+  if (text.length > 400) {
+    await page.evaluate((t) => {
+      const i = document.getElementById('input');
+      i.focus(); i.value = t;
+      i.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
+    }, text);
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await sleep(600);
+    return;
+  }
+  await page.evaluate(() => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = ''; i.focus(); });
+  await page.type('#input', text);
+  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+  await sleep(600);
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP: Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  await rm(OUT, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  await mkdir(OUT, { recursive: true });
+
+  const project = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-hunt-'));
+  await runGit(project, ['init', '-q']);
+  await runGit(project, ['config', 'user.email', 'dev@local']);
+  await runGit(project, ['config', 'user.name', 'Dev']);
+  // A Maven-shaped project, because <phase> in a pom is exactly the shape that broke on the .mil machine.
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'README.md'), '# hunt\nA small Java/Maven project used for live testing.\n', 'utf8');
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'pom.xml'), [
+    '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>',
+    '<project xmlns="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0">',
+    '  <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>',
+    '  <groupId>com.example</groupId>',
+    '  <artifactId>hunt</artifactId>',
+    '  <version>1.0.0</version>',
+    '</project>',
+  ].join('\n'), 'utf8');
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'slow-build.js'), [
+    '// Stands in for a long build: prints progress for ~25s, then succeeds.',
+    'let n = 0;',
+    "const t = setInterval(() => { console.log('compiling module ' + (++n) + '/50'); if (n === 50) { clearInterval(t); console.log('BUILD SUCCESS'); process.exit(0); } }, 500);",
+  ].join('\n'), 'utf8');
+  await commitAll(project, 'seed');
+
+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS || {} });
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: MODEL, baseURL: PROXY }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' },
+    defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: project, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic',
+  });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const transcript = [];
+
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1400, height: 1000, deviceScaleFactor: 1 });
+    const pageErrors = [];
+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => pageErrors.push(String(e.message).slice(0, 200)));
+    await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT + '/');
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 });
+    log(`\nlive bug hunt — model=${MODEL} project=${project}\n`);
+
+    // ── STEP 1: write XML containing <phase> — the shape that corrupted a real pom ────────────────────────
+    log('[1] add a maven plugin execution (the <phase> shape)');
+    await send(page, 'Add a maven-antrun-plugin execution to pom.xml bound to the clean phase, with an echo saying "cleaning". Just edit the file, do not run maven.');
+    let r = await settle(page, { label: 'step 1' });
+    log(`    ended: ${r.end} in ${Math.round(r.ms / 1000)}s`);
+    captureSlow('step 1 (write the pom)', r.ms);
+    await shot(page, '1-phase-write');
+    transcript.push({ step: 'write pom with <phase>', rows: await newRows(page) });
+    {
+      const pom = await readFile(path.join(project, 'pom.xml'), 'utf8').catch(() => '');
+      const hasPair = /<phase>\s*clean\s*<\/phase>/.test(pom);
+      const bare = /^\s*clean\s*$/m.test(pom) && !hasPair;
+      if (bare) note('CRITICAL', 'pom <phase> tags stripped', 'the file contains a bare `clean` with no <phase> wrapper — the production corruption');
+      else if (!hasPair && /antrun/.test(pom)) note('MEDIUM', 'plugin added without a <phase> pair', pom.slice(0, 400));
+      else if (!/antrun/.test(pom)) note('LOW', 'the agent did not edit the pom', 'it may have asked a question instead — see the transcript');
+      else log('    pom keeps its <phase> pair');
+    }
+
+    // ── STEP 2: a question the agent should ASK, and we answer ────────────────────────────────────────────
+    log('[2] an ambiguous request — does it ask, and does answering work?');
+    await send(page, 'Now add a second execution. I have a preference about which phase, so check with me before you write anything.');
+    r = await settle(page, { label: 'step 2' });
+    log(`    ended: ${r.end} in ${Math.round(r.ms / 1000)}s`);
+    await shot(page, '2-question');
+    const afterAsk = await newRows(page);
+    transcript.push({ step: 'ambiguous request', rows: afterAsk });
+    {
+      const last = [...afterAsk].reverse().find((x) => x.kind === 'agent');
+      const asked = last && /\?/.test(last.text);
+      if (!asked) note('MEDIUM', 'did not ask despite being told to', (last && last.text.slice(0, 200)) || '(no agent text)');
+      else log('    it asked; answering as a developer');
+      await send(page, 'Use generate-sources, and echo "generating".');
+      r = await settle(page, { label: 'step 2 answer' });
+      log(`    answer handled: ${r.end} in ${Math.round(r.ms / 1000)}s`);
+      captureSlow('step 2 (answering the question)', r.ms);
+      await shot(page, '2b-answered');
+      transcript.push({ step: 'answered the question', rows: await newRows(page) });
+    }
+
+    // ── STEP 3: a long foreground process + the ⇱ control ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    log('[3] a long build in the foreground, then background it with the tool-row control');
+    await send(page, 'Run `node slow-build.js` in the foreground and tell me the result when it finishes.');
+    // Wait for a running tool row rather than for the run to end.
+    let sawRow = false;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) {
+      await sleep(1000);
+      sawRow = await page.evaluate(() => !!document.querySelector('#log .row.tool.tool-running .tool-bg-btn'));
+      if (sawRow) break;
+      const st = await page.evaluate(() => (window.__sessionRef || {}).status);
+      if (['done', 'idle', 'error'].includes(st)) break;
+    }
+    if (!sawRow) note('MEDIUM', 'no backgroundable tool row appeared', 'the agent may have detached it itself, or never ran it in the foreground');
+    else {
+      await shot(page, '3-running');
+      await page.click('#log .row.tool.tool-running .tool-bg-btn');
+      log('    clicked ⇱');
+      r = await settle(page, { label: 'step 3 after ⇱' });
+      log(`    ended: ${r.end} in ${Math.round(r.ms / 1000)}s`);
+      captureSlow('step 3 (backgrounded build)', r.ms);
+      await shot(page, '3b-backgrounded');
+      const rows = await newRows(page);
+      transcript.push({ step: 'backgrounded a running tool', rows });
+      const said = rows.filter((x) => x.kind === 'agent').slice(-2).map((x) => x.text).join(' ');
+      // The live run said "since the output was empty" and started diagnosing — none of the words the first
+      // version of this check looked for. Match on what it DID say, not on what a failure sounds like.
+      if (/cancel|abort|stopp|kill|fail/i.test(said)) note('MEDIUM', 'agent read the backgrounding as a failure', said.slice(0, 300));
+      else if (/output was empty|no output|empty output|didn'?t (?:print|return|produce)|longer timeout/i.test(said)) {
+        note('HIGH', 'agent misread the backgrounded result as empty output', said.slice(0, 400));
+      }
+      // "I'll run it with a generous timeout so it can finish" — the model's second attempt at re-running it,
+      // which matched none of again/re-run/retry. Match the intent, not one phrasing of it.
+      else if (/(again|re-?run|retry|generous timeout|longer timeout|larger timeout|run it (?:with|using))/i.test(said)) {
+        note('MEDIUM', 'agent looks set to re-run the backgrounded process', said.slice(0, 300));
+      }
+    }
+
+    // ── STEP 4: interrupt mid-run, then continue ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    log('[4] interrupt mid-run, then continue — the repetition shape');
+    await send(page, 'Read every file in the project and give me a detailed summary of each one.');
+    await sleep(12000);
+    await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+    log('    pressed Esc');
+    await sleep(2500);
+    await shot(page, '4-interrupted');
+    await send(page, 'continue');
+    r = await settle(page, { label: 'step 4 continue' });
+    log(`    ended: ${r.end} in ${Math.round(r.ms / 1000)}s`);
+    captureSlow('step 4 (interrupt then continue)', r.ms);
+    await shot(page, '4b-continued');
+    const afterCont = await newRows(page);
+    transcript.push({ step: 'interrupt then continue', rows: afterCont });
+    {
+      // The bug this shape produced in production: the same thing said several times over.
+      // Only within ONE uninterrupted stretch. After the developer says "continue", the agent restarting killed
+      // work and announcing it again is CORRECT — it flagged that as a 100% restatement twice before I read the
+      // flow and saw the developer turn sitting between the two. The production bug was the same content four or
+      // five times inside a SINGLE reply, which this still catches.
+      const sinceLastDeveloper = (() => {
+        const i = afterCont.map((x) => x.kind).lastIndexOf('developer');
+        return afterCont.slice(i + 1);
+      })();
+      const texts = sinceLastDeveloper.filter((x) => x.kind === 'agent').map((x) => x.text).slice(-6);
+      const words = (t) => new Set(t.toLowerCase().split(/[^a-z0-9]+/).filter((w) => w.length > 3));
+      const norm = (t) => t.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]+/g, ' ').replace(/  +/g, ' ').trim();
+      let worst = 0, pair = null;
+      for (let i = 0; i < texts.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < texts.length; j++) {
+        // An EXACT repeat counts however short it is. The first version of this required 8 content words, and
+        // the live run then said "no restatement" about a lead-in the agent had emitted twice VERBATIM
+        // ("Reading all project files in one batch." — four content words). A floor that hides the case you are
+        // hunting is worse than no check.
+        if (norm(texts[i]) && norm(texts[i]) === norm(texts[j])) { worst = 100; pair = [texts[i].slice(0, 200), texts[j].slice(0, 200)]; continue; }
+        const a = words(texts[i]), b = words(texts[j]);
+        if (a.size < 8 || b.size < 8) continue;
+        let hit = 0; for (const w of b) if (a.has(w)) hit++;
+        const ov = Math.round((hit / Math.min(a.size, b.size)) * 100);
+        if (ov > worst) { worst = ov; pair = [texts[i].slice(0, 200), texts[j].slice(0, 200)]; }
+      }
+      if (worst >= 60) note('HIGH', `restated itself after the interrupt (${worst}% overlap)`, pair ? pair.join('\n    ---\n') : '');
+      else log(`    no restatement (worst pair ${worst}%)`);
+      const claimsLost = afterCont.filter((x) => x.kind === 'agent')
+        .some((x) => /(context|history|conversation).{0,40}(lost|reset|wiped|cleared)|cannot see .{0,30}earlier/i.test(x.text));
+      if (claimsLost) note('HIGH', 'claimed its history was lost after the interrupt', 'the transcript is always preserved — this is the confabulation we reworded the note to prevent');
+    }
+
+    // ── STEP 5: a full-file REWRITE — the two-pane diff, and the round-trip through <write> ────────────────
+    log('[5] rewrite a whole file — the two-pane rewrite diff');
+    await send(page, 'Rewrite slow-build.js completely: make it print "step N of 10" ten times, one every 200ms, then print "DONE" and exit 0. Replace the whole file.');
+    r = await settle(page, { label: 'step 5' });
+    log(`    ended: ${r.end} in ${Math.round(r.ms / 1000)}s`);
+    captureSlow('step 5 (rewrite a file)', r.ms);
+    await shot(page, '5-rewrite');
+    transcript.push({ step: 'rewrite a whole file', rows: await newRows(page) });
+    {
+      const src = await readFile(path.join(project, 'slow-build.js'), 'utf8').catch(() => '');
+      if (!/DONE/.test(src) || !/step/i.test(src)) note('MEDIUM', 'the rewrite did not land as asked', src.slice(0, 300));
+      else log('    the file was rewritten');
+      // The rewrite renderer draws two panes (old + new). A rewrite that renders as ONE pane, or throws, is the bug.
+      const panes = await page.evaluate(() => {
+        const rw = document.querySelector('#log .diff-rewrite');
+        return rw ? rw.querySelectorAll('.diff-pane').length : 0;
+      });
+      if (panes && panes !== 2) note('MEDIUM', `rewrite diff rendered ${panes} pane(s), expected 2`, '');
+      else if (panes === 2) log('    the rewrite rendered as two panes');
+    }
+
+    // ── STEP 6: COMPACTION at a low ceiling — the just-fixed "compacts every message" ─────────────────────
+    log('[6] lower the compaction ceiling, then talk — it must not compact every message');
+    {
+      // Set a low ceiling through the real Settings endpoint, the way the developer did on the .mil machine.
+      const set = await page.evaluate(async () => {
+        const r = await fetch('/api/tuning', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ compactTokens: 12000 }) });
+        return r.ok;
+      });
+      log(`    ceiling set to 12000: ${set}`);
+      const countDone = () => page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('.compaction-row').length);
+      // Push the conversation over the ceiling with one big message, then hold several SHORT exchanges. After the
+      // first fold the persisted checkpoint should carry it — so the row count must NOT climb by one per message.
+      // A LARGE but SUMMARISABLE seed — enough to cross the ceiling once, but ordinary prose the summariser can
+      // condense (NOT "quote this back verbatim", which makes the model preserve the block so nothing ever folds
+      // under budget — that thrashes compaction on every message and is a pathological input, not the bug we fixed).
+      const para = 'The enrollment service validates each applicant against the eligibility rules, then writes an audit record and notifies the downstream fee gateway. The gateway retries transient failures with a capped backoff and records the final disposition. ';
+      // ~90k chars ≈ ~23k tokens — comfortably over the 12k ceiling so the fold fires, but ordinary repeated prose
+      // the summariser collapses to almost nothing, so it folds ONCE and does NOT re-fire (the behaviour to verify).
+      await send(page, 'Here is some background on the system for context; just acknowledge it in one line. ' + para.repeat(400));
+      await settle(page, { label: 'step 6 seed' });
+      const afterSeed = await countDone();
+      if (afterSeed === 0) note('LOW', 'compaction step never crossed the ceiling', 'the seed message did not push the conversation over 12000 tokens, so the every-message check did not run');
+      let compactedEach = 0;
+      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
+        const before = await countDone();
+        await send(page, `Short follow-up ${i + 1}: what is 2 + ${i}? One number, nothing else.`);
+        await settle(page, { label: `step 6 msg ${i + 1}` });
+        const after = await countDone();
+        if (after > before) compactedEach++;
+        log(`    msg ${i + 1}: compaction rows ${before} → ${after}`);
+      }
+      await shot(page, '6-compaction');
+      transcript.push({ step: 'compaction at a low ceiling', rows: await newRows(page) });
+      if (compactedEach >= 3) note('HIGH', 'compaction fires on nearly every message', `${compactedEach} of 4 short follow-ups triggered a fresh compaction after the seed — the persisted checkpoint is not being reused`);
+      else log(`    compaction fired on ${compactedEach} of 4 short follow-ups (seed rows: ${afterSeed})`);
+    }
+
+    // ── STEP 7: SUB-AGENTS — spawn, report, no page errors, nothing left hanging ──────────────────────────
+    log('[7] delegate to a sub-agent and let it report back');
+    const errBefore7 = pageErrors.length;
+    await send(page, 'Use a sub-agent to review this project and tell me what the build tool is and what each file does. Carry on while it works, then summarise its report.');
+    r = await settle(page, { label: 'step 7', timeoutMs: 360000 });
+    log(`    main run ended: ${r.end} in ${Math.round(r.ms / 1000)}s`);
+    const spawned = await page.evaluate(() => [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .row.tool')].some((el) => /spawn.?agent|sub-agent/i.test(el.textContent || '')));
+    // THE MAIN RUN ENDING IS THE POINT — the agent should end its turn, not sit on the sub-agent. So the report
+    // arrives LATER, as its own delivery. Wait for it (up to 3 min) rather than checking the instant the turn ends.
+    let reportRow = false;
+    if (spawned) {
+      for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) {
+        reportRow = await page.evaluate(() => !!document.querySelector('#log .row.tool.subagent-report-row'));
+        if (reportRow) break;
+        await sleep(2000);
+      }
+      // Give the main agent's follow-up turn (its summary of the report) time to settle — a real synthesis of a
+      // sub-agent report is a full model call and can run for minutes, so this budget is generous, not tight.
+      await settle(page, { label: 'step 7 report', timeoutMs: 300000 });
+    }
+    log(`    sub-agent report delivered: ${reportRow}`);
+    captureSlow('step 7 (sub-agent)', r.ms);
+    await shot(page, '7-subagent');
+    transcript.push({ step: 'delegate to a sub-agent', rows: await newRows(page) });
+    {
+      const info = await page.evaluate(() => ({
+        bubbleReport: [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .bubble.user')].some((b) => /sub-agent-report/.test(b.textContent || '')),
+        endedIdle: (window.__sessionRef || {}).status,
+      }));
+      if (!spawned) note('LOW', 'the agent did not spawn a sub-agent', 'it may have done the review itself — see the transcript');
+      else if (!reportRow) note('MEDIUM', 'a sub-agent was spawned but its report never arrived within 3 minutes', 'the delivery may have been dropped');
+      else log('    a sub-agent reported back as a tool row, delivered on its own after the turn ended');
+      if (info.bubbleReport) note('HIGH', 'a sub-agent report rendered as a developer message', 'it should be a tool-shaped row, not a chat bubble');
+      if (pageErrors.length > errBefore7) note('HIGH', 'sub-agent step raised page errors', pageErrors.slice(errBefore7).join('\n    '));
+    }
+
+    // ── Cross-cutting checks ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    if (pageErrors.length) note('HIGH', `${pageErrors.length} page error(s) in the browser`, [...new Set(pageErrors)].slice(0, 5).join('\n    '));
+    {
+      // Read what the UI DISPLAYS. The first version summed session.tokens.{up,down} and reported "2" for a
+      // whole live conversation — a broken probe, not a broken counter, and it reported it twice before I
+      // noticed because the number was in the log rather than in the findings.
+      const tok = await page.evaluate(() => ({
+        shown: (document.getElementById('tokenCounter') || {}).textContent || null,
+        total: (typeof totalTokens === 'number') ? totalTokens : null,
+      }));
+      log(`
+    tokens: counter shows ${JSON.stringify(tok.shown)} (total ${tok.total})`);
+      if (tok.total != null && tok.total < 1000) note('MEDIUM', 'token counter looks wrong', `reads ${tok.total} after a full live conversation`);
+    }
+  } catch (e) {
+    note('CRITICAL', 'the hunt itself threw', String(e?.stack || e).slice(0, 600));
+  } finally {
+    // The report is the deliverable.
+    const lines = ['# Live bug hunt', '', `model: ${MODEL}`, `when: ${new Date().toISOString()}`, '',
+      `## Findings (${findings.length})`, ''];
+    if (!findings.length) lines.push('None. Every step behaved.', '');
+    for (const f of findings) lines.push(`### [${f.severity}] ${f.title}`, '', '```', String(f.detail || '').slice(0, 1500), '```', '');
+    if (slowSteps.length) {
+      lines.push('## Slow steps (stream diagnostics)', '',
+        'A step taking minutes while its neighbours take seconds is the production phenomenon. `after` says how the',
+        'text following a gap relates to the text before it: exact/prefix = something re-delivered it, similar = the',
+        'model rewording, new = ordinary streaming (a long silence with `new` after it is the model thinking).', '');
+      for (const s2 of slowSteps) {
+        lines.push(`### ${s2.label} — ${Math.round(s2.ms / 1000)}s`, '', '| stream | chunks | chars | gaps |', '|---|---|---|---|');
+        for (const r2 of s2.recs) {
+          const g = r2.gaps.map((x) => `${(x.gapMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s@${x.atChars}->${x.after}${x.overlapPct ? '/' + x.overlapPct + '%' : ''}`).join(' ') || '-';
+          lines.push(`| ${(r2.ms / 1000).toFixed(1)}s | ${r2.chunks} | ${r2.chars} | ${g} |`);
+        }
+        lines.push('');
+      }
+    }
+    lines.push('## Transcript', '');
+    for (const t of transcript) {
+      lines.push(`### ${t.step}`, '');
+      for (const r of t.rows) lines.push(`- **${r.kind}**: ${r.text}`);
+      lines.push('');
+    }
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'report.md'), lines.join('\n'), 'utf8').catch(() => {});
+    try { await browser.close(); } catch {}
+    server.close();
+    console.log(`\nreport: ${path.join(OUT, 'report.md')}`);
+    console.log(`project kept for inspection: ${project}`);
+    console.log(`\n${findings.length} finding(s): ` + findings.map((f) => f.severity).join(', '));
+  }
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/live-concurrent-compare.mjs
new 9cdf5695a5902f0773188dbe
+/**
+ * R7 — concurrent-task eval: is CB_ORCHESTRATOR (delegate + Acceptance review loop)
+ * NET-POSITIVE vs running two independent tasks serially? Boots the REAL server against the
+ * live proxy (CB_ORCHESTRATOR=1) and runs the SAME two functionally-checked coding tasks
+ * two ways, each on a fresh git repo:
+ *
+ *   SERIAL     — send T1, wait idle, send T2, wait idle  (sequential → no overlap → no delegate)
+ *   CONCURRENT — send T1, then T2 WHILE T1 runs          (overlap → T2 delegates to a sub-agent)
+ *
+ * Both produce files that are require()'d + asserted (success = the code runs). We measure
+ * wall-time + tokens for each. NET-POSITIVE = concurrent BOTH succeed AND concurrent
+ * wall-time < serial wall-time (the concurrency win), reporting the token overhead.
+ *
+ * `live-` prefix → run-all.mjs skips it. Skips gracefully (exit 0) if the proxy is down.
+ *   node test/live-concurrent-compare.mjs
+ *   env: CB_PROXY (default http://127.0.0.1:8080), CB_MODEL (default claude-sonnet-4-6)
+ */
+process.env.CB_ORCHESTRATOR = '1';
+import { mkdtemp, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import { makeUsageSink } from './quality/harness.mjs';
+import { runGit, commitAll } from './_git.mjs';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const PROXY = process.env.CB_PROXY || 'http://127.0.0.1:8080';
+const MODEL = LIVE_MODEL;
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+// Two INDEPENDENT tasks (disjoint files → clean concurrent integrate), each functionally checked.
+const T1 = { prompt: 'Create a CommonJS module `add.js` in the project root exporting `add(a,b)` that returns a+b. Use module.exports.', file: 'add.js', expr: 'typeof m.add==="function" && m.add(2,3)===5' };
+const T2 = { prompt: 'Create a CommonJS module `sub.js` in the project root exporting `sub(a,b)` that returns a-b. Use module.exports.', file: 'sub.js', expr: 'typeof m.sub==="function" && m.sub(5,2)===3' };
+
+function fnCheck(dir, file, expr) {
+  if (!existsSync(path.join(dir, file))) return false;
+  const code = `try{const m=require(${JSON.stringify('./' + file)});process.exit((${expr})?0:1)}catch(e){process.exit(2)}`;
+  return spawnSync(process.execPath, ['-e', code], { cwd: dir, encoding: 'utf8' }).status === 0;
+}
+async function freshRepo() {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-conc-'));
+  await runGit(dir, ['init', '-q']); await runGit(dir, ['config', 'user.email', 't@t']); await runGit(dir, ['config', 'user.name', 'T']);
+  await runGit(dir, ['checkout', '-q', '-b', 'CODE_BOSS_eval']);
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'README.md'), '# eval\n', 'utf8'); await commitAll(dir, 'base');
+  return dir;
+}
+async function post(port, text) { const r = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text, model: MODEL }) }); return { status: r.status, body: await r.json().catch(() => ({})) }; }
+async function state(port) { return (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/api/state`)).json(); }
+async function waitIdle(port, ms = 180000) { const t0 = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { const s = await state(port); if (['idle', 'done', 'error'].includes(s.status)) return; await sleep(400); } throw new Error('timeout waiting idle'); }
+async function waitCard(port, ms = 180000) { const t0 = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { const s = await state(port); if ((s.events || []).some((e) => e.type === 'subagent-card' && (e.status === 'done'))) return true; await sleep(400); } return false; }
+
+async function boot(dir, port, usageSink) {
+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: () => 'x', onUsage: (u) => usageSink.add(u) });
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: MODEL, port, initialProject: dir, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter });
+  await sleep(300);
+  return server;
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  try { await fetch(PROXY + '/health'); } catch { console.log(`SKIP — proxy not reachable at ${PROXY}/health`); process.exit(0); }
+
+  // ── SERIAL: T1 then T2, sequentially (no overlap) ──────────────────────────
+  const serialDir = await freshRepo();
+  const serialUsage = makeUsageSink();
+  const sServer = await boot(serialDir, 28801, serialUsage);
+  let serial;
+  try {
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    await post(28801, T1.prompt); await waitIdle(28801);
+    await post(28801, T2.prompt); await waitIdle(28801);
+    serial = { wallMs: Date.now() - t0, tokens: serialUsage.totals().total, t1: fnCheck(serialDir, T1.file, T1.expr), t2: fnCheck(serialDir, T2.file, T2.expr) };
+  } finally { sServer.close(); }
+
+  // ── CONCURRENT: T1 inline, T2 delegated while T1 runs ──────────────────────
+  const concDir = await freshRepo();
+  const concUsage = makeUsageSink();
+  const cServer = await boot(concDir, 28802, concUsage);
+  let conc;
+  try {
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    const r1 = await post(28802, T1.prompt);
+    await sleep(250);                                  // let T1 enter 'running'
+    const r2 = await post(28802, T2.prompt);           // while T1 runs → should delegate
+    const delegated = r2.status === 200 && r2.body.delegated === true;
+    await waitIdle(28802);                              // T1 (inline) done
+    const carded = await waitCard(28802);              // T2 (delegated sub-agent) done + reviewed
+    conc = { wallMs: Date.now() - t0, tokens: concUsage.totals().total, delegated, carded, t1: fnCheck(concDir, T1.file, T1.expr), t2: fnCheck(concDir, T2.file, T2.expr) };
+  } finally { cServer.close(); }
+
+  await rm(serialDir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  await rm(concDir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+
+  const fmt = (r) => `wall=${(r.wallMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s tokens=${r.tokens} T1=${r.t1 ? 'ok' : 'FAIL'} T2=${r.t2 ? 'ok' : 'FAIL'}`;
+  console.log('\n── R7 concurrent-task eval ──');
+  console.log(`SERIAL     ${fmt(serial)}`);
+  console.log(`CONCURRENT ${fmt(conc)}  (delegated=${conc.delegated}, reviewed-card=${conc.carded})`);
+  const bothOk = conc.t1 && conc.t2 && serial.t1 && serial.t2;
+  const faster = conc.wallMs < serial.wallMs;
+  const tokenRatio = serial.tokens ? (conc.tokens / serial.tokens).toFixed(2) : 'n/a';
+  console.log(`\nconcurrency: ${conc.delegated ? 'engaged (T2 delegated)' : 'NOT engaged (T2 did not delegate!)'}`);
+  console.log(`wall: ${faster ? 'FASTER' : 'slower'} (${(serial.wallMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s → ${(conc.wallMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s)`);
+  console.log(`tokens: ${tokenRatio}x serial (concurrency overhead = the Acceptance agent + worktree)`);
+  const netPositive = bothOk && conc.delegated && faster;
+  console.log(`\nVERDICT: ${netPositive ? 'NET-POSITIVE — both tasks correct + concurrency saved wall-time' : 'NOT net-positive (see above)'}`);
+  process.exit(netPositive ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });
+
=== add test/live-console-reference-puppeteer.mjs
new 614a78a2ee5b3414021df966
+/**
+ * LIVE: can an agent that has NEVER SEEN code_boss write a snippet that runs here?
+ *
+ * That is the whole premise of the >_ console. When the budget is gone you paste the reference into some other
+ * agent, ask it for a snippet, paste the snippet in, and paste the result back. Every part of that loop is a
+ * guess until a real model is on the other end of it — so this test IS the loop:
+ *   1. fetch /api/snippet-reference, exactly as the Settings button does
+ *   2. hand it to the real model with a task and NOTHING else — no code_boss prompt, no tools, no conversation
+ *   3. take the snippet it writes and run it through the >_ console, in the real UI
+ *   4. check it actually worked, and that the ⧉ copy is what you would paste back
+ *   5. do it again for a SECOND task that needs the scratchpad, because "each run is isolated" is the one thing
+ *      about this API that a model cannot guess and must have been told
+ *
+ * A failure here is a documentation failure, not a model failure: it means the reference does not say enough.
+ *
+ * `live-` prefix → run-all skips it. Skips (exit 0) if the proxy or Chrome is unavailable.
+ *   node test/live-console-reference-puppeteer.mjs        (env CB_PROXY, CB_MODEL — default claude-sonnet-4-6)
+ */
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+import { PROXY_BASE, PROXY_HEADERS, PROXY_API_KEY, describeCreds } from './_proxy-creds.mjs';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-lref-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PROXY = PROXY_BASE || process.env.CB_PROXY || 'https://home-proxy.philhignight.com';
+const MODEL = LIVE_MODEL;
+const PORT = 17911;
+const SHOTS = path.join(__dirname, 'live-skills-screenshots');
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;

+function findChrome() {
+  const c = process.platform === 'win32'
+    ? [(process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', 'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe']
+    : ['/usr/bin/google-chrome'];
+  return c.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });

+/** One plain completion — no agent loop, no code_boss prompt. The other agent in the other window. */
+async function askModel(adapter, prompt) {
+  let out = '';
+  for await (const chunk of adapter.completeStream({ model: MODEL, max_tokens: 2000, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: prompt }] })) {
+    const d = chunk?.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content;
+    if (typeof d === 'string') out += d;
+  }
+  return out;
+}
+/** The code out of whatever it replied — a fenced block if there is one, otherwise the whole thing. */
+function extractCode(reply) {
+  const fenced = /```(?:js|javascript)?\s*\n([\s\S]*?)```/.exec(String(reply || ''));
+  return (fenced ? fenced[1] : String(reply || '')).trim();
+}

+const member = ${MEMBER_SRC};
+const CONTACTS = ${JSON.stringify(CONTACTS)};
+export default {
+  description: 'The membership database. sql_query puts a query result in a data set; sql_push writes a data set back.',
+  tools: [
+    { verb: 'sql_query', name: 'sql_query',
+      schema: { description: 'Run a SQL query. Tables: members (id, name, ssn, status, plan, amount - ssn is sensitive; id, status, plan and amount are shown; name is masked as a file would be) and contacts (id, name, remark, phone, alt_phone, grade, note - remark, phone and alt_phone are sensitive; id, grade and note are shown). Returns a data set reference (#@ds#...): read it with the ds functions.', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: { sql: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['sql'] } },
+      impl: async (args, ctx) => {
+        const contacts = /contacts/i.test(args.sql);
+        const rows = contacts ? CONTACTS : Array.from({ length: ${N} }, (_, i) => member(i));
+        const r = await ctx.host.dataSets.create(rows, contacts ? { sensitive: ['remark', 'phone', 'alt_phone'], shown: ['id', 'grade', 'note'], origin: { sql: args.sql } } : { sensitive: ['ssn'], shown: ['id', 'status', 'plan', 'amount'], origin: { sql: args.sql } });

+    { verb: 'sql_push', name: 'sql_push',
+      schema: { description: 'Write a data set back to the database (every row, by id).', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: { set: { type: 'string', description: 'the data set reference', token: 'ds' } }, required: ['set'] } },
+      impl: async (args, ctx) => {
+        const r = await ctx.host.dataSets.read(args.set, { limit: 100000 });

+        return { content: 'pushed ' + r.rows.length + ' rows' };
+      } },
+  ],

+// ---- reading a value back out of a file the way its language would
+const xmlText = (s) => s.replace(/&lt;/g, '<').replace(/&gt;/g, '>').replace(/&quot;/g, '"').replace(/&apos;/g, "'").replace(/&#39;/g, "'").replace(/&amp;/g, '&');
+function shWord(s) {   // a POSIX shell word: '...' runs, "..." runs, \x outside quotes
+  let out = '', i = 0;
+  while (i < s.length && !/[\s;]/.test(s[i])) {
+    if (s[i] === "'") { const j = s.indexOf("'", i + 1); if (j < 0) return null; out += s.slice(i + 1, j); i = j + 1; }
+    else if (s[i] === '"') { i++; while (i < s.length && s[i] !== '"') { if (s[i] === '\\' && /[\\"$`]/.test(s[i + 1])) { out += s[i + 1]; i += 2; } else out += s[i++]; } i++; }
+    else if (s[i] === '\\') { out += s[i + 1]; i += 2; }
+    else out += s[i++];
+  }
+  return out;
+}
+function psString(s) {   // a PowerShell string literal at the start of s
+  if (s[0] === "'") { let out = '', i = 1; while (i < s.length) { if (s[i] === "'") { if (s[i + 1] === "'") { out += "'"; i += 2; continue; } return out; } out += s[i++]; } return null; }
+  if (s[0] === '"') { let out = '', i = 1; while (i < s.length) { if (s[i] === '`') { out += s[i + 1]; i += 2; continue; } if (s[i] === '"') { if (s[i + 1] === '"') { out += '"'; i += 2; continue; } return out; } out += s[i++]; } return null; }
+  return null;
+}

+  catch (e) { console.log(`SKIP — proxy not reachable (${e.message})`); process.exit(0); }
+  note(`answers: ${AG_COUNT} ACTIVE GOLD, amount ${AG_SUM}; ${SSN4} SSNs start with 4; ${INACTIVE} INACTIVE`);

+  const inner = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY_BASE + '/v1', apiKey: () => (PROXY_API_KEY || 'x'), extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS, onRateLimit: (ms) => console.log(`  [rate-limit] ${ms}ms`) });
+  const adapter = { ...inner, completeStream: (req) => { requests.push(JSON.stringify(req)); return inner.completeStream(req); }, complete: (req) => { requests.push(JSON.stringify(req)); return inner.complete(req); } };
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: PROXY_BASE, apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' }, defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: project, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic' });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'], protocolTimeout: 900000 });
+  const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  const errs = [], dialogs = [];
+  page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+  page.on('dialog', async (d) => { dialogs.push(d.message()); await d.dismiss().catch(() => {}); });
+  await page.setViewport({ width: 1300, height: 950 });
+  await page.goto(B + '/');
+  await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 });
+  const status = async () => { try { return (await (await fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=1')).json()).status; } catch { return 'running'; } };
+  const settled = async (timeout) => { const t0 = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t0 < 15000 && (await status()) !== 'running') await sleep(250); while (Date.now() - t0 < timeout) { if ((await status()) !== 'running') { await sleep(2000); if ((await status()) !== 'running') return true; } await sleep(500); } return null; };
+  (async () => { while (true) { try { const b = await page.$('#approvalApproveBtn'); if (b && await b.isIntersectingViewport().catch(() => false)) await b.click(); } catch {} await sleep(700); } })();
+  const allEvents = async () => ((await (await fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=100000')).json()).events || []);
+  // events after a point, by id: a session past 100,000 events (a data set scan logs a step a call) returns the last
+  // 100,000, and counting them no longer says where a turn began
+  // (appendedAtVersion, not the id: a ds-table event carries its own id, dst-<run>-<n>)
+  const evNum = (e) => Number(e && e.appendedAtVersion) || 0;
+  const lastEv = async () => (await allEvents()).reduce((a, e) => Math.max(a, evNum(e)), 0);
+  const eventsSince = async (n) => (await allEvents()).filter((e) => evNum(e) > n);
+  const send = async (text) => { await page.evaluate((t) => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = t; i.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); i.focus(); }, text); await page.keyboard.press('Enter'); await sleep(800); };
+  const leaksIn = (texts) => {
+    const all = texts.join('\n');
+    const found = REAL.filter((v) => all.includes(v) || all.includes(JSON.stringify(v).slice(1, -1)));
+    for (const m of all.matchAll(/(?<!\d)\d{3}-\d{2}-\d{4}(?!\d)/g)) if (SSNS.has(m[0])) { found.push('an SSN ' + m[0].slice(0, 3) + '…'); break; }
+    for (const m of all.matchAll(/(?<!\d)\d{9}(?!\d)/g)) if (SSN_DIGITS.has(m[0])) { found.push('an SSN as digits'); break; }
+    return found;
+  };
+  const turn = async (text, { timeout = 600000 } = {}) => {
+    const before = await lastEv(), rb = requests.length, t0 = Date.now();
+    console.log('\n=== ' + text.slice(0, 140));
+    await send(text);

+    const ms = Date.now() - t0;
+    const ev = await eventsSince(before);
+    const reply = ev.filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').map((e) => e.content).join('\n').trim();
+    const code = ev.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.name === 'js_eval').map((e) => String(e.args?.code || '')).join('\n---\n');
+    const res = ev.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result').map((e) => String(e.content || '')).join('\n---\n');
+    const mine = requests.slice(rb);
+    const biggest = mine.reduce((a, r) => Math.max(a, r.length), 0);
+    note(`${(ms / 1000).toFixed(0)} s, ${mine.length} requests, the biggest ${(biggest / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`);
+    note('code: ' + code.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 500));
+    note('reply: ' + reply.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 300));
+    ok(!!s, 'settled');
+    const lk = leaksIn(mine);
+    ok(lk.length === 0, `no request carried a value (${lk.join(', ') || 'none'})`);
+    // where it was (the values here are the test's own, made up): what was around it, the value itself replaced
+    for (const v of lk.filter((x) => typeof x === 'string' && !/^an SSN/.test(x)).slice(0, 2)) {
+      const q = mine.find((r) => r.includes(v)); const at = q.indexOf(v);
+      note(`"${v}" was in: ` + JSON.stringify(q.slice(Math.max(0, at - 400), at + 200).split(v).join('<<VALUE>>')));
+    }
+    return { reply, code, res, ev, ms, biggest };
+  };
+  const hoverPopup = async (sel) => {
+    const at = await page.evaluate((sel) => {
+      for (const sp of [...document.querySelectorAll(sel)].filter((s) => /^#@/.test(s.dataset.token || '')).reverse()) {
+        sp.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' });
+        const r = sp.getBoundingClientRect(); if (!r.width) continue;
+        const x = r.left + r.width / 2, y = r.top + r.height / 2;
+        if (sp.contains(document.elementFromPoint(x, y))) return { x, y, tok: sp.dataset.token };
+      }
+      return null;
+    }, sel);
+    if (!at) return null;
+    await page.mouse.move(at.x, at.y); await sleep(500);
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => { const p = document.getElementById('revealPop'); return p && /data set|plugin|filter/.test(p.querySelector('.rp-src').textContent); }, { timeout: 4000 }).catch(() => {});
+    const st = await page.evaluate(() => { const p = document.getElementById('revealPop'); return p && { shown: getComputedStyle(p).display !== 'none', head: p.querySelector('.rp-head').textContent, src: p.querySelector('.rp-src').textContent, button: getComputedStyle(p.querySelector('.rp-unmask')).display !== 'none' }; });
+    await page.mouse.move(5, 5);
+    return st && { ...st, tok: at.tok };
+  };

+    let t = null, pushed = null;

+    ok(num(t.reply).includes(String(AG_COUNT)), `the ACTIVE GOLD count: ${AG_COUNT}`);
+    ok(num(t.reply).includes(String(AG_SUM)), `their total amount: ${AG_SUM}`);
+    ok(t.biggest < 300 * 1024, `no request ballooned with ${N.toLocaleString()} rows behind it (${(t.biggest / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB)`);
+
+    t = await turn(`How many members have an SSN that starts with 4? Work it out from the data set; don't ask me for any values.`, { timeout: 900000 });
+    ok(num(t.reply).includes(String(SSN4)), `SSNs starting with 4: ${SSN4}, a masked column filtered over ${N.toLocaleString()} rows in ${(t.ms / 1000).toFixed(0)} s`);
+
+    const beforeShow = await lastEv();
+    t = await turn(`Show me the 25 ACTIVE GOLD members with the highest amount, highest first.`);

+    const last = tables[tables.length - 1];
+    ok(last && last.rows.length === 25, `a table of 25 went into the chat (${last ? last.rows.length : 'none'})`);

+      }
+      ok(good === AG_COUNT, `every line holds that member's real SSN and name (${good}/${AG_COUNT}${bad.length ? '; e.g. ' + bad.join(' | ') : ''})`);

+/**
+ * LIVE DATA SETS (build 2342) - the real model (sonnet via the proxy) in the real UI works with a set a plugin made:
+ * reads it, filters it (a masked column by asking its token), copies it, edits the copy - a token written into a shown
+ * column among the edits - and hands it to the plugin's push tool, which receives the REAL values. No request may carry
+ * a value; the pushed rows must be right.

+const PLUGIN = `export default {
+  description: 'The enrollment database. sql_query puts a query result in a data set; sql_push writes a data set back to the database.',
+  tools: [
+    { verb: 'sql_query', name: 'sql_query',
+      schema: { description: 'Run a SQL query against the enrollment database. Returns a data set reference (#@ds#...): read it with dsRows etc. The ssn column is sensitive; member_id, status, plan and note are shown; name is masked as a file would be.', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: { sql: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['sql'] } },
+      impl: async (args, ctx) => {
+        const r = await ctx.host.dataSets.create(${JSON.stringify(ROWS)}, { sensitive: ['ssn'], shown: ['member_id', 'status', 'plan', 'note'], origin: { sql: args.sql } });
+        return { content: 'query ran: ' + r.rows + ' rows in data set ' + r.set + ' (columns member_id, name, ssn, status, plan, note)' };
+      } },
+    { verb: 'sql_push', name: 'sql_push',
+      schema: { description: 'Write a data set back to the enrollment database (every row, by member_id).', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: { set: { type: 'string', description: 'the data set reference', token: 'ds' } }, required: ['set'] } },
+      impl: async (args, ctx) => {
+        const r = await ctx.host.dataSets.read(args.set, { limit: 1000 });
+        require_fs().writeFileSync(${JSON.stringify(PUSHED)}, JSON.stringify(r.rows));

+function require_fs() { return { writeFileSync }; }

+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }

+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: PROXY_BASE, apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' }, defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: project, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic' });

+  const settled = async (timeout = 420000) => { const t0 = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t0 < 15000 && (await status()) !== 'running') await sleep(250); while (Date.now() - t0 < timeout) { if ((await status()) !== 'running') { await sleep(2000); if ((await status()) !== 'running') return true; } await sleep(500); } return null; };

+  const leaks = (from) => REAL.filter((v) => requests.slice(from).map((r) => JSON.stringify(r)).join('\n').includes(v));

+    const m1 = pushed && pushed.find((r) => r.member_id === '1001'), m2 = pushed && pushed.find((r) => r.member_id === '1002');
+    ok(m2 && m2.status === 'ACTIVE', `member 1002 is ACTIVE (${m2 && m2.status})`);
+    ok(m1 && m1.note === '482-11-9037', `member 1001's note holds the real SSN, moved without being seen (${m1 && m1.note ? (m1.note === '482-11-9037' ? 'the SSN' : 'something else') : 'empty'})`);

=== add test/live-dev-act.mjs
new ca1e43520a4c8b9250804fe2
+/**
+ * One developer action against the live UI (connects to the persistent
+ * harness's browser; never closes it). INPUT is real mouse clicks + keystrokes
+ * only — no /api calls, no page.evaluate that drives the app. page.evaluate is
+ * used ONLY for mechanical timing (is the run done? is a modal up?) and, on
+ * explicit `dom`/`domlast` actions, to read text for long diffs the screenshot
+ * can't fully convey. All RESULT interpretation is done by a human looking at
+ * the screenshot this writes.
+ *
+ * Usage:
+ *   node test/live-dev-act.mjs chat            # types D:/tmp/cbdev/msg.txt into #input, presses Enter, waits
+ *   node test/live-dev-act.mjs gitwizard       # clicks the git branch wizard's Continue
+ *   node test/live-dev-act.mjs clickbtn "Accept"   # real-clicks a button whose text contains "Accept"
+ *   node test/live-dev-act.mjs wait            # just waits for the current run to settle
+ *   node test/live-dev-act.mjs shot            # screenshot + status only
+ *   node test/live-dev-act.mjs domlast         # text of the last ~8 log rows (long-diff fallback)
+ *   node test/live-dev-act.mjs dom ".diff-block"   # text of matching elements
+ */
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+
+const SESS = process.env.CB_SESS || 'D:/tmp/cbdev';
+const sess = JSON.parse(readFileSync(SESS + '/session.json', 'utf8'));
+const action = process.argv[2] || 'shot';
+const arg = process.argv[3];
+
+function nextN() { let n = 0; try { n = parseInt(readFileSync(SESS + '/shotn.txt', 'utf8')) || 0; } catch {} n++; writeFileSync(SESS + '/shotn.txt', String(n)); return n; }
+async function getPage(browser) {
+  const pages = await browser.pages();
+  for (const p of pages) { try { if (p.url().includes(':' + sess.port)) return p; } catch {} }
+  return pages[0];
+}
+// ── mechanical timing only (not result interpretation) ──
+const STATUS_JS = "(() => { const e=[...document.querySelectorAll('.status')]; return e.length ? e[e.length-1].textContent : ''; })()";
+const MODAL_JS = "(() => { const m=[...document.querySelectorAll('.modal-bg')].find(x => x.classList.contains('show') && getComputedStyle(x).display!=='none'); return m ? { id:m.id, text:(m.innerText||'').replace(/\\s+/g,' ').slice(0,500) } : null; })()";
+async function status(page) { try { return await page.evaluate(STATUS_JS); } catch { return ''; } }
+async function modal(page) { try { return await page.evaluate(MODAL_JS); } catch { return null; } }
+// Running iff the status row shows the interrupt hint OR the input placeholder
+// is in its running state (driven by session.status — robust to transient
+// status-row re-renders). Mechanical timing only.
+async function isRunning(page) {
+  try {
+    return await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const els = [...document.querySelectorAll('.status')];
+      const st = els.length ? els[els.length - 1].textContent : '';
+      const inp = document.getElementById('input');
+      const ph = inp ? (inp.placeholder || '') : '';
+      // Intake runs without the main running-lock; detect its own busy state.
+      const sb = document.getElementById('intakeSendBtn');
+      const is = document.getElementById('intakeStatus');
+      const intakeBusy = (sb && sb.disabled) || (is && (is.textContent || '').trim().length > 0);
+      return /esc to interrupt/.test(st) || /queue|stop|running|thinking|working|reviewing|checking/i.test(ph) || !!intakeBusy;
+    });
+  } catch { return false; }
+}
+async function settle(page, maxMs = 300000) {
+  const t0 = Date.now(); let sawRunning = false; let idle = 0;
+  while (Date.now() - t0 < maxMs) {
+    const md = await modal(page);
+    if (md) return { status: 'MODAL', modal: md };
+    if (await isRunning(page)) { sawRunning = true; idle = 0; }
+    else idle++;
+    if (sawRunning && idle >= 3) return { status: 'done' };          // debounce: 3 consecutive idle polls
+    if (!sawRunning && Date.now() - t0 > 12000) return { status: 'no-start' };
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1000));
+  }
+  return { status: 'TIMEOUT' };
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const browser = await puppeteer.connect({ browserWSEndpoint: sess.wsEndpoint, defaultViewport: null });
+  const page = await getPage(browser);
+  const out = { action, arg };
+  try {
+    if (action === 'chat') {
+      const msg = readFileSync(SESS + '/msg.txt', 'utf8').replace(/\r?\n/g, ' ').trim();
+      out.sent = msg;
+      await page.click('#input');
+      await page.evaluate(() => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); if (i) i.value = ''; }); // clear any stray text (control, not drive)
+      await page.click('#input');
+      await page.type('#input', msg);                 // real keystrokes
+      await page.keyboard.press('Enter');             // real Enter
+      Object.assign(out, await settle(page));
+    } else if (action === 'gitwizard') {
+      const el = await page.$('#gw-submit');
+      if (!el) { out.status = 'no-wizard'; } else { await el.click(); Object.assign(out, await settle(page)); }
+    } else if (action === 'clickbtn') {
+      const h = await page.evaluateHandle((t) => {
+        const b = [...document.querySelectorAll('button')].filter((x) => getComputedStyle(x).display !== 'none' && (x.innerText || '').toLowerCase().includes(String(t).toLowerCase()));
+        return b[b.length - 1] || null;
+      }, arg);
+      const el = h.asElement();
+      if (!el) { out.status = 'no-button'; } else { await el.click(); Object.assign(out, await settle(page)); }
+    } else if (action === 'clicksel') {
+      // Real-click the first element matching a CSS selector (for non-button clickables like the git badge).
+      const h = await page.evaluateHandle((sel) => document.querySelector(sel), arg);
+      const el = h.asElement();
+      if (!el) { out.status = 'no-el'; } else { await el.click(); Object.assign(out, await settle(page)); }
+    } else if (action === 'intake') {
+      const msg = readFileSync(SESS + '/msg.txt', 'utf8').replace(/\r?\n/g, ' ').trim();
+      out.sent = msg;
+      const open = await page.evaluate(() => { const f = document.getElementById('intakeForm'); return !!(f && !f.classList.contains('hidden')); });
+      if (!open) {
+        const h = await page.evaluateHandle(() => [...document.querySelectorAll('button')].find((b) => /chat/i.test(b.innerText)));
+        const el = h.asElement(); if (el) await el.click();
+      }
+      await page.click('#intakeText');
+      await page.type('#intakeText', msg);     // real keystrokes into the intake box
+      const sb = await page.$('#intakeSendBtn'); if (sb) await sb.click();   // real click Send
+      Object.assign(out, await settle(page));
+    } else if (action === 'click') {
+      await page.click(arg); Object.assign(out, await settle(page));
+    } else if (action === 'wait') {
+      Object.assign(out, await settle(page));
+    } else if (action === 'shot') {
+      out.status = 'shot';
+    } else if (action === 'dom') {
+      out.dom = await page.evaluate((sel) => [...document.querySelectorAll(sel)].map((e) => e.innerText).join('\n----\n').slice(0, 16000), arg || '.diff-block');
+    } else if (action === 'domlast') {
+      out.dom = await page.evaluate(() => {
+        const rows = [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .bubble, #log .row, #log .diff-block, #log .task-wrap')];
+        return rows.slice(-8).map((r) => r.innerText).join('\n========\n').slice(0, 18000);
+      });
+    } else if (action === 'delta') {
+      // Full #log innerText minus what we'd already seen — the NEW transcript text
+      // since the last delta call. Lets the developer (me) read each turn's output
+      // incrementally instead of re-reading the whole log. `delta reset` clears the
+      // watermark (call at the start of each scenario/arm).
+      if (arg === 'reset') { writeFileSync(SESS + '/prevlen.txt', '0'); out.reset = true; }
+      const full = await page.evaluate(() => { const l = document.getElementById('log'); return l ? l.innerText : ''; });
+      let prev = 0; try { prev = parseInt(readFileSync(SESS + '/prevlen.txt', 'utf8')) || 0; } catch {}
+      // If the log shrank (new run cleared it) start from 0.
+      if (prev > full.length) prev = 0;
+      out.delta = full.slice(prev).slice(0, 24000);
+      out.totalLen = full.length;
+      writeFileSync(SESS + '/prevlen.txt', String(full.length));
+    }
+    // scroll to newest (or honor an explicit scroll fraction) + screenshot.
+    try { await page.evaluate((f) => { const s = document.getElementById('logScroll'); if (s) s.scrollTop = (f == null ? s.scrollHeight : f * s.scrollHeight); }, action === 'scroll' ? Number(arg) : null); } catch {}
+    const md = await modal(page);
+    const n = nextN();
+    const shot = `${SESS}/shots/${String(n).padStart(3, '0')}-${action}.png`;
+    // Clip to the log content column so the PNG renders legibly for vision;
+    // full viewport when a modal is up (it's centered, outside the column).
+    await page.screenshot(md ? { path: shot } : { path: shot, clip: { x: 222, y: 520, width: 884, height: 470 } });
+    out.shot = shot;
+    out.statusText = (await status(page)).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 160);
+    if (md) out.modal = md;
+    // surface fresh JS errors
+    try { if (existsSync(SESS + '/jserrors.log')) { const e = readFileSync(SESS + '/jserrors.log', 'utf8').trim().split('\n'); out.jsErrorCount = e.filter(Boolean).length; out.jsErrorsTail = e.slice(-3); } } catch {}
+  } finally {
+    browser.disconnect();
+  }
+  console.log(JSON.stringify(out, null, 2));
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('ACT_FATAL', e?.message || e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * Persistent live-dev harness: boots the real code_boss server (Sonnet via the
+ * local Anthropic proxy) + a headless browser on the real UI + a fresh GIT
+ * project, then stays alive so per-turn driver scripts (live-dev-act.mjs) can
+ * connect and drive it via real clicks/keystrokes. Stops when a STOP file
+ * appears. Run in the BACKGROUND.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/live-dev-server.mjs
+ */
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, writeFileSync, appendFileSync, mkdirSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { execSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const SESS = process.env.CB_SESS || 'D:/tmp/cbdev';
+const PROJECT = SESS + '/project';
+const PORT = Number(process.env.CB_PORT || 28970);
+const PROXY = process.env.CB_PROXY || 'http://127.0.0.1:8080';
+// Live-proxy runs use haiku by default per the standing testing directive.
+const MODEL = LIVE_MODEL;
+const LAYOUT = process.env.CB_PROMPT_LAYOUT === 'shared-front' ? 'shared-front' : 'legacy';
+
+function findChrome() {
+  const c = process.platform === 'win32' ? [
+    (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+  ] : ['/usr/bin/google-chrome'];
+  return c.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+}
+const log = (m) => { try { appendFileSync(SESS + '/harness.log', `${new Date().toISOString()} ${m}\n`); } catch {} };
+
+async function main() {
+  mkdirSync(SESS, { recursive: true });
+  mkdirSync(SESS + '/shots', { recursive: true });
+  try { rmSync(SESS + '/STOP', { force: true }); } catch {}
+  try { rmSync(SESS + '/jserrors.log', { force: true }); } catch {}
+  try { rmSync(SESS + '/usage.jsonl', { force: true }); } catch {}
+  log(`[boot] CB_PROMPT_LAYOUT=${LAYOUT} model=${MODEL} proxy=${PROXY} port=${PORT}`);
+  // Fresh git project.
+  rmSync(PROJECT, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  mkdirSync(PROJECT, { recursive: true });
+  writeFileSync(PROJECT + '/README.md', '# calc\n\nA tiny command-line calculator (work in progress).\n');
+  writeFileSync(PROJECT + '/.gitignore', 'node_modules/\n*.log\n');
+  execSync('git init', { cwd: PROJECT });
+  execSync('git add -A', { cwd: PROJECT });
+  execSync('git -c user.email=dev@example.com -c user.name=dev commit -m "initial commit"', { cwd: PROJECT });
+  log(`[boot] project git repo created at ${PROJECT}`);
+
+  // Record per-call usage (incl. cache fields if the proxy surfaces them) so the
+  // A/B run has a backend cost number to compare against the deterministic
+  // cross-agent front shared-prefix measurement. One JSON line per call.
+  const onUsage = (u) => {
+    try {
+      const row = {
+        t: new Date().toISOString(),
+        layout: LAYOUT,
+        model: MODEL,
+        prompt: u?.prompt_tokens ?? u?.input_tokens ?? null,
+        completion: u?.completion_tokens ?? u?.output_tokens ?? null,
+        cacheRead: u?.cache_read_input_tokens ?? u?.prompt_tokens_details?.cached_tokens ?? null,
+        cacheWrite: u?.cache_creation_input_tokens ?? null,
+      };
+      appendFileSync(SESS + '/usage.jsonl', JSON.stringify(row) + '\n');
+    } catch {}
+  };
+  // CF Access creds (home-proxy.philhignight.com sits behind Cloudflare Access) — passed as extra request headers
+  // when present. Absent → a plain local proxy (127.0.0.1:8080) works unchanged.
+  const cfHeaders = (process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_ID && process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_SECRET)
+    ? { 'CF-Access-Client-Id': process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_ID, 'CF-Access-Client-Secret': process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_SECRET }
+    : {};
+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', extraHeaders: cfHeaders, onRateLimit: (ms) => log(`[rate-limit] ${ms}ms`), onUsage });
+  const html = (await readFile(ROOT + '/src/ui.html', 'utf8')).replace(
+    '</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: MODEL, baseURL: PROXY }) + ';</script></head>'
+  );
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: 'proxy-adds-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], initialProject: PROJECT,
+  });
+  log(`[boot] server up on ${PORT}`);
+
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: findChrome(), headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  await page.setViewport({ width: 1320, height: 1000 });
+  page.on('pageerror', (e) => { appendFileSync(SESS + '/jserrors.log', `[pageerror] ${e.message}\n`); });
+  page.on('console', (m) => { if (m.type() === 'error' && !/Failed to load resource/i.test(m.text())) appendFileSync(SESS + '/jserrors.log', `[console.error] ${m.text()}\n`); });
+  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+  await page.waitForSelector('#input', { timeout: 25000 });
+
+  writeFileSync(SESS + '/session.json', JSON.stringify({ wsEndpoint: browser.wsEndpoint(), port: PORT, project: PROJECT }, null, 2));
+  log(`[boot] browser+UI ready; session.json written`);
+  console.error('[harness] READY on ' + PORT);
+
+  while (!existsSync(SESS + '/STOP')) { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1000)); }
+  log('[stop] STOP file seen — shutting down');
+  try { await browser.close(); } catch {}
+  try { server.close(); } catch {}
+  process.exit(0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { log('FATAL ' + (e?.stack || e)); console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/live-dev-session-2.mjs
new 279c6ea2be98ac982d3ba3ad
+/**
+ * LIVE DEVELOPMENT SESSION — the real UI, the real model, a developer at the keyboard.
+ *
+ * One continuous session on a small project, driven through the browser the way a developer works: type a
+ * request, wait, read what came back, ask the next thing. Every feature shipped in builds 1923-1938 is exercised
+ * by the WORK rather than by a mock, and checked at each step from the developer's side of the screen:
+ *
+ *   collapsed snippet lines    every snippet is ONE line ("2 files read…"), no result line, click → its calls
+ *   step events                the opened line shows read(...)/write(...)/git(...) rows in order
+ *   live-output masking        a server that prints a real SSN into its log — the dock and the chat show a token
+ *   chat never scrolls sideways
+ *   javac / javap in a snippet
+ *   curl -i in a snippet
+ *   the render window           the window is lowered so the session crosses it; the marker + load-older work
+ *   status bar                  count bubbles level, one icon size
+ *   the empty-reply nudge       recorded if it ever fires
+ *
+ * Nothing is asserted about the AGENT's judgement — that is read afterwards from the transcript + screenshots
+ * this writes to test/live-session-2-out/. What IS asserted is the product: what the developer sees.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/live-dev-session-2.mjs        (CB_MODEL=claude-haiku-4-5 for the floor model)
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, readFile, rm, copyFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';

+const PROXY = PROXY_BASE;

+const PORT = 17961;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const note = (l) => console.log('  ·', l);
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+// The people in the test-data store. These strings must NEVER appear on the developer's screen.
+const SSN = '482-11-9037', LAST = 'Okonkwo-Zed', FIRST = 'Gwendolyn';
+const RAW = [SSN, LAST, FIRST];
+const FAMILY = { label: 'smiths', description: 'sponsor + dependents', family: { deers_family_id: '20240001' }, members: { sponsor: { first_name: FIRST, last_name: LAST, ssn: SSN } } };
+
+// The project: a tiny node API with a real bug, and a Java class to compile. The server LOGS the sponsor's SSN
+// on every request — the way a real app server leaks — so the live-output masking is exercised for real.
+const SERVER_JS = `const http = require('http');
+const users = { 7: { id: 7, name: '${FIRST} ${LAST}', ssn: '${SSN}' } };
+const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
+  console.log(new Date().toISOString() + ' ' + req.method + ' ' + req.url + ' sponsor=' + users[7].ssn);
+  if (req.url === '/health') { res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'x-app': 'demo' }); return res.end('{"ok":true}'); }
+  const m = req.url.match(/^\\/users\\/(\\d+)$/);
+  if (m && users[m[1]]) { res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'application/json' }); return res.end(JSON.stringify({ userId: users[m[1]].id, fullname: users[m[1]].name })); }
+  res.writeHead(404); res.end('not found');
+});
+server.listen(process.env.PORT || 7171, () => console.log('listening on ' + (process.env.PORT || 7171)));
+`;
+const TEST_JS = `// expected shape of GET /users/:id — the API contract is { id, name }
+const assert = require('assert');
+const http = require('http');
+http.get('http://127.0.0.1:7171/users/7', (res) => { let b = ''; res.on('data', (d) => b += d); res.on('end', () => {
+  const j = JSON.parse(b);
+  assert.strictEqual(typeof j.id, 'number', 'id must be a number (got ' + Object.keys(j) + ')');
+  assert.strictEqual(typeof j.name, 'string', 'name must be a string');
+  console.log('TEST OK'); process.exit(0);
+}); }).on('error', (e) => { console.error('TEST FAILED: ' + e.message); process.exit(1); });
+`;
+const GREETER = `public class Greeter {
+  public String greet(String who) { return "hello " + who; }
+  public int count(String s) { return s == null ? 0 : s.length(); }
+  public static void main(String[] a) { System.out.println(new Greeter().greet("world")); }
+}
+`;
+const README = `# demo api
+A tiny HTTP API. \`node server.js\` starts it on port 7171. \`node test.js\` checks GET /users/:id against the
+contract in API.md. Greeter.java is a helper class used by a reporting job.
+`;
+const API_MD = `# API contract
+GET /health → { ok: true }
+GET /users/:id → { id: number, name: string }
+`;
+
+// THE DEVELOPER'S SIDE OF THE CONVERSATION. Each has a check that runs after the agent settles.
+const DIRECTIVES = [
+  { id: 'orient', text: 'Take a look around this project and tell me briefly what is here and how the pieces fit.' },
+  { id: 'bug', text: 'GET /users/:id returns the wrong field names — API.md has the contract. Fix server.js, then run test.js to prove it (start the server first if you need to, and stop it after).' },
+  { id: 'server', text: 'Start the server in the background and hit /health with curl -i so I can see the headers. Leave it running.' },
+  { id: 'java', text: 'Compile Greeter.java into a build/ folder and use javap to tell me what public methods it exposes.' },
+  { id: 'log', text: 'Hit /users/7 once, then look at what the running server printed to its log and tell me if it is logging anything it should not.' },
+  { id: 'commit', text: 'Stop the server, then commit everything with a sensible message.' },
+  { id: 'wrap', text: 'One last thing: list every file you changed this session and what you changed in each, in a short table.' },
+];
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  try { execFileSync('javac', ['-version'], { stdio: 'ignore' }); } catch { console.log('SKIP — no javac'); process.exit(0); }
+  console.log(`[live] ${PROXY} · model=${MODEL} · ${describeCreds()}`);
+  try { const r = await fetch(PROXY + '/v1/models', { headers: { ...PROXY_HEADERS, 'x-api-key': PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', 'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01' } }); if (!r.ok && r.status !== 404) throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status); }
+  catch (e) { console.log(`SKIP — proxy not reachable at ${PROXY} (${e.message})`); process.exit(0); }
+
+  await rm(OUT, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  await mkdir(OUT, { recursive: true });
+  const project = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-live2-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'server.js'), SERVER_JS);
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'test.js'), TEST_JS);
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'Greeter.java'), GREETER);
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'README.md'), README);
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'API.md'), API_MD);
+  await mkdir(path.join(project, 'testdata'), { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'testdata', 'smiths.json'), JSON.stringify(FAMILY, null, 2));
