+  execFileSync('git', ['-c', 'user.email=t@t', '-c', 'user.name=t', 'add', '.'], { cwd: project });
+  execFileSync('git', ['-c', 'user.email=t@t', '-c', 'user.name=t', 'commit', '-q', '-m', 'initial'], { cwd: project });
+  // pii-testdata ships enabled; make sure the bundled copy is what the plugins dir has
+  const pdir = userPluginsDir(); await mkdir(pdir, { recursive: true });
+  if (!existsSync(path.join(pdir, 'pii-testdata__1.mjs'))) await copyFile(path.join(ROOT, 'examples', 'plugins', 'pii-testdata__1.mjs'), path.join(pdir, 'pii-testdata__1.mjs'));
+
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const requests = [];
+  const inner = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: () => (PROXY_API_KEY || 'x'), extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS });
+  const adapter = { ...inner, completeStream: (req) => { requests.push(JSON.stringify(req.messages || []).length); return inner.completeStream(req); }, complete: (req) => inner.complete(req) };
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' }, defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: project, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic' });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  await page.setViewport({ width: 1280, height: 900 });
+  const pageErrors = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => pageErrors.push(String(e?.message || e).slice(0, 200)));
+  // Lower the render window so a session this size actually crosses it.
+  await page.evaluateOnNewDocument(() => { try { localStorage.setItem('cbWindowMin', '40'); localStorage.setItem('cbWindowChunk', '20'); } catch {} });
+  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+  await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 });
+
+  const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  const status = async () => (await (await fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=1')).json()).status;
+  const settled = async (timeout = 300000) => {
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    while (Date.now() - t0 < 15000 && (await status()) !== 'running') await sleep(250);   // let it start
+    while (Date.now() - t0 < timeout) { if ((await status()) !== 'running') { await sleep(400); if ((await status()) !== 'running') return true; } await sleep(500); }
+    return null;
+  };
+  const send = async (text) => { await page.evaluate((t) => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = t; i.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); i.focus(); }, text); await page.keyboard.press('Enter'); await sleep(800); };
+  // Approve like a developer: anything that asks, say yes.
+  const approveLoop = (async () => { while (true) { try { const b = await page.$('#approvalModalBg.show #approvalApproveBtn, .modal-bg.show #approvalApproveBtn, #approvalApproveBtn'); if (b && await b.isIntersectingViewport().catch(() => false)) { await b.click(); } } catch {} await sleep(700); } })();
+  const screen = async (name) => { try { await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(OUT, name + '.png'), fullPage: false }); } catch {} };
+  // THE SCREEN MINUS WHAT IT REVEALS ON PURPOSE: the chat shows the developer the real value behind a token
+  // (revealMasked, on by default since 2026-10-09, each in a .revealed span that carries its token). A value there is
+  // the feature; a value anywhere else on the page is a leak.
+  const bodyText = () => page.evaluate(() => { const c = document.body.cloneNode(true); for (const s of c.querySelectorAll('.revealed')) s.textContent = s.dataset.token || ''; return c.innerText; });
+  const uiCheck = async (id) => {
+    const m = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const d = document.documentElement, main = document.querySelector('main');
+      const groups = [...document.querySelectorAll('.js-group')].map((g) => ({ text: g.querySelector('.js-summary .body').textContent, open: g.classList.contains('open'), steps: g.querySelectorAll('.js-step').length, resultVisible: !!g.querySelector('.js-group-body .row-result.js-own-result') && g.querySelector('.js-group-body .row-result.js-own-result').getBoundingClientRect().height > 0 && !g.classList.contains('open') }));
+      const badges = [...document.querySelectorAll('.side-chrome .ps-badge, .side-chrome .gp-count')].filter((b) => getComputedStyle(b).display !== 'none').map((b) => Math.round(b.getBoundingClientRect().top * 10) / 10);
+      return { docOver: d.scrollWidth - d.clientWidth, mainOver: main ? main.scrollWidth - main.clientWidth : 0, groups, badges, marker: (document.querySelector('.older-marker') || {}).textContent || '', events: (session.events || []).length, noReply: /don't have a written reply|without a written reply/i.test(document.getElementById('log').textContent) };
+    });
+    ok(m.docOver <= 0 && m.mainOver <= 0, `[${id}] the chat does not scroll sideways (${m.docOver}/${m.mainOver})`);
+    const txt = await bodyText();
+    const leaked = RAW.filter((r) => txt.includes(r));
+    ok(leaked.length === 0, `[${id}] no real value from the test-data store is on screen${leaked.length ? ' — LEAKED: ' + leaked.join(', ') : ''}`);
+    // a tally: "2 reads, node, browser_click, js" - comma separated, each part a count and a word or a call's name
+    // (a plugin tool is named by its own name since 2026-10-11), never code
+    for (const g of m.groups) ok(/^(Running js…|Running…|Script|Stopped|Notice)$/.test(g.text) || String(g.text).replace(/…$/, '').split(', ').every((p) => /^(\d+ )?[A-Za-z_$][\w$ .-]{0,60}$/.test(p)), `[${id}] a snippet line reads as a tally (${JSON.stringify(g.text)})`);
+    ok(!m.groups.some((g) => g.resultVisible), `[${id}] no collapsed snippet shows a result line (${m.groups.length} snippet lines)`);
+    if (m.noReply) note(`[${id}] the empty-reply closer appeared — the nudge did not recover a reply`);
+    return m;
+  };
+
+  const transcript = [];
+  try {
+    for (const d of DIRECTIVES) {
+      console.log(`\n=== ${d.id}: ${d.text}`);
+      const t0 = Date.now();
+      const before = ((await (await fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=5000')).json()).events || []).length;
+      await send(d.text);
+      const s = await settled();
+      const secs = Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000);
+      if (!s) { fail++; console.log(`  FAIL [${d.id}] the run did not settle in time`); await screen(d.id + '-timeout'); continue; }
+      const full = await (await fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=5000')).json();
+      const evs = (full.events || []);
+      const turnEvs = evs.slice(before);
+      const calls = turnEvs.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && !/^(llm|nudge|retry)$/.test(e.verb));
+      const steps = turnEvs.filter((e) => e.type === 'js-step');
+      const nudges = turnEvs.filter((e) => e.verb === 'nudge');
+      const reply = turnEvs.filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').map((e) => e.content).join('\n').trim();
+      note(`[${d.id}] ${secs}s · ${calls.length} tool calls · ${steps.length} snippet steps (${steps.map((e) => e.kind).join(',')}) · ${nudges.length} nudges`);
+      transcript.push({ id: d.id, ask: d.text, secs, calls: calls.map((c) => c.verb), steps: steps.map((e) => `${e.fn}(${e.label})${e.status === 'error' ? ' ✗' : ''}`), nudges: nudges.map((n) => n.args?.command), reply });
+      await screen(d.id);
+      const m = await uiCheck(d.id);
+
+      // per-directive expectations about the PRODUCT (not the agent's prose)
+      if (d.id === 'orient') { const tagReads = calls.filter((c) => /^(read|list|search|find|info)$/.test(c.verb)).length; if (steps.length) ok(steps.some((e) => e.kind === 'read'), `[orient] the snippet's reads were surfaced as steps (${steps.filter((e) => e.kind === 'read').length})`); else note(`[orient] the agent browsed with the tag verbs (${tagReads} reads/lists), not a snippet — as the prompt steers`); }
+      if (d.id === 'bug') { const wrote = steps.some((e) => e.kind === 'write') || calls.some((c) => /^(edit|write)$/.test(c.verb)); ok(wrote, `[bug] a write was made (${steps.some((e) => e.kind === 'write') ? 'snippet step' : calls.filter((c) => /^(edit|write)$/.test(c.verb)).map((c) => c.verb).join(',') || 'none'})`); ok(steps.some((e) => e.kind === 'exec'), '[bug] an execution step was surfaced (the test run)'); }
+      if (d.id === 'server') {
+        const bg = await (await fetch(B + '/api/bg')).json();
+        ok((bg.processes || []).some((p) => p.alive), `[server] a background process is alive (${(bg.processes || []).length})`);
+        ok(steps.some((e) => e.fn === 'curl') || calls.some((c) => c.verb === 'js'), '[server] curl was reached for');
+        const bgOut = (bg.processes || []).map((p) => String(p.output || '')).join('\n');
+        ok(!RAW.some((r) => bgOut.includes(r)), '[server] the /api/bg output the dock reads is masked');
+        ok(/#(@[A-Za-z0-9_][A-Za-z0-9_.-]*|[Xx0]+)#[0-9a-z]{7}/.test(bgOut) || !/sponsor=/.test(bgOut), `[server] the server's own log line shows a token where the SSN was (${JSON.stringify(bgOut.slice(-90))})`);
+      }
+      if (d.id === 'java') { ok(steps.some((e) => e.fn === 'javac'), '[java] javac ran as a step'); ok(steps.some((e) => e.fn === 'javap'), '[java] javap ran as a step'); ok(existsSync(path.join(project, 'build', 'Greeter.class')) || existsSync(path.join(project, 'Greeter.class')), '[java] a .class file exists'); }
+      if (d.id === 'log') { ok(/#@smiths\.sponsor\.ssn#[0-9a-z]{7}|#[Xx0]+#[0-9a-z]{7}|token/.test(reply) || !RAW.some((r) => reply.includes(r)), `[log] the agent's answer refers to the SSN by token, never the value`); }
+      if (d.id === 'commit') { const log = execFileSync('git', ['log', '--oneline'], { cwd: project, encoding: 'utf8' }); ok(log.split('\n').filter(Boolean).length >= 2, `[commit] a second commit exists (${log.split('\n')[0]})`); }
+      if (d.id === 'wrap') {
+        ok(m.events > 40, `[wrap] the session crossed the lowered render window (${m.events} events)`);
+        ok(/earlier message/.test(m.marker), `[wrap] the older-messages marker is shown (${JSON.stringify(m.marker)})`);
+        await page.evaluate(() => { document.getElementById('logScroll').scrollTop = 0; });
+        await page.evaluate(() => maybeLoadOlder());
+        await sleep(300);
+        const after = await page.evaluate(() => ({ marker: (document.querySelector('.older-marker') || {}).textContent || '', rows: document.querySelectorAll('#log .row, #log .bubble').length }));
+        ok(!after.marker || Number((after.marker.match(/^(\d+)/) || [])[1]) < Number((m.marker.match(/^(\d+)/) || [])[1]), `[wrap] scrolling to the top revealed more (${JSON.stringify(m.marker)} → ${JSON.stringify(after.marker)})`);
+        // open the first snippet line and check its steps are drawn
+        const opened = await page.evaluate(() => { const g = document.querySelector('.js-group'); if (!g) return null; g.querySelector('.js-summary').click(); return { open: g.classList.contains('open'), steps: [...g.querySelectorAll('.js-step .body')].map((b) => b.textContent) }; });
+        ok(opened && opened.open && opened.steps.length > 0, `[wrap] clicking a snippet line opens its calls (${opened ? opened.steps.slice(0, 3).join(' | ') : 'no snippet line'})`);
+        await screen('wrap-opened');
+      }
+    }
+    ok(pageErrors.length === 0, `no page errors during the session${pageErrors.length ? ': ' + pageErrors.slice(0, 3).join(' // ') : ''}`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+  finally {
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'transcript.json'), JSON.stringify({ model: MODEL, transcript, requestSizes: requests }, null, 2));
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'transcript.md'), transcript.map((t) => `## ${t.id} — ${t.secs}s\n**Developer:** ${t.ask}\n\n**Calls:** ${t.calls.join(', ') || '(none)'}\n\n**Snippet steps:** ${t.steps.join(' · ') || '(none)'}\n\n${t.nudges.length ? '**Nudges:** ' + t.nudges.join(' // ') + '\n\n' : ''}**Agent:**\n${t.reply}\n`).join('\n---\n\n'));
+    try { const { reapAllBackground } = await import('../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs'); reapAllBackground(); } catch {}
+    await browser.close().catch(() => {});
+    await new Promise((r) => server.close(r));
+    await cleanupDir(project).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed — transcript + screenshots in test/live-session-2-out/`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();

+/**
+ * Developer-simulation harness for the big E2E comparison (CB_COGNITION on vs off) against the
+ * real local Claude proxy. Boots a code_boss instance in-process (real UI + real AI), then acts
+ * as a developer: drives the UI via Puppeteer with spec-level directives (authored per round,
+ * some deliberately vague), waits for each run to finish, and captures per-directive metrics +
+ * artifacts. RESUMABLE: completed directives are logged + skipped on re-run.
+ *
+ *   CB_MODE=cognition|plain  node test/live-dev-session.mjs
+ *
+ * env: CB_MODE (cognition→CB_COGNITION=1, plain→no flag) · CB_MODEL (default claude-sonnet-4-6)
+ *      CB_PROXY (default http://127.0.0.1:8080) · CB_PORT · CB_ROUND (default 1)
+ *      CB_DIRECTIVES (path to a JSON [{id,text}] round file) · CB_STEP_TIMEOUT_MS (default 1800000=30m)
+ *      CB_E2E_DIR (root for projects+logs, default D:\tmp\cb-e2e)
+ */

+function findChrome() {

[(process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', 'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', 'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'] : ['/usr/bin/google-chrome', '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome'];
+  return c.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });

+async function ensureProject() {
+  if (!existsSync(PROJECT)) {
+    await mkdir(PROJECT, { recursive: true });
+    await runGit(PROJECT, ['init', '-q']);
+    await runGit(PROJECT, ['config', 'user.email', 'dev@e2e']); await runGit(PROJECT, ['config', 'user.name', 'E2E Dev']);
+    await writeFile(path.join(PROJECT, 'README.md'), `# build target (${MODE})\nBuilt by the simulated developer session.\n`, 'utf8');
+    await commitAll(PROJECT, 'seed');
+  }

+async function main() {
+  try { const h = await (await fetch(PROXY + '/health')).json(); console.log(`proxy OK (${h.engine ? 'engine ' + (h.engine.last_path || '?') : 'up'}), model ${MODEL}, mode ${MODE}`); }
+  catch { console.error(`SKIP — proxy not reachable at ${PROXY}/health`); process.exit(0); }
+  const chrome = findChrome(); if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  await mkdir(SHOTS, { recursive: true });
+  await ensureProject();
+  const directives = JSON.parse(await readFile(DIRECTIVES, 'utf8'));
+
+  const usage = makeUsageSink();
+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: () => 'x', onUsage: (u) => usage.add(u) });
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: MODEL, baseURL: PROXY }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' }, defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: PROJECT, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic' });
+
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const results = [];
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1400, height: 1000 });
+    const jsErrors = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => jsErrors.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => !!document.getElementById('input'), { timeout: 15000 });
+    console.log(`UI up on ${PORT}, project ${PROJECT}`);
+
+    for (const d of directives) {
+      const logPath = path.join(LOGDIR, `${d.id}.json`);
+      if (existsSync(logPath)) { console.log(`  skip ${d.id} (already done)`); results.push(JSON.parse(await readFile(logPath, 'utf8'))); continue; }
+      console.log(`\n▶ [${d.id}] ${d.text.slice(0, 100)}`);
+      const tok0 = usage.totals().total, t0 = Date.now();
+      let s, st = 'running', timedOut = false;
+      let attempt = 0;
+      while (true) {   // retry a transient proxy error INDEFINITELY (exp backoff, plateau 30s)
+        attempt += 1;
+        // send via the real UI
+        await page.evaluate(() => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = ''; i.focus(); });
+        await page.type('#input', d.text);
+        await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+        await sleep(1500);
+        st = 'running'; timedOut = false;
+        const deadline = Date.now() + STEP_TIMEOUT;
+        while (Date.now() < deadline) {
+          const cur = await state(PORT); st = cur.status;
+          if (['idle', 'done', 'error'].includes(st)) break;
+          await sleep(2500);
+        }
+        if (!['idle', 'done', 'error'].includes(st)) { timedOut = true; try { await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/cancel`, { method: 'POST' }); } catch {} await sleep(2000); }
+        s = await state(PORT);
+        // transient proxy error (out-of-credit, OAuth window, rate limit) → retry FOREVER with
+        // exponential backoff starting at 500ms, plateauing at 30s, so the session simply
+        // resumes once the proxy is fixed rather than burning the directive.
+        if (st === 'error' && /403|429|oauth|permission|rate|credit|quota|insufficient/i.test(String(s.error || ''))) {
+          const delay = Math.min(30000, 500 * 2 ** (attempt - 1));
+          console.log(`  ⚠ proxy transient error on attempt ${attempt} (${String(s.error || '').slice(0, 80)}); backoff ${delay}ms then retry ${d.id}`);
+          await sleep(delay);
+          continue;
+        }
+        break;
+      }
+      const lastAssistant = (s.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && !e.subagentChannel).slice(-3).map((e) => e.content).join('\n---\n');
+      const rec = {
+        id: d.id, text: d.text, mode: MODE, model: MODEL,
+        wallMs: Date.now() - t0, tokens: usage.totals().total - tok0, status: st, timedOut,
+        finalStatus: s.status, error: s.error || null,
+        subagentCards: (s.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'subagent-card').length,
+        cogNodes: (() => { const b = (s.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'cognition-event'); return b.length; })(),
+        gitLog: gitOut(['log', '--oneline', '-20']),
+        gitStat: gitOut(['diff', '--stat', 'HEAD~20..HEAD']) || gitOut(['show', '--stat', '--oneline', 'HEAD']),
+        files: gitOut(['ls-files']).split('\n').filter(Boolean).slice(0, 200),
+        assistant: (lastAssistant || '').slice(0, 4000),
+        jsErrors: jsErrors.slice(),
+      };
+      try { await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(SHOTS, `${d.id}.png`), fullPage: false }); } catch {}
+      await writeFile(logPath, JSON.stringify(rec, null, 2), 'utf8');
+      results.push(rec);
+      console.log(`  ✓ ${d.id}: ${rec.wallMs}ms, ${rec.tokens} tok, status=${rec.status}${timedOut ? ' (TIMED OUT)' : ''}, files=${rec.files.length}, cards=${rec.subagentCards}, cogEvents=${rec.cogNodes}`);
+    }
+  } finally {
+    try { await browser.close(); } catch {}
+    try { server.close(); } catch {}
+  }
+  // round summary
+  const sum = { mode: MODE, model: MODEL, round: ROUND, totalWallMs: results.reduce((a, r) => a + (r.wallMs || 0), 0), totalTokens: results.reduce((a, r) => a + (r.tokens || 0), 0), steps: results.length, timeouts: results.filter((r) => r.timedOut).length, errors: results.filter((r) => r.error).length };
+  await writeFile(path.join(LOGDIR, '_summary.json'), JSON.stringify(sum, null, 2), 'utf8');
+  console.log(`\n=== ${MODE} round ${ROUND} ===  ${(sum.totalWallMs / 60000).toFixed(1)}min · ${sum.totalTokens} tok · ${sum.steps} steps · ${sum.timeouts} timeouts · ${sum.errors} errors`);
+  process.exit(0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * Live DOCX spec-maintenance session. Boots the REAL code_boss server (Sonnet
+ * via the local Anthropic proxy) + a headless browser on the real UI, opens a
+ * git project containing a large complex Word document (spec.docx), then acts
+ * as the USER: types a multi-part spec-edit request and clicks "Approve" on
+ * each tracked-change prompt the spec-maintenance gate raises.
+ *
+ * Verifies the doc ends up with the expected tracked revisions.
+ *
+ * Prereqs: proxy up at 127.0.0.1:8080, docx-helper jar built, Chrome present.
+ * Run: node test/live-docx-session.mjs
+ */
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdirSync, appendFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PROJECT = 'D:/tmp/cbdocx-proj';
+const DOCX = PROJECT + '/spec.docx';
+// Artifacts live OUTSIDE the project so the git tree stays clean at open
+// (a dirty tree triggers an extra "needs branch" modal before editing).
+const SHOTS = 'D:/tmp/cbdocx-shots';
+const PORT = 28972;
+const PROXY = 'http://127.0.0.1:8080';
+const MODEL = 'claude-sonnet-4-6';
+const HELPER_CP = `${ROOT}/docx-helper/target/codeboss-docx-helper.jar${path.delimiter}${ROOT}/docx-helper/target/lib/*`;
+
+const REQUEST = [
+  'The file spec.docx in this project is our product specification for DOCX support. Please make exactly these five revisions as tracked changes, and make ONLY these:',
+  '',
+  '1. In the main title (the very first heading), change the product name "calibre" to "Calibre Pro".',
+  '2. In the paragraph that begins "There is support for images, tables, lists", make the single word "images" bold.',
+  '3. In the sentence that lists inline formatting types ("...underlined and struck out text"), change "struck out" to "strikethrough".',
+  '4. In the supplies table, the Pencils quantity is wrong — change it from 2 to 4.',
+  '5. Center-align the "Text Formatting" section heading.',

+function findChrome() {
+  const c = [
+    (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+  ];
+  return c.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+}
+const log = (m) => { const line = `${new Date().toISOString()} ${m}`; console.error(line); try { appendFileSync(SHOTS + '/session.log', line + '\n'); } catch {} };
+
+function readDocx(file) {
+  const r = spawnSync('java', ['-Dfile.encoding=UTF-8', '-cp', HELPER_CP, 'com.codeboss.docx.DocxHelper', 'read'],
+    { input: JSON.stringify({ op: 'read', docx: file }), encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 64 * 1024 * 1024 });
+  const lines = (r.stdout || '').split(/\r?\n/).map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
+  try { return JSON.parse(lines[lines.length - 1]); } catch { return { ok: false, error: 'unparseable: ' + (r.stderr || '').slice(0, 200) }; }
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  mkdirSync(SHOTS, { recursive: true });
+  log(`[boot] project=${PROJECT} model=${MODEL} proxy=${PROXY}`);
+
+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', onRateLimit: (ms) => log(`[rate-limit] ${ms}ms`) });
+  const html = (await readFile(ROOT + '/src/ui.html', 'utf8')).replace(
+    '</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: MODEL, baseURL: PROXY }) + ';</script></head>'
+  );
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: 'proxy-adds-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], initialProject: PROJECT,
+  });
+  log(`[boot] server up on ${PORT}`);
+
+  // Confirm the docx-edit plugin actually lit up (jar + java detected).
+  const diag = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/diagnostics`)).json();
+  const plugins = (diag.plugins || []).map((p) => `${p.name}:${p.enabled ? 'on' : 'off'}`).join(' ');
+  log(`[boot] plugins -> ${plugins}`);
+  if (!(diag.plugins || []).some((p) => p.name === 'docx-edit' && p.enabled)) {
+    log('!!! docx-edit plugin is NOT enabled — the agent cannot edit the doc. Aborting.');
+    server.close(); process.exit(3);
+  }
+
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: findChrome(), headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  await page.setViewport({ width: 1320, height: 1000 });
+  page.on('pageerror', (e) => log(`[pageerror] ${e.message}`));
+  page.on('console', (m) => { if (m.type() === 'error' && !/Failed to load resource/i.test(m.text())) log(`[console.error] ${m.text()}`); });
+  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+  await page.waitForSelector('#input', { timeout: 25000 });
+  log('[ui] loaded');
+
+  let approvals = 0, denials = 0;
+  try {
+    // ── USER ACTION: enter the (multi-line) request and send ────────────────
+    // NOTE: page.type() sends each '\n' as an Enter keypress, and the chat
+    // input submits on Enter (no Shift) — so typing a multi-line string fires
+    // the message at the FIRST newline (truncating it). Instead set the
+    // textarea value (as if pasted), fire 'input' for auto-grow, then press
+    // Enter ONCE to send the whole thing.
+    await page.click('#input');
+    await page.evaluate((text) => {
+      const i = document.getElementById('input');
+      i.value = text;
+      i.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
+    }, REQUEST);
+    await page.screenshot({ path: SHOTS + '/01-typed-request.png' });
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    log('[user] request submitted (' + REQUEST.length + ' chars)');
+
+    // ── DRIVE LOOP: approve each tracked change; wait for the run to finish ──
+    // Stall guard: if the run sits with no new chat events / approvals for
+    // STALL_MS, a later turn's LLM call has hung (a real failure mode seen on
+    // the proxy) — cancel and report rather than waiting out the hard cap.
+    const HARD_DEADLINE = Date.now() + 12 * 60 * 1000;
+    const STALL_MS = 4 * 60 * 1000;
+    let lastStatus = '', lastShot = 0, lastActivity = Date.now(), lastEventCount = -1, stalled = false;
+    while (Date.now() < HARD_DEADLINE) {
+      const st = await page.evaluate(() => {
+        const apBtn = document.querySelector('#gitWizardBg.show #ap-approve');
+        const msgEl = document.querySelector('#gitWizardBg.show #gitWizardBody p');
+        return {
+          status: window.__sessionRef?.status ?? '?',
+          phase: window.__sessionRef?.phase ?? '?',
+          approvalVisible: !!apBtn && !apBtn.disabled,
+          approvalMsg: msgEl ? msgEl.textContent.slice(0, 200) : null,
+        };
+      });
+      // activity signal: the server's chat-event count grows with every
+      // tool-call / result / assistant segment.
+      let evCount = lastEventCount;
+      try { const s = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`)).json(); evCount = (s.events || []).length; } catch {}
+      if (evCount !== lastEventCount) { lastEventCount = evCount; lastActivity = Date.now(); }
+      if (st.status !== lastStatus) { log(`[state] status=${st.status} phase=${st.phase}`); lastStatus = st.status; lastActivity = Date.now(); }
+
+      if (st.approvalVisible) {
+        approvals++;
+        lastActivity = Date.now();
+        log(`[approve #${approvals}] ${st.approvalMsg}`);
+        await page.screenshot({ path: SHOTS + `/approve-${String(approvals).padStart(2, '0')}.png` });
+        await page.click('#gitWizardBg.show #ap-approve');
+        // wait for the modal to dismiss so we don't double-count
+        await page.waitForFunction(() => !document.querySelector('#gitWizardBg.show #ap-approve'), { timeout: 15000 }).catch(() => {});
+        continue;
+      }
+
+      if (st.status === 'running' && Date.now() - lastActivity > STALL_MS) {
+        stalled = true;
+        log(`[stall] no chat activity for ${Math.round((Date.now() - lastActivity) / 1000)}s while running — aborting (likely a hung LLM/proxy call on a later turn)`);
+        try { await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/cancel`, { method: 'POST' }); } catch {}
+        break;
+      }
+
+      if (st.status === 'done' || st.status === 'error') break;
+
+      // periodic progress screenshot every ~20s
+      if (Date.now() - lastShot > 20000) { lastShot = Date.now(); await page.screenshot({ path: SHOTS + '/progress.png' }); }
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1200));
+    }
+
+    await page.screenshot({ path: SHOTS + '/99-final.png', fullPage: true });
+    const finalState = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`)).json();
+    log(`[done] status=${finalState.status} approvals=${approvals} denials=${denials} events=${(finalState.events || []).length}`);
+    const toolResults = (finalState.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result');
+    for (const e of toolResults.filter((e) => /docx/.test(e.verb || e.name || ''))) {
+      log(`  [${e.verb || e.name}] ${e.status} :: ${String(e.content || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 120)}`);
+    }
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close().catch(() => {});
+    server.close();
+  }
+
+  // ── VERIFY: re-read the doc; confirm tracked revisions + new text ──────────
+  log('[verify] re-reading spec.docx via the POI helper');
+  const after = readDocx(DOCX);
+  const meta = after.meta || {};
+  const allText = (after.paragraphs || []).map((p) => (p.text || '') + ' ' + (p.deletedText || '')).join('\n');
+  const checks = [
+    ['has tracked changes', meta.hasTrackChanges === true],
+    [`>=3 revision paragraphs (got ${meta.revisionParagraphs})`, (meta.revisionParagraphs || 0) >= 3],
+    ['title now says "Calibre Pro"', /Calibre Pro/.test(allText)],
+    ['"strikethrough" present', /strikethrough/i.test(allText)],
+  ];
+  log('[verify] meta=' + JSON.stringify(meta));
+  let passed = 0;
+  for (const [label, ok] of checks) { log(`   ${ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${label}`); if (ok) passed++; }
+  log(`[result] ${passed}/${checks.length} verifications passed; ${approvals} changes approved.`);
+  log(`[artifacts] screenshots + log in ${SHOTS}`);
+  process.exit(passed === checks.length ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { log('FATAL ' + (e?.stack || e)); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * LIVE edge-case hunt — drives the REAL code_boss UI against REAL Claude (Sonnet)
+ * via the local Anthropic proxy, targeting the RECENT changes:

+async function main() {

+  catch (e) { console.error('PROXY NOT REACHABLE at ' + PROXY + ' — start it and retry. (' + (e?.message || e) + ')'); process.exit(2); }

+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: () => (PROXY_API_KEY || 'x'), extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS, onRateLimit: (ms) => console.error(`  [rate-limit] ${ms}ms`) });
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace(
+    '</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: MODEL, baseURL: PROXY }) + ';</script></head>'

+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  await page.setViewport({ width: 1320, height: 1000 });
+  page.on('pageerror', (e) => jsErrors.push(e.message));
+  page.on('console', (m) => { if (m.type() === 'error' && !/Failed to load resource/i.test(m.text())) jsErrors.push('console: ' + m.text()); });
+
+  const getStatus = () => page.evaluate(() => window.__sessionRef?.status);
+  async function waitDone(ms = 240000) {
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__sessionRef?.status === 'running', { timeout: 25000 }).catch(() => {});
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) {
+      const s = await getStatus();
+      if (s === 'done' || s === 'idle' || s === 'error') return s;
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));

+  const findings = [];
+  const note = (sev, msg) => { findings.push({ sev, msg }); console.log(`    [${sev}] ${msg}`); };
+
+  async function scenario(name, fn) {

+    records.push({ scenario: name, ok, info, durationMs: Date.now() - t0 });

+

+    await scenario('wrap-structure-and-badges', async () => {

+      const s = await waitDone();
+      if (s === 'TIMEOUT') throw new Error('never reached terminal');

+      const r = await page.evaluate(() => {

+        if (!wrap) return { err: 'no .task-wrap' };
+        const head = wrap.querySelector('.task-head');
+        const foot = wrap.querySelector('.task-foot');

+        const sum = wrap.querySelector('.task-foot-summary');
+        const chev = wrap.querySelector('.task-foot-toggle');
+        const commit = wrap.querySelector('.task-foot-commit');
+        const review = wrap.querySelector('.task-foot-review');

+        const color = (el) => el ? getComputedStyle(el).color : '';
+        return {
+          collapsed: wrap.classList.contains('collapsed'),
+          headDisplay: disp(head),
+          summaryDisplay: disp(sum),
+          bodyDisplay: disp(body),
+          footDisplay: disp(foot),
+          hasChevron: !!chev,
+          commitText: commit?.textContent || '', commitColor: color(commit),
+          reviewText: review?.textContent || '', reviewColor: color(review),
+        };
+      });
+      if (r.err) throw new Error(r.err);
+      const GREEN = /108,\s*196,\s*108/;   // old #6cc46c
+      if (r.collapsed) note('MED', 'wrap is collapsed by default (should be expanded)');
+      if (r.headDisplay !== 'none') note('HIGH', `header VISIBLE when expanded (display=${r.headDisplay}) — should be hidden`);
+      if (r.summaryDisplay !== 'none') note('HIGH', `summary VISIBLE when expanded (display=${r.summaryDisplay}) — should be hidden`);
+      if (r.bodyDisplay === 'none') note('HIGH', 'body hidden when expanded — should be visible');
+      if (r.hasChevron) note('MED', 'leaf wrap still shows a chevron — expected none (collapse is via clicking the foot now)');
+      if (r.commitText && GREEN.test(r.commitColor)) note('MED', `commit badge still GREEN (${r.commitColor}) — should be muted`);
+      if (r.reviewText && GREEN.test(r.reviewColor)) note('MED', `review badge still GREEN (${r.reviewColor}) — should be muted`);
+      if (!r.commitText) note('MED', 'no ✓ committed badge (auto-commit may not have fired in the git repo)');
+      return `collapsed=${r.collapsed} head=${r.headDisplay} summary=${r.summaryDisplay} body=${r.bodyDisplay} chevron=${r.hasChevron} commit="${r.commitText}"(${r.commitColor}) review="${r.reviewText}"(${r.reviewColor})`;
+    });
+
+    // ── 2) Collapse via foot click + persistence across in-session re-render ─────
+    await scenario('collapse-toggle-and-persistence', async () => {
+      const before = await page.$('.task-wrap .task-foot');
+      if (!before) throw new Error('no task foot to click');
+      await page.click('.task-wrap .task-foot');
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 200));
+      const afterClick = await page.evaluate(() => {
+        const w = document.querySelector('.task-wrap');
+        const head = w.querySelector('.task-head'); const body = w.querySelector('.task-body'); const sum = w.querySelector('.task-foot-summary');
+        return { collapsed: w.classList.contains('collapsed'), headDisplay: getComputedStyle(head).display, bodyDisplay: getComputedStyle(body).display, summaryDisplay: sum ? getComputedStyle(sum).display : 'absent' };
+      });
+      if (!afterClick.collapsed) note('HIGH', 'foot click did NOT collapse the wrap');
+      if (afterClick.headDisplay === 'none') note('HIGH', 'header still hidden when collapsed — should show');
+      if (afterClick.bodyDisplay !== 'none') note('HIGH', 'body still visible when collapsed — should hide');
+      if (afterClick.summaryDisplay === 'none') note('MED', 'summary still hidden when collapsed — should show');
+      // Force the in-session full re-render (clearLog + rebuild) the _taskCollapsed map must survive.

+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 200));
+      const afterRerender = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector('.task-wrap')?.classList.contains('collapsed'));
+      if (!afterRerender) note('HIGH', 'collapse state LOST after in-session re-render (_taskCollapsed not honored)');
+      // Expand again via the collapsed header.
+      await page.click('.task-wrap .task-head').catch(() => {});
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 150));
+      const reExpanded = await page.evaluate(() => !document.querySelector('.task-wrap')?.classList.contains('collapsed'));
+      if (!reExpanded) note('MED', 'could not re-expand via the header after collapse');

+    });
+
+    // ── 3) Markdown-in-backticks rendered from REAL model output ──────────────
+    await scenario('markdown-in-backticks', async () => {
+      await sendChat('Without using any tools, reply with EXACTLY this, including the backticks: an inline code span `# TODO: fix this` and then a fenced code block containing three lines: `# a comment`, `- not a bullet`, and `a | b | c`. Use a ``` fenced block for the three lines.');
+      const s = await waitDone();
+      if (s === 'TIMEOUT') throw new Error('never reached terminal');
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 400));
+      const r = await page.evaluate(() => {
+        const log = document.getElementById('log');
+        const html = log ? log.innerHTML : '';
+        // Any heading/list/table whose text looks like it came from CODE content?
+        const headings = Array.from(log.querySelectorAll('h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6')).map((e) => e.textContent.trim());
+        const badHeading = headings.find((t) => /TODO|a comment/i.test(t));
+        const codeEls = Array.from(log.querySelectorAll('code,pre')).map((e) => e.textContent);
+        const hashLiteralInCode = codeEls.some((t) => /#/.test(t));
+        const pipeLiteralInCode = codeEls.some((t) => /\|/.test(t));
+        const hasList = !!log.querySelector('ul.md li');
+        const listText = Array.from(log.querySelectorAll('ul.md li')).map((e) => e.textContent.trim());
+        const badList = listText.find((t) => /not a bullet/i.test(t));
+        const hasTable = !!log.querySelector('table.md');
+        return { badHeading: badHeading || null, hashLiteralInCode, pipeLiteralInCode, badList: badList || null, hasTable, codeCount: codeEls.length };
+      });
+      if (r.badHeading) note('HIGH', `# inside backticks rendered as a HEADING: "${r.badHeading}"`);
+      if (r.badList) note('HIGH', `- inside backticks rendered as a LIST item: "${r.badList}"`);
+      if (r.codeCount === 0) note('LOW', 'model produced no code spans/blocks — rendering not exercised (model content)');
+      else if (!r.hashLiteralInCode) note('LOW', 'no literal # found inside code (model may have phrased differently)');
+      return `codeEls=${r.codeCount} hashLiteral=${r.hashLiteralInCode} pipeLiteral=${r.pipeLiteralInCode} badHeading=${r.badHeading} badList=${r.badList} st6rayTable=${r.hasTable}`;
+    });
+
+    // ── 4) Cancel via Escape on a long powershell ────────────────────────────
+    await scenario('escape-cancels-long-powershell', async () => {
+      await sendChat('Use your powershell tool to run exactly: Start-Sleep -Seconds 60. Then tell me it finished. Run it now.');
+      // Wait for the run to start AND a tool-call (the sleep) to appear.
+      await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__sessionRef?.status === 'running', { timeout: 25000 }).catch(() => {});
+      const sawTool = await page.waitForFunction(
+        () => (window.__sessionRef?.events || []).some((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && /powershell|sleep/i.test((e.verb || '') + ' ' + JSON.stringify(e.args || {}))),
+        { timeout: 60000 }
+      ).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+      if (!sawTool) return 'model did not start a powershell sleep within 60s — recorded (model content)';
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 800));
+      const t0 = Date.now();
+      await page.keyboard.press('Escape');     // cancel
+      // Wait for terminal — should be FAST (<15s), not the full 60s sleep.
+      let s = 'TIMEOUT';
+      while (Date.now() - t0 < 30000) { const cur = await getStatus(); if (cur === 'error' || cur === 'idle' || cur === 'done') { s = cur; break; } await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300)); }
+      const ms = Date.now() - t0;
+      if (s === 'TIMEOUT') note('HIGH', 'Escape did NOT cancel the powershell within 30s (cancel broken)');
+      else if (ms > 20000) note('MED', `cancel took ${ms}ms (slow — expected well under the 60s sleep but should be quick)`);
+      return `afterEscape status=${s} in ${ms}ms (expected fast, NOT ~60s)`;
+    });
+
+    // ── 5) Queue while idle ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    await scenario('queue-while-idle', async () => {
+      // Ensure idle.
+      await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__sessionRef?.status !== 'running', { timeout: 30000 }).catch(() => {});
+      const btnVisible = await page.evaluate(() => { const b = document.getElementById('queueBtn'); return b && getComputedStyle(b).display !== 'none'; });
+      if (!btnVisible) note('MED', 'queue button hidden while idle (queue-when-idle regressed)');
+      await page.evaluate(() => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = ''; i.focus(); });
+      await page.type('#input', 'first queued draft while idle');
+      await page.click('#queueBtn').catch(() => {});
+      await page.type('#input', 'second queued draft while idle');
+      await page.click('#queueBtn').catch(() => {});
+      const items = await page.$$eval('#queueStack .queue-item .queue-preview', (els) => els.map((e) => e.textContent));
+      if (items.length !== 2) note('MED', `expected 2 queued items while idle, got ${items.length}`);
+      const playEnabled = await page.evaluate(() => { const p = document.querySelector('#queueStack .queue-play'); return p && !p.disabled; });
+      if (!playEnabled) note('MED', 'play button disabled while idle — cannot fire a queued item');
+      return `queueBtnVisible=${btnVisible} queued=${items.length} playEnabled=${playEnabled}`;
+    });
+
+  } finally {
+    const full = await page.evaluate(() => window.__sessionRef ?? {}).catch(() => ({}));
+    const diags = (full.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'manager-diagnostic');
+    console.log('\n\n════════ EDGE-CASE REPORT ════════');
+    for (const r of records) console.log(`  [${r.ok ? 'OK ' : 'FAIL'}] ${r.scenario} (${Math.round(r.durationMs / 1000)}s) ${r.info}`);
+    console.log(`\n  FINDINGS (${findings.length}):`);
+    for (const f of findings) console.log(`    [${f.sev}] ${f.msg}`);
+    console.log(`\n  JS errors: ${jsErrors.length}`);
+    for (const e of jsErrors.slice(0, 12)) console.log('    - ' + e.slice(0, 200));
+    console.log(`\n  manager-diagnostics: ${diags.length}`);
+    for (const d of diags) console.log(`    [${d.kind}] ${d.reason}`);
+    console.log('\n  screenshots: ' + SHOTS);
+    await browser.close();
+    server.close();
+    await rm(project, { recursive: true, force: true });
+    const high = findings.filter((f) => f.sev === 'HIGH').length;
+    console.log(`\n════════ ${jsErrors.length || high ? (high + ' HIGH findings, ' + jsErrors.length + ' JS errors') : 'no HIGH findings, no JS errors'} ════════`);
+    process.exit(0);   // exit 0 — this is a hunt; findings are the output, not a pass/fail gate
+  }
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * LIVE edge-case hunt, round 2 (real Sonnet via the proxy) — nastier cases:
+ *   - HTML/XSS escaping in the rewritten renderInline (user + code spans)
+ *   - multi-task run → several stacked wraps, each with its own badges
+ *   - a REAL markdown table renders as <table>, but a table INSIDE a ``` fence
+ *     stays literal
+ *   - editing a task summary in the COLLAPSED view (the only place it's visible)
+ *     saves + keeps collapse across a re-render
+ *
+ * Run: node test/live-edgecases2.mjs
+ */

+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 28956;

+const MODEL = LIVE_MODEL;
+const SHOTS = path.join(__dirname, 'live-edgecases2-screenshots');

+    'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });

+  const getStatus = () => page.evaluate(() => window.__sessionRef?.status);
+  async function waitDone(ms = 240000) {

+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { const s = await getStatus(); if (s === 'done' || s === 'idle' || s === 'error') return s; await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500)); }
+    return 'TIMEOUT';
+  }
+  async function sendChat(text) {

+        sum.click();
+        return true;
+      });

+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 150));
+      // Type into the edit input and save with Enter.
+      const typed = await page.evaluate(() => {
+        const wraps = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.task-wrap')).filter((w) => !w.classList.contains('project'));
+        const w = wraps[wraps.length - 1];
+        const input = w.querySelector('.task-foot input, .task-foot textarea, .task-foot-edit');
+        if (!input) return false;

+        input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
+        const ev = new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Enter', bubbles: true });
+        input.dispatchEvent(ev);
+        return true;
+      });
+      if (!typed) { note('MED', 'no edit input appeared after clicking the summary'); return 'no edit input'; }
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 400));
+      // Force a re-render and confirm: still collapsed + summary updated.
+      const after = await page.evaluate(() => {
+        if (window.__sessionRef) renderSessionInChat(window.__sessionRef);
+        const wraps = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.task-wrap')).filter((w) => !w.classList.contains('project'));
+        const w = wraps[wraps.length - 1];
+        return { collapsed: w.classList.contains('collapsed'), summary: (w.querySelector('.task-foot-summary')?.textContent || '') };
+      });
+      if (!after.collapsed) note('MED', 'wrap lost its collapsed state after editing the summary + re-render');
+      if (!/EDITED SUMMARY/.test(after.summary)) note('MED', `summary edit did not persist (got: "${after.summary.slice(0, 50)}")`);

+    for (const r of records) console.log(`  [${r.ok ? 'OK ' : 'FAIL'}] ${r.scenario} (${Math.round(r.durationMs / 1000)}s) ${r.info}`);

+    for (const e of jsErrors.slice(0, 12)) console.log('    - ' + e.slice(0, 200));
+    console.log(`\n  manager-diagnostics: ${diags.length}`);
+    for (const d of diags) console.log(`    [${d.kind}] ${d.reason}`);
+    console.log('\n  screenshots: ' + SHOTS);
+    await browser.close(); server.close(); await rm(project, { recursive: true, force: true });
+    const high = findings.filter((f) => f.sev === 'HIGH').length;
+    console.log(`\n════════ ${jsErrors.length || high ? (high + ' HIGH, ' + jsErrors.length + ' JS errors') : 'no HIGH findings, no JS errors'} ════════`);
+    process.exit(0);
+  }

+        pulsingNoBg: [...document.querySelectorAll('.js-group > .js-summary.tool-running')].filter((s) => !s.querySelector('.js-bg-tag')).length,
+        stuckRunning: [...document.querySelectorAll('.row-result.running')].filter((r) => !document.querySelector(`.row-result.bg-note[data-call-id="${CSS.escape(r.dataset.callId || '')}"]`)).length,
+        streaming: document.querySelectorAll('[data-streaming]').length,
+        delim: [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .row:not(.user)')].some((r) => /^\s*\/?---\s*(you|developer)\b[^\n]*---\s*$/m.test(r.innerText)),
+        over: document.documentElement.scrollWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth,
+        bars: [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .tbar:not(.live)')].map((e) => [Number(e.dataset.a), Number(e.dataset.b)]),
+        liveBars: document.querySelectorAll('#log .tbar.live').length,
+        tbOn: tbOn(),
+      }));
+      ok(d.pulsingNoBg === 0 && d.stuckRunning === 0 && d.streaming === 0, `[${m.id}] nothing left running (${d.pulsingNoBg} lines, ${d.stuckRunning} rows, ${d.streaming} streams)`);
+      ok(!d.delim, `[${m.id}] no turn delimiter in the agent's rows`);
+      ok(d.over <= 0, `[${m.id}] no sideways scroll (${d.over})`);
+      const serverCalls = (await llmCalls()).length;
+      let overlap = 0; for (let i = 1; i < d.bars.length; i++) if (d.bars[i][0] < d.bars[i - 1][1] - 1) overlap++;
+      ok(d.tbOn && d.bars.length > 0 && overlap === 0, `[${m.id}] time bars: ${d.bars.length} in the chat, none overlapping (${overlap}); ${serverCalls} model calls recorded`);
+      // a live lane is right while its call is still in flight on the server - the commit-recs pass runs after a turn,
+      // and this proxy took up to 24s to first token for it (build 1974's run flagged exactly that as "stuck")
+      const inFlight = (await llmCalls()).filter((c) => c.endedAt == null).length;
+      ok(d.liveBars === 0 || (await bgSnippetsAlive()).length > 0 || (await status()) === 'running', `[${m.id}] time bars: no bar left running once idle (${d.liveBars})`);
+      if (calls.some((c) => (c.chunks || []).length)) {
+        const raw = await page.evaluate(async () => { const seg = [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .tbar .tb-stream[data-call]')].find((r) => (_tbCalls.get(r.dataset.call) || {}).rawId); if (!seg) return null; seg.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('click', { bubbles: true })); for (let i = 0; i < 40 && !/Request/.test(document.getElementById('rawLogBody').textContent); i++) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100)); const t = document.getElementById('rawLogBody').textContent; closeRawLog(); return t; });
+        ok(raw && /Request/.test(raw), `[${m.id}] time bars: a model segment opens that call's request and response (${raw ? raw.length : 0} chars)`);
+      }
+      if (!reply && !m.esc) note(`[${m.id}] no written reply`);
+      if (reveals.length) { const seen = [...new Set(reveals)]; reveals.length = 0; note(`[${m.id}] the agent asked to see masked values (answered "Just this once"): ${seen.join(' // ')}`); }
+      if (nudges.length) note(`[${m.id}] nudges: ${nudges.map((n) => n.slice(0, 100)).join(' // ')}`);
+      if (calls.some((c) => c.error)) note(`[${m.id}] model call error(s): ${calls.filter((c) => c.error).map((c) => c.error.slice(0, 100)).join(' // ')}`);
+      if (ttft.some((x) => x > 20000)) note(`[${m.id}] slow first token: ${ttft.map((x) => (x / 1000).toFixed(1)).join(', ')}s`);
+
+      // per-message expectations about the product
+      if (m.steer) ok(turn.some((e) => e.type === 'user-message' && e.content === m.steer.text) || evs.some((e) => e.type === 'user-message' && e.content === m.steer.text), `[${m.id}] the steer reached the conversation`);
+      if (m.id === 'slow') ok(pressed && results.some((r) => /BACKGROUNDED/.test(r.content || '')), `[slow] ⇱ backgrounded the snippet (pressed ${pressed})`);
+      if (m.id === 'meanwhile') { const all = await settled(180000); ok(all && (await allEvents()).some((e) => e.type === 'snippet-job-done'), '[meanwhile] the backgrounded check finished and was delivered'); }
+      if (m.id === 'esc') ok((await status()) !== 'running', '[esc] Esc stopped the run');
+      if (m.escEarly && !escLate) {
+        const n = (await allEvents()).filter((e) => e.type === 'user-message' && String(e.content || '').startsWith(m.text)).length;
+        const nb = await page.evaluate((t) => [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .bubble.user')].filter((b) => b.innerText.includes(t)).length, m.text);
+        ok(n === 1 && nb === 1, `[${m.id}] after resending, exactly one copy (${n} in the conversation, ${nb} on screen)`);
+        ok(!!reply, `[${m.id}] and it was answered`);
+      }
+      if (m.id === 'plugin' || m.id === 'plugin-2') {
+        // every step that installed a plugin carries its load report; a failure must have been seen AND followed by a load
+        const steps = turn.filter((e) => e.type === 'js-step' && typeof e.plugin === 'string');
+        const failed = steps.filter((e) => /FAILED TO LOAD/.test(e.plugin));
+        const loaded = steps.filter((e) => /loaded:/.test(e.plugin));
+        note(`[${m.id}] plugin installs: ${steps.length} (${loaded.length} loaded, ${failed.length} failed): ${steps.map((e) => e.plugin.slice(0, 90)).join(' // ')}`);
+        ok(steps.length > 0, `[${m.id}] the install reported whether the plugin loaded`);
+        ok(loaded.length > 0 && (!failed.length || turn.indexOf(loaded[loaded.length - 1]) > turn.indexOf(failed[failed.length - 1])), `[${m.id}] it ended LOADED (a failed load, if any, was fixed after)`);
+        const want = m.id === 'plugin' ? /cart_functions/ : /cart_todos/;
+        ok(want.test(loaded.map((e) => e.plugin).join(' ')), `[${m.id}] with the tool asked for`);
+        const called = turn.some((e) => e.type === 'js-step' && want.test(String(e.fn || '')) && e.status === 'ok');
+        ok(called, `[${m.id}] and the agent called it`);
+        const rowShown = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('#log .js-step-plugin').length);
+        ok(rowShown > 0, `[${m.id}] the developer sees the load result under the write row (${rowShown})`);
+      }
+      if (m.id === 'reload') ok(evs.slice(before).some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text'), '[reload] the run finished and its reply is there after the reload');
+      if (['fix', 'feature', 'hunt-fix', 'rename', 'tests2'].includes(m.id)) {
+        let passes = false; try { passes = /all tests passed/.test(execFileSync('node', ['test.js'], { cwd: project, encoding: 'utf8' })); } catch {}
+        ok(passes, `[${m.id}] the project's tests pass`);
+      }
+    }
+    await settled(180000);
+    ok((await bgSnippetsAlive()).length === 0, 'no background snippet left running at the end');
+    ok(pageErrors.length === 0, `no page errors${pageErrors.length ? ': ' + pageErrors.slice(0, 3).join(' // ') : ''}`);
+    // the session in numbers
+    const all = transcript.flatMap((t) => t.llmCalls);
+    const tt = all.map((c) => c.ttft).filter((x) => x != null).sort((a, b) => a - b);
+    const q = (p) => tt.length ? tt[Math.min(tt.length - 1, Math.floor(p * tt.length))].toFixed(1) + 's' : '-';
+    note(`model calls: ${all.length}; first token median ${q(0.5)}, p90 ${q(0.9)}, max ${q(1)}; errors ${all.filter((c) => c.error).length}`);
+    const sn = transcript.flatMap((t) => t.snippets);
+    note(`snippets: ${sn.length}; avg ${(sn.reduce((s, x) => s + x.code.split('\n').length, 0) / (sn.length || 1)).toFixed(1)} lines; mode=read ${sn.filter((x) => x.mode === 'read').length}; background ${sn.filter((x) => x.background).length}`);
+  } catch (e) { ok(false, 'harness: ' + (e?.stack || e)); }
+  finally {
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'transcript.json'), JSON.stringify({ model: MODEL, transcript }, null, 2));
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'transcript.md'), transcript.map((t) => `## ${t.id} — ${t.secs}s · ${t.llmCalls.length} model calls\n**Developer:** ${t.ask}\n\n${t.snippets.map((s) => '```js' + (s.mode ? ' mode=' + s.mode : '') + (s.background ? ' background' : '') + '\n' + s.code + '\n```\n→ ' + s.result.replace(/\n/g, '\n  ')).join('\n\n')}\n\n${t.nudges.length ? '**Nudges:** ' + t.nudges.join(' // ') + '\n\n' : ''}**Agent:**\n${t.reply}\n`).join('\n---\n\n'));
+    try { const { reapAllBackground } = await import('../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs'); reapAllBackground(); } catch {}
+    await browser.close().catch(() => {});
+    await new Promise((r) => server.close(r));
+    await rm(project, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed — transcript + screenshots in test/live-general-out/`);
+  if (findings.length) console.log('\nFINDINGS:\n- ' + findings.join('\n- '));
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

+/**
+ * LIVE: /help and /demo with the REAL model, through the real UI (the developer, 2026-10-29).

+/**
+ * LIVE INTEGRATION HUNT (Puppeteer + the REAL model via the proxy): many features and plugins working together in

+    { verb: 'hunt_peek', name: 'hunt_peek', readOnly: true, schema: { description: 'A harmless read-only peek.', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: {} } }, impl: async () => ({ content: 'PEEK_OK' }) },

+/**
+ * LIVE Java/Spring development benchmark (real model + real tests). For each curated business
+ * method in spring-ddd-bank: reset to baseline → STUB the method (compiles, fails tests) → confirm
+ * the stub breaks tests → drive code_boss to implement it → GRADE by running the real test suite →
+ * capture success / tokens / wall-time → reset. The OBJECTIVE measure of "how good is it at real
+ * Java dev", and the A/B harness for CB_PARALLEL_CRITIC.
+ *
+ *   CB_PARALLEL_CRITIC=1  node test/live-java-benchmark.mjs        # critic ON
+ *   node test/live-java-benchmark.mjs                              # critic OFF (baseline)
+ * env: CB_BENCH_TASKS (limit, default all) · CB_MODEL (default sonnet) · CB_PROXY · CB_BENCH_PROJECT
+ * `live-` prefix → run-all skips it. Skips (exit 0) if the proxy/JDK/project are unavailable.
+ */

+// ── STUB: replace a Java method's body with a throwing stub (compiles for any signature, fails any
+// test that exercises it). Brace-matches the body, skipping strings + comments. ──
+function stubJavaMethod(src, methodName) {
+  const sig = new RegExp(`\\b(?:public|private|protected)[^\\n;{}]*\\b${methodName}\\s*\\(`);
+  const m = sig.exec(src);
+  if (!m) return null;
+  // walk past the param list to its closing ')'
+  let i = src.indexOf('(', m.index); let depth = 0;
+  for (; i < src.length; i++) { if (src[i] === '(') depth++; else if (src[i] === ')') { if (--depth === 0) { i++; break; } } }
+  while (i < src.length && src[i] !== '{') { if (src[i] === ';') return null; i++; }   // ';' → abstract/interface, skip
+  if (src[i] !== '{') return null;
+  const bodyOpen = i; i++; depth = 1;
+  let inStr = null, inLine = false, inBlock = false;
+  for (; i < src.length && depth > 0; i++) {
+    const c = src[i], n = src[i + 1];
+    if (inLine) { if (c === '\n') inLine = false; continue; }
+    if (inBlock) { if (c === '*' && n === '/') { inBlock = false; i++; } continue; }
+    if (inStr) { if (c === '\\') i++; else if (c === inStr) inStr = null; continue; }
+    if (c === '/' && n === '/') { inLine = true; i++; continue; }
+    if (c === '/' && n === '*') { inBlock = true; i++; continue; }
+    if (c === '"' || c === "'") { inStr = c; continue; }
+    if (c === '{') depth++; else if (c === '}') depth--;
+  }
+  if (depth !== 0) return null;
+  const stub = '{\n        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("stub \\u2014 implement me");\n    }';
+  return src.slice(0, bodyOpen) + stub + src.slice(i);
+}
+
+// ── run `mvnw test` and parse the surefire totals. ──
+function mvnTest() {
+  return new Promise((resolve) => {
+    let out = '';
+    // cmd.exe won't resolve a bare relative "mvnw.cmd"; use the absolute path (quoted), via shell:true
+    // so the shell does the quoting (Node's manual cmd-arg quoting mangles a spaced quoted string).
+    // cwd=PROJECT so the wrapper builds the right project.
+    const mvnw = path.join(PROJECT, process.platform === 'win32' ? 'mvnw.cmd' : 'mvnw');
+    const child = spawn(`"${mvnw}" -B test`, { cwd: PROJECT, env: { ...process.env, JAVA_HOME }, shell: true, windowsHide: true });
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => { try { child.kill(); } catch {} }, 600000);
+    child.stdout.on('data', (d) => { out += d; });
+    child.stderr.on('data', (d) => { out += d; });
+    child.on('close', (code) => {
+      clearTimeout(timer);
+      const compileErr = /COMPILATION ERROR|BUILD FAILURE/.test(out) && !/Tests run:/.test(out);
+      // last "Tests run: T, Failures: F, Errors: E" wins (the aggregate)
+      let pass = null, fail = 0, err = 0; let mm;
+      const re = /Tests run: (\d+), Failures: (\d+), Errors: (\d+)/g;
+      while ((mm = re.exec(out))) { pass = +mm[1]; fail = +mm[2]; err = +mm[3]; }
+      resolve({ exitCode: code, compileErr, total: pass, fail, err, green: code === 0 && fail === 0 && err === 0 && !compileErr });
+    });
+    child.on('error', () => { clearTimeout(timer); resolve({ exitCode: -1, compileErr: false, total: null, fail: 0, err: 0, green: false, spawnError: true }); });
+  });
+}

+async function main() {
+  // CF Access headers (home proxy) from env — passed to the health probe + the adapter.

+  // _adapterOpts.onUsage (the ONLY feeder of session.totalTokens / chatTokens) is bypassed —
+  // so /api/state.tokens read ~0 in earlier runs. Hook onUsage on OUR adapter instead: it
+  // fires for EVERY LLM call through it (main agent turns, reviewer, deep_think proposers/
+  // judge, sub-agents), giving true cumulative usage. u is OpenAI-shaped (mapUsage).
+  let llmTokens = 0, llmCalls = 0;
+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: () => 'x', extraHeaders: eh, onUsage: (u) => {
+    llmTokens += u?.total_tokens ?? ((u?.prompt_tokens || 0) + (u?.completion_tokens || 0));
+    llmCalls++;

+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' }, defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: PROJECT, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic' });
+  const api = (p, opts) => fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}${p}`, opts).then((r) => r.json());
+  const state = () => api('/api/state');
+  const waitIdle = async (ms) => { const t0 = Date.now(); await sleep(1500); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { const s = await state(); if (s.status === 'idle' || s.status === 'done' || s.status === 'error') return s; await sleep(2000); } return null; };
+
+  const store = await loadResults();
+  const results = [];
+  try {
+    await sleep(1000);
+    for (const task of tasks) {
+      const label = task.label;
+      // RESUME: a task already recorded done (real run, not an infra failure) is reused, not re-run.
+      if (store.byLabel[label]?.done) { console.log(`  skip ${label} (done: ${store.byLabel[label].success ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'})`); results.push(store.byLabel[label]); continue; }
+      // reset to a clean baseline + clear the conversation
+      await runGit(PROJECT, ['reset', '--hard', BASELINE]); await runGit(PROJECT, ['clean', '-fdq']);
+      await api('/api/clear-conversation', { method: 'POST' }).catch(() => {});
+      await sleep(500);
+      // stub every method in the task (grouped by file, applied sequentially per file)
+      const byFile = {};
+      for (const st of task.stubs) (byFile[st.file] = byFile[st.file] || []).push(st.method);
+      let stubbedCount = 0;
+      for (const [file, methods] of Object.entries(byFile)) {
+        const abs = path.join(PROJECT, file);
+        let s = await readFile(abs, 'utf8');
+        for (const m of methods) { const out = stubJavaMethod(s, m); if (out && out !== s) { s = out; stubbedCount++; } else console.log(`  WARN ${label}: could not stub ${m}`); }
+        await writeFile(abs, s, 'utf8');
+      }
+      if (!stubbedCount) { console.log(`  SKIP ${label}: could not stub any method`); results.push({ label, skipped: 'stub-failed' }); continue; }
+      // confirm the stub compiles AND breaks tests (else the task is invalid)
+      const pre = await mvnTest();
+      console.log(`  [pre] ${label}: ${JSON.stringify(pre)}`);
+      if (pre.compileErr) { console.log(`  SKIP ${label}: stub did not compile`); results.push({ label, skipped: 'stub-compile' }); continue; }
+      if (pre.green || (pre.fail + pre.err) === 0) { console.log(`  SKIP ${label}: stub broke no tests (not covered)`); results.push({ label, skipped: 'no-coverage' }); continue; }
+      const brokeN = pre.fail + pre.err;
+
+      // drive code_boss to implement it
+      const t0 = Date.now();
+      const tokBefore = llmTokens, callsBefore = llmCalls;
+      await api('/api/message', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: task.prompt, model: MODEL }) });
+      const done = await waitIdle(HARD ? 2700000 : 1500000);   // hard tasks (multi-method) get up to 45 min
+      const wallMs = Date.now() - t0;
+      // grade
+      const post = await mvnTest();
+      const tokens = llmTokens - tokBefore;   // true cumulative LLM usage for this task (see onUsage above)
+      const r = { label, success: post.green, brokeN, leftFail: post.fail + post.err, compileErr: post.compileErr, tokens, llmCalls: llmCalls - callsBefore, wallMs, settled: !!done };
+      results.push(r);
+      // INFRA-FAILURE GUARD: zero LLM tokens means the proxy never served a call (auth drop / 401) —
+      // the task didn't really execute, so do NOT persist it as done; a re-invocation retries it.
+      if (tokens === 0) { console.log(`  ${label}: 0 tokens (proxy/infra failure) — NOT recording; will retry on resume`); }
+      else { store.byLabel[label] = { ...r, done: true }; await saveResults(store); }
+      console.log(`  ${post.green ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} ${label} — broke ${brokeN}, left ${post.fail + post.err}${post.compileErr ? ' (compile err)' : ''}, ${Math.round(tokens / 1000)}k tok, ${Math.round(wallMs / 1000)}s`);
+    }
+    await runGit(PROJECT, ['reset', '--hard', BASELINE]); await runGit(PROJECT, ['clean', '-fdq']);
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+  }
+  const graded = results.filter((r) => !r.skipped);
+  const passed = graded.filter((r) => r.success).length;
+  const totTok = graded.reduce((a, r) => a + (r.tokens || 0), 0);
+  console.log(`\n=== RESULT (critic ${CRITIC ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}) — ${passed}/${graded.length} passed · ${Math.round(totTok / 1000)}k tokens total · ${results.filter((r) => r.skipped).length} skipped ===`);
+  console.log(JSON.stringify({ critic: CRITIC, model: MODEL, results }, null, 1));
+  process.exit(0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * LIVE — how the agent works with builds 1963-1964, through the real UI, on the real model (sonnet via the proxy).

+ * What changed under it: it now sees every call's result (the record); a non-zero exit throws ({allowFail}); it has

+function lineTotal(item) { return item.price * item.qty; }
+function subtotal(items) { return items.reduce((s, i) => s + lineTotal(i), 0); }
+function tax(amount, rate) { return Math.round(amount * rate * 100) / 100; }
+function discount(amount, pct) { return amount - Math.floor(amount * pct) / 100; }   // TODO: pct handling looks off
+function total(items, rate, pct) { const s = discount(subtotal(items), pct || 0); return s + tax(s, rate); }
+function applyCoupon(amount, coupon) {
+  if (!coupon) return amount;
+  if (coupon.type === 'percent') return amount - amount * coupon.value / 100;
+  if (coupon.type === 'fixed') return amount - coupon.value;   // TODO: can go negative
+  return amount;

+module.exports = { lineTotal, subtotal, tax, discount, total, applyCoupon };

+      }
+      if (m.id === 'meanwhile') {
+        const q = snips.find((x) => x.queued);
+        if (q) note('[meanwhile] the agent\'s snippet was QUEUED behind the backgrounded one (it did not use mode="read")');
+        else if (snips.some((x) => x.mode === 'read')) note('[meanwhile] the agent used mode="read" and answered while the check ran');
+        const settledAll = await settled(180000);
+        ok(settledAll, '[meanwhile] everything settled, including the backgrounded check');
+        const evs2 = await allEvents();
+        ok(evs2.some((e) => e.type === 'snippet-job-done' && e.status === 'ok'), '[meanwhile] the backgrounded check finished and was delivered');
+        ok(evs2.some((e) => e.type === 'user-message' && /<background-snippet[^>]*status="finished"[\s\S]*slow check ok/.test(e.content || '')), '[meanwhile] the agent was woken with its result');
+      }
+      if (m.id === 'bg') {
+        if (!snips.some((x) => x.background)) note('[bg] the agent did NOT use background="true" for "start it in the background"');
+        const f = await readFile(path.join(project, 'test.js'), 'utf8');
+        ok(/^\s*\/\//.test(f), '[bg] the comment was added to test.js');
+      }
+      if (m.id === 'fix' || m.id === 'rename') {
+        let passes = false; try { passes = /all tests passed/.test(execFileSync('node', ['test.js'], { cwd: project, encoding: 'utf8' })); } catch {}
+        ok(passes, `[${m.id}] the project's tests pass`);
+      }
+      if (m.id === 'hunt') ok(execFileSync('git', ['diff', '--stat', 'HEAD', '--', 'cart.js'], { cwd: project, encoding: 'utf8' }).trim() === '' || true, '[hunt] (checked by reading: did it change nothing?)');
+    }
+    // SESSION-WIDE: the product
+    await settled(180000);
+    const left = await bgSnippetsAlive();
+    ok(left.length === 0, `no background snippet left running (${left.join(', ')})`);
+    const pulsing = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('.js-group > .js-summary.tool-running').length);
+    ok(pulsing === 0, `no snippet line left pulsing (${pulsing})`);
+    ok(pageErrors.length === 0, `no page errors${pageErrors.length ? ': ' + pageErrors.slice(0, 3).join(' // ') : ''}`);
+    // SESSION-WIDE: the agent's habits
+    const n = allSnips.length || 1;
+    const avg = (f) => Math.round(allSnips.reduce((s, x) => s + f(x), 0) / n * 10) / 10;
+    note(`snippets: ${allSnips.length}; avg ${avg((x) => x.lines)} lines, ${avg((x) => x.calls)} calls; max ${Math.max(0, ...allSnips.map((x) => x.lines))} lines`);
+    note(`modes: read ${allSnips.filter((x) => x.mode === 'read').length}, write(default/explicit) ${allSnips.filter((x) => x.mode !== 'read').length}; background ${allSnips.filter((x) => x.background).length}; queued ${allSnips.filter((x) => x.queued).length}`);
+    note(`parallel() ${allSnips.filter((x) => x.parallel).length}, Promise.all ${allSnips.filter((x) => x.promiseAll).length}, allowFail ${allSnips.filter((x) => x.allowFail).length}; failed snippets ${allSnips.filter((x) => x.status === 'error').length}`);
+  } catch (e) { ok(false, 'harness: ' + (e?.stack || e)); }
+  finally {
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'transcript.json'), JSON.stringify({ model: MODEL, transcript, snippets: allSnips }, null, 2));
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'transcript.md'), transcript.map((t) => `## ${t.id} — ${t.secs}s${t.pressedAt != null ? ` (⇱ pressed at ${t.pressedAt}s)` : ''}\n**Developer:** ${t.ask}\n\n${t.snippets.map((s) => '```js' + (s.attrs.mode ? ` mode=${s.attrs.mode}` : '') + (s.attrs.background ? ' background' : '') + '\n' + s.code + '\n```\n→ ' + s.result.replace(/\n/g, '\n  ')).join('\n\n')}\n\n${t.wakes.length ? '**Woken with:** ' + t.wakes.join('\n\n') + '\n\n' : ''}${t.nudges.length ? '**Nudges:** ' + t.nudges.join(' // ') + '\n\n' : ''}**Agent:**\n${t.reply}\n`).join('\n---\n\n'));
+    try { const { reapAllBackground } = await import('../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs'); reapAllBackground(); } catch {}
+    await browser.close().catch(() => {});
+    await new Promise((r) => server.close(r));
+    await rm(project, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed — transcript + screenshots in test/live-jobs-out/`);
+  if (findings.length) console.log('\nFINDINGS:\n- ' + findings.join('\n- '));
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();

=== add test/live-longsession.mjs
new 0596c9094a6e1524f534eeb3
+/**
+ * LIVE LONG SESSION — one developer, one project, many turns, until the context fills and compaction happens.
+ *
+ * Not an assertion suite and not a compaction test. It is a developer sitting down with code_boss and building
+ * something over a long afternoon, poking at different parts of the product as they go, writing down anything
+ * that looks wrong. Compaction is in scope because a long conversation eventually crosses the 200k ceiling — not
+ * because it is the target. The value is VOLUME: rare bugs surface from sustained real use, not from a fixture.
+ *
+ * The developer acts like one (the standing rule): answers when asked, approves when asked, changes their mind
+ * mid-task, references things decided twenty turns ago, and interrupts when a run goes on too long.
+ *
+ * After every turn it records the new chat rows, screenshots, times the turn, and runs an ANOMALY SCAN — leaked
+ * signal tags, forged developer turns, duplicated bubbles, empty answers, page errors, a status that never
+ * settles. Findings accumulate into a numbered list in report.md. Nothing "passes"; the output is a report.
+ *
+ * It also USES the product's own instruments as it goes: every few turns it opens /snapshot and reads the turn
+ * anatomy (#74), the cache/idle buckets (#75) and the stream timeline (#69), so a slow turn is diagnosed while
+ * the evidence is fresh rather than reconstructed afterwards.
+ *
+ *   node test/live-longsession.mjs
+ *
+ * env: CB_LS_PORT (28996) · CB_MODEL · CB_LS_MAX_MIN (total wall budget, default 150)
+ *      CB_LS_TARGET_TOKENS (stop after compaction is seen OR this much context, default 200000)
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import { runGit, commitAll } from './_git.mjs';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+import { PROXY_API_KEY, PROXY_BASE, PROXY_HEADERS } from './_proxy-creds.mjs';
+import { streamDiagSnapshot } from '../src/agent/turn-stream.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const OUT = path.join(__dirname, 'live-longsession-out');
+const PORT = Number(process.env.CB_LS_PORT || 28996);
+const PROXY = PROXY_BASE || process.env.CB_PROXY || 'https://home-proxy.philhignight.com';
+const MODEL = process.env.CB_MODEL || LIVE_MODEL;
+const MAX_MS = Number(process.env.CB_LS_MAX_MIN || 150) * 60000;
+const TARGET_TOKENS = Number(process.env.CB_LS_TARGET_TOKENS || 200000);
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const SEP = String.fromCharCode(92);
+const chrome = [
+  'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '').split(SEP).join('/') + '/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  'C:/Program Files (x86)/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });

+const findings = [];
+const seenFinding = new Set();
+const log = (s) => console.log(s);
+function note(severity, title, detail) {
+  // A long run repeats itself; the same defect logged forty times buries the other thirty-nine. Dedupe on the
+  // title, keep the first detail, and count the rest.
+  const hit = findings.find((f) => f.title === title);
+  if (hit) { hit.count++; return; }
+  findings.push({ severity, title, detail, count: 1, at: new Date().toISOString().slice(11, 19) });
+  console.log(`  [${severity}] ${title}`);
+}
+
+const T0 = Date.now();
+const budgetLeft = () => MAX_MS - (Date.now() - T0);
