+// ── the developer's eyes ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+async function readChat(page) {
+  return page.evaluate(() => [...document.querySelectorAll('#log > *')].map((el) => {
+    const cls = el.className || '';
+    const kind = /bubble user/.test(cls) ? 'developer'
+      : /row assistant|bubble assistant/.test(cls) ? 'agent'
+      : /row-result/.test(cls) ? 'result'
+      : /row tool/.test(cls) ? 'tool'
+      : /status/.test(cls) ? 'status' : 'other';
+    return { kind, cls, text: (el.innerText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 700) };
+  }));
+}
+let _seen = 0;
+async function newRows(page) {
+  const all = await readChat(page);
+  const fresh = all.slice(_seen);
+  _seen = all.length;
+  return fresh;
+}
+
+/**
+ * READ THE TURN THE WAY A PERSON WOULD. The lesson from the previous hunt was that a scanner reporting "0
+ * findings" while three real bugs sat in the transcript is worse than no scanner: it certifies. So this looks
+ * for things that are wrong ON THE SCREEN, not for internal state.
+ */
+function scanTurn(step, rows, pageErrors) {
+  const agent = rows.filter((r) => r.kind === 'agent');
+  const all = rows.map((r) => r.text).join('\n');
+
+  if (pageErrors.length) note('HIGH', 'a JavaScript error reached the page', `${step}: ${pageErrors.slice(0, 3).join(' | ')}`);
+
+  // Signal tags are ours; the developer must never see one. But NOT every occurrence of one of our names is a
+  // leak: one turn deliberately asks for an XML export containing <phase> and <plan>, so the agent writing them
+  // and then talking about them is the product working. The first run flagged exactly that as HIGH. Drop those
+  // two names on that turn, and ignore anything inside a code span either way.
+  const asked = /awkward input/.test(step);
+  const names = (asked ? [] : ['plan', 'phase'])
+    .concat(['thinking', 'checklist', 'decide', 'recall', 'completion-text', 'workflow-run', 'resolve-ticket']).join('|');
+  const leaked = all.replace(/`[^`]*`/g, ' ').match(new RegExp('</?(' + names + ')\\b[^>]*>', 'gi'));
+  if (leaked) note('HIGH', 'a signal tag leaked into the chat', `${step}: ${[...new Set(leaked)].slice(0, 5).join(' ')}`);
+  if (/<remote-workspace/i.test(all)) note('HIGH', 'a raw workspace block rendered as text', `${step}: the block markup is visible instead of a tool row`);
+  // The one that produced the 223-second turns: the model writing the developer's side of the conversation.
+  if (/---\s*(developer|you)\s+[0-9a-z]{3,8}\s*---/i.test(all)) note('HIGH', 'a forged turn heading rendered in the chat', `${step}: the model wrote a turn heading and it was displayed`);
+
+  // Two consecutive agent bubbles with the same text is the duplication reported from production.
+  for (let i = 1; i < agent.length; i++) {
+    const a = agent[i - 1].text, b = agent[i].text;
+    if (a.length > 80 && a === b) { note('HIGH', 'the same answer rendered twice in a row', `${step}: ${a.slice(0, 140)}`); break; }
+  }
+  // An agent turn that produced tool rows but no sentence leaves the developer with no idea what happened.
+  if (rows.some((r) => r.kind === 'tool') && !agent.some((r) => r.text.length > 12)) {
+    note('MED', 'a turn ran tools but said nothing', `${step}: ${rows.filter((r) => r.kind === 'tool').length} tool row(s), no prose`);
+  }
+  if (agent.some((r) => r.text.trim() === '')) note('MED', 'an empty agent bubble rendered', step);
+  // Our own error strings surfacing verbatim as an answer.
+  if (/\b(undefined|\[object Object\]|NaN)\b/.test(agent.map((r) => r.text).join(' '))) {
+    note('MED', 'a placeholder value rendered in an answer', `${step}: undefined / [object Object] / NaN in agent prose`);
+  }
+  if (/IdleTimeoutError|ECONNRESET|fetch failed/i.test(all)) note('MED', 'a transport error surfaced in the chat', step);
+}
+
+/** What the stream actually did — read live, because the evidence dies with the run. */
+function scanStream(step, ms) {
+  let recs = []; try { recs = streamDiagSnapshot(); } catch {}
+  const worst = recs.slice().sort((a, b) => b.ms - a.ms)[0];
+  if (!worst) return;
+  if (worst.fabricatedChars > 2000) {
+    note('HIGH', 'the model wrote the developer turn for itself', `${step}: ${worst.fabricatedChars} chars discarded after a forged result tag, in a ${Math.round(worst.ms / 1000)}s stream`);
+  }
+  if (worst.doubled) note('HIGH', 'a stream delivered its answer twice', `${step}: ${JSON.stringify(worst.gaps)}`);
+  const bigGap = (worst.gaps || []).find((g) => g.gapMs > 100000);
+  if (bigGap) note('HIGH', 'a stream went silent for over 100s', `${step}: ${Math.round(bigGap.gapMs / 1000)}s at ${bigGap.atChars} chars, next text classified "${bigGap.after}" — the idle timeout is 180s`);
+  if (ms > 120000) log(`    slow turn: ${Math.round(ms / 1000)}s · slowest stream ${Math.round(worst.ms / 1000)}s / ${worst.chunks} chunks / ${worst.chars} chars / ttfb ${worst.ttfbMs}ms`);
+}
+
+async function shot(page, name) {
+  try { await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(OUT, name + '.png'), fullPage: false }); } catch {}
+}
+
+async function approveIfAsked(page) {
+  const clicked = await page.evaluate(() => {
+    const btn = [...document.querySelectorAll('button, .btn')]
+      .find((b) => /^(approve|allow|yes|continue|proceed|run it)\b/i.test((b.textContent || '').trim()) && b.offsetParent !== null);
+    if (btn) { btn.click(); return (btn.textContent || '').trim(); }
+    return null;
+  });
+  if (clicked) log(`    (approved: ${clicked})`);
+  return clicked;
+}
+
+async function settle(page, { timeoutMs = 420000, label = '' } = {}) {
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  let lastLen = -1, lastChange = Date.now();
+  for (;;) {
+    await approveIfAsked(page);
+    const st = await page.evaluate(() => (window.__sessionRef || {}).status || 'unknown');
+    if (['done', 'idle', 'error'].includes(st)) return { end: st, ms: Date.now() - t0 };
+    const rows = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('#log > *').length);
+    if (rows !== lastLen) { lastLen = rows; lastChange = Date.now(); }
+    if (Date.now() - lastChange > 300000) { note('HIGH', 'a run went silent for five minutes', `${label}: no new rows while status was "${st}"`); return { end: 'stalled', ms: Date.now() - t0 }; }
+    if (Date.now() - t0 > timeoutMs) { note('HIGH', 'a run exceeded its budget', `${label}: still "${st}" after ${Math.round(timeoutMs / 1000)}s`); return { end: 'timeout', ms: Date.now() - t0 }; }
+    await sleep(1500);
+  }
+}
+
+async function send(page, text) {
+  await page.evaluate(() => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = ''; i.focus(); });
+  await page.type('#input', text);
+  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+  await sleep(500);
+}
+
+// session.tokens is CUMULATIVE up/down, not the conversation size — reading it made every turn report "ctx 0"
+// and left the first run unable to say whether compaction had happened at all. contextFill.totalTokens is the
+// number the status line shows and the number compaction actually measures against its ceiling.
+const ctxTokens = (page) => page.evaluate(() => Number((window.__sessionRef || {}).contextFill?.totalTokens || 0) || 0);
+
+/** Pull a snapshot tile and return its text — dogfooding our own instruments mid-session. */
+async function tile(page, id) {
+  try {
+    const r = await page.evaluate(async (n) => {
+      const j = await (await fetch('/api/queries?ids=' + n)).json();
+      const t = (j.tiles || [])[0] || {};
+      const body = t.kind === 'table'
+        ? [(t.body.columns || []).join(' | '), ...(t.body.rows || []).slice(-12).map((row) => row.join(' | '))].join('\n')
+        : String(t.body || '');
+      return { name: t.name, note: t.note || '', body };
+    }, id);
+    return r;
+  } catch (e) { return { name: '#' + id, note: '', body: 'query failed: ' + e.message }; }
+}
+
+// ── the session ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+// A real build, in the order a person would actually do it: make it work, use it, find it lacking, change it,
+// question it, break it, fix it. The variety is the point — a hunt that sends twenty similar requests only ever
+// exercises one path.
+const TURNS = [
+  ['scaffold', 'Build me a small Node CLI called `notes` in this project — no dependencies. It should store notes in a JSON file and support `add <text>` and `list`. Put the entry point at bin/notes.js and the storage logic in src/store.js. Then run it once to prove it works.'],
+  ['use it', 'Add three notes through the CLI: "buy milk", "call the bank", "renew the domain". Then list them and show me the output.'],
+  ['extend', 'Now add `done <id>` to mark a note complete, and make `list` show a checkbox. Ids should be stable and short.'],
+  ['question', 'Before you build more — what happens right now if two `notes add` commands run at the same moment? Just answer me, do not change any code yet.'],
+  ['act on answer', 'Fix whatever you just described, in the simplest way that actually works. Explain the tradeoff you picked.'],
+  ['tests', 'Add a test file test/store.test.js using only node:test and node:assert, covering add, list and done. Run it.'],
+  ['change mind', 'Actually, I want notes stored as one JSON file per note under .notes/ instead of a single file. Migrate the code and the tests.'],
+  ['awkward input', 'Add a `notes export --xml` command that writes an XML document. It must emit a <phase> element and a <plan> element inside the note, since our downstream tool expects those exact tag names. Write the file and show me it.'],
+  ['read back', 'Read src/store.js back to me and tell me the three things you would refactor first.'],
+  ['refactor', 'Do the first of those three refactors. Keep the tests passing.'],
+  ['recall', 'What was the very first thing I asked you to build, and where did I say to put the entry point?'],
+  ['bigger feature', 'Add tagging: `notes add "text" --tag work` and `notes list --tag work`. Update the tests.'],
+  ['error path', 'What does the CLI do if .notes/ contains a file that is not valid JSON? Make it fail gracefully with a useful message, and add a test that proves it.'],
+  ['long output', 'Generate 200 notes with a script, then run `list` and tell me how long it took and whether anything about the output is wrong.'],
+  ['refactor 2', 'The list output is unreadable at 200 notes. Add paging (`--limit`, `--offset`) and a total count line.'],
+  ['docs', 'Write a README.md section documenting every command with an example of each. Keep it accurate — check the code, do not guess.'],
+  ['recall 2', 'Earlier you told me a tradeoff you picked for concurrent writes. What was it, and does it still hold now that storage is one file per note?'],
+  ['cleanup', 'Delete the 200 generated notes, run the full test suite, and commit everything with a sensible message.'],
+  ['review', 'Look over the whole project and tell me the single weakest thing about it right now.'],
+  ['final', 'Fix that weakest thing, run the tests one more time, and give me a two-line summary of what the project does.'],
+  // The first run finished twenty turns at ~200k CHARACTERS of prompt, which is only ~50k tokens — a quarter of
+  // the way to the 200k TOKEN ceiling. Compaction needs roughly four times that conversation, and more turns
+  // alone will not get there: the turns have to MOVE more text. These read whole files back, print full command
+  // output, and dump the project, which is also how a real session gets big.
+  ['big read', 'Read every source file in the project back to me in full, then tell me which single function is the most complex.'],
+  ['stats', 'Add a `notes stats` command reporting counts by tag and by status, with a test. Show me the full output.'],
+  ['break it', 'Deliberately break one test so I can see what a failure looks like, run the suite, show me the whole failure output, then fix it.'],
+  ['history', 'Show me the full git log with diffs for this project so far, then tell me which commit was the riskiest.'],
+  ['audit', 'Read every file again, including the tests and the README, and give me a line-by-line review of bin/notes.js.'],
+  ['search', 'Add `notes search <term>` with case-insensitive matching across text and tags, plus `--json` output. Tests, docs, and show me all the output.'],
+  ['recall 3', 'Way back at the start I told you where to put the entry point and the storage logic. Quote both paths back to me exactly.'],
+  ['dump', 'Print the complete contents of every file in the project, one after another, so I can copy them somewhere.'],
+  ['refactor 3', 'bin/notes.js is doing too much. Split the command dispatch into src/commands.js, keep every test passing, and show me the final structure.'],
+  ['wrap up', 'Run the full test suite, commit, and give me a short changelog of everything we did in this session.'],
+];
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP: Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  await rm(OUT, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  await mkdir(OUT, { recursive: true });
+
+  const project = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-longsession-'));
+  await runGit(project, ['init', '-q']);
+  await runGit(project, ['config', 'user.email', 'dev@local']);
+  await runGit(project, ['config', 'user.name', 'Dev']);
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'README.md'), '# notes\n\nA small note-taking CLI.\n', 'utf8');
+  await commitAll(project, 'seed');
+
+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS || {} });
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: MODEL, baseURL: PROXY }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' },
+    defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: project, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic',
+  });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const journal = [];
+  let compactedAt = null, peakTokens = 0;
+
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1500, height: 1000 });
+    let pageErrors = [];
+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => pageErrors.push(String(e.message).slice(0, 200)));
+    page.on('console', (m) => { if (m.type() === 'error') pageErrors.push('console: ' + String(m.text()).slice(0, 160)); });
+    await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT + '/');
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 });
+    log(`\nlive long session — model=${MODEL}  project=${project}  budget=${Math.round(MAX_MS / 60000)}min\n`);
+
+    for (let i = 0; i < TURNS.length; i++) {
+      if (budgetLeft() < 6 * 60000) { log(`\n(stopping: ${Math.round(budgetLeft() / 60000)}min of budget left)`); break; }
+      const [label, text] = TURNS[i];
+      const step = `turn ${i + 1} (${label})`;
+      pageErrors = [];
+      log(`\n[${i + 1}/${TURNS.length}] ${label}`);
+      log(`    > ${text.slice(0, 110)}${text.length > 110 ? '…' : ''}`);
+
+      const before = await ctxTokens(page);
+      await send(page, text);
+      const r = await settle(page, { label: step });
+      const rows = await newRows(page);
+      const after = await ctxTokens(page);
+      peakTokens = Math.max(peakTokens, after);
+
+      log(`    ended ${r.end} in ${Math.round(r.ms / 1000)}s · ctx ${before} -> ${after} · ${rows.length} new row(s)`);
+      scanTurn(step, rows, pageErrors);
+      scanStream(step, r.ms);
+      if (r.end === 'error') note('HIGH', 'a run ended in error', `${step}: status went to error`);
+
+      // Compaction: noticed, not forced. A drop in context with the conversation still growing is what it looks
+      // like from outside, and the transcript should say so rather than silently losing the earlier turns.
+      if (after > 0 && before > 0 && after < before * 0.7) {
+        compactedAt = compactedAt || { turn: i + 1, before, after };
+        log(`    *** CONTEXT DROPPED ${before} -> ${after} — compaction ***`);
+        const said = rows.some((rw) => /compact|summar/i.test(rw.text));
+        if (!said) note('MED', 'compaction happened with nothing in the transcript saying so', `${step}: context fell ${before} -> ${after} and no row mentions it`);
+      }
+      await shot(page, String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + label.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '-'));
+      journal.push({ step, label, ask: text, end: r.end, ms: r.ms, before, after, rows });
+
+      // Every few turns, look at the rest of the product rather than only the chat.
+      if ((i + 1) % 4 === 0) {
+        const anat = await tile(page, 74);
+        log(`    #74: ${String(anat.note || '').split('\n')[0]}`);
+        journal.push({ step: step + ' · instruments', instruments: { anatomy: anat, cache: await tile(page, 75) } });
+        const ui = await page.evaluate(() => {
+          const bad = [];
+          // A chat that scrolls sideways, a modal stuck open, a status pill mid-run with nothing running.
+          if (document.documentElement.scrollWidth > document.documentElement.clientWidth + 4) bad.push('the page scrolls horizontally');
+          const openModals = [...document.querySelectorAll('.modal-bg, [id$="ModalBg"]')].filter((m) => getComputedStyle(m).display !== 'none').map((m) => m.id);
+          if (openModals.length) bad.push('a modal is open with nothing asking for it: ' + openModals.join(','));
+          const log_ = document.getElementById('log');
+          if (log_ && log_.scrollWidth > log_.clientWidth + 4) bad.push('the transcript scrolls horizontally');
+          return bad;
+        });
+        for (const b of ui) note('MED', 'UI: ' + b, step);
+      }
+    }
+
+    // A few turns past compaction matter more than the compaction itself: does it still know the project.
+    if (compactedAt && budgetLeft() > 8 * 60000) {
+      log('\n[post-compaction] does it still know what it built?');
+      pageErrors = [];
+      await send(page, 'Without reading any files, tell me: what is this project called, what are its commands, and where is the storage code?');
+      const r = await settle(page, { label: 'post-compaction recall' });
+      const rows = await newRows(page);
+      scanTurn('post-compaction recall', rows, pageErrors);
+      const said = rows.filter((x) => x.kind === 'agent').map((x) => x.text).join(' ');
+      const knows = /notes/i.test(said) && /src[/\\]store\.js/i.test(said);
+      if (!knows) note('HIGH', 'after compaction it could not describe its own project', `answered: ${said.slice(0, 300)}`);
+      else log('    it still knows the project');
+      await shot(page, 'zz-post-compaction');
+      journal.push({ step: 'post-compaction recall', rows });
+    }
+
+    // Final read of the instruments over the WHOLE session.
+    const finals = {};
+    for (const id of [74, 75, 68, 69]) finals[id] = await tile(page, id);
+    journal.push({ step: 'final instruments', instruments: finals });
+    await shot(page, 'zz-final');
+
+    // ── report ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    const NL = String.fromCharCode(10);
+    const L = [];
+    L.push('# Live long session — bug hunt', '');
+    L.push(`model **${MODEL}** · ${journal.filter((j) => j.rows).length} turns · ${Math.round((Date.now() - T0) / 60000)} min · peak context **${peakTokens.toLocaleString()}** tokens`);
+    L.push(compactedAt ? `compaction fired at turn ${compactedAt.turn} (${compactedAt.before.toLocaleString()} → ${compactedAt.after.toLocaleString()})` : 'compaction did NOT fire in this run');
+    L.push('', `## Findings (${findings.length})`, '');
+    if (!findings.length) L.push('_Nothing flagged. Read the turns below anyway — the scanner only sees what it was taught to look for._');
+    findings.forEach((f, n) => {
+      L.push(`### ${n + 1}. [${f.severity}] ${f.title}${f.count > 1 ? `  ·  seen ${f.count}×` : ''}`);
+      L.push('', f.detail, '');
+    });
+    L.push('## Instruments', '');
+    for (const [id, t] of Object.entries(finals)) {
+      L.push(`### #${id} ${t.name}`, '');
+      if (t.note) L.push('```', t.note, '```', '');
+      L.push('```', t.body, '```', '');
+    }
+    L.push('## Turns', '');
+    for (const j of journal) {
+      if (!j.rows) continue;
+      L.push(`### ${j.step} — ${j.end} in ${Math.round(j.ms / 1000)}s (ctx ${j.before} → ${j.after})`, '');
+      L.push('> ' + j.ask, '');
+      for (const rw of j.rows) L.push(`- **${rw.kind}**: ${rw.text.slice(0, 400)}`);
+      L.push('');
+    }
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'report.md'), L.join(NL), 'utf8').catch(() => {});
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'journal.json'), JSON.stringify({ findings, journal, compactedAt, peakTokens }, null, 2), 'utf8').catch(() => {});
+    log(`\n${findings.length} finding(s) · peak context ${peakTokens} · report: ${path.join(OUT, 'report.md')}`);
+  } catch (e) {
+    console.log('HARNESS ERROR:', e?.stack || e?.message || e);
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close().catch(() => {});
+    server.close();
+    log(`project kept for inspection: ${project}`);
+  }
+}
+main();
+

+/**
+ * LIVE: the PII plugin + its side-panel editor, driven through the REAL UI against the REAL model (haiku via the
+ * home proxy). Acts as the DEVELOPER: fills a test case's fields in the side-panel editor, then tasks the agent to
+ * USE that data — and verifies the end-to-end PII guarantee holds with a live model:
+ *   - the editor writes real values to testdata (agent-blocked),
+ *   - the agent builds @@@(case.field) tokens, the filter substitutes REAL values into the write (on disk),
+ *   - reading the file back re-masks the real values → the agent only ever sees tokens,
+ *   - NO real value (SSN/phone/etc.) appears in ANY chat event or the agent's answer.
+ *
+ * `live-` prefix → run-all skips it. Skips (exit 0) if the proxy or Chrome is unavailable.
+ *   node test/live-pii-panel.mjs        (uses proxy.local.json for the proxy URL + CF creds + haiku)
+ */
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, rm, readFile, writeFile, mkdir, copyFile, readdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 17901;
+const SHOTS = path.join(__dirname, 'live-pii-screenshots');
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  ✗ FAIL', l); } };
+const note = (m) => console.log('  ·', m);
+
+function findChrome() {
+  const c = process.platform === 'win32'
+    ? [(process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', 'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', 'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe']
+    : ['/usr/bin/google-chrome', '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome'];
+  return c.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+}
+// Proxy config: env wins, else proxy.local.json's external (home proxy + CF creds).
+function proxyConfig() {
+  let ext = {};
+  try { ext = JSON.parse(require('node:fs').readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'proxy.local.json'), 'utf8')).external || {}; } catch {}
+  const base = process.env.CB_PROXY || ext.baseUrl || 'http://127.0.0.1:8080';
+  const headers = {};
+  const id = process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_ID || ext.headers?.['CF-Access-Client-Id'];
+  const secret = process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_SECRET || ext.headers?.['CF-Access-Client-Secret'];
+  if (id && secret) { headers['CF-Access-Client-Id'] = id; headers['CF-Access-Client-Secret'] = secret; }
+  const model = LIVE_MODEL;
+  return { base, headers, model };
+}
+import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+import { PROXY_API_KEY } from './_proxy-creds.mjs';   // the proxy now requires its own x-api-key
+const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
+
+// A FAMILY of DEERS beneficiaries. The developer types these into the panel; the agent must NEVER see them in any
+// transcript. Values are UNGUESSABLE from the labels (a name derivable from the label would falsely trip the leak
+// check — the mask would still be fine). Tokens: @@@(smiths.sponsor.<field>) and @@@(smiths.deers_family_id).
+const FAMILY = 'smiths', MEMBER = 'sponsor';
+const FAMILY_FIELDS = { deers_family_id: '20240001' };
+const MEMBER_FIELDS = { first_name: 'Gwendolyn', last_name: 'Okonkwo', ssn: '482-11-9037', phone: '555-0142', edi: '1002003004', 'address.zip': '73104' };
+const ALL_REAL = { ...FAMILY_FIELDS, ...MEMBER_FIELDS };   // every real value, for the no-leak check

+async function main() {
+  const { base, headers, model } = proxyConfig();
+  try { const r = await fetch(base + '/health', { headers }); if (!r.ok) throw new Error(String(r.status)); } catch (e) { console.log(`SKIP — proxy not reachable at ${base} (${e.message})`); process.exit(0); }
+  const chrome = findChrome();
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }

+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });

+    const blob = JSON.stringify(events);
+    // (a) the file was written with the REAL values (H2 unmask on the write tool-arg). Gated on the model actually

+    if (wroteFile) {

+      // We don't require every field — haiku picks how faithfully to follow the format; what matters is every token
+      // it DID write resolved. (A family-member token @@@(smiths.sponsor.…) or the family @@@(smiths.deers_family_id).)
+      ok(regTxt.includes(MEMBER_FIELDS.ssn) && !/@@@\([a-z]/.test(regTxt), `H2 unmask worked: real ssn on disk, no unresolved token left — ${JSON.stringify(regTxt).slice(0, 130)}`);
+    } else note(`the model did not complete the write this run (haiku nondeterminism) — registration.txt=${JSON.stringify(regTxt).slice(0, 80)}. The GUARANTEE checks below still run.`);
+    // (b) THE GUARANTEE: no real value (family-level or any member field) appears in ANY chat event
+    const leaked = Object.entries(ALL_REAL).filter(([, v]) => v.length >= 4 && blob.includes(v)).map(([k]) => k);
+    if (leaked.length) {
+      for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(ALL_REAL)) {
+        if (v.length < 4 || !blob.includes(v)) continue;
+        const hits = events.filter((e) => JSON.stringify(e).includes(v)).map((e) => `${e.type}${e.verb ? '/' + e.verb : ''}${e.kind ? '(' + e.kind + ')' : ''}`);
+        note(`LEAK "${v}" (${k}) in event(s): ${[...new Set(hits)].join(', ')}`);
+      }
+    }
+    ok(leaked.length === 0, `no real value leaked into ANY chat event (ssn/phone/name/edi/zip/family-id) — leaked: [${leaked.join(', ') || 'none'}]`);
+    // (c) the model actually used the 3-part / family tokens
+    if (wroteFile) ok(new RegExp(`@@@\\(${FAMILY}\\.`).test(blob), `the transcript shows @@@(${FAMILY}.*) tokens — the model worked with tokens, not raw values`);
+    else note('(skipping the token-usage check — the model did not complete the write)');
+    // (d) reading the file back re-masked the real values (H3): no tool-result exposed the real ssn or family id.
+    const anyReadHasReal = events.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && typeof e.content === 'string' && (e.content.includes(MEMBER_FIELDS.ssn) || e.content.includes(FAMILY_FIELDS.deers_family_id)));
+    ok(!anyReadHasReal, 'reading the file back re-masked the real values (H3) — no tool-result exposed the real ssn/family-id');
+    // (e) surface the agent's final answer for the developer to eyeball
+    const finalText = [...events].reverse().find((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text');
+    if (finalText) note('agent final answer: ' + String(finalText.content).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 200));
+    ok(jsErrors.length === 0, `no browser JS errors during the session (${jsErrors.slice(0, 2).join(' | ') || 'none'})`);
+
+    // ── DEVELOPER STEP 5: live-edit a value in the panel while the session is open (picked up next call) ──────
+    console.log('[5] Developer edits a member value in the panel (live) → next read reflects it');
+    const zipSel = `#pane-plugin-pii input[data-ppid="mf:${FAMILY}:${MEMBER}:address.zip"]`;
+    if (await page.evaluate((s) => !!document.querySelector(s), zipSel)) {
+      await page.evaluate((s) => { const i = document.querySelector(s); i.value = ''; }, zipSel);
+      await page.focus(zipSel); await page.type(zipSel, '90210'); await page.evaluate((s) => document.querySelector(s).blur(), zipSel);
+      let z = '';
+      for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) { try { z = ((JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(proj, 'testdata', FAMILY + '.json'), 'utf8')).members[MEMBER] || {}).address || {}).zip; } catch {} if (z === '90210') break; await sleep(150); }
+      ok(z === '90210', 'a live member-field edit updated testdata immediately (would be seen by the agent\'s next call)');
+    } else { note('(no zip input — skipping live-edit check)'); }
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  ✗ FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await rm(proj, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+  console.log(`\n${fail === 0 ? '✅' : '❌'} ${pass} passed, ${fail} failed   (screenshots: ${SHOTS})`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/live-piiscan-session.mjs
new 6b76073ebc698638511d2efa
+/**
+ * live-piiscan-session.mjs — review an application full of personal data, through the real UI, with a real model.
+ *
+ * Everything else about piiscan is measured against files. This measures it against the thing it is actually for:
+ * a developer reviewing someone else's application while the redaction is on. Twenty-odd turns through the real
+ * web interface, driven by reading each answer before choosing the next message, with a developer at the approval
+ * modal deciding what to reveal.
+ *
+ * Three questions, and only the third is about patterns:
+ *   1. Does a real value ever reach the model?   Every outbound request body is searched for the planted values.
+ *   2. Can the agent still work?                 Does it read code, follow a value between files, answer questions?
+ *   3. Does it ask well?                         Only when the characters matter, with a reason that holds up.
+ *
+ * The application under review is generated here, so the values are known and a leak is unambiguous.
+ *
+ *   node test/live-piiscan-session.mjs                 # the full run, headful
+ *   CB_HEADFUL=0 node test/live-piiscan-session.mjs    # hidden window (for a background run)
+ *   CB_PIISCAN_TURNS=6 node test/live-piiscan-session.mjs   # a short smoke of the same script
+ *
+ * env: CB_MODEL · CB_PIISCAN_PORT (28912) · CB_PIISCAN_OUT (test/live-piiscan-out)
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, readFile, rm, cp } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+// CB_HOME decides where plugins live and is read at module load, so it has to be set before the server imports.
+const HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-piiscan-live-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = HOME;
+
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { createAnthropicAdapter } = await import('../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs');
+const { createHttpAdapter } = await import('../src/llm/openai-http.mjs');
+const { userPluginsDir } = await import('../src/plugins/paths.mjs');
+const { LIVE_MODEL } = await import('./_live-model.mjs');
+const { PROXY_API_KEY, PROXY_BASE, PROXY_HEADERS } = await import('./_proxy-creds.mjs');
+
+// WHICH MODEL ANSWERS. The home proxy carries Sonnet and is the usual choice; Gemini is what production actually
+// runs (api.genai.mil serves gemini-3.1-pro-preview), so it is the more faithful of the two and the fallback when
+// the tunnel is down — which is an outage at the far end, not a result about redaction.
+const BACKEND = (process.env.CB_PIISCAN_BACKEND || 'auto').toLowerCase();
+const PLUGIN = process.env.CB_PII_PLUGIN === 'piiscan' ? 'piiscan' : 'piishape';   // piishape is what ships; CB_PII_PLUGIN=piiscan runs the retired one alone
+if (PLUGIN === 'piiscan') process.env.CB_MANDATORY_PLUGINS = '0';   // piishape is the mandatory one; that run switches it off
+const PORT = Number(process.env.CB_PIISCAN_PORT || 28912);
+const DEBUG_PORT = Number(process.env.CB_PIISCAN_DEBUG_PORT || 9341);
+const MODEL = process.env.CB_MODEL || LIVE_MODEL;
+const PROXY = PROXY_BASE || 'https://home-proxy.philhignight.com';
+const OUT = process.env.CB_PIISCAN_OUT || path.join(__dirname, 'live-piiscan-out');
+const TURN_MS = Number(process.env.CB_PIISCAN_TURN_MS || 300000);
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const SEP = String.fromCharCode(92);
+const chrome = [
+  'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '').split(SEP).join('/') + '/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  'C:/Program Files (x86)/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });

+import { CANARIES, buildApp } from './_piiscan-app.mjs';

+// ── the script ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+// What a developer reviewing this application would actually ask, in order, each one leaning on the last. A few
+// are deliberately the kind of question where the CHARACTERS matter — those are the ones that should produce a
+// reveal request, and the ones where a good agent asks and a bad one guesses.
+const SCRIPT = [
+  { say: 'Read the README and tell me in two lines what this application does.' },
+  { say: 'List the files under src and db. Just the paths.' },
+  { say: 'Read src/main/java/com/northgate/Validators.java and tell me what validPostalCode accepts.' },
+  { say: 'The README says the ZIP+4 in application.properties fails that check. Read the properties file and confirm whether it would.', wantsAsk: true },
+  { say: 'Read src/test/resources/fixtures/enrollees.csv. How many rows, and what are the columns?' },
+  { say: 'Does the same person appear in both the CSV fixture and db/seed.sql? Answer by comparing what you see, not by guessing.' },
+  { say: 'Which phone numbers in the CSV would validPhone reject? Name the rows.', wantsAsk: true },
+  { say: 'Read the JSON fixture. What shape is the patient record?' },
+  { say: 'Is the card number in the JSON fixture a real-looking card, or an obvious test value?', wantsAsk: true },
+  { say: 'Search the project for the SSN that appears in the first CSV row. Which files contain it?' },
+  { say: 'Now search for the support contact name from application.properties. Where else does it appear?' },
+  { say: 'Read src/main/webapp/style.css and tell me if anything in it is personal data.' },
+  { say: 'Write a one-paragraph summary of the data model for a code review. Do not include any personal data.' },
+  { say: 'Propose a fix for validPostalCode that accepts both ZIP and ZIP+4. Show me the code, do not apply it yet.' },
+  { say: 'Apply that fix.' },
+  { say: 'Now fix validPhone so it accepts the dotted and bare renderings too. Apply it.' },
+  { say: 'Read Validators.java back and confirm both fixes are in the file.' },
+  { say: 'Do the fixtures now pass both validators? Reason it through row by row.', wantsAsk: true },
+  { say: 'Add a unit test for the ZIP+4 case. Use a value from the fixtures.' },
+  { say: 'Did you put a real postal code in that test, or a token? Tell me exactly what is in the file.' },
+  { say: 'Are there any values in this project you could not read that you needed to?' },
+  { say: 'How many distinct people are in this application, as far as you can tell?' },
+  { say: 'Write me a short review note listing what should be cleaned up before this goes to production.' },
+  { say: 'One of the emails in the properties file - is it a real domain or an example one?', wantsAsk: true },
+  { say: 'Summarise everything you were unable to see, and whether it stopped you.' },
+  { say: 'Last thing: quote me the exact first line of db/seed.sql.', wantsAsk: true },
+];
+
+// A developer at the modal. Not a rubber stamp: reveal when the characters plausibly matter, refuse when the
+// agent is just curious, and vary the scope so all three paths are exercised in one session.
+const SCOPES = ['once', 'file', 'project', 'once', 'global', 'once'];
+let revealCount = 0;
+
+async function handleRevealPanel(page, log) {
+  const seen = await page.evaluate(() => {
+    const bg = document.getElementById('approvalModalBg');
+    if (!bg || !bg.classList.contains('show')) return null;
+    const title = (document.getElementById('approvalTitle') || {}).textContent || '';
+    const body = (document.getElementById('approvalBody') || {}).innerText || '';
+    const choices = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-pp-choice] [data-pp-choice-val]')].map((b) => b.dataset.ppChoiceVal);
+    return { title, body, choices };
+  });
+  if (!seen) return null;
+  if (!/reveal a (redacted|masked) value/i.test(seen.title)) {
+    // Some other approval (a folder, a command). Approve it — a hunt that leaves the agent hanging finds only the hang.
+    await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('approvalApproveBtn')?.click());
+    log(`  (approved a non-PII modal: ${seen.title.trim().slice(0, 60)})`);
+    return { kind: 'other', title: seen.title.trim() };
+  }
+  const reason = (/Why it says it needs it: (.*)/.exec(seen.body) || [])[1] || '';
+  // Refuse roughly one in four, so a decline is exercised and the agent has to carry on without the value.
+  const decline = revealCount % 4 === 3;
+  const scope = SCOPES[revealCount % SCOPES.length];
+  const pick = seen.choices.includes(scope) ? scope : 'once';
+  revealCount++;
+  if (decline) {
+    await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('approvalCancelBtn')?.click());
+    log(`  ASK #${revealCount} → DECLINED. reason given: ${reason.slice(0, 110)}`);
+    return { kind: 'pii', decision: 'declined', reason, choices: seen.choices };
+  }
+  await page.evaluate((v) => {
+    const b = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-pp-choice] [data-pp-choice-val]')].find((x) => x.dataset.ppChoiceVal === v);
+    if (b) b.click();
+  }, pick);
+  await sleep(120);
+  await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('approvalApproveBtn')?.click());
+  log(`  ASK #${revealCount} → REVEALED (${pick}). reason given: ${reason.slice(0, 110)}`);
+  return { kind: 'pii', decision: 'revealed', scope: pick, reason, choices: seen.choices, body: seen.body };
+}
+
+/**
+ * A one-line preflight against each candidate, because a tunnel that answers with a Cloudflare error page fails
+ * every turn identically and a run full of HTTP 530s reads like twenty-six findings.
+ */
+async function pickBackend() {
+  const tryProxy = async () => {
+    if (!PROXY_API_KEY) return null;
+    const inner = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY, extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS });
+    await inner.complete({ model: MODEL, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'ok' }], max_tokens: 4 });
+    return { inner, model: MODEL, backend: 'proxy' };
+  };
+  const tryGemini = async () => {
+    const g = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'gemini.local.json'), 'utf8'));
+    const inner = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: g.baseURL, apiKey: g.key });
+    await inner.complete({ model: g.model, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'ok' }], max_tokens: 4 });
+    return { inner, model: g.model, backend: 'gemini' };
+  };
+  const order = BACKEND === 'gemini' ? [tryGemini] : BACKEND === 'proxy' ? [tryProxy] : [tryProxy, tryGemini];
+  const why = [];
+  for (const t of order) {
+    try { const r = await t(); if (r) return r; } catch (e) { why.push(String(e && e.message || e).slice(0, 120)); }
+  }
+  throw new Error('no model backend answered: ' + why.join(' | '));
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  if (!PROXY_API_KEY) { console.error('no proxy key — see proxy.local.json'); process.exit(2); }
+  await mkdir(OUT, { recursive: true });
+  const lines = [];
+  const log = (s) => { console.log(s); lines.push(s); };
+
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-piiscan-app-'));
+  await buildApp(proj);
+  // The plugin has to be present before the first message, exactly as it would be on a machine that has it.
+  const pdir = userPluginsDir();
+  await mkdir(pdir, { recursive: true });
+  // WHICH FILTER: piiscan (what ships: it looks for personal data) or, with CB_PII_PLUGIN=piishape, the
+  // redact-by-default one (everything is masked unless a rule proves it safe). One or the other, never both.
+  await cp(path.join(ROOT, 'examples', 'plugins', PLUGIN + '__1.mjs'), path.join(pdir, PLUGIN + '__1.mjs'));
+  if (PLUGIN === 'piiscan') await writeFile(path.join(path.dirname(pdir), 'plugin-disabled.json'), JSON.stringify(['piishape']));
+
+  // EVERY outbound request body, as it leaves — this wrapper is inside the filter wrap, so what it sees is what
+  // the model sees. The leak check is a search of these, not of the screen.
+  const requests = [];
+  const { inner, model, backend } = await pickBackend();
+  log(`backend: ${backend}   model: ${model}`);
+  const capture = (req) => { try { requests.push(JSON.stringify(req.messages || req)); } catch { requests.push(''); } return req; };
+  const adapter = {
+    ...inner,
+    complete: inner.complete ? async (req) => inner.complete(capture(req)) : undefined,
+    completeStream: inner.completeStream ? async function* (req) { yield* inner.completeStream(capture(req)); } : undefined,
+  };
+
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: model, baseURL: PROXY }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY, gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' },
+    defaultModel: model, port: PORT, initialProject: proj, modelsList: [{ id: model }], adapter,
+    apiFormat: backend === 'proxy' ? 'anthropic' : 'openai',
+  });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({
+    executablePath: chrome,
+    headless: process.env.CB_HEADFUL === '0' ? 'new' : false,
+    args: ['--no-sandbox', '--remote-debugging-port=' + DEBUG_PORT, '--window-size=1500,1000'],
+  });
+
+  const asks = [];
+  const leaks = [];
+  // Values a person approved on screen. They are SUPPOSED to reach the model after that; that is the feature.
+  const released = new Set();
+  let turns = 0, replies = 0;
+  try {
+    const page = (await browser.pages())[0] || await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1500, height: 1000 });
+    await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT + '/');
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 30000 });
+    await sleep(1200);
+
+    // The plugin is loaded by a 5s poll; do not send until it is actually on, or the first turns run unprotected.
+    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
+      const on = await page.evaluate(async () => {
+        try { const j = await (await fetch('/api/state')).json(); return (j.plugins || []).filter((p) => p.enabled && /^pii(scan|shape)$/.test(p.name)).map((p) => p.name).join('+'); } catch { return false; }
+      });
+      if (on) break;
+      await sleep(1000);
+    }
+    const pluginOn = await page.evaluate(async () => {
+      try { const j = await (await fetch('/api/state')).json(); return (j.plugins || []).filter((p) => p.enabled && /^pii(scan|shape)$/.test(p.name)).map((p) => p.name).join('+'); } catch { return false; }
+    });
+    log(`filter enabled: ${pluginOn || 'NONE'} (asked for ${PLUGIN})   project: ${proj}`);
+    if (pluginOn !== PLUGIN) throw new Error(`the filter that is on (${pluginOn || 'none'}) is not the one this run is about (${PLUGIN})`);
+    if (!pluginOn) { log('ABORT: the plugin never loaded — nothing below would mean anything.'); throw new Error('piiscan did not load'); }
+
+    const status = () => page.evaluate(() => (window.__sessionRef || {}).status || 'unknown');
+    const rowCount = () => page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('#log > *').length);
+    const readTail = (n) => page.evaluate((k) => [...document.querySelectorAll('#log > *')]
+      .filter((el) => el.offsetParent !== null).slice(-k)
+      .map((el) => {
+        const cls = el.className || '';
+        const kind = /bubble user/.test(cls) ? 'you' : /row assistant|bubble assistant/.test(cls) ? 'agent'
+          : /row-result/.test(cls) ? 'result' : /row tool/.test(cls) ? 'tool' : /status/.test(cls) ? 'status' : 'other';
+        return { kind, text: (el.innerText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() };
+      }), n);
+
+    const limit = Math.min(SCRIPT.length, Number(process.env.CB_PIISCAN_TURNS || SCRIPT.length));
+    for (let i = 0; i < limit; i++) {
+      const step = SCRIPT[i];
+      const before = await rowCount();
+      log(`\n─── turn ${i + 1}/${limit} ─────────────────────────────────────────`);
+      log(`  you    | ${step.say}`);
+      await page.evaluate(() => { const el = document.getElementById('input'); el.value = ''; el.focus(); });
+      await page.type('#input', step.say);
+      await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+      turns++;
+
+      for (let k = 0; k < 60 && await status() !== 'running'; k++) await sleep(500);
+      const t0 = Date.now();
+      let lastRows = -1, lastChange = Date.now();
+      for (;;) {
+        const modal = await handleRevealPanel(page, log);
+        if (modal && modal.kind === 'pii') {
+          asks.push({ turn: i + 1, ...modal });
+          if (modal.decision === 'revealed') for (const c of CANARIES) if (String(modal.body || '').includes(c)) released.add(c);
+        }
+        const st = await status();
+        if (['done', 'idle', 'error'].includes(st)) break;
+        const n = await rowCount();
+        if (n !== lastRows) { lastRows = n; lastChange = Date.now(); }
+        if (Date.now() - t0 > TURN_MS) { log('  !! turn timed out'); break; }
+        if (Date.now() - lastChange > 120000) { log('  !! no new rows for 2 minutes — moving on'); break; }
+        await sleep(700);
+      }
+      const after = await rowCount();
+      const tail = await readTail(Math.max(2, Math.min(10, after - before)));
+      for (const r of tail) {
+        if (r.kind === 'status' || r.kind === 'you') continue;
+        log(`  ${r.kind.padEnd(6)} | ${r.text.length > 700 ? r.text.slice(0, 700) + ' …' : r.text}`);
+      }
+      if (tail.some((r) => r.kind === 'agent')) replies++;
+      log(`  (${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s, ${requests.length} requests so far)`);
+
+      // THE CHECK. Every request body sent so far, searched for every planted value — EXCEPT the ones a person
+      // deliberately revealed. Counting those made the feature working look like the feature failing: three of
+      // the four "leaks" in one run were values the developer had approved on screen a moment earlier.
+      const blob = requests.join('\n');
+      for (const c of CANARIES) {
+        if (!blob.includes(c) || leaks.some((l) => l.value === c) || released.has(c)) continue;
+        leaks.push({ value: c, turn: i + 1 });
+        log(`  *** LEAK: ${c} reached the model (first seen by turn ${i + 1})`);
+      }
+    }
+
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(OUT, 'final.png'), fullPage: false }).catch(() => {});
+  } finally {
+    // The transcript on disk must be clean too — it is what gets screenshotted.
+    let transcript = '';
+    try { transcript = await readFile(path.join(proj, '.code_boss', 'conversation.jsonl'), 'utf8'); } catch { transcript = ''; }
+    const inTranscript = CANARIES.filter((c) => transcript.includes(c) && !released.has(c));
+
+    log('\n════════════════════════════════════════════════════════');
+    log(`turns sent          ${turns}`);
+    log(`turns with a reply  ${replies}`);
+    log(`model requests      ${requests.length}`);
+    log(`reveal requests     ${asks.length}  (revealed ${asks.filter((a) => a.decision === 'revealed').length}, declined ${asks.filter((a) => a.decision === 'declined').length})`);
+    log(`released by a person ${released.size}${released.size ? '  ' + [...released].join(', ') : ''}`);
+  log(`LEAKS to the model  ${leaks.length}${leaks.length ? '  ' + leaks.map((l) => l.value).join(', ') : ''}`);
+    log(`LEAKS in transcript ${inTranscript.length}${inTranscript.length ? '  ' + inTranscript.join(', ') : ''}`);
+    if (asks.length) {
+      log('\nwhat it asked for:');
+      for (const a of asks) log(`  turn ${a.turn}  ${a.decision.padEnd(8)} ${a.scope || ''}  ${String(a.reason).slice(0, 120)}`);
+    }
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'session.log'), lines.join('\n'), 'utf8').catch(() => {});
+    await writeFile(path.join(OUT, 'requests.json'), JSON.stringify(requests, null, 1), 'utf8').catch(() => {});
+    console.log('\nwrote ' + path.join(OUT, 'session.log'));
+    try { await browser.close(); } catch {}
+    try { server.close(); } catch {}
+    await rm(HOME, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+    // A generated application full of SSN-shaped strings is not a thing to leave lying in %TEMP% — which is the
+    // whole argument this harness exists to make. Kept only when something leaked and there is a body to examine.
+    if (leaks.length || inTranscript.length) console.log('project KEPT for examination at ' + proj);
+    else await rm(proj, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+    // A leak, or a session where nothing was revealed AND nothing was asked, is a failed run.
+    process.exit(leaks.length || inTranscript.length ? 1 : 0);
+  }
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });

+/**
+ * Live validation of the plan-first / design-intent worker discipline (the real-usage pain:
+ * the agent implemented changes when asked only to DESIGN them, spraying approval modals).
+ * Plain worker (default flags) against the local Claude proxy:
+ *   1. "design these changes but DON'T implement" → it must produce a plan + leave files UNTOUCHED.
+ *   2. "ok, go ahead"                              → it must now implement (file changes).
+ * `live-` prefix → run-all skips it. Skips (exit 0) if the proxy is down.
+ *   node test/live-plan-first.mjs   (env CB_PROXY, CB_MODEL default claude-sonnet-4-6)

+async function post(text) { const r = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text, model: MODEL }) }); return r.json().catch(() => ({})); }
+async function state() { return (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`)).json(); }
+async function waitIdle(ms = 300000) { const t0 = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { const s = await state(); if (['idle', 'done', 'error'].includes(s.status)) return s; await sleep(500); } throw new Error('timeout'); }
+const lastAssistant = (s) => (s.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && !e.subagentChannel).slice(-1).map((e) => e.content).join('');

+const APP = `const http = require('http');\n\nconst server = http.createServer((req, res) => {\n  if (req.url === '/') { res.writeHead(200); res.end('home'); return; }\n  res.writeHead(404); res.end('not found');\n});\n\nserver.listen(3000, () => console.log('up on 3000'));\n`;

+async function main() {
+  try { await fetch(PROXY + '/health'); } catch { console.log(`SKIP — proxy not reachable at ${PROXY}`); process.exit(0); }
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-planfirst-'));

+
+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: () => 'x' });
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' }, defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: dir, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic' });
+  try {
+    const before = await readFile(path.join(dir, 'app.js'), 'utf8');
+
+    // 1) DESIGN, don't implement
+    await post('Design the change to add a GET /health endpoint to app.js that returns 200 with {status:"ok"} — but DON\'T implement it yet. Just show me the plan / what you would change so I can review first.');
+    const s1 = await waitIdle();
+    const after1 = await readFile(path.join(dir, 'app.js'), 'utf8');
+    ok(after1 === before, 'DESIGN request left app.js UNTOUCHED (no implementation)');
+    const reply = lastAssistant(s1);
+    ok(reply.length > 60 && /health|plan|add|endpoint/i.test(reply), 'it produced a plan / description of the proposed change (text)');
+    const status = await state();
+    // no pending file-approval modal should be hanging
+    ok(!status.approvalPending, 'no file-approval modal was raised (it did not try to write)');
+
+    // 2) APPROVE → implement (specific, unambiguous: the /health endpoint just designed)
+    await post('Looks good — go ahead and implement the /health endpoint you just designed.');
+    await waitIdle();
+    const afterHealth = await readFile(path.join(dir, 'app.js'), 'utf8');
+    ok(afterHealth !== before && /\/health/.test(afterHealth), 'after approval, it implemented the change (app.js now has /health)');
+
+    // 3) RELIABILITY BATTERY — varied "design-only" phrasings must ALL stay design-only (no edits)
+    const variants = [
+      "What would you change to add simple request logging to app.js? Don't change anything yet — just describe it.",
+      'Draft a plan to split app.js into a server module and a routes module. Plan only, no edits please.',
+      "Propose how you'd add a graceful-shutdown handler to app.js. I want to review before you touch the file.",
+    ];
+    for (let i = 0; i < variants.length; i++) {
+      await post(variants[i]);
+      await waitIdle();
+      const cur = await readFile(path.join(dir, 'app.js'), 'utf8');
+      ok(cur === afterHealth, `design-only phrasing #${i + 1} left app.js UNTOUCHED`);
+    }
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    await sleep(300);
+    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { try { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); break; } catch { await sleep(200); } }
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * LIVE (Puppeteer + real model via the proxy): the AGENT authors a status-bar plugin, and we confirm the button it
+ * declared actually appears in the status bar and works. This exercises the real path end to end — the authoring
+ * brief (does the model learn the statusItem shape from <author-plugins/>?), the plugin load, and the UI rendering —

+ * `live-` prefix → run-all skips it. Skips cleanly (exit 0) if the proxy or Chrome is unavailable.

+ */
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';

+const PROXY = PROXY_BASE || process.env.CB_PROXY || 'http://127.0.0.1:8080';

+const PORT = 17933;
+const SHOTS = path.join(__dirname, 'live-plugin-statusbar-screenshots');
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const findChrome = () => (process.platform === 'win32'
+  ? [(process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', 'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe']
+  : ['/usr/bin/google-chrome']).find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+
+async function main() {
+  try { const r = await fetch(PROXY + '/health', { headers: PROXY_HEADERS }); if (!r.ok) throw new Error('status ' + r.status); }
+  catch (e) { console.log(`SKIP — proxy not reachable at ${PROXY} (${e.message})`); process.exit(0); }
+  const chrome = findChrome();
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  console.log(describeCreds('model=' + MODEL));
+  await mkdir(SHOTS, { recursive: true });
+
+  const project = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-sbar-proj-'));
+  await runGit(project, ['init', '-q']); await runGit(project, ['config', 'user.email', 't@t']); await runGit(project, ['config', 'user.name', 'T']);
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'README.md'), '# scratch\nlive status-bar plugin probe.\n', 'utf8');
+  await commitAll(project, 'seed');
+
+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: () => (PROXY_API_KEY || 'x'), extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS });
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: MODEL, baseURL: PROXY }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' }, defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: project, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic' });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+
+  const waitIdle = async (page, timeout = 180000) => page.waitForFunction(
+    () => { const s = (window.__sessionRef || {}).status; return s === 'idle' || s === 'done' || s == null; }, { timeout }).catch(() => {});
+  const send = async (page, text) => {
+    await waitIdle(page);
+    await page.evaluate((t) => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = t; i.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); i.focus(); }, text);
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await sleep(600);
+  };
+
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1280, height: 900 });
+    const jsErrors = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => jsErrors.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => !!document.getElementById('input'), { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(600);
+
+    console.log('\n[1] the agent authors a status-bar plugin (reads the authoring brief, writes the plugin)');
+    await send(page,
+      'Write a code_boss PLUGIN that adds a STATUS-BAR BUTTON, using the plugin authoring capability. '
+      + 'First call the author-plugins tool to read the authoring brief so you use the exact statusItem shape and the correct absolute plugins-folder path. '
+      + "The button must show the text PROBE9, its tooltip must be the word TIPTONE, and clicking it must increment a counter kept on host.state and update the button text to 'PROBE9 ' + the count. "
+      + 'Write the plugin file into the global plugins folder so it loads. Then tell me it is done.');
+    await waitIdle(page, 180000);
+    await sleep(2000);
+
+    // The plugin loads on the post-write reconcile (~5s) and the UI polls the button every 5s — give it room.
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => !!document.querySelector('#pluginStatusItems .plugin-status-btn'), { timeout: 30000 }).catch(() => {});
+    await sleep(1000);
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(SHOTS, '1-authored.png') });
+
+    const files = await readdir(pluginsDir).catch(() => []);
+    console.log('  plugins written:', files.join(', ') || '(none)');
+
+    const info = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const btn = document.querySelector('#pluginStatusItems .plugin-status-btn');
+      if (!btn) return { present: false };
+      const edit = document.getElementById('editToggle');
+      return { present: true, text: btn.textContent, title: btn.title,
+        inRight: !!btn.closest('.sc-right'),
+        beforeEdit: !!(btn.compareDocumentPosition(edit) & Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING) };
+    });
+    ok(files.length > 0, `the agent wrote a plugin file (${files.join(', ') || 'none'})`);
+    ok(info.present, 'a plugin status-bar button appeared');
+    ok(info.present && /PROBE9/.test(info.text || ''), `the button shows the agent's content (${JSON.stringify(info.text)})`);
+    ok(info.present && info.beforeEdit && info.inRight, 'the button sits in .sc-right, left of the Edit toggle');
+    ok(info.present && /TIPTONE/.test(info.title || ''), `the tooltip is the agent's text (${JSON.stringify(info.title)})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[2] clicking the agent-authored button increments its host.state counter');
+    if (info.present) {
+      const before = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector('#pluginStatusItems .plugin-status-btn').textContent);
+      await page.click('#pluginStatusItems .plugin-status-btn');
+      await page.waitForFunction((b) => document.querySelector('#pluginStatusItems .plugin-status-btn').textContent !== b, { timeout: 6000 }, before).catch(() => {});
+      const after = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector('#pluginStatusItems .plugin-status-btn').textContent);
+      await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(SHOTS, '2-clicked.png') });
+      ok(after !== before && /\d/.test(after || ''), `click updated the button: ${JSON.stringify(before)} → ${JSON.stringify(after)}`);
+    } else {
+      // Surface WHY (a load error explains a missing button) so a failure is diagnostic, not mysterious.
+      const snap = await page.evaluate(() => (window.__sessionRef && window.__sessionRef.plugins) || []);
+      console.log('  plugin snapshot:', JSON.stringify(snap));
+      ok(false, 'no button to click — see the plugin snapshot above for the load error');
+    }
+
+    ok(jsErrors.length === 0, `no page JS errors (${jsErrors.slice(0, 2).join(' | ')})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e?.message || e); }
+  finally {
+    await browser.close().catch(() => {});
+    try { server.close(); } catch {}
+    await rm(project, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+    await rm(process.env.CB_HOME, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed  (screenshots: ${SHOTS})`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/live-powershell-write-repro.mjs
new 2254be1ef51cb5c83d08265d
+/**
+ * REPRODUCTION — "the agent writes files with PowerShell (Set-Content / here-strings /
+ * redirection) instead of <write>/<edit>, and the reviewer keeps flagging it."
+ * (User report, prod genai.mil/Gemini. Image #9.)
+ *
+ * The worker prompt (src/agent/worker-prompt.mjs) EXPLICITLY forbids shell file writes, and
+ * the run tool does NOT block them — so the model CAN do it and the write succeeds. This drives
+ * the real worker against the live proxy on a file-creation + shell task (the mix that primes
+ * "shell mode") and reports whether the behaviour reproduces.
+ *
+ *   node test/live-powershell-write-repro.mjs                 # Sonnet (default)
+ *   CB_MODEL=claude-haiku-4-5 node test/live-powershell-write-repro.mjs   # weaker adherence
+ *   CB_SEED_SETCONTENT=1 node test/live-powershell-write-repro.mjs        # prime history w/ a prior Set-Content turn
+ *
+ * Skips gracefully (exit 0) if the proxy at 127.0.0.1:8080 isn't reachable.
+ * Writes a UI-shaped transcript to D:\tmp\cb-transcripts\ for reading.
+ *
+ * PORTED 2026-08-14 from runPromptedAgent (src/agent/prompted.mjs, deleted 2026-07-01) to
+ * runMinimalWorker. TWO HALVES OF THE ORIGINAL REPORT, and only one survives:
+ *   · The WRITE half — does the model write files with Set-Content / a here-string / redirection
+ *     instead of <write>/<edit>? — is intact, and is what this probe still measures.
+ *   · The LOOP half — the continuous reviewer flagged the write FIRST-PERSON, the finding was
+ *     injected into history as the agent's own note, and the agent did it again — is GONE: the
+ *     continuous reviewer was removed with the old loop. The reviewer counters below stay so the
+ *     original report is still legible, but they can only ever read 0, and the printout says so.
+ * Also repointed: the shell verb is <run> (alias <exec>), not <powershell>, which no longer parses.
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import { runMinimalWorker } from '../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs';
+import { runGit, commitAll } from './_git.mjs';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+
+const PROXY = process.env.CB_PROXY || 'http://127.0.0.1:8080';
+const MODEL = LIVE_MODEL;
+const SEED = /^(1|true|on|yes)$/i.test(process.env.CB_SEED_SETCONTENT || '');
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const OUTDIR = 'D:\\tmp\\cb-transcripts';
+
+// Detect a PowerShell command that WRITES file content (the forbidden pattern). Excludes the
+// sanctioned out_file mechanism (that's a tool arg, not in the command string).
+const WRITE_CMDLET = /\b(Set-Content|Add-Content|Out-File|Tee-Object|New-Item)\b|`?@["'][\s\S]*?["']@|(^|\s)>>?\s*['"]?[\w./\\-]+/im;
+const isPsWrite = (cmd) => !!cmd && WRITE_CMDLET.test(cmd);
+// The shell verb is <run> (alias <exec>) — <powershell> was never a verb the current parser accepts, so
+// matching on that name alone would count ZERO shell calls and report "not reproduced" unconditionally.
+// The tool underneath is still powershell.exe, so the cmdlet detector above is unchanged.
+const isShellCall = (e) => /^(run|exec|powershell)$/i.test(e.verb || e.name || '');
+const flagsPsWrite = (text) => /set-content|out-file|here-string|here string|powershell|redirect|@"|@'/i.test(text || '')
+  && /\b(write|edit)\b|<write>|<edit>|write tool|should (use|have used)|instead of|mangle/i.test(text || '');
+
+async function main() {
+  try { const h = await fetch(PROXY + '/health'); if (!h.ok) throw new Error('bad health'); }
+  catch { console.log(`SKIP — proxy not reachable at ${PROXY}/health`); process.exit(0); }
+
+  await mkdir(OUTDIR, { recursive: true });
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-pswrite-'));
+  // A tiny but real Node project so the agent has a genuine workspace to extend.
+  await runGit(dir, ['init', '-q']);
+  await runGit(dir, ['config', 'user.email', 't@t']);
+  await runGit(dir, ['config', 'user.name', 'T']);
+  await runGit(dir, ['checkout', '-q', '-b', 'CODE_BOSS_repro']);
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'package.json'), JSON.stringify({ name: 'svc', version: '1.0.0', type: 'module', main: 'index.js', scripts: { start: 'node index.js' } }, null, 2) + '\n', 'utf8');
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'index.js'), "import { greet } from './src/util.js';\nconsole.log(greet('world'));\n", 'utf8');
+  await mkdir(path.join(dir, 'src'), { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'src', 'util.js'), "export const greet = (n) => `hello, ${n}`;\n", 'utf8');
+  await commitAll(dir, 'base project');
+
+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', onRateLimit: (ms) => console.error(`[rate-limit] ${ms}ms`) });
+
+  // A task that creates SEVERAL new files AND runs a shell command — the mix that primes the
+  // model into "shell mode" and tempts a Set-Content / here-string write.
+  const task = [
+    'Extend this small Node service. Please:',
+    '1. Create src/config.js exporting a default config object: { port: 3000, logLevel: "info", appName: "svc" }.',
+    '2. Create src/logger.js exporting a `log(level, msg)` function that prints `[level] msg` and a `banner()` that prints the appName + port from config.',
+    '3. Update index.js to import the config + logger and call banner() at startup.',
+    '4. Run `node index.js` to verify it prints the banner, and report the output.',
+  ].join('\n');
+
+  // Optional: prime the history with a prior assistant turn that used Set-Content, to test the
+  // self-reinforcing bleed (does the model CONTINUE the pattern once it's in its own voice?).
+  // The old loop took these as leading entries of `messages`; the minimal worker reads only the LAST
+  // message as the developer's ask and takes prior turns as `history`, so they are passed there instead.
+  const seedMessages = SEED ? [
+    { role: 'user', content: 'Quick warmup: create a NOTES.md file with the text "scratch".' },
+    { role: 'assistant', content: 'Done.\n<remote-workspace>\n<run>Set-Content -Path NOTES.md -Value "scratch"</run>\n</remote-workspace>' },
+    { role: 'user', content: '(tool) wrote NOTES.md (1 line).' },
+  ] : [];
+
+  const events = [];
+  console.log(`Repro: PowerShell file-write vs ${MODEL} @ ${PROXY}${SEED ? '  [seeded w/ prior Set-Content]' : ''}`);
+  console.log(`project: ${dir}\n`);
+
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  let result = null, error = null;
+  try {
+    result = await runMinimalWorker({
+      adapter, model: MODEL, projectDir: dir,
+      messages: [{ role: 'user', content: task }],
+      history: seedMessages,
+      maxTurns: 18,
+      // DROPPED in the prompted.mjs → minimal-worker port, both features that no longer exist:
+      //   parallelCritic     — the continuous reviewer (the flagging side of the original loop)
+      //   enforceMinTaskSize — belonged to the task system (src/agent/tasks.mjs), removed
+      // requireBranch/requireApproval are kept: the worker still forwards them to the tool layer,
+      // which only calls them when they are functions, so `false` is the same "no gate" it always was.
+      requireBranch: false, requireApproval: false,
+      onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e),
+      onPhase: () => {},
+      onLog: () => {},
+    });
+  } catch (e) { error = e?.message || String(e); }
+  const wallMs = Date.now() - t0;
+
+  // ── Analysis ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+  const psCalls = events.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && isShellCall(e));
+  const psWrites = psCalls.filter((e) => isPsWrite(e.args?.command ?? e.args?.cmd));
+  const writeToolCalls = events.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && /write|edit/.test(e.verb || e.name || ''));
+  // Always empty now — the continuous reviewer that produced these was removed. Kept so the shape of
+  // the original report survives; the printout labels it rather than showing a bare 0.
+  const reviewerNotes = events.filter((e) => e.type === 'reviewer-note');
+  const reviewerFlagsWrite = reviewerNotes.filter((e) => flagsPsWrite(e.content));
+
+  // ── UI-shaped transcript (read it for flow) ──────────────────────────────────
+  const lines = [];
+  lines.push(`# PowerShell-write repro — ${MODEL}${SEED ? ' (seeded)' : ''}`);
+  lines.push(`task: create config.js + logger.js + wire index.js + run node\n`);
+  for (const e of events) {
+    if (e.type === 'assistant-text' && (e.content || '').trim()) lines.push(`\n**agent:** ${e.content.trim()}`);
+    else if (e.type === 'tool-call') {
+      const a = e.args || {};
+      const arg = a.command != null ? a.command : (a.path != null ? a.path + (a.content != null ? ` …(${String(a.content).length} chars)` : '') : JSON.stringify(a).slice(0, 120));
+      const flag = (isShellCall(e) && isPsWrite(a.command ?? a.cmd)) ? '   ⚠ POWERSHELL FILE-WRITE' : '';
+      lines.push(`\n\`${e.verb || e.name}\` ${String(arg).replace(/\n/g, ' ⏎ ').slice(0, 200)}${flag}`);
+    } else if (e.type === 'tool-result') lines.push(`  → [${e.status || 'ok'}] ${String(e.content || '').replace(/\n/g, ' ').slice(0, 160)}`);
+    else if (e.type === 'reviewer-note') lines.push(`\n🔍 **self-review (${e.level || 'local'}):** ${String(e.content || '').replace(/\n/g, ' ').slice(0, 400)}${flagsPsWrite(e.content) ? '   ⚠ FLAGS PS-WRITE' : ''}`);
+    else if (e.type === 'diff') lines.push(`  ± diff ${e.path || ''}`);
+  }
+  const tag = new Date(t0).toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-').slice(0, 19);
+  const outFile = path.join(OUTDIR, `powershell-write-repro-${MODEL}-${SEED ? 'seeded-' : ''}${tag}.md`);
+  await writeFile(outFile, lines.join('\n'), 'utf8');
+
+  // ── Verdict ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+  console.log(`\n${'─'.repeat(60)}`);
+  console.log(`turns=${result?.stats?.turns ?? '?'}  terminated=${result?.terminated ?? error}  wall=${(wallMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s`);
+  console.log(`shell (<run>) calls:         ${psCalls.length}`);
+  console.log(`  └─ that WRITE files:       ${psWrites.length}   ${psWrites.length ? '⚠ FORBIDDEN (should use <write>/<edit>)' : ''}`);
+  console.log(`<write>/<edit> tool calls:   ${writeToolCalls.length}`);
+  console.log(`reviewer findings (total):   ${reviewerNotes.length}   (the continuous reviewer was removed — this is always 0)`);
+  console.log(`  └─ flagging the PS-write:  ${reviewerFlagsWrite.length}`);
+  if (psWrites.length) {
+    console.log(`\nThe forbidden PowerShell writes:`);
+    for (const e of psWrites) console.log(`  • ${String(e.args.command ?? e.args.cmd).replace(/\n/g, ' ⏎ ').slice(0, 140)}`);
+  }
+  if (reviewerFlagsWrite.length) {
+    console.log(`\nReviewer first-person flags (these bleed into history):`);
+    for (const e of reviewerFlagsWrite) console.log(`  • ${String(e.content).replace(/\n/g, ' ').slice(0, 160)}`);
+  }
+  const reproduced = psWrites.length >= 1;
+  // The reviewer term can no longer contribute (see the header) — a LOOP is now purely "it did it twice".
+  const loop = psWrites.length >= 2 || (psWrites.length >= 1 && reviewerFlagsWrite.length >= 1);
+  console.log(`\nREPRODUCED (model used PowerShell to write a file): ${reproduced ? 'YES' : 'no'}`);
+  console.log(`LOOP (repeated the write):                          ${loop ? 'YES' : 'no'}`);
+  console.log(`transcript: ${outFile}`);
+
+  try { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); } catch {}
+  process.exit(0);

+/**
+ * LIVE Puppeteer + real model — EDGE-FINDING by observation (not code review). Drives the real UI
+ * against the local Claude proxy through the new features and probes edge interactions, capturing JS
+ * errors + asserting states. NON-repo project (so shadow-git undo applies).

+ *

+function findChrome() {
+  const c = process.platform === 'win32'

+async function main() {
+  console.log(describeCreds('model=' + MODEL));
+  try { const r = await fetch(PROXY + '/v1/models', { headers: { ...PROXY_HEADERS, 'x-api-key': PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', 'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01' } }); if (!r.ok && r.status !== 404) throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status); }

+    await page.waitForFunction(() => !!document.getElementById('input') && !!document.getElementById('reviewLabel'), { timeout: 8000 });

+    ok(!(await vis(page, '#tabbtn-cognition')) && !(await vis(page, '#tabbtn-subagents')), 'initial: on-demand side-tabs hidden');

+    ok(await page.$eval('#reviewToggle', (e) => e.checked), 'review toggled on');

+    await page.click('#editToggle');    // discuss ON

+    await page.click('#editToggle');    // discuss OFF again
+    ok(await vis(page, '#reviewLabel'), 'review controls return when discuss off');
+
+    // ── A: review → propose → approve → implement ──
+    // (Robust to the model's choice: it may propose with a <checklist> OR in prose; both are valid.
+    //  We wait for the PROPOSE run to finish, not specifically a checklist.)
+    const idle = (ms = 240000) => page.waitForFunction(() => { const s = window.__sessionRef; return s && (s.status === 'idle' || s.status === 'done' || s.status === 'error'); }, { timeout: ms, polling: 500 }).catch(() => {});
+    const orig = await readFile(path.join(dir, 'app.js'), 'utf8');
+    await page.type('#input', 'Add these to app.js, step by step: a GET /health endpoint, a GET /version endpoint, a GET /ping endpoint, request logging, and a graceful-shutdown handler.');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await sleep(1500); await idle(); await sleep(500);
+    ok((await readFile(path.join(dir, 'app.js'), 'utf8')) === orig, 'A: app.js UNTOUCHED during the propose (read-only) run');
+    // Checklists now live in the side-tab (panel open by default), not the footer #checklistPanel.
+    const hadChecklist = (await page.$$eval('#checklistTab .checklist-item', (e) => e.length).catch(() => 0)) >= 1;
+    if (hadChecklist) ok(true, 'A: a checklist appeared (Checklists tab) for the proposal');
+    else note('propose run produced a PROSE proposal (no <checklist> tag) — the checklist tab only populates when the model tags it');
+    const proposalLen = await page.$$eval('.bubble', (els) => (els[els.length - 1]?.textContent || '').length).catch(() => 0);
+    ok(proposalLen > 40, `A: a proposal message was produced (${proposalLen} chars)`);
+    if (!(await vis(page, '#approveImplementBtn'))) note('approve button NOT shown after a propose run completed'); else ok(true, 'A: approve button shown after the proposal');
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(SHOTS, 'edge-A-proposal.png') });
+    await page.click('#approveImplementBtn').catch(() => note('approve button not clickable'));
+    let after = orig;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 300; i++) { after = await readFile(path.join(dir, 'app.js'), 'utf8'); if (/\/health/.test(after)) break; await sleep(1000); }
+    ok(after !== orig && /\/health/.test(after), 'A: after approve, the implement run changed app.js (proposal applied)');
+    await idle(); await sleep(2500);
+    if (hadChecklist) {
+      // After implement + task-close, the checklist tab follows the current task — it must NOT
+      // linger showing the old proposal's unticked items (the per-task store drops a closed task's
+      // checklist on the task-close clear). Footer (#checklistPanel) stays hidden while panel open.
+      const undone = await page.$$eval('#checklistTab .checklist-item:not(.done)', (e) => e.length).catch(() => 0);
+      const footerShown = await vis(page, '#checklistPanel');
+      if (footerShown) note('footer #checklistPanel showed while the side panel was open (should be tab-only)');
+      else ok(true, `A: checklist did not linger stale after implement (tab undone=${undone}, footer hidden)`);
+    }
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => { const s = window.__sessionRef; return s && (s.status === 'idle' || s.status === 'done'); }, { timeout: 30000 }).catch(() => {});
+    if (await vis(page, '#approveImplementBtn')) note('approve button still visible after the IMPLEMENT run (should hide)');
+
+    // ── C: cancel mid-run → prompt, clean ──
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => { const s = window.__sessionRef; return s && (s.status === 'idle' || s.status === 'done'); }, { timeout: 30000 }).catch(() => {});

+    await page.waitForFunction(() => { const s = window.__sessionRef; return s && (s.status === 'idle' || s.status === 'done' || s.status === 'error'); }, { timeout: 40000, polling: 250 }).catch(() => {});
