+/**
+ * LIVE Puppeteer + real model — ORCHESTRATOR / interaction edge-finding by OBSERVATION.
+ * CB_ORCHESTRATOR on + a real git repo. Drives the "submit while it's working" flow + the
+ * feature×orchestrator interactions, capturing JS errors + state anomalies.
+ *   A: send a task → while running, send a 2nd message → it FORKS (managing) + a sub-agent appears,
+ *      no 409, no lost messages, the manager responds.
+ *   B: while busy, a REVIEW-ON message → 409 (must not fork to a write-mode sub-agent).
+ *   C: cancel while busy/forked → settles, clean.

+
+    // ── A: fork — a 2nd message while a task is running ──
+    const r1 = await (await post('Implement a small in-memory key/value store module kv.js with get/set/delete/has/keys and a few inline tests, then wire a /kv route into app.js. Work through it step by step.')).json();
+    ok(r1 && (r1.status === 'running' || r1.ok !== false), 'A: first task accepted');
+    const running = await waitStatus((s) => s === 'running', 30000);
+    ok(!!running, 'A: first task is running');
+    await sleep(1500);
+    const r2 = await post('Quick question while you work: what does app.js currently do?');
+    const r2b = await r2.json().catch(() => ({}));
+    ok(r2.status === 200, `A: 2nd message while busy accepted (status ${r2.status})`);
+    if (r2b.managing) ok(true, `A: 2nd message routed to the manager (forked=${r2b.forked})`); else note(`2nd message while busy was NOT managed (got ${JSON.stringify(r2b)}) — fork/orchestrator path may not have engaged`);
+    // a sub-agent should appear (the forked task) — the Sub-agents tab reveals on a subagent event
+    const forked = await page.waitForFunction(() => { const t = document.getElementById('tabbtn-subagents'); return t && t.offsetParent !== null; }, { timeout: 60000 }).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+    if (!forked) note('Sub-agents tab never revealed — the fork did not surface in the UI'); else ok(true, 'A: the fork surfaced (Sub-agents tab revealed)');
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(SHOTS, 'orch-A-fork.png') });
+
+    // ── B: review-on while busy → 409 (must not fork to a write-mode sub-agent) ──
+    let stillBusy = (await state()).status === 'running';
+    if (!stillBusy) note('agent already idle before the review-on-busy probe (timing) — skipping B'); else {
+      const rRev = await post('Add a /health route to app.js', { review: true });
+      ok(rRev.status === 409, `B: review-on message while busy is rejected 409, not forked (got ${rRev.status})`);
+    }
+
+    // ── C: cancel everything → settles clean ──
+    await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/cancel`, { method: 'POST' });
+    const settled = await waitStatus((s) => s === 'idle' || s === 'done' || s === 'error', 40000);
+    ok(!!settled, `C: cancel settled the session (status ${settled && settled.status})`);
+    const orphans = await page.$$eval('.bubble', (els) => els.filter((e) => e.classList.contains('streaming') || e.querySelector('.streaming')).length).catch(() => 0);
+    ok(orphans === 0, `C: no orphan streaming bubbles after cancel (${orphans})`);
+
+    ok(jsErrors.length === 0, `no JS errors across the session (${jsErrors.length}: ${jsErrors.slice(0, 3).join(' | ')})`);
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close();
+    server.close();
+    await sleep(400);
+    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { try { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); break; } catch { await sleep(300); } }
+  }
+  if (notes.length) { console.log(`\nPOSSIBLE EDGES (${notes.length}):`); notes.forEach((n) => console.log('  - ' + n)); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: MODEL, baseURL: PROXY }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' }, defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: dir, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic' });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const jsErrors = [];
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1280, height: 900 });
+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => jsErrors.push(String(e.message)));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => !!document.getElementById('input'), { timeout: 8000 });
+
+    // Turn Auto-approve OFF (propose-first). The label is "Auto-approve"; clicking toggles reviewToggle.
+    await page.evaluate(() => toggleReview());
+    const autoOff = await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('reviewToggle').checked === true && document.getElementById('reviewLabel').classList.contains('off'));
+    ok(autoOff, 'Auto-approve turned OFF (label struck-through, review mode on)');
+
+    // Propose a non-trivial change → propose run + #7 critics + revision. Drive via the UI's send()
+    // (not a direct POST) so _lastSendReview is set and the approve button surfaces on completion.
+    await page.evaluate((t) => { document.getElementById('input').value = t; send(); }, 'Add input validation to divide() so dividing by zero is handled, and add a /health route. Plan it.');
+    ok(true, 'propose sent via the UI (Auto-approve off → review)');
+    await waitStatus((s) => s === 'running', 20000);
+
+    // The plan-review-start row should appear while the critics read the code.
+    const started = await page.waitForFunction(() => !!document.querySelector('.row.plan-review'), { timeout: 180000 }).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+    if (started) ok(true, '#7: plan-review row appeared (adversarial critics engaged)'); else note('no plan-review row appeared — #7 critics did not run (check CB_PLAN_REVIEW / degradation)');
+
+    // Run settles (propose + critics + revision all inline).
+    const settled = await waitStatus((s) => s === 'idle' || s === 'done' || s === 'error', 300000);
+    ok(!!settled, `propose+review settled (status ${settled && settled.status})`);
+    await sleep(1500);
+
+    // App must be UNTOUCHED (propose is read-only — critics + revision included).
+    const appNow = await readFile(path.join(dir, 'app.js'), 'utf8');
+    ok(appNow === APP, '#7: app.js UNTOUCHED through propose + adversarial review (all read-only)');
+
+    // The plan-review outcome row is present (findings or "no concerns").
+    const prText = await page.$$eval('.row.plan-review', (els) => els.map((e) => e.textContent).join(' || ')).catch(() => '');
+    ok(/plan review/i.test(prText) || /hardening the plan/i.test(prText), `#7: plan-review surfaced in the UI ("${prText.slice(0, 80)}")`);
+
+    // Approve button should be available on the (hardened) plan.
+    const approve = await page.waitForFunction(() => { const b = document.getElementById('approveImplementBtn'); return b && b.offsetParent !== null; }, { timeout: 10000 }).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+    if (approve) ok(true, '#7: approve button shown after the hardened plan'); else note('approve button not shown after the propose+review run');
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(SHOTS, 'plan-review.png') });
+
+    // Approve → implement the hardened plan → app.js finally changes.
+    if (approve) {
+      await page.evaluate(() => approveImplement());
+      await waitStatus((s) => s === 'running', 20000);
+      const done2 = await waitStatus((s) => s === 'idle' || s === 'done' || s === 'error', 240000);
+      ok(!!done2, `implement settled (status ${done2 && done2.status})`);
+      await sleep(1000);
+      const appImpl = await readFile(path.join(dir, 'app.js'), 'utf8');
+      ok(appImpl !== APP, '#7: after approve, the hardened plan was IMPLEMENTED (app.js changed)');
+    }
+
+    ok(jsErrors.length === 0, `no JS errors across the session (${jsErrors.length}: ${jsErrors.slice(0, 3).join(' | ')})`);
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close();
+    server.close();
+    await sleep(400);
+    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { try { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); break; } catch { await sleep(400); } }
+  }
+  if (notes.length) { console.log(`\nPOSSIBLE EDGES (${notes.length}):`); notes.forEach((n) => console.log('  - ' + n)); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * LIVE end-to-end Puppeteer suite — drives the REAL code_boss UI against REAL
+ * Claude (Sonnet) via the local Anthropic proxy (http://127.0.0.1:8080).
+ *
+ * Not part of run-all (no `test-` prefix): needs the proxy + Chrome, and makes
+ * real (slow, nondeterministic) model calls. Assertions are STRUCTURAL (status
+ * returns to done, expected event types appear, files land on disk, ZERO JS
+ * errors). Model CONTENT choices are recorded, not asserted. Every
+ * manager-diagnostic (a parse miss = prompt/format drift vs Sonnet) is captured
+ * and reported — that's the point of the exercise.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/live-puppeteer-suite.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, readFile, mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 28950;
+const PROXY = (process.env.CB_PROXY || 'http://127.0.0.1:8080').replace(/\/+$/, '');
+// The proxy sits behind an access check: without these two headers every request is a 403, and this suite never ran.
+// From the environment only (the runner reads them from the git-ignored proxy.local.json) - never written here.
+const PROXY_HEADERS = process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_ID ? { 'CF-Access-Client-Id': process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_ID, 'CF-Access-Client-Secret': process.env.CB_CF_ACCESS_SECRET || '' } : {};
+const MODEL = LIVE_MODEL;
+const SHOTS = path.join(__dirname, 'live-puppeteer-screenshots');
+
+const records = [];     // { scenario, ok, note, events, durationMs }
+let jsErrors = [];
+
+function findChrome() {
+  const c = process.platform === 'win32' ? [
+    (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+  ] : ['/usr/bin/google-chrome', '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome'];
+  return c.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const chrome = findChrome();
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  // Proxy reachability.
+  // Endpoint reachability — a tiny real /v1/messages call (works for the local proxy AND a direct
+  // Anthropic-shaped endpoint; CB_API_KEY supplies x-api-key when the endpoint needs it).
+  try {
+    const r = await fetch(PROXY + '/v1/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { ...PROXY_HEADERS, 'x-api-key': process.env.CB_API_KEY || 'x', 'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01', 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ model: MODEL, max_tokens: 8, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'ok' }] }) });
+    if (!r.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status);
+  } catch (e) { console.error('ENDPOINT NOT REACHABLE at ' + PROXY + ' — (' + (e?.message || e) + ')'); process.exit(2); }
+
+  await mkdir(SHOTS, { recursive: true });
+
+  // Scratch project with a tiny real codebase for the agent to read/edit.
+  const project = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-live-'));
+  await mkdir(path.join(project, 'src'), { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'README.md'), '# calc\nA tiny calculator library. Entry point: src/math.js.\n', 'utf8');
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'package.json'), JSON.stringify({ name: 'calc', version: '1.0.0', main: 'src/math.js' }, null, 2), 'utf8');
+  await writeFile(path.join(project, 'src', 'math.js'), 'function add(a, b) {\n  return a + b;\n}\n\nmodule.exports = { add };\n', 'utf8');
+
+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: process.env.CB_API_KEY || undefined, extraHeaders: PROXY_HEADERS, onRateLimit: (ms) => console.error(`  [rate-limit] waiting ${ms}ms`) });
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace(
+    '</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: MODEL, baseURL: PROXY }) + ';</script></head>'
+  );
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: process.env.CB_API_KEY || 'proxy-adds-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], initialProject: project,
+  });
+
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  await page.setViewport({ width: 1320, height: 1000 });
+  page.on('pageerror', (e) => jsErrors.push(e.message));
+  // Ignore benign resource 404s (e.g. /favicon.ico) — only real script errors.
+  page.on('console', (m) => { if (m.type() === 'error' && !/Failed to load resource/i.test(m.text())) jsErrors.push('console: ' + m.text()); });
+
+  // ── helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+  const getStatus = () => page.evaluate(() => window.__sessionRef?.status);
+  const getEvents = () => page.evaluate(() => (window.__sessionRef?.events ?? []).map((e) => ({ type: e.type, taskId: e.taskId, kind: e.kind, reason: e.reason, fulfilled: e.fulfilled, senderRole: e.senderRole })));
+  const rawState = () => page.evaluate(() => window.__sessionRef ?? {});
+  async function waitDone(ms = 300000) {
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    // Wait for THIS run to actually START (status -> running) before watching
+    // for terminal — otherwise we'd see the PREVIOUS scenario's stale 'done'
+    // and return immediately, letting runs overlap.
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__sessionRef?.status === 'running', { timeout: 25000 }).catch(() => {});
+    while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) {
+      const s = await getStatus();
+      if (s === 'done' || s === 'idle' || s === 'error') return s;
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));
+    }
+    return 'TIMEOUT';
+  }
+  async function sendChat(text) {
+    await page.evaluate(() => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = ''; i.focus(); });
+    await page.type('#input', text);
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+  }
+  let shotN = 0;
+  const shot = async (name) => { try { await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(SHOTS, `${String(++shotN).padStart(2, '0')}-${name}.png`) }); } catch {} };
+
+  async function scenario(name, fn) {
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    console.log(`\n=== ${name} ===`);
+    let ok = true, note = '';
+    try { note = (await fn()) || ''; }
+    catch (e) { ok = false; note = 'THREW: ' + (e?.message || e); console.log('  ! ' + note); }
+    const evs = await getEvents().catch(() => []);
+    await shot(name.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '-').toLowerCase());
+    records.push({ scenario: name, ok, note, eventTypes: [...new Set(evs.map((e) => e.type))], durationMs: Date.now() - t0 });
+    console.log(`  -> ${ok ? 'OK' : 'FAIL'} ${note ? '(' + note + ')' : ''}  [${Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000)}s]`);
+  }
+
+  try {
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#input', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 800));
+    await shot('loaded');
+
+    // 1) Basic protocol: can Sonnet drive <remote-workspace> read tools?
+    await scenario('read-and-summarize', async () => {
+      await sendChat('Look at the files in this project (use your tools) and tell me in one sentence what src/math.js does. Do not change anything.');
+      const s = await waitDone();
+      const evs = await getEvents();
+      const usedTools = evs.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-call');
+      const answered = evs.some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text');
+      if (s === 'TIMEOUT') throw new Error('worker never reached a terminal state');
+      return `status=${s} usedTools=${usedTools} answered=${answered}`;
+    });
+
+    // 2) Write + task close + (optional) review.
+    await scenario('write-and-close-task', async () => {
+      await sendChat('Add a function subtract(a, b) that returns a - b to src/math.js and export it too. When the change is complete, close the task with a <next-task> summary.');
+      const s = await waitDone();
+      const evs = await getEvents();
+      const wrote = evs.some((e) => e.type === 'diff');
+      const closed = evs.some((e) => e.type === 'task-end');
+      const reviewed = evs.some((e) => e.type === 'review');
+      const onDisk = existsSync(path.join(project, 'src', 'math.js')) && /subtract/.test(await readFile(path.join(project, 'src', 'math.js'), 'utf8').catch(() => ''));
+      if (s === 'TIMEOUT') throw new Error('worker never reached a terminal state');
+      return `status=${s} diff=${wrote} taskEnd=${closed} review=${reviewed} subtractOnDisk=${onDisk}`;
+    });
+
+    // 3) Conversational intake → editable requirement card → capture.
+    await scenario('intake-to-card', async () => {
+      // The side panel defaults collapsed now — open the Assignments tab (auto-expands) before the
+      // inline intake form is reachable.
+      await page.evaluate(() => { selectSideTab('assignments'); openIntake(); });
+      await page.waitForFunction(() => { const f = document.getElementById('intakeForm'); return f && !f.classList.contains('hidden'); }, { timeout: 5000 });
+      await page.type('#intakeText', 'We need src/math.js to validate its inputs: add() and subtract() should throw a TypeError if either argument is not a finite number. Acceptance: calling add("x",1) throws.');
+      await page.click('#intakeSendBtn');
+      // Wait for either a manager bubble or a requirement card.
+      await page.waitForSelector('.bubble.intake-manager, .row.requirement-card', { timeout: 110000 });
+      // If only a clarifying question came back, answer once to push toward a card.
+      let card = await page.$('.row.requirement-card');
+      if (!card) {
+        await page.type('#intakeText', 'Yes, throw TypeError with a clear message. Apply to both add and subtract. That is the whole requirement.');
+        await page.click('#intakeSendBtn');
+        await page.waitForSelector('.row.requirement-card', { timeout: 110000 }).catch(() => {});
+        card = await page.$('.row.requirement-card');
+      }
+      if (!card) return 'no requirement-card produced (manager kept clarifying) — recorded';
+      const req = await page.$eval('.row.requirement-card .rc-field-requirement', (el) => el.value).catch(() => '');
+      // Capture the card.
+      await page.evaluate(() => { const b = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.row.requirement-card .rc-actions button')).find((x) => /capture/i.test(x.textContent)); if (b) b.click(); });
+      await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelectorAll('#backlogList .backlog-row .asg-play').length > 0, { timeout: 8000 }).catch(() => {});
+      const ready = await page.$$eval('#backlogList .backlog-row .asg-play', (els) => els.length > 0).catch(() => false);
+      return `cardRequirement="${req.slice(0, 60)}" capturedReady=${ready}`;
+    });
+
+    // 4) Work the captured assignment → acceptance gate.
+    await scenario('work-assignment-to-gate', async () => {
+      const hasWork = await page.$('#backlogList .asg-play');
+      if (!hasWork) return 'no ready assignment to work (intake did not capture) — skipped';
+      await page.click('#backlogList .asg-play');
+      const s = await waitDone();
+      const gate = await page.$('.row.assignment-gate');
+      const fulfilled = gate ? await page.$eval('.row.assignment-gate', (el) => el.classList.contains('ok')) : null;
+      const validatesOnDisk = /typeof|TypeError|isFinite|Number\.isFinite|isNaN/.test(await readFile(path.join(project, 'src', 'math.js'), 'utf8').catch(() => ''));
+      if (s === 'TIMEOUT') throw new Error('assignment run never reached a terminal state');
+      return `status=${s} gate=${!!gate} fulfilled=${fulfilled} validationOnDisk=${validatesOnDisk}`;
+    });
+
+    // 5) Codebase memory now lives in the side panel's Memory TAB (the old modal was retired).
+    await scenario('memory-panel', async () => {
+      const mem = await page.evaluate(async () => (await fetch('/api/memory')).json());
+      await page.evaluate(() => openMemory());   // selectSideTab('memory') — auto-expands the panel
+      await page.waitForFunction(() => { const p = document.getElementById('pane-memory'); return p && !p.classList.contains('hidden') && !!document.getElementById('memoryList'); }, { timeout: 5000 });
+      const boxes = await page.$$eval('#memoryList .bullet-box', (els) => els.length).catch(() => 0);
+      return `memoryEntries=${mem.entries?.length ?? 0} memoryListBoxes=${boxes}`;
+    });
+  } finally {
+    // ── Report ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    const full = await rawState().catch(() => ({}));
+    const allEvents = full.events ?? [];
+    const diags = allEvents.filter((e) => e.type === 'manager-diagnostic');
+    console.log('\n\n════════ LIVE SUITE REPORT ════════');
+    for (const r of records) console.log(`  [${r.ok ? 'OK ' : 'FAIL'}] ${r.scenario}  (${Math.round(r.durationMs / 1000)}s)  ${r.note}\n        events: ${r.eventTypes.join(', ')}`);
+    console.log(`\n  JS errors: ${jsErrors.length}`);
+    for (const e of jsErrors.slice(0, 10)) console.log('    - ' + e.slice(0, 200));
+    console.log(`\n  manager-diagnostic (prompt/format drift vs Sonnet): ${diags.length}`);
+    for (const d of diags) {
+      console.log(`    [${d.kind}] ${d.reason}`);
+      console.log('      raw: ' + String(d.raw || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 300));
+    }
+    console.log('\n  screenshots: ' + SHOTS);
+    await browser.close();
+    server.close();
+    await rm(project, { recursive: true, force: true });
+
+    const structuralFail = jsErrors.length > 0 || records.some((r) => !r.ok);
+    console.log(`\n════════ ${structuralFail ? 'STRUCTURAL ISSUES FOUND' : 'COMPLETED CLEAN'} ════════`);
+    process.exit(structuralFail ? 1 : 0);
+  }
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/live-quality-baseline.mjs
new d8515ccb3affabc4cc221e78
+/**
+ * Capture the SERIAL quality baseline against the real model (local Anthropic
+ * proxy). `live-` prefix → run-all.mjs skips it. Run this NOW to record how the
+ * current serial agent does on the fixed scenarios; later, run the continuous
+ * agent through the same harness and compareRuns(serial, continuous).
+ *
+ *   node test/live-quality-baseline.mjs
+ *
+ * Skips gracefully (exit 0) if the proxy at 127.0.0.1:8080 isn't reachable.
+ * Writes test/quality/baselines/serial-baseline.json (commit it).
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import { SCENARIOS } from './quality/scenarios.mjs';
+import { captureBaseline, makeUsageSink } from './quality/harness.mjs';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+
+const PROXY = process.env.CB_PROXY || 'http://127.0.0.1:8080';
+const MODEL = LIVE_MODEL;
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const OUT = path.join(__dirname, 'quality', 'baselines', 'serial-baseline.json');
+
+async function main() {
+  try { await fetch(PROXY + '/health'); }
+  catch { console.log(`SKIP — proxy not reachable at ${PROXY}/health`); process.exit(0); }
+
+  // Capture tokens via the adapter's onUsage hook (the streaming Anthropic
+  // adapter reports usage there, not on stream chunks).
+  const usageSink = makeUsageSink();
+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', onUsage: (u) => usageSink.add(u), onRateLimit: (ms) => console.error(`[rate-limit] ${ms}ms`) });
+  console.log(`Capturing SERIAL baseline: ${SCENARIOS.length} scenarios vs ${MODEL} @ ${PROXY}`);
+
+  const record = await captureBaseline({
+    adapter, model: MODEL, scenarios: SCENARIOS, label: 'serial-baseline',
+    capturedAt: new Date().toISOString(), usageSink,
+    projectFactory: () => mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-qbase-')),
+  });
+
+  await mkdir(path.dirname(OUT), { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(OUT, JSON.stringify(record, null, 2));
+
+  console.log('\nPer-scenario:');
+  for (const r of record.scenarios) {
+    console.log(`  ${r.success ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${r.id.padEnd(14)} ` +
+      `tokens=${r.tokensTotal} turns=${r.turns} tools=${r.toolCalls} wall=${(r.wallMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s` +
+      (r.notes ? `  (${r.notes})` : '') + (r.error ? `  ERROR ${r.error}` : ''));
+  }
+  const a = record.aggregate;
+  console.log(`\nAggregate: success ${(a.successRate * 100).toFixed(0)}%  tokens ${a.tokensTotal}  turns ${a.turns}  wall ${(a.wallMs / 1000).toFixed(1)}s`);
+  console.log(`Wrote ${OUT}`);
+  process.exit(0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/live-quality-compare.mjs
new cc996bae620567d001a32314
+/**
+ * Run the CURRENT build through the fixed quality scenarios against the live
+ * proxy and COMPARE to a committed baseline — the ship gate from NORTH-STAR.md:
+ * success >= baseline AND tokens <= 1.5x baseline. Reusable for every phase
+ * (Phase 1: kernel vs serial; later: continuous vs serial). `live-` prefix →
+ * run-all.mjs skips it. Skips gracefully (exit 0) if the proxy is down.
+ *
+ *   node test/live-quality-compare.mjs [label]      (label defaults to 'current')
+ *
+ * Writes test/quality/baselines/<label>.json (the current run) and exits 0 iff
+ * the gate is met (1 otherwise) so it can gate CI / a phase.
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import { SCENARIOS } from './quality/scenarios.mjs';
+import { captureBaseline, compareRuns, makeUsageSink } from './quality/harness.mjs';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+
+const PROXY = process.env.CB_PROXY || 'http://127.0.0.1:8080';
+const MODEL = LIVE_MODEL;

+    adapter, model: MODEL, scenarios: SCENARIOS, label,

+  }

+  console.log(`\nAggregate: success ${(a.successRateBaseline * 100).toFixed(0)}% → ${(a.successRateCurrent * 100).toFixed(0)}%  ·  tokens ×${a.tokensTotalRatio ?? '—'}`);

+  if (a.fixes.length) console.log(`Fixes: ${a.fixes.join(', ')}`);
+  console.log(`\nSHIP GATE (≥ success at ≤ 1.5× tokens): ${a.meetsGate ? 'PASS ✓' : 'FAIL ✗'}`);
+
+  const OUT = path.join(__dirname, 'quality', 'baselines', `${label}.json`);
+  await mkdir(path.dirname(OUT), { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(OUT, JSON.stringify(current, null, 2));
+  console.log(`Wrote ${OUT}`);
+  process.exit(a.meetsGate ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/live-review-propose.mjs
new ed3557026c54ff7c901680d6
+/**
+ * Live validation of REVIEW (propose-first) mode — the "approve the whole plan where I can see
+ * the diffs, then it does it all" vision (no per-edit modals). Plain worker + local Claude proxy:
+ *   1. review ON  + a change request → the agent PROPOSES (summary + checklist + diffs), makes
+ *      NO edits (file untouched), and raises NO approval modal.
+ *   2. review OFF (the "approve & implement" button) → it implements the proposal (file changes).
+ * `live-` prefix → run-all skips it. Skips (exit 0) if the proxy is down.
+ *   node test/live-review-propose.mjs   (env CB_PROXY, CB_MODEL default claude-sonnet-4-6)
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import { runGit, commitAll } from './_git.mjs';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const PROXY = process.env.CB_PROXY || 'http://127.0.0.1:8080';
+const MODEL = LIVE_MODEL;
+const PORT = 17855;
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+async function post(text, review) { const r = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text, model: MODEL, review }) }); return r.json().catch(() => ({})); }
+async function state() { return (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`)).json(); }
+async function waitIdle(ms = 300000) { const t0 = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { const s = await state(); if (['idle', 'done', 'error'].includes(s.status)) return s; await sleep(500); } throw new Error('timeout'); }
+const lastAssistant = (s) => (s.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && !e.subagentChannel).slice(-1).map((e) => e.content).join('');
+
+const APP = `const http = require('http');\n\nconst server = http.createServer((req, res) => {\n  if (req.url === '/') { res.writeHead(200); res.end('home'); return; }\n  res.writeHead(404); res.end('not found');\n});\n\nserver.listen(3000, () => console.log('up on 3000'));\n`;
+
+async function main() {
+  try { await fetch(PROXY + '/health'); } catch { console.log(`SKIP — proxy not reachable at ${PROXY}`); process.exit(0); }
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-review-'));
+  await runGit(dir, ['init', '-q']); await runGit(dir, ['config', 'user.email', 't@t']); await runGit(dir, ['config', 'user.name', 'T']);
+  await runGit(dir, ['checkout', '-q', '-b', 'CODE_BOSS_test']);
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'app.js'), APP, 'utf8'); await commitAll(dir, 'seed app.js');
+
+  const adapter = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: PROXY + '/v1', apiKey: () => 'x' });
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' }, defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: dir, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic' });
+  try {
+    const before = await readFile(path.join(dir, 'app.js'), 'utf8');
+
+    // 1) review ON → propose, change nothing
+    await post('Add a GET /health endpoint to app.js that returns 200 with {status:"ok"}.', true);
+    const s1 = await waitIdle();
+    const after1 = await readFile(path.join(dir, 'app.js'), 'utf8');
+    ok(after1 === before, 'review ON → app.js UNTOUCHED (it proposed, did not implement)');
+    const reply = lastAssistant(s1);
+    ok(/health/i.test(reply) && /(checklist|step|plan|propos|diff|```)/i.test(reply), 'the reply is a PROPOSAL (summary/checklist/diff), not just done');
+    ok(!(await state()).approvalPending, 'no per-edit approval modal was raised');
+
+    // 2) approve & implement → review OFF, it applies the proposal
+    await post('Approved — go ahead and implement the proposal you just described.', false);
+    await waitIdle();
+    const after2 = await readFile(path.join(dir, 'app.js'), 'utf8');
+    ok(after2 !== before && /\/health/.test(after2), 'after approve (review OFF) → it implemented the proposal (app.js has /health)');
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    await sleep(300);
+    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { try { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); break; } catch { await sleep(200); } }
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/live-skills-chain-puppeteer.mjs
new 63cf1811287a504238423723
+/**
+ * LIVE: Puppeteer + the REAL model — the part that matters for a LONG process.
+ *
+ * live-skills-puppeteer.mjs asks the three basic questions (use one, save one, react to a failure). This asks the
+ * ones that decide whether skills survive contact with the job they were built for — a twelve-step run through a web
+ * app that cannot be got through in one go:
+ *   1. CHAIN — with four short skills that call each other, does it drive them, rather than doing the steps itself?
+ *   2. A REFUSAL MID-CHAIN — when a link says "case 999 is not open — call app_open_case first", does the agent DO
+ *      that and finish the job? An unactionable error is the whole reason a long run dies in the middle.
+ *   3. NO REWORK — after a snippet stops on a failing command, does it answer from the record instead of re-running?
+ *   4. A BROKEN SKILL — one file in the folder does not load and the listing says why. Does it FIX the file?
+ *   5. A NEW SESSION — the promise is "neither of us has to work it out again". Server restarted, same project: is
+ *      the skill still listed, and still used?
+ *
+ * One OBSERVATION is printed rather than asserted: whether it saves a skill it was NOT told to save. The prompt
+ * invites it ("SAVE WHAT YOU WORK OUT"), but whether a given task is worth saving is a judgement call, and a red
+ * suite is the wrong way to carry a judgement call. The line is printed every run so the answer is visible.
+ *
+ * `live-` prefix → run-all skips it. Skips (exit 0) if the proxy or Chrome is unavailable.
+ *   node test/live-skills-chain-puppeteer.mjs        (env CB_PROXY, CB_MODEL — default claude-sonnet-4-6)
+ */
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile, readdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import { runGit, commitAll } from './_git.mjs';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { LIVE_MODEL } from './_live-model.mjs';
+import { PROXY_BASE, PROXY_HEADERS, PROXY_API_KEY, describeCreds } from './_proxy-creds.mjs';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-lchain-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { loadSkills } = await import('../src/agent/skills.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PROXY = PROXY_BASE || process.env.CB_PROXY || 'https://home-proxy.philhignight.com';
+const MODEL = LIVE_MODEL;
+const SHOTS = path.join(__dirname, 'live-skills-screenshots');
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const notes = [];

+function findChrome() {
+  const c = process.platform === 'win32'

+    : ['/usr/bin/google-chrome', '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome'];
+  return c.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });

+// Four short skills standing in for a run through a web app. Each leaves a trace the next one checks for, and every

+export const notFor = 'Not for editing a note that is already there.';

+    await page.waitForFunction(() => !!document.getElementById('input'), { timeout: 20000 });
+    // The 409 a busy server answers IS the idle signal: the input clears only when the message was accepted, and an
+    // assistant row appears several times DURING a run, so "a new row" would send the next message far too early.
+    const say = async (text, { timeout = 900000, quietMs = 12000 } = {}) => {
+      const deadline = Date.now() + timeout;
+      for (;;) {
+        await page.click('#input');
+        await page.evaluate(() => { document.getElementById('input').value = ''; });
+        await page.type('#input', text);
+        await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+        await sleep(1500);
+        if (await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('input').value.trim() === '')) break;
+        if (Date.now() > deadline) throw new Error('the agent never went idle, so this message was never sent');
+        await sleep(5000);
+      }
+      let last = -1, lastChange = Date.now();
+      for (;;) {
+        const n = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('#log .row').length);
+        if (n !== last) { last = n; lastChange = Date.now(); }
+        else if (Date.now() - lastChange > quietMs) break;
+        if (Date.now() > deadline) break;
+        await sleep(2000);
+      }
+      const shown = await page.evaluate(() => document.body.innerText);
+      if (!shown.includes(text.slice(0, 40))) throw new Error('the message never reached the agent: ' + text.slice(0, 60));
+      return shown;
+    };
+    return { s, page, say };
+  };
+

+/**
+ * LIVE: the skills maintainer with the REAL model (SKILLS-MAINTAINER-SPEC.md), through the real UI.
+ *
+ *   1. FIX - the project's stamp skill has an after-check that is out of date (the app writes VERSION.txt, the check
+ *      looks for RELEASE.txt). The developer asks for a stamp; the agent calls the skill; the check fails. After the
+ *      run, does the maintainer change the skill, with tests that pass (run here again, independently), and record
+ *      it? Does the next stamp then go through the skill cleanly?
+ *   2. KEEP - the developer has the agent do the same multi-step job twice, without asking for a skill. Is it kept as
+ *      one (by the maintainer, or by the agent itself - which one is reported)?
+ *   3. QUIET - a run with nothing in it to keep: the maintainer changes nothing.
+ *
+ * `live-` prefix: run-all skips it. Skips (exit 0) when the proxy or Chrome is not there. The project is KEPT (its
+ * path is printed) so the conversation can be resumed.

+export const notFor = 'Not for tagging git; it writes the version file only.';

+/**
+ * LIVE: Puppeteer + the REAL model — does the agent USE a skill it already has, SAVE one when it works something
+ * out, and REACT to what a snippet actually did?
+ *
+ * The mocked E2E (test-skills-e2e.mjs) proves the plumbing: the roster reaches the model, a skill is callable, the
+ * record comes back. Only a real model can answer the questions that decide whether the feature is worth having:
+ *   1. USE — with a skill in its listing that does exactly the job asked for, does it call the skill, or write the
+ *      same thing again from scratch? (The skill leaves a marker only it writes, so there is no ambiguity.)
+ *   2. SAVE — told to keep what it worked out, does it write a file that actually loads, with the before/after
+ *      checks the brief asks for?
+ *   3. REACT — after a snippet stops on a failing command, does its next turn speak to the REAL failure (the
+ *      record is right there) instead of guessing or re-running the same thing?
+ *
+ * `live-` prefix → run-all skips it. Skips (exit 0) if the proxy or Chrome is unavailable.
+ *   node test/live-skills-puppeteer.mjs        (env CB_PROXY, CB_MODEL — default claude-sonnet-4-6)

+  return c.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });

=== add test/live-skills-repair-puppeteer.mjs
new 1bce884174ebe26490c64193
+/**
+ * LIVE: Puppeteer + the REAL model — WHEN A SKILL IS WRONG.
+ *
+ * The first two live suites ask whether skills get used, saved and driven. This one asks the question that decides
+ * whether they are worth having in six months, when fifty developers have folders full of them and the apps have
+ * moved on underneath: a skill that is WRONG is worse than no skill, because it looks like knowledge. So —
+ *   1. A STALE SKILL — its before-check refuses because the app changed (config.ini became config.yaml). Whether it
+ *      repairs the file is OBSERVED, not asserted: staleness is deferred on this line by decision (skills here are
+ *      for the job in hand), and it only bites once an app has moved on. The observation is kept because the day
+ *      this moves to the migrated line, staleness is the first thing to handle and this is the measurement.
+ *   2. AN AFTER-CHECK FAILS — the skill RAN and did not achieve what it claims. That is the case where re-running
+ *      is exactly wrong. Does the agent understand the difference, or does it just call it again?
+ *   3. A CHAIN STOPS AT A LINK — "skill X stopped at the skill it called — skill Y DID NOT RUN…". Does the agent
+ *      act on the LINK that stopped, rather than on the flow it called?
+ *   4. A NAME THAT COULD NEVER WORK — asked to save a skill called read, does it heed the refusal and rename?
+ *
+ * `live-` prefix → run-all skips it. Skips (exit 0) if the proxy or Chrome is unavailable.
+ *   node test/live-skills-repair-puppeteer.mjs      (env CB_PROXY, CB_MODEL — default claude-sonnet-4-6)
+ */
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile, readdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';

+function findChrome() {
+  const c = process.platform === 'win32'
+    ? [(process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', 'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', 'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe']
+    : ['/usr/bin/google-chrome', '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome'];
+  return c.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+}

+// 1. STALE: written when the config was an .ini. The project has moved to .yaml, so its before-check refuses and

+const READ_SETTING = `export const purpose = 'Reads one setting out of the app config.';
+export const params = '{ key: string } — the setting name';
+export const returns = '{ value: string }';
+export const notFor = 'Not for secrets; this reads the plain config only.';
+export async function pre(args, api) {
+  api.assert(args.key, 'pass { key: "…" } — which setting to read');
+  api.assert(await api.exists('config.ini'), 'config.ini is not there — this project has no app config where this skill expects it');
+}
+export default async function (args, api) {
+  api.log('reading ' + args.key + ' from config.ini');
+  const t = await api.read('config.ini');
+  const m = new RegExp('^' + args.key + '\\\\s*=\\\\s*(.+)$', 'm').exec(t);
+  api.assert(m, args.key + ' is not in the config');
+  return { value: m[1].trim() };
+}
+export async function post(args, result, api) {
+  api.assert(result.value, 'came back with an empty value, which the config never contains');
+}

+    await page.waitForFunction(() => !!document.getElementById('input'), { timeout: 20000 });
+    const say = async (text, { timeout = 900000, quietMs = 12000 } = {}) => {
+      const deadline = Date.now() + timeout;
+      for (;;) {

+        if (await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('input').value.trim() === '')) break;
+        if (Date.now() > deadline) throw new Error('the agent never went idle, so this message was never sent');
+        await sleep(5000);

+    const fixed = afterSetting !== beforeSetting;
+    // NOT ASSERTED, by decision (2026-09-29): skills on this line are a short-term feature for the job in hand, and
+    // a stale skill only bites once the app has moved on underneath it — the "over time" problem. If this is pulled
+    // over to the migrated line, staleness gets handled there and this check is already written. Measured today: it
+    // answers the question by doing the job in the snippet and leaves the stale skill as it found it. (The contrast
+    // in phase 2 is the interesting part: an AFTER-check failure, whose message says "fix the skill rather than
+    // calling it again", does get the skill fixed. The wording drives it either way.)
+    note(`a stale skill (its before-check refuses because the app moved): it ${fixed ? 'REPAIRED the file' : 'worked around it and left the file stale'}`);

+    const calls = (seg2.match(/export_ledger\(/g) || []).length;
+    ok(calls <= 3, `and it did not just call the skill again and again (${calls} mentions)`);
+    const afterExport = await readFile(path.join(sdir, 'export_ledger.mjs'), 'utf8');
+    note(`after an after-check failure it ${afterExport !== beforeExport ? 'FIXED the skill' : 'did not change the skill'}`);
+

+    ok(jsErrors.length === 0, `no JS errors in the page (${jsErrors.length}: ${jsErrors.slice(0, 2).join('; ')})`);
+    await writeFile(path.join(SHOTS, 'repair-transcript.txt'), await page.evaluate(() => document.body.innerText), 'utf8');
+    console.log(`(transcript: ${path.join(SHOTS, 'repair-transcript.txt')})`);
+  } finally {
+    try { await browser.close(); } catch {}

+    await sleep(300);
+    await cleanupDir(dir);
+  }
+  if (notes.length) console.log('\nobservations:\n' + notes.map((n) => '  · ' + n).join('\n'));
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

+/**
+ * LIVE (real model via the proxy): a SQL-style plugin loads its rows as test data through host.use('pii-testdata')
+ * and the REAL agent works with the row tokens — reads the result, hands a token to <write>, and the file on disk
+ * holds the real value while no request ever carried it. `live-` prefix → run-all skips it; skips cleanly if the
+ * proxy or Chrome is unavailable. Run: node test/live-sql-testdata.mjs
+ */
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';

+const PLUGIN = `export default {
+  description: 'SQL: run a query against the enrollment database. Results are loaded as test data and shown as tokens.',
+  dependencies: { 'pii-testdata': true },
+  tools: [{ verb: 'sql_query', name: 'sql_query',
+    schema: { description: 'Run a SQL query. The rows come back as test-data TOKENS (#@q1.<row>.<column>#<id>) which you can pass to any tool as if they were the values.', parameters: { type: 'object', properties: { sql: { type: 'string', description: 'the query' } }, required: ['sql'] } },
+    impl: async (args, ctx) => {
+      const rows = ${JSON.stringify(ROWS)};
+      await ctx.host.use('pii-testdata').loadFamily({ projectDir: ctx.projectDir, label: 'q1', description: args.sql, members: rows });
+      return { content: 'query: ' + args.sql + '\\n' + Object.entries(rows).map(([k, r]) => k + ': ' + Object.entries(r).map(([c, v]) => c + '=' + v).join(', ')).join('\\n') };
+    } }],
+};

+async function main() {

+  const adapter = { ...inner, completeStream: (req) => { requests.push(req); return inner.completeStream(req); }, complete: (req) => { requests.push(req); return inner.complete(req); } };
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: PROXY, apiKey: PROXY_API_KEY || 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' }, defaultModel: MODEL, port: PORT, initialProject: project, modelsList: [{ id: MODEL }], adapter, apiFormat: 'anthropic' });

+  const send = async (text) => { await waitIdle(); await page.evaluate((t) => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = t; i.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); i.focus(); }, text); await page.keyboard.press('Enter'); await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1500)); };
+  const toolEvents = () => page.evaluate(() => (session.events || []).filter((e) => /^tool-/.test(e.type)).map((e) => ({ t: e.type, v: e.name || e.verb, s: e.status, c: String(e.content || '').slice(0, 2000), a: e.args ? JSON.stringify(e.args).slice(0, 1500) : '' })));
+  // THE TRANSCRIPT, not the screen: the chat reveals masked tokens to the developer (revealMasked is on by default),
+  // so the bubble's text shows VALUES the model never saw. What the model said is the assistant-text events.
+  const lastReply = () => page.evaluate(() => { const ev = session.events || []; let i = ev.length - 1; while (i >= 0 && ev[i].type !== 'user-message') i--; return ev.slice(i + 1).filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').map((e) => String(e.content || '')).join('\n').trim(); });
+  const reqText = () => requests.map((r) => JSON.stringify(r)).join('\n');
+  const TOK = (p) => new RegExp('#@' + p.replace(/\./g, '\\.') + '(?:~[^#\\s]+)?#[0-9a-z]{7}');   // with the value's format (2324)
+  try {
+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => note('pageerror: ' + e.message));
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 850 });
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => (window.__sessionRef || {}).status != null || true, { timeout: 5000 }).catch(() => {});
+
+    console.log('\n[1] the agent runs the query, reads tokens, and hands one to <write>');
+    await send('Use the sql_query tool to run: SELECT * FROM enrollees. Then create the file out.txt containing ONLY the last_name value of row r1 (use the token the tool gave you — the system substitutes the real value when the file is written). Finally tell me which token you wrote.');
+    await waitIdle(300000);
+    const evs = await toolEvents();
+    note('tools: ' + evs.filter((e) => e.t === 'tool-call').map((e) => e.v).join(' → '));
+    // The agent may call the plugin tool by TAG or from inside a <js> snippet (plugin tools are js functions too —
+    // sonnet chose the snippet on the first live run). Either is the same contract; find the call and its result.
+    const viaTag = evs.some((e) => e.t === 'tool-call' && e.v === 'sql_query');
+    const jsCalls = evs.filter((e) => e.t === 'tool-call' && e.v === 'js_eval' && /sql_query\s*\(/.test(e.a));
+    ok(viaTag || jsCalls.length > 0, `the agent called sql_query (${viaTag ? 'by tag' : jsCalls.length ? 'from <js>' : 'not at all'})`);
+    const qres = evs.filter((e) => e.t === 'tool-result' && (e.v === 'sql_query' || (e.v === 'js_eval' && /#@q1\./.test(e.c)))).pop();
+    ok(!!qres && TOK('q1.r1.last_name').test(qres.c) && !RAW.some((v) => qres.c.includes(v)), `the query result reached the transcript as tokens only (${(qres && qres.c || '').replace(/\n/g, ' | ').slice(0, 100)})`);
+    // the write is a tag verb or a write(...) inside a <js> snippet (sonnet's usual choice): either way the token is in the call
+    const wcall = evs.filter((e) => e.t === 'tool-call' && (/write/.test(e.v) || (e.v === 'js_eval' && /\bwrite\s*\(/.test(e.a)))).pop();
+    ok(!!wcall && TOK('q1.r1.last_name').test(wcall.a), `the write call carries the TOKEN (${(wcall && wcall.a || '').slice(0, 100)})`);
+    const fileText = await readFile(path.join(project, 'out.txt'), 'utf8').catch(() => null);
+    ok(fileText != null && fileText.trim() === 'Okonkwo-Zed', `out.txt holds the REAL value via H2 (${JSON.stringify(fileText)})`);
+    const leaked = RAW.filter((v) => reqText().includes(v));
+    ok(leaked.length === 0, `no request to the model ever carried a row value (${requests.length} requests; leaked: ${leaked.join(',') || 'none'})`);
+    const reply = await lastReply();
+    ok(TOK('q1.r1.last_name').test(reply), `the agent names the token it used (${reply.slice(0, 120)})`);
+    ok(!RAW.some((v) => reply.includes(v)), 'and never a value in its reply');
+
+    console.log('\n[2] a follow-up question about the data stays in tokens');
+    await send('Which row has the other last name? Answer with its token only.');
+    await waitIdle(180000);
+    const reply2 = await lastReply();
+    ok(TOK('q1.r2.last_name').test(reply2) && !RAW.some((v) => reply2.includes(v)), `the agent answers with the r2 token (${reply2.slice(0, 100)})`);
+    ok(RAW.filter((v) => reqText().includes(v)).length === 0, 'still no value in any request');
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(ROOT, 'test', 'live-sql-testdata.png') }).catch(() => {});
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(project); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();
+
=== end
